# Life and Miracles of Sāmu’ēl of Wāldebbā (Vie et miracles de Sāmu’ēl de Wāldebbā)

**Author**: Anonymous  
**Series**: PO Tome 53  
**Columns**: pp. 6–286  

[p. 6] {#po.53.p6}

### LIFE OF SAMU'ËL
ገድለ ፡ ሳሙኤል ።
Invocation to the Trinity

1 In the name of God the first, to whose first existence there is no beginning, nor end to the last existence, who alone has a unique name; three in his uniqueness, one single God in his triplicity. The Father, we know through his Son that he is the Father of the latter; in the Son we believe as he is his Son, he who exists from the beginning with his Father in the divinity of the latter; to the Holy Spirit we offer adoration as he is the Spirit of the Father and of the Son; they are alike and equal all three, their nature being one. Having received this sword whose edge is triple, we shall cut down the one who does not believe in it. He who denies the Son is not with the Father, and the Father neither, he does not know him. Whom would he call Father, he who does not believe in the Son? Likewise, he who does not believe in the Holy Spirit is not with the Father and the Son, for (the Holy Spirit) is the Spirit of the Father and of the Son, inferior neither in glory nor in greatness to the Father and the Son. The Father (is) perfect, the Son (is) perfect, the Holy Spirit (is) perfect. Consubstantial is the Trinity, without addition and there will never be added to it; by it were made the heaven, the earth, and every creature; by it was accomplished everything that appears and does not appear. By always carrying this Trinity, we shall be alive unto the ages of ages, amen. Prologue

2 We begin with the help of God and with his beautiful gift to write the (spiritual) combat and the beautiful history of our just father, the glorious, learned, accomplished, pure and elevated, vigilant and chosen abba Sämu’el, (man of God) clothed in purity and a luminous garment, who accomplished his (spiritual) combat well and died on the twelfth of the month of Tähsäs. May the blessing of his prayer be with us, amen. 3 Listen, believing brothers whom Our Lord Jesus Christ has redeemed by his blood, that we may make known to you the history of the just one which has been heard in our days, the star of the desert which appeared in our region, the pure branch which came out of the trunk of our house, Israelite without guile (Jn 1:47) who grew up with the breasts of our mother, whose name is mentioned at the four corners of our land; Sämu'ël the blessed who fled and turned away from the delights of the century, for whom the expanse of the desert was father and mother. The buffaloes, the lions, the elephants, and the boars were companions to him;

all the animals of the desert, God smiled (them) to him, at his feet; Sämu’el, our blessed father, whom God chose from his mother's womb and from his father's loins, according to what John says in his vision: Blessed are those who have been called to the wedding of his Lamb (Rev 19:9). Solomon also says: Distinguished among ten thousand. Bathed in milk, they who dwell in a basin of filled water (Song 5:10, 12) which flowed from the two joined breasts (?).

4 Our blessed father, your mother the Church praises you by saying: "Cluster that blossomed from the adolescent my brother, in the midst of my breasts he rests; in truth your soul rests inside the one who nourished you! (Song 1:13-14; 8:2)" Sämu’el our father, adorned star and luminous sun, whose appearance is lightning, who shines in all the ends of the universe! Father of pure fathers, treasure of purity and holiness, treasure of monastic life and virginity! How shall I praise, I whose knowledge is deficient, him whom God praised by saying: My hand shall help him and my arm shall strengthen him; the enemy shall not have the advantage over him and the iniquitous shall not harm him again; I shall cut down his enemies before his face and I shall overwhelm his adversaries; my mercy and my justice shall be with him (Ps 88[89]:21-25). He shall call upon me and I shall answer him. I am with him at the moment of his misfortune; I shall save him and glorify him. For long days I shall satisfy him and make him see my salvation (Ps 90[91]:15-16)?

5 Now, abba, I beseech you: bless yours and also the place of rest of your body. May the blessing of your holy hand, of your right faith, and of your firm prayer rest, cast its shadow, and dwell upon us all unto the ages of ages, amen.

### Prayer of the author to speak well
ኣቀድም ፡ አእኩቶቶ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ በእንተ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወበእንተኒ ፡ ኩልክሙ ፡ እስመ ፡ ተሰምዐት"'' ፡ ሃይማኖትክሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ዓለም ። ይትባረክ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ አቡሁ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፡ ወመ ድኀኒነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ዘወለደነ ፡ በብዝኀ ፡ ምሕረቱ ፡ ወለተስፋ ፡ እንተ ፡ ባቲ ፡ ነሐዩ ፡ በተንሥኦቱ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ እምነ ፡ ምውታን ፡ ለተዋርሶ ፡ ዘኢይበሊ ፡ ወዘኢይረኩስ ፡ ወዘኢይጸመሂ ፡ ዘዕቁብ ፡ ለነ ፡ ወለክሙ ፡ በሰማያት ፡ ለእለ ፡ ንትዐቀብ ፡ በኃይለ ፡ እግዚአብ ሔር ፡ ወበሃይማኖትክሙ ። ሰላም ፡ ወምሕረት ፡ ወጸጋ ፡ ወሣህል ፡ ታዕርፍ ፡ ላዕሌክሙ ። ኦአበዊየ ፡ ወአኀዊየ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ አምላክነ ፡ ይረሲክሙ ፡ ድል ዋነ ፡ ለሰሚዓ ፡ ቃሉ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ዘይቤ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ሀሎኩ ፡ አነ ፡ ህየ ፡ ትሄልዉ ፡ ምስሌየ ፡ ወትሬእዩ ፡ ስብሐትየ ፡ ወዘአቡየ ፡ ዘሀሎ ፡ እምቅድመ ፡ ይትፈጠር ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን'' ።
6 I begin the praise of God for Our Lord Jesus Christ and also for you all, for your faith has been heard in the whole universe: Blessed be God, the Father of Our Lord and Savior Jesus Christ, who has regenerated us in the greatness of his mercy for the hope in which we live thanks to the resurrection of Our Lord Jesus Christ from the dead, to inherit that which does not grow old, does not defile, and does not wither, which is kept for us and for you in the heavens; for us who are kept by the power of God and by your faith (1 Pet 1:3-5). May peace, mercy, grace, and clemency rest upon you; my fathers and my brothers, may Christ our God make you worthy to hear his holy word which says: "Where I am, there you shall be with me and you shall see my glory and that of my Father which was before the world was created (Jn 17:24), amen."

7 Come to me, my Lord Jesus Christ, well of intelligence, source of wisdom, guide of the blind and educator of the foolish (Rom 2:19-20). Send me your Holy Spirit, the principle of knowledge that moves in the heart of the wise; teach me, professor of hidden things, all the beautiful teachings that flow from you; open to me, Lord, the door of your treasure of love so that I may narrate a little of the history of your servant; give me, Lord, the hook of your Spirit so that I may cast it into the well of knowledge in order to draw from it the wine of knowledge; give light to the eyes of my heart and open the interior of my intelligence so that I may narrate some wonders of your beloved; make my lips and my stammering tongue speak by constraining my spirit which is not accustomed to it, you who spoke to Balaam through the mouth of a donkey (Num 22:22-30), to Jeremiah through the mouth of a gazelle, and to Eustathius through the horns of a stag. Speak through me also, through the sinner, so that I may recount the prowess of the (spiritual) combat of the just man and reveal the greatness of his virtue to these gathered (people) who lend an ear with love, who welcome the seed of your word like good earth that receives the seed; make them bear fruit: for a thirty, sixty, and hundredfold (Mt 13:8). Grant me the grace that with my word your word may hasten so that the listeners may draw a profit from your remarks according to your word which (is) in the holy Gospel, which you said to your disciples: "But the Spirit of truth, the Paraclete, the Holy Spirit whom the Father will send from heaven in my name, he will teach you everything and will remind you of everything that I have said to you (Jn 14:26)." You also said to them: "Having come, he, the Spirit of truth, will teach you all the truth (Jn 16:13)."
ርሐክሙ ፡ በኩሉ ፡ ጽድቅ ። በእንተ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ይፈቱ ፡ ልብየ ፡ ወይጽህቅ ፡ 'ለነጊረ ፡ ዜና" ፡ ጻድቅ ። ወባሕቱ ፡ የሐምም ፡ ልብየ ፡ በኢተምህሮ ፡ ቃል ፡ ዘያቀውመኒ ፡ ለተናግሮ ፡ አላ ፡ እሴፈዎ ፡ ለምሕረትከ ፡ እስመ ፡ ሠናያቲከ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ጻድቃኒከ ፡ ዘነበብኩ ፡ በአፈ ፡ ነቢይከ ፡ ወትቤ ። ወይፈኑ ፡ ለከ ፡ ረድ” ኤተ ፡ እመቅደሱ ፡ ወእ ምጽዮን ፡ ይትወከፍከ ፡ ወይዝክር ፡ ለከ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ መዋሥዕተከ ወያጥልል ፡ ለከ ፡ ሞርባነከ ። የሀብከ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ዘከመ ፡ ልብከ ። እስመ ፡ ፍቅ ርከ ፡ ይመውኦ ፡ ለሕማምየ ፡ ወጸሎቶሙ ፡ ለቅዱሳን ፡ አበውየ ፡ ያሴስል ፡ ድካመ ፡ ነፍስየ ፡ ወይጹግወኒ ፡ ኀይለ ። ቅስም ፡ ልስሐትየ ፡ እምባሕረ ፡ ጹው ፡ ዘተቀድኃ ፡ በጽርኃ ፡ ጽዮን ፡ ለአበዊነ ፡ ሐዋርያት ፡ ንፋሕ ፡ በላዕ ሌየ ፡ መንፈሰከ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ወአልዕል ፡ ቃልየ ፡ ለስባሔከ ፡ መጠነ ፡ ብየ ፡ ኣቄርብ ፡ ለከ ፡ አስትዓ ፡ ለክብረ ፡ ውዳሴከ ።
8 Because of that, my heart has the desire and the longing to narrate the history of the just one; but my heart suffers from not having learned the word that would enable me to speak, on the other hand I hope in your mercy, for your benefits (are) with your just ones, (to) you who spoke through the mouth of your prophet and said: May he send you help from his sanctuary, may he welcome you from Zion and may he remember for you all your sacrifices; may he make your offering fat for you; may God give you according to your heart (Ps 19[20]:3-5). Indeed your love shall overcome my suffering and the prayer of my holy fathers shall remove the weakness of my soul and shall gratify me with power. Raise my blandness with the sea of salt that was drawn from the chamber of Zion for our fathers the apostles. Breathe upon me your Holy Spirit and raise my voice for your glorifications; in proportion to what I have, I shall offer you a gift for the glory of your praise.
ቀረብኩ ፡ ለወጢን ፡ ከመ ፡፡ እንግር ፡ ዜናሁ ፡ ለጻድቅ ፡ እንዘ ፡ እፈርህ ፡ ወእርዕድ ፡ ወእብላ ፡ ለነፍስየ ፡ እፎ ፡ ደፈርኪ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትባኢ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ባህል ፡ ዘለውእቱ ፡ ዓምድ ፡ መብርሂ ፡ ዘያሐቱ ፡ ብርሃኑ ፡
9 I am about to begin narrating the history of the righteous one, fearing, trembling, and saying to my soul: «How have you dared to enter into this discourse which (is) for this illuminating pillar whose light shines in all

the ends of the universe, while there are people of sharp tongue who pick out the perfumes of the flower with the Old and the New (Testament)?» When those who are not of sharp tongue speak, applying themselves to the genre of writing to embellish the word, they are broken by vain pride. Those of sharp speech, while God does not approve that they speak, it is as if it had happened, they not having meditated on what our Savior says in the glorious Gospel: He who exalts himself will be humbled and he who humbles himself will be exalted (Mt 23:12), but will be glorified by those of stammering tongue. In the first days, those who were called «people of the Most High», whom for forty years (God) nourished with manna by the hand of his angel who guarded them, when they broke out against his prophet Moses, the Lord God of hosts said: «It is a stiff-necked people» (Ex 32:9). And when Our Lord entered Jerusalem, he did not want to be glorified by the wise nor by the learned in the Law, but he was glorified by the old men and the little children, and also by the stones which do not know speech; by the mouth of those, therefore, God was glorified (Mt 21:15; Lk 19:40). As for those who come in humility, like the prostitute woman to whom it fell to touch the foot of Our Lord and to wet (it) with her tears (Lk 7:37-38) saying: «Be merciful, Lord, toward me, a sinner and a transgressor, who am like the animals, have made myself like them and have subjected myself to the yoke of sin. Save me, Lord, from the hand of the Enemy and make me bear your light yoke (Mt 11:30). Open for me, Lord, the doors of your mercy; welcome me, I who have come to you hoping in the clemency of your mercy.» God accepts one who is like these.

10 Now, my fathers and my brothers, come, let us implore him with hands outstretched and with a pure heart, saying: «Show us, Lord, your clemency and give us, our God, your salvation (Ps 84[85]:8). Let us not be far from you, give us life and we will call upon your name (Ps 79[80]:19). Look toward your servants and toward your work, Lord; guide their children. May the light of the Lord our God be upon us and may he make the work of our hands prosper for us» (Ps 89[90]:16-17), he to whom (belongs) the glory in the ages of ages, amen. Eulogy of Sämu'el 11 Let us return then to the evocation of the praise of the righteous one, for behold the sheep are waiting to absorb the bread of divinity which is broken in this northern region. Rejoice, young men and young women, for behold your mother rejoices and calls (those who are) in flight far from union with her to draw the blood (of her Son) in the cup and (place) his body on the (holy) table, she about whom Solomon says: Wisdom has built for her a house and has erected for her seven pillars, she has slaughtered her sacrificial beasts, she has mixed her wine in the crater, she has prepared her table, she

has sent her servants, calling with a proclamation (come from) on high (Pr 9:1-3). Behold the king's daughter calls you, sons of thunder (Mk 3:17), people of the Diaspora (Jas 1:1), you for whom she offers with the crater the smoke of glorification, saying: «Behold my sons who serve you always, every day; expand the wineskins and open the mouths to receive from the table set in the ancient time which was made when the Passover of the lamb was prepared. It is not with the blood of an ox and a kid that you will be sprinkled, you, but with the holy blood (of Christ); not like a people of soft ones who are sprinkled with the blood of an ox and a kid with water, scarlet wool, and a sprig of hyssop (Heb 9:19), but a holy people of elect (1 Pet 2:9) (will be) your nature, you who will have been anointed with the blood of his Law. Behold the Scriptures have arrived to us suddenly and have drawn us with the nets of their sweetness; indeed behold she awaits the stranger, the new plant which has grown in the midst of two streams, which has flowered in the garden and which gives off fragrance in the deserts.»
ትዌድሰከ ፡ እምከ ፡ መርዓት ፡ ወትብለከ ፡ አስካል ፡ ዘጸገየ ፡ ዘወልደ ፡ እቱየ ፡ ሊተ ። አስካል ፡ ዘሐንበብከ ፡ እምሥርወ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ወጸገይከ ፡ ማእ ከለ ፡ እምከ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን” = ሳሙኤል ፡ ጽጌ ፡ ደንጐላት ፡ ዘቄላት ፡ አፈወ ፡ ውዳሴከ ፡ ይምዕዝ ፡ እምርጌ ፡ ወይጥዕም” ፡ እማእድ ፡ ሥሩዕ = ሳሙ ኤል ፡ ቅብአ ፡ ጽጌ ፡ ረዳ ፡ ዘይትሌዓል ፡ ምዕዘቱ ፡ መልዕልተ ፡ መልዕልት ፡ እስከ ፡ ሰማያት ፡ ሰብዐቱ ። ሳሙኤል ፡ ሐመረ ፡ ብዕል ፡ ዘኢጐድአከ ፡ ማዕ በል ። ሳሙኤል ፡ ኦመ ፡ ገነት ፡ ኅሩይ ፡ ዘሰከዩከ ፡ ፪አፍላግ ፡ እሉ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ መጽግየ ፡ ሕግ” ፡ ወኢያይበሰከ ፡ ነፋስ ፡ ሐሩር ። እፎ ፡ ጸገይከ ፡ ወእፎ ፡ ፈረይከ ፡ በዝንቱ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ አእዋመ ፡ ዘአይበሰ ፡ ወአቀጽለ ፡ ዘአንገፈ 'በከመ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ በራዕዩ” ፡ ወሶበ ፡ ጠቅዓ ፡ ቀዳማዊ ፡ መል አክ ፡ ወበእንተዝ ፡ ኮነ ፡ በረድ ፡ ወእሳት ፡ ምስለ ፡ ደም ፡ ድሙር ፡ ወወ ረደ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ምድር ፡ ወውዕየ ፡ ኩሉ” ፡ ሣዕር ፡ ሐመልሚል ። እፎ ፡ በጻሕከ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ምርሳይ ፡ እንዘ ፡ መጠነዝ ፡ ይትጓዳእ ፡ ማዕበለ ፡ ባሕር ። ወሶበ ፡ ጠቅዐ ፡ ካልእ ፡ መልአክ ፡ ወረደ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ባሕር ፡ እሳት ፡ ዐቢይ ፡ ዘመጠነ ፡ ደብር ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይነድድ ። ወኮነ ፡ ደመ ፡ ሣል ስተ ፡ እዴሃ ፡ ለባሕር ፡ ወሞተ ፡ ሣልስተ ፡ እዴሁ ፡ ለዘሀሎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ባሕር ፡ ዘተፈጥረ ፡ ዘቦ ፡ መንፈሰ ፡ ሕይወት ። ወሣልስተ ፡ እዴሆን ፡ ለአሕ ማር ፡ ማሰነ ። እፎ ፡ ጸገይከ ፡ ወእፎ ፡ ፈረይከ ፡ በዝንቱ ፡ ሐጋይ ፡ ዘእን በለ ፡ ዝናም” = ወሶበ ፡ ጠቅዐ ፡ ሣልስ ፡ መልአክ ፡ ወረደ ፡ እምሰማይ ፡ ኮከብ ፡ ዐቢይ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይነድድ ፡ ከመ ፡ እሳት ፡ ወወረደ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሣል ስተ ፡ እዴሆሙ ፡ ለአፍላግ ፡ ወለአንቅዕተ ፡ ማያት ። ወስሙ ፡ ለውእቱ ፡ ኮከብ ፡ ዕጐስታር ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ዕጐሥታረ ፡ ሣልስተ ፡ እዴሆሙ ፡ ለማያት ፡ ወብዙኅ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ዘሞተ” ፡ እምነ ፡ ምረሩ ፡ ለማያት ። ወአንተሰ ፡ ፈረይከ" ፡
12 Your mother, the Bride, praises you and says to you: A flowering cluster is the adolescent my brother for me (Ct 1:14), a cluster, you who have budded from the roots of Christianity and have flowered in the midst of your mother the Church. Sämu’el, lily of the valleys (Ct 2:1), the fragrance of your praise gives off more fragrance than perfume and is sweeter than a set table. Sämu'ël, rose oil whose aroma rises above the heights to the seventh heaven. Sämu'el, vessel of wealth that the waves do not sink. Sämu’el, chosen tree of the Garden which the four rivers have watered, which are the efflorescence of the Law and which the torrid wind does not dry up. How have you flowered and how have you borne fruit in these days which have dried up the trees and which have made the leaves fall? 13 According to what John says in his Vision: and when the first angel sounded the trumpet, because of that, there was hail and fire mixed with blood, and they fell upon the earth; a third of the earth was consumed and all green grass was consumed. How did you reach the port while the waves of the sea were clashing to this point? And when the second angel sounded the trumpet, a great fire the size of a mountain fell into the sea, burning. And a third of the sea became blood, and a third of what was in the sea, which had been created, which had a spirit of life, died, and a third of the boats perished. How have you flowered and how have you borne fruit in this summer without rain? And when the third angel sounded the trumpet, a great star fell from heaven, burning like fire, and it fell upon a third of the rivers and the springs of waters. And the name of the star is «wormwood», and a third of the waters became wormwood, and many (were) the men who died from the bitterness of the waters (Rev 8:7-11). As for you,


[p. 16] {#po.53.p16}
ብዙኀ ፡ ዝኅዙኃ ፡ ወንሕኑኀ ። ፍሬ ፡ ትሕትና ፡ ዘምስለ ፡ ትዕግሥት ። ፍሬ ፡ ጾም ፡ ዘምስለ ፡ አርምሞ ። ፍሬ ፡ ድንግልና ፡ ዘምስለ ፡ ምሕረት ። ዐቂቦ ፡ ንጽሕ ፡ ወኢዘምዎ ፡ በኩሉ ። ጸሎት ፡ 'ዘእንበለ ፡ ጽርዕት"! ። ንቅሀት ፡ ዘእን በለ ፡ ሀኬት ፡ ሌሊተ ፡ ወማዐልተ ። አፍቅሮ ፡ ቢጽ ፡ ከመ ፡ ነፍስከ ፡ ለኩሉ ፡ ለጻድቅ ፡ ወለኃጥእ ፡ ለሰብእ ፡ ወለእንስሳ ። የወሀት ፡ እንበለ ፡ ተጸውጎ ። ተአ ኅዎ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ተአንትሎ ። ዝኩሉ ፡ ፍረያተ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ኢኀጸ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ እምካልኡ ፡ አላ ፡ ተመዓክበ ፡ ፍሬሆሙ ፡ ወወሀቡ ፡ መዓዛ ፡ በበጾታሆሙ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይሜግቦሙ ፡ በዐቂበ ፡ ሕጉ ፡ ለልዑል ።፡
you have borne much fruit, in abundance and in quantity, fruit of humility which (is) with constancy; fruit of fasting which (is) with silence; fruit of virginity which (is) with mercy, the observance of purity and continence in everything; prayer without interruption; vigil without laziness night and day; love of neighbor as of yourself; for all — righteous and sinner, men and beasts — gentleness without cruelty; brotherly friendship with all without impatience. Of all these fruits of the Spirit one was not less than the other, but their fruit was doubled and they gave an aroma with each of their kinds, (Sämu'ël) surrounding them by the observance of the Law of the Most High.
ሳሙኤል ፡ ሐራ ፡ መዋኢ ፡ ፈረሰ ፡ ሃይማኖት ፡ ተጽዒኖ ፡ ወፅአ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይትቃተል ፡ ምስለ ፡ ሐራ ፡ ጽልመት ፡ እኩያን ፡ ሰሚዖ ፡ እምቃለ ፡ ጳውሎስ ፡ ዘይቤ ፡ እስመ ፡ ቀትልክሙ ፡ ኢኮነ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ዘሥጋ ፡ ወደም ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ምስለ ፡ መኳንንት ፡ ወአጋእዝት ፡ ዘይመልኩ ፡ ዓለመ ፡ ጽልመት ፡ አጋንንት ፡ ወምስለ ፡ መናፍስት ፡ ርኩሳን ፡ እለ ፡ መትሕተ ፡ ሰማይ ። ሳሙኤል ፡ ነሥአ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ንዋየ ፡ ሐቅል ፡ ዘመስቀል ፡ ከመ ፡ ይክሀል ፡ ተቃውሞታ ፡ ለዕለት ፡ እኪት ፡ ወከመ ፡ ይክሀል ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ገቢረ ። ሳሙኤል ፡ ቀነተ ፡ ቅናተ ፡ ወንጌል ፡ ሰሚዖ ፡ እምቃለ ፡ እግዚኡ ፡ ዘይቤ ፡ ወይኩን ፡ ቅኑተ ፡ ሐቃክሙ ፡ ወሕትወ ፡ መኃትዊክሙ ። ወአንትሙኒ ፡ ኩኑ ፡ ከመ ፡ ዕደው ፡ እለ ፡ ይጸንሑ ፡ እግዚኦሙ ፡ እስከ ፡ የአቱ ፡ እምከብካብ ፡ ከመ ፡ መጺኦ ፡ ወጐድዬጐዶ ፡ ሶቤሃ ፡ ያርሕውዎ ፡ ሶበ ፡ የአቱ ።= ሳሙኤል ፡ ለብሰ ፡ ልብሰ ፡ ሐዲሰ ፡ ዘእምዮርዳ ኖስ ፡ ተአንመ ፡ ወበውስቴቱ ፡ ወደየ ፡ ድርዓ ፡ ሃይማኖት ፡ በአምሳለ ፡ ልብሰ ፡ መስተቃትላን ፡ ወተጽዕነ ፡ ፈረሰ ፡ መዊእ ፡ ወወፅአ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይማእ ፡ ወረ ፡ አቡሁ” = ሳሙኤል ፡ ተቀጸለ ፡ ጌራ ፡ ብርሃን" ፡ ህየንተ ፡ ቀጸላ ፡ ወርቅ ፡ ዘሐራ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ወወሰቀ ፡ ቀስቶ ፡ ወአስተዳለወ ፡ አሕፃሁ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይን ድፍ ፡ ኩልያቶ ፡ ለዘ ፡ ይትመነገን” ፡ በኅቡእ ። ሳሙኤል ፡ ተሥእነ ፡ ኃይለ” ፡ ወንጌል ፡ ዘኢይትበተክ ፡ አሣእኑ ፡ ወኬደ ፡ ዘባነ ፡ ከይሲ ፡ ረ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ ወእሙ ፡= ሳሙኤል ፡ ነሥአ ፡ ኩናተ ፡ እሳት ፡ በእዴሁ ፡ ወጾረ ፡ ሰይፈ ፡ መለኮት ፡ ውስተ ፡ መንቅዕቱ ፡ ዘውእቱ ፡ ሐዲስ ፡ ወብሉይ ። ወሰ ምዐ ፡ እምአፈ ፡ እግዚኡ ፡ ዘይቤ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ዘይመጽእ ፡ ኅቤየ ፡ ወይሰምርኒ ፡ ቃልየ ፡ ወይገብሮ ፡ እነግረክሙ ፡ ዘይመስል ። ይመስል ፡ ብእሴ ፡ ጠቢበ ፡ ዘሐነጸ ፡ ቤቶ ፡ ከርዮ ፡ መሠረቶ ፡ ሣሪሮ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ኩኩሕ ። ወሶበ ፡ መጽአ ፡ ብዙኅ ፡ ዝናም ፡ ወገፍእዎ ፡ ወሐይዝት ፡ ለውእቱ ፡ ቤት ፡ ወኢያንቀልቀለ ፡ እስመ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ኩኩሕ ፡ ተሣረረ ፡ ወሠናይ ፡ ሕንጸቱ ። ዘንተ ፡ ሰይፈ ፡ ነሚኦ ፡ ተደለወ ፡ ለፀብእ ፡ ከመ ፡ ፅኑዕ ፡ ሐራ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ወኢተቃወመቶኖ" ፡ እብነ ፡ እቅፈት ፡ ውስተ ፡ ፍኖቱ ፡ እስመ ፡ ጥቡዕ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወተኀየለ ፡ እስከ ፡ ይጸይኅ ፡ ፍኖተ ፡ መብእሰ ፡ ወመጐጻ?ጐጻጸ ፡ ረሰየ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ምሕዋረ ፡ ንጽሕተ ። ያስተምሕር ፡ በእንቲአነ ፡ ቀዊሞ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ሥሉስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ዘአልቦ ፡ ፍልጠተ ። አንጺሖ ፡ ዘዚአነ ፡ ርስሐተ ፡ ቀዲሶ ፡ ዘኩልነ ፡ ነፍሳተ ፡ ያብአነ ፡ ሀገረ ፡
14 Sämu’el, victorious soldier, riding the horse of faith, went out to fight with the perverse soldiers of darkness, having heard the word of Paul who says: For your struggle is not with what (is) flesh and blood, but with the Principalities and the Lordships who govern the world of darkness, the demons, and with the impure spirits which (are) under the heaven (Eph 6:12). Sämu'ël took all the armor of the Cross to be able to resist on the day of evil and to be able to do everything. Sämu'ël girded himself with the belt of the Gospel, having heard the word of his Lord who says: Let your loins be girded and your lamps lit. And you, be like men who await their master until he returns from the wedding so that, (he) having come and having knocked, they may open to him immediately, when he returns (Lk 12:35-36). Sämu’el put on the new garment which, since the Jordan, has been woven and, upon it, he put the breastplate of faith (1 Thess 5:8) in the likeness of the garment of the combatants; He mounted a victorious horse and went out to defeat the enemy of his Father. Sämu’el girded himself with a helmet of light in place of the golden helmet of a king's soldier; he drew his bow and prepared his arrows to pierce the entrails of the one who plots in secret. Sämu’el shod himself with the power of the Gospel, whose sandals do not break, and trampled the tail of the serpent enemy of his Father and his Mother. Sämu’el took the spear of fire in his hand and carried the sword of divinity at his hip, they which are the New and the Old (Testament) (Eph 6:13-17). He heard from the mouth of his Lord what says: «Whoever comes to me, and hears my word and puts it into practice, I will tell you whom he resembles: he resembles a wise man who built his house, having dug (and) established his foundations on the rock. When an abundant rain came and torrents beat against the house, it was not shaken, for it was founded on the rock and its construction was good» (Mt 7:24-25//). Having taken this sword, he prepared himself for combat like a solid soldier of the king and the stumbling block did not oppose him, in his path, for he was firm and struggled to the point of clearing the steep and rocky path and made (of it) for himself a pure walk. He implored mercy for us, standing beside the holy Trinity which has no separation; having purified our defilement, sanctified our souls to all, he introduces us into the adorned city. Sämu’el, excellent plowman and faithful farmer,


[p. 18] {#po.53.p18}
ስርጉተ"' ። ሳሙኤል ፡ ዔር ፡ ሐረሳዊ ፡ ወምእመን” ፡ ገበራዊ ፡ ስርናየ ፡ እዙለ ፡ ዘበበ ፡ ፻፻ ዘይሰዊ ።= ኀይለ ፡ ጸሎቱ ፡ ያድኅነነ ፡ እምእኩይ ፡ ፀራዊ ። አመ ፡ ዕለተ ፡ ፍዳ ፡ ወደይን ፡ እምግርማ ፡ መዓቱ ፡ ለሰማያዊ” ፡ ይኩነነ ፡ ቤዛዊ ።
who makes the grown grain ripen, (one) in ten thousand. The power of his prayer saves us from the perverse enemy. On the day of retribution and punishment, may he be for us a redeemer from the terror of the wrath of the Celestial One.
ሳሙኤል ፡ ነሥአ ፡ አርዑተ ፡ ወዕርፈ ፡ ወድጐረ ፡ ወኩሎ ፡ መጻምደ ፡ አስተናቢሮ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይኅርስ ፡ ተደለወ ። ህየንተ ፡ አርዑት ፡ ጾረ ፡ በክሳዱ” ፡ ቃለ ፡ ወንጌል ፡ ዘይቤ ፡ ንሥኡ ፡ ተሠክሙ ፡ አርዑትየ ፡ እስመ ፡ አርዑት የኒ ፡ ሠናይ ፡ ወጾርየኒ ፡ ቀሊል ፡ ውእቱ ፍ ኀበ ፡ ንወፍር ፡ ወነአቱ ፡ ይትማ ኅፀነነ ፡ ኀይለ!፤ ፡ ጸሎቱ ቱ ወህየንተ ፡ ዕርፍ ፡ ነሥአ ፡ መስቀሎ ፡ ለቅዱሰ ፡ እስራኤል ፡ ወጾረ ፡ በመዝራዕቱ ። ያስተምሕር ፡ በእንቲአነ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ዘዕሩይ ፡ ትሥልስቱ ። በኑኃ ፡ አርያም ፡ ከዊኖ ፡ ለዘይሬኢ ፡ በታሕቱ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ምዕረፈ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ በሰላም ፡ ንእቱ ፍ ወህየንተ ፡ መጻምድ ፡ ነሥአ ፡ ኀብለ ፡ ወንጌል ፡ ዘኢይትበተክ ፡ ወወደየ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ክሳዱ ፡ ወተስሕበ ፡ በፈቃዱ ። ያስተምሕር ፡ በእንቲአነ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ዘአርያም ፡ ማኅፈዱ ፍ ይክፍለነ ፡ ርስተ ፡ ዐስበ ፡ ተቀንዮ ፡ ዘብርሃን ፡ ዓጸዱ ። ወህየንተ ፡ ድጐር ፡ ረሰየ ፡ ብሊተ ፡ ወሐዲሰ ፡ በይምን ፡ ወበጽግም ፡ ከመ ፡ ይግሥሥ ፡ ቦቶን ፡ እምሥጋሁ ፡ ሦከ ፡ ወአሜካለ ። ያስተምሕር ፡ በእንቲአነ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ በቋዔ ፡ ተድላ ። ሰማይ ፡ ወምድር ፡ እምቅድሜሁ” ፡ አመ ፡ ይሴስላ ፡= 'ነፍሳተ ፡ ኀጣኣን ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሲኦል ፡ አመ ፡ ይኤይላ”' ፥ ቁመት ፡ እንተ ፡ የማን ፡ ለነፍስነ ፡ ይክፍላ” ።« ህየንተ ፡ አባዕር ፡ ፀመደ ፡ ነፍሶ ፡ ወሥጋሁ ፡ በትእዛዘ ፡ ሐዋርያት ፡ ወሊቃውንት ። ወህየንተ ፡ ጥብጣቤ ፡ ኀብል ፡ ዘአባዕር ፡ ረሰየ ፡ ቀኖና ፡ ዘሲኖዶስ ፡ ዘሕግ ፡ ወዘሥርዐት ። ኀይለ ፡ ጸሎቱ ፡ ይክድነነ ፡ ከመ ፡ ሐይ መት ። ወያድኅነነ ፡ እምተቃርኖቱ ፡ ለመስሕት ። ወህየንተ ፡ ቃለ ፡ ሐረሳዊ ፡ ዘይኬልሕ ፡ በድኅረ” ፡ አባዕር ፡ ነሥአ ፡ ቃለ ፡ ጳውሎስ ፡ ዘይቤ” ፡ ንሥኡ ፡ ጌራ ፡ መድኀኒትክሙ ፡ ወሰይፈ ፡ ዘመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ዘውእቱ ፡ ቃለ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ።ፍ ጸሎት ፡ ወስእአለት ፡ እንዘ ፡ ትጹጴልዩ ፡ በኩሉ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ በመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ። ወምስሌሁ ፡ ትግሁ ፡ ወተጸመዱ ፡ በኩሉ ፡ ሰዓት ። ወህ የንተ ፡ ምድር ፡ ሠናይት ፡ ረሰየ ፡ ገራህተ ፡ ሥጋሁ ። ወህየንተ ፡ ዘርእ ፡ ረሰየ ፡ ቃለ ፡ ወንጌል ፡ ዘይቤ ፡ ወአንትሙሰ ፡ ኅሠ ፡ መቅድመ ፡ መንግ ሥቶ ፡ ወጽድቆ ፡ ወዝንቱ ፡ ኩሉ'' ፡ ይትዌሰከክሙ ። በዝንቱ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ተሰር ጊዎ ፡ ወፅአ ፡ ወኢገብአ ፡ ድኅሬሁ ፡ አላ ፡ አርትዓ ፡ ትልሞ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡
15 Sämu’el took the yoke, the plow, and the pole; having well arranged all the straps, he prepared to plow. In place of the yoke, he carried on his neck the word of the Gospel which says: «Take, load my yoke, for my yoke is benign and my burden light» (Mt 11:29-30). Where we go out and come in, the power of his prayer protects us. In place of the plow, he took the cross of the Saint of Israel and carried it on his arm. May he implore mercy for us before the equal Trinity of him who, standing on high in the heavens, sees below, so that we may gain the resting place of the saints in peace. In place of the straps, he took the rope of the Gospel which does not break, put it on his neck and was pulled by his will. May he implore mercy for us before him whose citadel is the high heavens. May he impart to us as an inheritance the reward of the service of him whose domain (is) the light. In place of the pole, he put the Old and the New (Testament), on the right and on the left, to touch with them, far from his body, the thorn and the thistle. May he implore mercy for us before Jesus Christ, dispenser of delights. When heaven and earth withdraw before him, when the souls of sinners wander in Sheol, may he impart to our soul the place on the right. 16 In place of oxen, he subjected his soul and his body to the command of the apostles and the elders. In place of the ox-goad, he put the precepts of the Sinodos, what (is) the law and what (is) the rule. May the power of his prayer cover us like a tent and save us from the assaults of the Seducer. In place of the word of the plowman who shouts behind the oxen, he took the word of Paul who says: Receive the helmet of your salvation and the sword of the Holy Spirit which is the word of God, (by) all prayer and request praying at every moment in the Holy Spirit; with that be vigilant and assiduous at every hour (Eph 6:17-18). In place of good earth, he made a field of his body; in place of the seed, he put the word of the Gospel which says: «As for you, seek first his kingdom and his justice and all that will be added to you» (Mt 6:33). Adorned with all that, he went out and did not turn back, but he traced his furrow straight without deviation from the beginning until the end (Lk 9:62).
ጽኑት ፡ እምጥንቱ ፡ እስከ ፡ ተፍጻሜቱ ። ያስተምሕር ፡ በእንቲአነ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ዘአርያም ፡ ጽር ፡ አመ ፡ ይመጽእ ፡ ለፍዳ” ፡ በድልቅልቅ ፡ በአፍርሆ ፡ ሞጣሕተ ፡ በቀል ፡ ተሞጢሖ ። ኢይር ሳዕ ፡ ዘክሮተነ ፡ አሜሃ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ዘይክል ፡ ባልሖ ። ኦአባ ፡ ኀሠሥከ ፡ ወረከብከ ፡
17 May he implore mercy for us before him whose palace is the high heavens, when he comes for retribution in trembling, in terror, having dressed himself in the garment of vengeance (Is 59:17). May he


[p. 20] {#po.53.p20}
ጐድጐድከ ፡ ወተርኅወ ፡ ለከ ። ዘራእከ ፡ ወአረርከ ፡ ወኢተረከበ” ፡ ኅሰር ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዐውደ ፡ እክልከ ፡ ወአስተርአየ ፡ ከምረ ፡ ከምርከ ፡ ስርናየ ፡“' ጻዓዳ ፡ ከመ ፡ በረድ ። ኦብእሴ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ዔር ፡ 'ንትፌሣሕ ፡ ወንትሐሠይ ፡ ብከ ፡ ነኀሥሥ"፤ ፡ በረከተ ፡ እዴከ ፡ እስመ ፡ ጸወን ፡ ጽኑዕ ፡ አንተ ፡ ንት መኀፀን ፡ ብከ ። እስመ ፡ ንሕነ ፡ እሊአከ ፡ ለባርኮተነ ፡ ስፉሕ ፡ እዴከ ። ስብሐት ፡ ለአብ ፡ ለዘሐረየከ ። ወሰጊድ ፡ ለወልድ ፡ ለዘአጽነዓከ ። አኩቴት ፡ ለመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ለዘቀደሰከ ፡ ወአምዓዘከ"' = ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ፍ፥
not forget to mention us then before him who can save. Abbä, you sought and you found, you knocked and it was opened to you (Mt 7:7//). You sowed and reaped and no chaff was found on your threshing floor and your heap had the appearance of a heap of wheat white as snow. Virtuous man of God, we rejoice and exult concerning you, we seek the blessing of your hand; because you are a solid refuge, we take shelter in you; indeed to bless us, we yours, your hand is outstretched. Glory to the Father who chose you! Adoration to the Son who gave you strength! Praise to the Holy Spirit who sanctified you and embalmed you, in the ages of ages, amen!

### Birth of Sämu’ël. His parents
" ስምዑ ፡ አበዊነ ፡ ወአኀዊነ ፡ ኩልክሙ ፡ እለ ፡ ትፈርህዎ ፡ ለእግዚአ ብሔር ፡ ዜናሁ" ፡ ለጻድቅ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይኩንክሙ ፡ በቀሞዔተ"' ፡ ለነፍስክሙ" ፡ እስመ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ሠናየ ፡ ዝክር ፡ ከመዝ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ፍጥረቱ ፡ እስመ ፡ ተወልዱ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ ወእሙ ፡ በሀገረ ፡ ጽራዕ ፍ ወዝንቱሰ ፡ ብእሴ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ተወልደ ፡ በሀገር ፡ እንተ ፡ ስማ ፡ አክሱም ፡ እሞን ፡ ለአህጉር ፡ ወስመ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ እስጢፋኖስ ፡ ወስመ ፡ እሙ ፡ አመተ ፡ ማርያም ፡ እስመ ፡ ራነ ፡ ዘመድ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ ወሠናያን ፡ በሥርዐቶሙ ፡ ወያፈቅርዎ ፡ ለክርስቶስ ፡ ወርቱዓን ፡ በሃይማኖቶሙ"' ፡ እሙንቱ ። ወእምድኅረ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ወለዱ ፡ ፪ውሉደ ፡ ለ8ስሙ ፡ ገብረ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወካልኡሰ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ 'ብእሴ ፡ እግዚአብሔር" ፡ ዘዮም ፡ አስተጋብአተነ ፡ ተዝካሩ” ። ውእቱ ፡ ይንእስ ፡ እምነ ፡ እሁ ፡ በከመ ፡ ይንእስ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ እምያዕቆብ ፡ ወጴጥሮስ ፡ እምእንድርያስ ። ወኩሎ ፡
18 Listen, our fathers and our brothers, all you who fear God, the history of the righteous one, so that it may be useful to your soul. Of this man of beautiful memory such is the origin: his father and his mother were born in the province of Serä. As for this man of God, he was born in the city by the name of Axum, the mother of cities; the name of his father was Estifanos and the name of his mother, Amata Märyäm: they were of excellent family and beautiful in their way of regulating themselves, they loved Christ and (were) upright in their faith. Subsequently, they begot two sons; the name of one (was) Gabra Krestos, the other (is) this man of God whose commemoration has gathered us today. He was younger than his brother, just as John was younger than James and Peter than Andrew. Every day, the grace of God manifests itself on the little ones
አሚረ ፡ ያስተርኢ ፡ ጸጋ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ በላዕለ ፡ ንኡሳን ።« ወሐፀንዎ ፡ በፈሪሀ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ። ወእምዝ ፡ ወሀብዎ ፡ ለ፳ ፡ አረጋዊ ፡ ካህን ፡ ከመ ፡ ይምሀሮ ፡ መዝሙረ ፡ ዳዊት ፡ ወመጽሐፈ ፡ ነቢያት ፡ ወሐዋርያት ፡ ወመሀሮ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ሕገ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ።= ወእምድኅረ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ተሰይመ ፡ ዲያቆነ ። ወኮነ ፡ ለባዌ ፡ በቃሉ ፡ ወበምግባሩ ፡ ወየሐሥሥ ፡ መጻሕፍተ ፡ እንተ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ እስትንፋሰ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ። ወይገይስ ፡ ሰርከ ፡ ወነግሀ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ወይጹሊ ፡ በውስቴታ ፡ ወይስእል ፡ ወያስተበቀሞዕ ። ወሶበ ፡ ርእይዎ ፡ ካህናተ ፡ እንትኩ ፡ ሀገር ፡ እንዘ ፡ ያስቆቁ ፡ ወይበኪ ፡ ያነክሩ ፡ እም ኔሁ ፡ ወይትባሀሉ ፡ ምንትኑ ፡ ኃጢአቱ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ሕፃን ፡ ከመዝ ፡ ይበኪ ፡ አኮሁ ፡ ኪያነ ፡ ይሳለቅ ። ወውእቱሰ ፡ አረጋዊ ፡ ዘመሀሮ ፡ ጸውዖ ፡ ለአቡሁ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ወልድከ ፡ ዐቢየ ፡ ይከውን?” ፡ ወጸጋ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ኅዱር ፡ ላዕሌሁ ። ወይትአመንዎ ፡ ነገሥት ፡ ወመኳንንት ፡ ወይወፅእ ፡ ስሙ ዓቱ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ዓለም ። ወዘንተ ፡ ብሂሎ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ እምድኅረ ፡ ሐይወ ፡ ፻ ዓመተ ። ወበ ፳ እመዋዕል ፡ ቦአ፣፡ ብድብድ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሀገር፣፡ ወሞቱ ፡ ብዙኃን፡ ሰብእ ፡ ወ፳እምሠራዊተ ፡ ብድብድ ፡ መጽአ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይንድፎ ፡ ለሕፃን ፡ ሳሙ ኤል ።= ወሶቤሃ ፡ መጽአ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ መላእክት ፡ ሚካኤል ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ኢትግበር ፡
19 (His parents) raised him in the fear of God. After that, they gave him to an old priest so that he might teach him the psalms of David and the writings of the prophets and the apostles; he taught him all the law of the Church and, after that, (the child) was ordained deacon. He became reflective in his word and in his action, he sought the writings which are the inspiration of God; he went evening and morning to the church, he prayed there, solicited and implored. When the priests of that city saw him lamenting and weeping, they were astonished concerning him and said to themselves: «What (is) the sin of this child (that) he weeps thus? Is he not mocking us?» But the old man who had instructed him called his father and said to him: «This son of yours will become great and the grace of God dwells upon him; kings and governors will have confidence in him and his fame will spread throughout the universe.» Having said that, he died after having lived one hundred years. One day, the plague entered the city, many people died and one of the soldiers of the plague came to strike the child Samu'ël. Immediately the archangel Michael came and said to him: «Do not do harm to this child,


[p. 22] {#po.53.p22}
እኩየ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ሕፃን ፡ እስመ ፡ ኅሩየ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወበ ጸሎቱ ፡ ያድኅን ፡ ነፍሰ ፡ ብዙኃን'! ። ወከመዝ ፡ ስምዐ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ለሊሁ ።
for he is an elect of God and, by his prayer, he will save the soul of many.» Thus (Sämu'ël) was himself a witness.

### The mother of Sämu'ël becomes a nun
ወካዕበ ፡ ስምዑ ፡ እንግርክሙ ፡ ዜናሃ ፡ ለቅድስት ፡ እሙ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ሀለወት ፡ ምስለ ፡ ምታ ፡ ወኢወለደት ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ክልኤ ፡ ደቂቅ ፡ ወትቤሎ ፡ ለምታ ፡ ኦእግዚእየ ፡ አንሰ ፡ እፈቅድ ፡ እኩን ፡ መነኮሳይተ ፡ ወአን ተኒ ፡ ለእመ ፡ ፈቀድከ ፡ ኩን ፡ ከማሁ ። ወባሕቱ ፡ ኪያየ ፡ መሐረኒ ፡ እስመ ፡ እምኔከ ፡ ኣፈቅሮ ፡ ለክርስቶስ ። ወይቤላ ፡ አንሰ ፡ ኢየሐድገኪ ፡ ወአንጠየቶ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይኅድጋ ። ወሶበ ፡ አበያ ፡ ትቤሎ ፡ ከመዝ ፡ አንሰ ፡ እፈ ቅድ ፡ ከመ ፡ ታውስብ ፡ ብእሲተ ፡ ወአነ ፡ አሐ ፡ ለከ ፡ በንዋይየ ።= ወእ ምዝ ፡ ኀፀየት ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ብእሲተ” ፡ በንዋያ ፡ ወገብረት ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ከብካበ ፡ ወአውረሰታ ፡ ቤታ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ሀለወት ፡ በሕይወታ ። ወእምድኅረ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡
20 And again, listen, that I may narrate to you the history of his holy mother: while she was with her husband and had borne him only two children, she said to her husband: «My lord, I want to become a nun and you, if you want, become (a monk) likewise, but have pity on me, for I love Christ more than you.» He said to her: «I will not leave you.» She pressed him much so that he would leave her. When he had refused (it) to her, she said to him thus: «I want you to marry a woman, I am going to buy (one) for you from my property.» After that, she bought for him a woman from her property, held a wedding for him, and gave (to the woman) her house as an inheritance while she (herself) was (still) alive. After that, she went out, went away, and became a nun.

21 See, my fathers and my brothers, how the love of God dwelt in her heart. Where have you heard such a thing, from what woman among those of before and those of after? While she was young, she showed renunciation of the world. She burned strongly with the love of Christ; she offered everything that (was) in her house — outside and inside — to the heiress, by her own will, while no one forced her, for she had been struck by the love of Christ and had heard from him the word which says: «The kingdom of heaven is like a merchant who seeks a beautiful pearl; he found a big pearl of great price, he went, sold everything he had and bought it» (Mt 13:45-46). Likewise she went to seek this pearl of the Father, Jesus the Savior — at the mention of whose name adoration is fitting — and delivered herself to a great trial to find him. Then she took the palm of a single hand of dried sycamore fruit and, thus, accomplished the fast of the forty holy (days) by adding nothing (to the fruit). She was shown her (heavenly) dwelling before she died, (she) having been taken up by the Spirit as fruit of her trial. After a great (spiritual) combat, she died, having accomplished her career, and received the crown which belongs to the (spiritual) combatants (2 Tim 4:7-8). May her prayer and her blessing be with us, amen. Sämu'el refuses marriage and becomes a monk

22 Let us return then to the history of the righteous one: after his mother had died, his parents said to themselves with the heiress: «Let us acquire for our son a woman so that we may rejoice in the marriage of (Sämu'ël) and that this one may be for us the plant of our memory after us so that our name may subsist through him.» When the man of God heard this proposal of his parents, he immediately ignited and burned with the love of God; his heart was struck by the history of the saints. 23 Immediately coals were lit from him, from the fire of divinity which (was) in his belly. Before the light of his face receded the walls of darkness which envelop the heart of the senseless: the love of the spouse and children, the love of the father and the mother, the love of the brother and the sister, the love of the friend and the family, the desire for food, drink, and precious clothing, all adornment and sweetness of the desires of this world; he considered that as nothing and threw it behind him like refuse (Phil 3:8). After that, he said: «For me, following God is the best and my trust (is) in God.» He said to his soul: «Of what profit is it, my soul, that which your father and your mother have spoken concerning you? Have you not heard, my soul, the one who has the keys of death and life in his hand cry out? He says: ‘What profit for man if he gains the whole world and destroys his life? And what will man give in exchange for his life?’ (Mt 16:26//) He also says: ‘He who will have found his life, let him lose it, and he who will have lost his life because of me, will find it (Mt 10:39).» He made this pact with his God.

24 He said to his father and to his mother: «I do not want the weddings of this transitory world, but I prefer the weddings which (are) in the heavens in the heavenly Jerusalem, where all the sons of light are glorified with Jesus their leader, where it is best for me if I am in the delights with them on the day of the wedding of the pure Bridegroom whom the saints wish and desire to see. I too have rendered Christ as a father and a mother so that he might give me what the eye has not seen and the ear has not heard, that God has prepared for those who love him (1 Cor 2:9). I too have delivered myself to him.»
ወዘንተ ፡ ብሂሎ ፡ ሖረ ፡ ኀበ ፡ አረጋዊ ፡ መነኮስ ፡ ዘስሙ ፡ ገብረ ፡ አምላክ ፡ ወነሥአ ፡ እምኔሁ" ፡ አርዑተ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ በቡራኬ ፡ ወኢወሀቦ ፡ በሕገ ፡ ምንኩስና ፡ አላ ፡ ወሀቦ ፡ በከመ ፡ ያለብሱ ፡ አበው ፡ ለወራዙት ፡ ውሉዶሙ ። ወእምድኅረ ፡ አልበስዎ ፡ በቡራኬ ፡ ወእምዝ ፡ ተመይጠ ፡ ኀበ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይቲጦ ፡ በፍቅረ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ኦአባ ፡ ምንተ ፡ ይበቀዓከ ፡ ንብረተ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ዘፍጡን ፡ ኅልፈቱ ። ኢሰማዕከኑ ፡ ዘይቤ ፡ ሐዋርያ ፡ ኢታፍቅርዎ ፡ ለዓለም ፡ ወኢዘሀሎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዓለም ።፥ ወዓለምኒ ፡ የኀልፍ ፡ ወፍትወቱኒ ፡ የኀልፍ ። ወዘሰ ፡ ይገብር ፡ ፈቃዶ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ይነብር ፡ ለዓለም ፍ ወበዝ' ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ምዕዳን ፡ ወዘይበዝኅኒ ፡ ሶበ ፡ መዓዶ ፡ ኢተመይጠ ፡ ለቃሉ” ፡ አላ ፡ አበዮ ፡ ወይቤ" ፡ አንሰ ፡ ኢየ ኀድግ ፡ ሥርዐተ ፡ ቤትየ ፡ ወኩሎ ፡ አዝማድየ ። ወሶበ ፡ አበዮ ፡ ገብአ ፡
25 Having said that, he went to an old monk by the name of Gabra Amläk and received from him the yoke of the saints by blessing; he did not give (it) to him according to the monastic rule, but he gave (it) to him as fathers clothe their adolescent sons. After he had been clothed with the blessing, after that, he returned to his father to persuade him by the love of God; he said to him: «Father, of what use to you is the way of living of this world whose passage is rapid? Have you not heard what the apostle says: Do not love the world and what is in the world. The world passes and its desire passes; but he who does the will of God remains forever?» (1 Jn 2:15, 17) When with all this exhortation and more he had exhorted him, (his father) did not convert to his word, but refused him and said: «I will not abandon the administration of my house and all my relatives.» When he


[p. 26] {#po.53.p26}
እንዘ ፡ ይበኪ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ ኦእግዚእየ ፡ ወአምላኪየ ፡ ወመድኀንየ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ኢትኅድጎ ፡ ለአቡየ ፡ ይትሐጐል ፡ በዝንቱ ፡ መኣስረ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ዘብ ዙኅ ፡ መሣግሪሁ ፡ ወባሕቱ ፡ ቅጽቦ ፡ በከመ ፡ ልማድከ ፡ ዘትመይጥ ፡ ኀጥኣነ ፥ ወዘንተ ፡ ብሂሎ ፡ ገብአ ፡ ውስተ” ፡ ማኅደሩ ።
had refused him, (Sämu'ël) returned weeping and said: «My Lord, my God, my Savior Jesus Christ, do not let my father be lost in these bonds of the age whose traps (are) numerous, but give him a sign according to your habit, you who convert sinners.» Having said that, he returned to his dwelling.

### Sämu’el becomes a monk at Bankwal with abbä Adhani
ወእምድኅረ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ተንሥአ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ አረጋዊ ፡ ዘአልበሶ ፡ ወሖረ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ደብረ ፡ አባ ፡ አድኀኒ ፡ እንተ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ በንኩል ። ወሶበ ፡ በጽሐ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ ወነገሮ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ትካዘ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ምንክስና ።= ወይቤሎ ፡ አባ ፡ አድኀኒ ፡ ንበር ፡ እስከ ፡ 'አመ ፡ ፈቀደ"' ፡ እግዚአብሔር ። ወውእቱኒ ፡ አረጋዊ ፡ ዘአልበሶ ፡ ተገድፈ ፡ ታሕተ ፡ ጽለሎተ ፡ ጸሎቱ ፡ ለአባ ፡ አድኀኒ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ንዋዩ ፡ ወበኀቤሁ ፡ አዕረፈ” ፡= ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ርእዩ ፡ ቲሩቶ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡ ወግዕዞ ፡ ሠናየ ፡ ደቂቁ ፡ ለአባ ፡ አድኀኒ ፡ ወይቤልዎ ፡ ኦአባ ፡ አልብሶ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ወልድ ፡ አልባሰ ፡ ምንኩስና ፡ በከመ ፡ ሕገ ፡ ሥርዐት ፡ እስመ ፡ ድልው ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይጹር ፡ አስኬማ ፡ መላእክት ። ወአዘዘ ፡ አባ ፡ አድኀኒ ፡ ይጥቅዑ” ፡ መጥቅዐ ።= ወተጋብኡ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ መነኮሳት ፡ ወገብሩ ፡ ጸሎተ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ አልባስ ፡ ወቅናት ፡ ወቆብዕ ፡ ወአስኬማ ። ወአልበሶ ፡ አባ ፡ አድኀኒ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ ይባርከ ፡ እግዚአብ ሔር ፡ በበረከተ ፡ ሰማይ ፡ ወምድር ። ወኩን ፡ ስሙዐ ፡ ከመ ፡ እንጦንዮስ ፡ ወመቃርዮስ ፡ አበዊነ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ እለ ፡ አስመርዎ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ በሥነ ፡ ገድሎሙ ፡ መንፈሳዊት ፡ ወይቤሉ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ አሜን ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ ተመ
26 After that, he arose with the old man who had clothed him and went to the monastery of abbä Adhani, which is Bankwal. When he arrived to (the abbot), he told him the whole affair concerning (his) monastic state. Abbä Adhani said to him: «Stay until when God wants (it).» As for the old man who had clothed him, he threw himself under the shadow of the prayer of abbä Adhani with all his property and died beside him. After that, when they saw the virtue of our father Sämu'el and his beautiful nature, the (spiritual) children of abbä Adhani said to him: «Abbä, clothe this young man with the monastic garment according to the law of the rule, for he is worthy to wear the habit of the angels.» Abbä Adhani ordered the bells to be rung: all the monks gathered and prayed over the garment, the belt, the skullcap, and the scapular. Abbä Adhani clothed (Sämu'ël) saying: «May God bless you with the blessing of heaven and earth. Be renowned like Anthony and Macarius, our holy fathers who pleased God by the beauty of their spiritual combat.» All said: «Amen.» After that, he received the holy mysteries and, when the mass was finished, returned to his dwelling.
ጠወ ፡ ምሥጢራተ ፡ ቅድሳት ፡ ወሶበ ፡ ተፈጸመ ፡ ቅዳሴ ፡ ገብአ ፡ ውስተ” ፡ ማኅደሩ ። ወእምአሜሃ ፡ ዕለት ፡ አሐዘ ፡ ይውጥን ፡ ጻማ ፡ ብዙኅ ፡ ወተባአሰ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ወይወቅሳ ፡ ለነፍሱ ፡ ወይቤላ ፡ ኦነፍስየ ፡ ናሁ ፡ ነሣእኪ ፡ አርዑተ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ እንተ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ አርአያ ፡ ድንግልና ፡ ዘአበው ፡ ቅዱሳን ። ወከመ ፡ ትዕቀቢ ፡ ንጽሓ ፡ ዘመላእክት ፡ ዮም ፡ ተካየድኪ ፡ በዝንቱ ፡ ኪዳን ፡ ምስለ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ በቅድመ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ወበቅድመ ፡ መላእክ ቲሁ ፡ ቅዱሳን ። ወእመሰ ፡ ፈጸምኪ ፡ ዘከመ ፡ ተካየድኪ ፡ ይትፌሥሑ ፡ ብኪ ፡ መላእክተ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወእመሰ ፡ ኢፈጸምኪ ፡ ይሳለቁ ፡ ብኪ ፡ አጋንንት ፍ« ኦነፍስየ ፡ ናሁ ፡ ተሐፀይኪ ፡ 'ለክርስቶስ ፡ ኀልዬ”' ፡ ዘትሁቢ ፡ ሕፄኪ ፡ ለመርዓዊ ፡ ንጹሕ ። ወዘንተ ፡ ብሂሎ ፡ አምጽአ” ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ጻማ ፡ ፅፁበ"' ፡ ወገድለ ፡ ዘመንክር ፡ ለሰሚዖቱ ። ወያስተጋብእ ፡ ሐሰረ ፡ እምውስተ ፡ እደዋት ፡ ወያርሕሶ ፡ በማይ ፡ ወይገለብቦ ፡ በብሑአ” ፡ ስርናይ ፡ ወያበስሎ ፡ በእሳት ፡ ከመ ፡ ይበልዎ ፡ አኃው ፡ ይበልዕ ፡ ኅብስተ ፡ ስርናይ ። ወሶበ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ሰርከ ፡ ያአትቶ ፡ ለውእቱ ፡ ስርናይ ፡ እንተ ፡ አፍኣሁ ፡ ወይገድፎ ፡ ለከለባት ፡ አው ፡ ይሁብ ፡ ለሕፃናት ፡ መዘምራን ፡ እንዘ ፡ አልቦ ፡ ዘያአምሮ ፡ ወኢመኑሂ ።= ወውእቱሰ ፡ ይበልዕ ፡ ውእተ ፡ ሐሰረ ፡ ዘአልቦ ፡ ጸብሐ ፡ ወኢ ፄወ ፡ ወስቴሁኒ ፡ ማይ ። ወከመዝ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይትጋደል ፡ ነበረ ፡ ዓመተ ።
27 From that day, he began to undertake a severe trial; he struggled with his body, he disputed with his soul and said to it: «My soul, behold you have received the yoke of the saints which is in the likeness of the virginity of the holy fathers. To keep the purity of the angels, today you have concluded this pact with God before the Church and before his holy angels. If you act according to the pact that you have concluded, the angels of God will rejoice concerning you; if you do not act (thus), the demons will mock you. My soul, behold you have been betrothed to Christ; think of your dowry that you will give to the pure Bridegroom.» Having said that, he brought upon his body a heavy trial and a combat prodigious to hear. He gathered straw in the refuse, wetted it with water, wrapped it in wheat leaven, and cooked it in the fire so that the brothers would say of him: «He eats wheat bread.» When evening had come, he removed the wheat from the outside and threw it to the dogs or gave it to the young chanters without anyone knowing. As for him, he ate the straw without sauce or salt; his drink (was) water. He remained seven years struggling (ascetically) thus.

28 Our father abbä Adhani assigned him to the mill so that he might grind with the brothers for the community. At night, he kept vigil without rest and implored his God in prayer and in abundant tears. When he took the mill, he worked with haste to weaken his strength, to the point that his sweat became like drops. After that, when his oven was filled with ground (wheat), he went to his companions who were grinding and observed if there was one who had weakened or who had a worry; after that, he took (his own) flour, went, having put (it) in a heap, and threw it into the other's flour oven without it being known; he always acted thus. Every thing that one said to him, he did with zeal without murmuring; he obeyed everyone without impatience saying: «Yes to your word, abbä.» All his action (was done) by the power of the Holy Spirit, according to what Gabriel says to Zechariah concerning John, the son of the latter: «And he himself will walk before God with the Holy Spirit and with the power of Elijah» (Lk 1:17). Likewise therefore to him also was given the power which (comes) from on high in the heavens. Everyone rejoiced concerning him, at the beauty of his conduct, and whoever had seen him loved him from the root of his heart. The father of Sämu’el becomes a monk at Bankwal
እዮ ፡ ያፈቅሮ ፡ እምሥርወ ፡ ልቡ ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ ሰምዓ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ገዓረ ፡ ልቡ ፡ በእንተ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ ወመጽአ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይመንኩስ ። ወአቀሞ ፡ ኅበ ፡ አባ ፡ አድኀኒ ፡ መም ህሩ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ናሁ ፡ መጽአ ፡ ኀቤከ ፡ አቡየ ፡ አረጋዊ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትባርኮ ፡ ወይንሣእ ፡ በረከተከ ። ወተወከፎ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ ወአልበሶ ፡ አልባሰ ፡ ምንኩስና ። ወእምድኅረ ፡ 'ኅዳጥ ፡ መዋዕል” ፡ ፈቀደ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ ይንበር ፡ በጽሙና ። ወነ ገሮ ፡ ለመምህሩ ፡ ሕሊናሁ ፡ ወሠምረ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ በዝንቱ ፡ ግብር ። ወእምዝ ፡ ጸውዖ ፡ ለሳሙኤል” ፡ ወልዱ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ አባ ፡ 'አድኀኒነ ፡ እግዚእ” ፡ ኅድግ ፡ እምይእዜሰ'" ፡ ማኅረፀ ፡ ሖር ፡ ተለአኮ ፡ ለአቡከ ፡ ወበከመ ፡ አስመ ርከኒ ፡ ሊተ ፡ ከማሁ ፡ ወሎቱኒ ፡ ኢታኅዝኖ''! ፡ በምንተኒ ፡ ወዘከመ ፡ ይቤ ለከ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ግበር ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ፈቃዶ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትንሣእ ፡ በረከቶ ፡ በከመ ፡ ነሣእከ ፡ እምኔየ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ለምንት ፡ ትሰድደኒ ፡ ኦአባ ፡ አሕዘንኩከኑ ፡ በቀዳሚ ፡ ግብርየ ፡ ኀበ ፡ አይሂ ፡ ዘአዘዝከኒ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ መምህሩ ፡ አልቦ ፡ ዘአሕዘንከኒ ፡ ኦወልድየ ፡ አላ ፡ ጥቀ ፡ አስተፈሣሕከኒ ። 'ወባሕቱ ፡ እፈቅድ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትምሃሮ ፡ ግብረ ፡ ምንኩስና''"" ፥ ወይቤሎ ፡ ኦሆ ፡ ለቃልከ ፡ ኦአባ ፡፥
29 After that, God heard the groaning of his heart concerning his father and (the latter) came to him to become a monk. (Sämu’ël) made him stand before abbä Adhani his master and said to him: «Behold my old father has come to you so that you may bless him and that he may receive your blessing.» (Abbä Adhani) welcomed (the man) to him and clothed him with the monastic garment. After a few days, the father of (Sämu'ël) wanted to dwell in solitude and told his master his thought, and (the latter) approved this thing for him; after that, abbä Adhani na Egzi' called Sämu’ël, the son (of the man), and said to him: «Leave the mill from now on; go, serve your father and, just as you have been pleasing to me, likewise, do not afflict him in anything either and, according to what he will tell you, do for him all his will in order to receive his blessing as you (have) received it from me.» (Sämu'ël) said to him: «Why are you chasing me away, abba? Have I afflicted you, with my first actions, in anything that you have ordered me?» His master said to him: «You have not afflicted me, my son, on the contrary you have greatly rejoiced me. Only I want you to teach (your father) the mode of monastic life.» (Sämu'ël) said to him: «Yes to your word, abbä.» (Abbä Adhani) blessed them and they went out from him in peace.
ወባረከ ፡ ላዕሌሆሙ ፡ ወወፅኡ ፡ እምኅቤሁ ፡ በሰላም ።፡ ወነበሩ ፡ በጽሙና ፡ ጥቃ ፡ በንኩል ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይገብሩ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ አምል ኮተ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ።ፍ ወኮኖ ፡ ከመ ፡ መምህር ፡ ለአቡሁ ፡ በከመ ፡ ይቤሎ ፡ አባ ፡ አድኀኒ ፡ ኩኖ ፡ መምህረ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ትገብር ፡ ፈቃዶ ፡ ወኢትኅድግ ፡ ምዒዶቶ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይኩን ፡ ንቁሀ ፡ ለገቢረ ፡ ሠናይ ። ወውእቱ ፡ ያመጽእ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ማየ ፡ ለስተይ ፡ ወሐምለ ፡ ምድር ፡ ለሲሳይ ። ወበ ፳-እመዋዕል ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይትለአኮ ፡ ከመዝ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ለአቡሁ ፡ ነሥአ ፡ ሐምለ ፡ ወአብሰሎ ፡
30 They dwelt in solitude near Bankwal doing many acts of piety toward God. (Sämu’el) was like a master toward his father, according to what abbä Adhani had told him: «Be a master to him by doing his will and do not cease to exhort him so that he may be vigilant in the practice of good.» And he brought him water for drink and vegetables from the earth for food. One day, while this saint was serving his father thus, he took vegetables, cooked them in water, covered them, and put them in a place until


[p. 30] {#po.53.p30}
በማይ ፡ ወከደኖ ፡ ወአንበሮ ፡ ውስተ ፡ መካን'"' ፡፣ እስከ ፡ ይጸይዕ ፡ ወእስከ ፡ ይትፈጠሩ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ዕፄ ። ወእምድኅረ ፡ ሐሙስ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ ከሠተ ፡ ክዳኖ ፡ ለሐምል ፡ ወረከበ ፡ ዘወፅኡ'" ፣ ወተፈጥሩ ፡ ዕፄያት ፡ በላዕሌሁ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ወሰደ ፡ ኀበ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ ወአርአዮ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ኦአባ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ 'እመ በላዕነ ፡ እምጸደቅነኑ''"' ።= ወይቤሎ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ ለምንት ፡ ዘኢንጸድቅ ፡ ለእመ ፡ ተወ ከፍነ ፡ ዘከመዝ ፡ ጺአተ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ክርስቶስ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ እመሰ ፡ ይከውነነ ፡ ዐስበ ፡ ንብላዕ ፡ እንከ ፡ ከመ ፡ ንርከብ ፡ ጽድቀ ። ወበእንተዝ''" ፡ ቃል ፡ ጌጦ ፡ ለአቡሁ ፡ ወነሥአ ፡ ቅድመ ፡ ወበልዐ ፡ ለሊሁ ፡ 'ወእምዝ ፡ ወሀቦ ፡ ለአቡሁ'''" ። ወበዘከመዝ ፡ ገድል'" ፡ ነበሩ ፡ ፪ ዓመተ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይትጋደሉ ፡ በውእቱ ፡ መካን ።
they rotted and until worms were created from them. After five days, he removed what was covering the vegetables and noted that worms had come out and had been created from them. After that, he brought (the vegetables) to his father, showed him, and said to him: «Father, if we eat this, will we be righteous?» His father said to him: «Why would we not be righteous if we accepted such rot for Christ?» (Sämu'ël) said to him: «If (this) must be a reward for us, let us eat then to obtain righteousness.» Because of this word, he persuaded his father; he himself took (of these vegetables) first and ate, then gave (some) to his father. They remained two years struggling (ascetically) such a struggle in this place. Sämu'ël and his father go to Waynä
'ወእንዘ ፡ ሀለዉ!" ፡ ከመዝ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ ወይመጽኡ ፡ ወትረ ፡ አዝማደ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ ወይነግርዎ ፡ ትካዞሙ ፡ ወያጽሕብዎ ፡ እስመ ፡ ቅሩብ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ደብር ፡ ጥቃ ፡ ሀገሮሙ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ለአቡሁ ፡ ኦአባ ፡ ናሁ ፡ እሬ እየከ ፡ ዘከመ ፡ ያጽሕቡከ ፡ አዝማዲከ ፡ ወኢየኀድጉከ ፡ ትንበር ፡ በጸሎት ። ትፈቅድኑ ፡ ንሖር ፡ ብሔረ ፡ ባዕድ ፡ እስመ ፡ ተብህለ ፡ ለመነኮስ ፡ ኢይ ትከሀል ፡ አስምሮ ፡ አምላኩ ፡ በብሔረ ፡ አቡሁ ። ወይቤ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ ይኩን ፡ በከመ ፡ ትቤ ፡ ኦወልድየ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ሖሩ ፡ ክልኤሆሙ ፡ ኀበ ፡ አባ ፡ አድኅኒ''' ፡ መምህሩ ፡ ወነገርዎ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ትካዞሙ ። ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ሠናየ ፡ ገበርክሙ ፡ ኦደቂቅየ ፡ ወባረከ ፡ ላዕሌሆሙ ፡ ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ሖሩ ፡ በሰላም ፡ ወእግዚአብሔር ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌክሙ ። 'ወተሰናአልዎ ፡ ወወፅኡ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ወሖሩ ፡ ርሑቀ ፡ ብሔረ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ኢይረክብዎሙ ፡ አዝማዲሁ!!! = ወበጽሑ ፡ ውስተ ፡ መካን ፡ እንተ ፡ ትሰመይ ፡ ወይና ። ወተወከፍዎሙ ፡ በፍሥሓ ፡ ወደ መርዎሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ማኅበሮሙ ፡ ወአእመሩ ፡ ከመ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ፍቅረ ፡ ክር ስቶስ ፡ መጽኡ ። ወነበሩ ፡ ህየ ፡ በብዙኅ ፡ ጾም ፡ ወጸሎት ፡ ወበዐቢይ ፡
31 While these saints were thus, the relatives of the father of (Sämu'ël) came constantly, told him their worries, and importuned him, for the monastery was near, next to their city. (Sämu'ël) said to his father: «Father, behold I see how your relatives importune you and do not let you dwell in prayer. Do you want us to go to another country, for it is said: It is not possible for a monk to please his God in the country of his father.» His father said: «May it be as you say, my son.» After that, they both went to abbä Adhani their master and told him all their worry. He said to them: «You have acted well, my children.» He blessed them and said to them: «Go in peace and may God be with you.» [They] took leave of him, went out from him, and went to a distant country where their relatives would not find them. They arrived in a place which was called Waynä. (The monks) welcomed them with joy and included them in their community: they had known that they had come for the love of Christ. They dwelt there in assiduous fasting and prayer and in a great vigil night and day.
ትጋህ ፡ ሌሊተ ፡ ወመዓልተ'' ፍ ወበ ፳ እምነ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ እንዘ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ መካን ፡ አንቅሃ ፡ ለነፍሱ ፡ ወይቤላ ፡ ኦነፍስየ ፡ እፎኑ ፡ ትነብሪ ፡ ጽሩዓ ፡ ወእስከ ፡ ማእዜኑ ፡ ትትሀከዬ ፡ ወኢትኀሥሚ ፡ 'መድኀኒተ ፡ ነፍስኪ''' ፡ አእምሪ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይረክበኪ ፡ ኩነኔሁ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ዘድልው ፡ ለሀካያን ፡ እለ ፡ ከማኪ ። ወዘንተ ፡ ብሂሎ ፡ ወፅአ ፡ እምውእቱ ፡ ምኔት ፡ ወነሥአ ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ እሳተ ፡ ወመጽሐፈ ፡ ዳዊትኒ ፡ በዘይትናዘዝ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ ወሖረ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ግዳም ፡ ወአ ልቦ ፡ ካልአ ፡ ዘነሥአ ፡ ወኢምንተኒ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ በትር ፡ ባሕቲታ ። ወእንዘ ፡ የሐውር ፡ ውስተ ፡ ፍኖት ፡ ረከበ ፡ ዐቢየ ፡ ፈለገ ፡ ዘምሉእ'" ፡ እስከ ፡ ድንጋጉ ፡ እስመ ፡ መዋዕለ ፡ ክረምት ፡ ውእቱ ። ወውእቱሰ ፡ ፈለግ ፡ እምብ ዝኃ ፡ ዝናም ፡ ወወሓይዝት ፡ ተላዓለ ፡ እስከ ፡ ከተማ ፡ አእዋም ። ወሶበ ፡ ርእየ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ቅዱሰ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ሐበከነ ፡ በሕቁ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ ሶበኒ ፡
32 One day, while he was in this place, (Sämu'ël) awakened his soul and said to it: «My soul, how do you remain idle and until when will you be lazy and will you not seek the salvation of yourself? Know that it will find you, the punishment of God which is prepared for the lazy who (are) like you.» Having said that, he went out from the convent, took with him fire and also the book of David with which he comforted himself, and went into the desert; he took nothing else except a staff only. While he was traveling, he encountered a great river which (was) full to its banks, for it was the days of winter. This river, because of the abundance of rain and the runoff, had risen to the top of the trees. When he saw that, the saint of God was greatly afflicted and said:


[p. 32] {#po.53.p32}
ተመየጥኩ ፡ ድኅሬየ ፡ የሐልፍ ፡ መዋዕልየ ፡ በጽርዓት ፡ ወይረክበኒ ፡ ሞት ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ንስሓ ፡ ወእመኒ ፡ ቦእኩ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ውሒዝ ፡ አልብየ ፡ ኀይለ ፡ በዘ ፡ አዓዱ ፡ ወባሕቱ ፡ ይጌይሰኒ ፡ ወዲቅ ፡ ውስተ ፡ እደ ፡ እግዚ አብሔር ፡ ወገደፍኩ ፡ ነፍስየ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ እምዘ ፡ እንበር ፡ ጽሩዓ ።«
“If I turn back, my days will pass in idleness and death will find me without penance; but if I enter this flood, I have no strength with which to cross. Only, it is better for me to fall into the hand of God and cast my soul upon Him than to remain idle.”

33 Having said this, he took the fire and the book, put them in the fold of his garment, and entered the flood. Immediately the flood took him, lifted him up, and placed him feet up, head down, with his arms extended like a corpse, and he despaired of his life. As for the flood, it swelled, its waves rose, and by the will of God, the wave cast (Samu'ël) onto the edge of the water where he wished to cross. As for him, he was like a corpse and his heart went out because of the blows of the water and the horror of this flood that had frightened him. Shortly after, his spirit returned to him and he regained consciousness where he was, for prodigious is the work of God (Ps 97[98]:1), and His name is blessed. His garment was upon him and his cap upon his head, soaked with water. After that, he thought of the book and the fire; he groped in the fold of his garment and found them having no moisture nor any trace of water upon them.
ውስተ ፡ 'ሕባኔ ፡ መልበሱ'” ፡ ወረከቦን ፡ ዘአልቦ ፡ ራሕሰ ፡ ወኢጠለ ፡ ማይ ፡ በላዐሌሆን ስምዑ ፡ ኦአበዊየ ፡ ወአኃውየ ፡ እንግርክሙ ፡ 'ከመ ፡ ታንክሩ'" ፡ ዕበዮ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ። ወለይእቲሰ ፡ መጽሐፍ ፡ አልባቲ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ወኢልብሰ”' ፡ በዘ ፡ ይጠበልላ ፡ ወኢማእሰረ ፡ በዘያጸንዖ ፡ ወባሕቱ ፡ በከመ ፡ ተጽሕፈት ፡ በማየ ፡ ኅመት ፡ ከማሁ ፡ ክመ ፡ ሀለወት ። ይትነከር ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ እስመ ፡ በስባሔ ፡ ተሰብሐ ፡ በከመ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ነቢይ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ አሕዛብ ፡ እለ ፡ ገበ ርከ ፡ ይምጽኡ ፡ ወይስግዱ ፡ ቅድሜከ ፡ እግዚኦ ፡ ወይሰብሑ ፡ ለስምከ ። አይኑ ፡ ልሳን ፡ እግዚኦ ፡ ዘይክል ፡ ፈክሮ ፡ በጠንቅቆ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይንግር ፡ ዕመቆ ፡ ለዕባይከ ፡ ዘአሜ ፡ ልደትከ ፡ እምድንግል ፡ እንዘ ፡ ሀሎከ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ጎል ፡ ሶበ ፡ ገሰሰቶ ፡ በድፍረት ፡ ለዕበየ ፡ ምሥጢርከ ፡ ጠባይዓ ፡ ብእሲት ፡ ድክምት ። በማይ ፡ አንደድከ ፡ ወበእሳት ፡ አብረድከ ፡ በአጥባተ ፡ ድንግል ፡ እንተ ፡ ኢታአምር ፡ ብእሴ ፡ ሐሊበ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ደም ፡ እንበለ ፡ ቱሳሔ ፡ ዐቀ ብከ ። እለ ፡ ኪሩቤል ፡ በጐጠተ ፡ እሳት ፡ ዘኢይክሉ ፡ ለኪፎተከ ፡ በአብ ራከ ፡ ድንግል ፡ ነቢረከ ፡ ወእምኔሁ ፡ ጠበውከ ። ሰኩና ፡ ብእሲ ፡ በማእከለ ፡ ባሕር ፡ 'ከመ ፡ እንተ ፡ የብስ'" ፡ አኬድከ ። እጐለ ፡ ርግብ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ሕምዘ ፡ ከይሲ ፡ በመዋዕለ ፡ አይኅ ፡ እንበለ ፡ ሙስና ፡ ዐቀብከ ። ወበህየኒ ፡ ፅንሳ ፡ ለምድር ፡ በደመ ፡ ሰማኒቱ ፡ ነፍስ ፡ አፍረይከ ። ስብሐተ ፡ ወአኩ ቴተ ፡ በአፈ ፡ ኩልነ ፡ ናዓርግ ፡ ለከ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ። እሙንቱሰ ፡ ሰብአ ፡ ውእቱ'" ፡ መካን ፡ ሶበ ፡ ኀጥእዎ ፡ ገብሩ ፡ አው ያተ ፡ እስመ ፡ ያፈቅርዎ ፡ ኩሎሙ!" ፡ ከመ ፡ ነፍሶሙ ፡ ወፈድፋደሰ ፡ ሶበ ፡
34 Listen, my fathers and my brothers, that I may tell you so that you may admire the greatness of God: this book had no case or cover with which to wrap it, nor any tie with which to hold it; just as it had been written with ink, so (was it) as it was (before). God is admired, for He is glorified by glorification, according to what the prophet says: Let all the peoples whom You have created come and prostrate themselves before You, Lord, and let them glorify Your name (Ps 85[86]:9). What tongue, Lord, can explain with exactitude so as to tell the depth of Your greatness which (was manifested) at the moment of Your birth from the Virgin, while You were in the manger, when the nature of the weak woman touched with boldness the greatness of Your mystery? With water You burned and with fire You refrigerated; in the breasts of a virgin who did not know man, You kept the milk unmixed with blood. You whom the cherubim cannot touch with tongs of fire (Is 6:6), seated on the knees of a virgin, You suckled; with the sole of man's foot, You walked in the midst of the sea as on dry land (Mt 14:25//). The young of the dove, in the days of the flood, You kept without corruption despite the venom of the serpent (Gn 7-8). There You made the pregnancy of the earth fruitful with the blood of eight souls (1P 3:20; 2P 2:5). To You, always, by the mouth of us all, we will send up glorification and praise, for ever and ever, amen. 35 When the people of the place did not find him, they raised cries, for they all loved him as themselves, especially when they saw his

father weeping and lamenting before them; they dispersed in every place and searched for him in all the caves and in all the deserts. When God saw their pain, He revealed to them where he was. When they arrived near him, they wanted to take him by force; he cried out and it seemed to him that they were making him abandon his mother's breast, according to what the prophet says: How sweet to my throat are Your words; more than honey and sugar, they are sweet to my mouth! (Ps 118[119]:103) He said to them: “My fathers, what have I taken from you and what do you want from me? Why do you not leave me, so that I may weep because of my sin?” These saints said to him: “Listen, our brother, that we may speak to you; do not be angry with us, for our father Gabra Iyasus, at the moment of his death, left us a word concerning you: he was a prophet and a saint and nothing was hidden from him.” The blessed one said to them: “What is the word that your father told you?” They answered him and said to him: “Our father said to us thus: ‘My children, listen to me, that I may speak to you: in this place, there is no lack of priests and deacons, and monks fill this place. Only know (this) concerning a poor man, so that you do not drive him away: in a short time will come (a man) by the name of Sämu'ël — the name of his father (is) Estifänos; welcome him, for he is an elect of God. If he goes into the desert and into the caves, if he goes upon the water and upon the dry land, search for him and bring him back here. Indeed, by his prayer, many souls who (are) in this place will be saved.’ Because of that, therefore, we are searching for you.” When he heard this from them, he was astonished at their discourse. After that, they took him and led him to their home. May his prayer and his blessing be with us, amen.
ከረ ፡ እምነገሮሙ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ነሥእዎ ፡ ወወሰድዎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ መካኖሙ ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ ተሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ። ስምዑ ፡ 'ካዕበ ፡ እንግርክሙ!” ፡ ዘኮነ ፡ በ ፳ እመዋዕል ፡ እንዘ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ በጊዜ ፡ መሥዋዕት ፡ ወነሥኡ ፡ ሎሙ ፡ 'እለ ፡ ሀለዉ!" ፡ ወተመጠዉ ፡ እምሥጢራተ ፡ ቅድሳት ፡ ወውእቱኒ ፡ ተመጠወ ፡ ምስሌሆሙ ። ወእምድኅረ ፡ ተመጠዉ!?' ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይወፅኡ ፡ ውስተ ፡ አብያቲሆሙ ፡ ወ ፳መነኮስ ፡ እምእለ ፡ ተመጠዉ ፡ እምሥጢር ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ወቄአ ፡ ሶቤሃ ፡ ወወረቀ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ጸፍጻፈ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ። ወአለ ፡ ርእይዎ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ ደንገጹ ፡ ወቆሙ ፡ ድንጉዳኒሆሙ ። ወዝንቱ ፡ ብእሴ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡ ነሥአ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ በአፉሁ ፡ ወተመጠወ ፡ በልሳኑ ፡ ወለሐሰ ፡ መሬቶሂ ። ወሰምዐ!" ፡ ቃለ!” ፡ እምሰማይ ፡ ዘይብል ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡ ሳሙኤል ። ወአንሥአ ፡ ገጾ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሰማይ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይጠየቅ ፡ እምአይቴ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ቃል ፡ ወሶበ ፡ አንቃዕደወ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ወርእየ ፡ በጊዜሃ ፡
36 Listen again, that I may tell you what happened one day: while this saint was in the church at the moment of the (Eucharistic) sacrifice, all those who were present took and received the holy mysteries; he too communed with them. After they had communed, as they were going out toward their houses, a monk among those who had received the holy mystery vomited then and spat on the pavement of the church. Those who saw it were all frightened and stopped in fear; this man of God, the blessed Sämu’ël, took everything into his mouth, gathered with his tongue, and licked the (man's) spittle. He heard a voice from heaven that said: “Sämu'ël, Sämu'ël!” He lifted his face toward heaven to know from where this voice (originated) and, when he lifted his eyes upward, he saw then the seven heavens opened. This voice from above resumed and said to him: “Just as you have glorified me before the face of the
፪ ሰማያተ ፡ ርኅዋን ። ወአውሥአ ፡ ዝኩ ፡ ቃል ፡ ዘመልዕልት ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ በከመ ፡ ሰባሕከኒ ፡ በቅድመ ፡ ገጸ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ወአክበርከኒ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ ወኢ ኀፈርከ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትንሣእ ፡ ቂአተ ፡ ካልእከ ። ወከመሁ ፡ አነኒ ፡ ኣከብረከ ፡ ወእ ሴብሐከ ፡ በውስተ ፡ ማኅበረ ፡ በኩር ፡ በቅድመ ፡ አቡየ ፡ ዘበሰማያት ። 'ወከመዝ ፡ ይቤሎ ፡ ቃል ፡ እምሰማይ" ፥ ወከመሰ ፡ ርእየ ፡ ገጾ ፡ ለዘይነብር ፡ ዲበ ፡ መንበሩ ፡ ወእመሂ ፡ ኢርእየ ፡ እንዳዒ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ያአምር ። ወባሕቱ ፡ የሐብእ ፡ ግብራቲሁ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢይኩኖ ፡ ውዳሴ ፡ ተዘኪሮ ፡ ዘይቤ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ በወንጌል ፡ አሌለክሙ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ሰናየ ፡ ይብሉ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ላዕሌክሙ ፡ ወይዌ
people, have honored me greatly, and have not been ashamed to take the vomit of your neighbor, so I too will honor you and glorify you in the community of the First-Born, before my Father who (is) in heaven.” Thus spoke the voice from heaven to him. Whether he saw the face of Him who is seated on His throne or did not see (it), I do not know — God knows; but he hid his actions so that they would not be a praise to him, remembering what Our Lord says in the Gospel: “Woe to you when people speak well of you and praise you” (Lk 6:26).

37 And when his (spiritual) children asked him for one of his miracles, he did not tell (it) completely, he divided: he told half to one man and half to another; such (was) his custom. By the will of God, sages gathered (these miracles) after his death so that they would be profitable to many, just as the bee gathers honey from scattered flowers and makes a tasty virgin honey. My beloved, does this man of God seem to you not to have seen the face of the Living One? Why then did (He) open the seven gates of heaven to him? Would it have been impossible for Him to send His word without the shutters of heaven being opened, (to) Him who entered the womb of a virgin without the seal of her virginity being broken — and His birth was the same? According to what the prophet says: He who sends His word to the earth, and His words run quickly (Ps 147:4 [147:15]). So that you may know precisely that he saw the nature of the holy Trinity seated on His throne — the cherubim —, listen to what (Jesus) Himself says in the holy Gospel: “He who has my commandments and keeps them, he loves me, and he who loves me, my Father will love him, and I too will love him and will show myself to him” (Jn 14:21). When did He show His face to His beloved? Is it not at the moment of their misfortune?

38 Listen again: the martyrs, when a sentence of death was prescribed upon them by impious kings, some by fire and some by the sword, immediately (God) showed them His living face with the joy of His word; for Him they offered themselves to death, so that (the opposite) would not be a fault for them, because of His word which says: “He who denies me before the face of men, I too will deny him before the face of my Father who (is) in heaven” (Mt 10:33//). Because of that, therefore, they were ready for torment and for execution, saying: “It is better for us, rather than the torment of there, to be tormented here — rather than to destroy eternal life.” 39 As for this man of God, if he had passed by this vomit as his companions had passed by it, (that) would not have been a fault for him. But inflamed with the love of his God, he lowered himself to lick the human spittle when he saw it fallen (impregnated) with the flesh of his Lord, which had been imparted from the body of Him who is seated on His throne, the

cherubim. Indeed (God) chose him from his mother's womb (Gal 1:15) and sanctified him so that he would become a casket for His pearl. Because of that, he hastened in the upheaval toward the vomit of his companion and it seemed to him that one of the members of (Christ) had been severed, according to what Adam said to Eve: “Flesh of my flesh and bone of my bone” (Gn 2:23). Thinking therefore thus also that it was the body of (Christ), he did not blush before the assembly to lick it with his tongue; on the contrary, he gathered it into his belly with the dust and the dirt, having been seized by the love of his God. Because of that, the glorification and the honor returned to him from the mouth of the Lord and (He) imparted to him to see His face; crowned with this grace, he returned in peace to his dwelling. May the prayer, the blessing, and the power of the help of this saint remove, strike, and suppress from upon us all the power of the Enemy that attacks us, amen.
ጸላኢ ፡ ዘይትቃረነነ ፡ አሜን ።« ወእንዘ ፡ ይነብር ፡ ውስተ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ምኔት ፡ ኮነ ፡ ይረፍቅ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ማኅበሮሙ ። ወሶበ ፡ ይሁብዎ ፡ ቀኖናሁ ፡ እመሂ ፡ ይሰቲ ፡ አው ፡ ኢይሰቲ ፡ ስታየ ፡ ኢይጥዕም ፡ ወኢምንተኒ ፡ አልቦ ፡ ዘያአምሮ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ነፍሱ ፡ ባሕ ቲቱ ፡ ወእግዚአብሔር ፡ ምስሌሁ ፍ ወበዘከመዝ ፡ ገድል ፡ ነበረ ፡ ፲ ወ፪ዓመተ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ኢይሰቲ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ማይ ፡ ባሕቲቱ ። ኦብእሴ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ እፎ ፡ ኢያስፈጠከ ፡ ሥጋከ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ትሬእዮሙ ፡ ይሰትዩ ፡ አብያጺከ ። በቀኖና ፡ መጻሕፍት!” ፡ ነሰገ ፡ እንተ ፡ ውስጥ!" ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢይመብልከ ፡ ሞገደ ፡ ስስዕት ። ወበእንተዝ ፡ ኢያንቀልቀለ ፡ ድደ ፡ ጠባይዕ ፡ ዘልማዱ ፡ ፍትወት ። ናሁ ፡ ተፈጸመ ፡ በላዕሌከ ፡ ኦብእሴ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ኃለ ፡ እግዚእከ ፡ ዘይቤ ፡ ወዘሰ ፡ ሰትየ ፡ እማይ ፡ ዘእሁቦ ፡ አነ ፡ ኢይጸምእ'" ፡ ለዓለም ፡ 'አላ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ማይ ፡ ዘእሁቦ ፡ አነ ፡ ይከውን ፡ ውስቴቱ ፡
40 While he was staying in this convent, he took his meals in the community; when a rule was given to him — whether he drank or did not drink, of drink he tasted nothing at all — no one knew it except himself alone and God with him. He remained in such a (spiritual) struggle for twelve years without drinking anything except water only. Man of God, how did your body not drag you down while you saw your companions drinking? With the rule of the Scriptures you locked from within so that the flood of greed might not dominate you; because of this, it was not set in motion, the foundation of nature whose habit is desire. Behold, it has been fulfilled in you, man of God, the word of your Lord who says: «But whoever drinks of the water that I will give him will never thirst again, but the water that I will give him will become in him a spring of water welling up to eternal life» (Jn 4:14).
ነቅዐ ፡ ማይ ፡ ዘይፈለፍል ፡ ለሕይወት ፡ ዘለዓለም!” ፥ አቡሁኒ'” ፡ ነበረ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይትጋደል ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ወብዙኃን ፡ ኮንዎ ፡ ሰማዕተ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብሉ ፡ ኢርኢነ ፡ ዘከመዝ ፡ መነኮሰ ። ወእምብዝኃ ፡ ቀዊም ፡ ሐብጠ ፡ እገሪሁ ፡ ወይሰግድ ፡ ከመ ፡ መንኩራር ፡ ወበዘከመዝ ፡ ገድል!" ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ በሰላም ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ ተሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ።፡
41 His father also remained to combat (ascetically) much and many were witnesses for him, saying: “We have (never) seen such a monk!” Because of the frequent standing, his legs swelled, and he prostrated himself like a wheel. In such an ascetic combat, he died in peace. May his prayer and his blessing be with us, amen. After seventeen years spent at Waynä, Sämu'el retires to desert places
ወእምድኅረ ፡ ፈጸመ ፡ ላሐ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ ብፁዕ ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡ አሐዘ ፡ ይዝልፋ ፡ ለነፍሱ ፡ ወይበላ ፡ ኦነፍስየ ፡ ምንተ ፡ ተኀሥሚ ፡ እምይእዜ ፡ ወምንተ ፡ ታመከንዬ ፡ ቀዲሙ ፡ ነበርኪ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ትበልዒ ፡ እክለ ፡ በም ክንያተ ፡ ተልእኮ ፡ ለመምህርኪ ፡ ወእምድኅሬሁ ፡ ነበርኪ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ትበልዒ ፡ በምክንያተ ፡ ተልእኮ ፡ ለአቡኪ ። እምይእዜሰ ፡ አልብኪ ፡ ምክንያተ ፡ ከመ ፡
42 After he had finished the mourning for his father, the blessed Sämu’ël began to scourge his soul and to say to it: “My soul, what will you seek henceforth and what will you pretext? First you remained to eat bread, under the pretext of serving your master and, after that, you remained to eat under the pretext of serving your father; but from now on you have no pretext

ትበልዒ ፡ እክለ ፍ ፃኢ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትኅሥሚ ፡ መድኀኒተኪ ፡ ኢታአምሪ ፡ ማእዜ ፡ ይመጽእ ፡ ኀዲስ ፡ በዓለ ፡ ቤት ። ወዘንተ ፡ ብሂሎ ፡ ወፅአ ፡ እምውእቱ ፡ መካን ፡ እምድኅረ ፡ ነበረ ፡ ፲ ወኒ ዓመተ ፡ ወጉጐየ'"' ፡ ርቀ ፡ ብሔረ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ኢይረክብዎ ። ወቦአ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ውሳጤ ፡ ገዳም ፡ ወረከበ' ፡ ንስቲተ ፡ በዐተ ፡ በአምሳለ ፡ ጾማዕት ፡ ወቦአ!” ፡ ውስቴታ ። ወኢነሥአ ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ ስንቀ ፡ ዘየ አክሎ ፡ ወኢፅፍነተ ፡ ዘይሰቲ ፡ አላ ፡ ረሰየ ፡ ስቴሁኒ ፡ ወመብልዑኒ ፡ ፍቅረ ፡ አምላኩ ። ወይፈቅድ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ መዊተ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይስልጥ ፡ ምግቦ ። ወነ በረ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ በዓት ፡ ጓመዓልተ ፡ ወቫጣሌሊተ ፡ 'ዘእንበለ ፡ ይጥ ዓም!" ፡ ምንተኒ ። አላ ፡ ነበረ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይትቀነይ ፡ ከመ ፡ መላእክት ፡ ወጠ ግአ ፡ አማዑቱ ፡ ወየብሰ ፡ ኖላሁ ፡ ወሰሰለ ፡ እምልቡ ፡ ፍትወተ ፡ መብልዕ ። ወእምአሜሃኬ ፡ ዕለት"''' ፡ ወእስከ ፡ ተፍጻሜቱ ፡ ኢገብአት ፡ ይእቲ ፡ አማዑቱ ፡
for eating bread. Go out to seek your salvation, you do not know when the new master of the house will come (Mt 24:47-51).” Having said this, he went out from this place after having remained (there) seventeen years and fled into a distant land where he would not be found; he entered into the interior of the desert, found a small cave in the likeness of a cell, and entered it. He did not take with him provisions that sufficed him nor a waterskin from which he drank, but he made both his drink and his food the love of his God; he wanted all the time to die to bring his task to an end. He remained in this cave forty days and forty nights without tasting anything, but he remained singing like the angels; his intestines stuck together, his skull dried up, and the desire for food disappeared from his heart. From that day, therefore, and until his end, his intestines did not take, according to habit, food; on the contrary, they dried up inside.
ለነሚአ ፡ መብልዕ ፡ በከመ ፡ ልማድ ፡ አላ ፡ የብሰት ፡ እንተ ፡ ውስጥ ። ወሰብአ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ደብር'" ፡ ነበሩ ፡ እንዘ ፡ የኃሥሥዎ ፡ በዐቢይ ፡ ጻህቅ ፡ ወረከብዎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ በዐት ፡ ይቡሰ ፡ ከመ ፡ ዕፅ ። ወነሥእዎ ፡ ሶቤሃ ፡ ወወሰድዎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ደብሮሙ ።፡ ወነበረ ፡ ንስቲተ ፡ መዋዕለ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ደብር ፡ ወእምድኅረዝኒ ፡ ጐየ ፡ ወተድህለ ፡ ወሖረ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገዳም ፡ ኀበ ፡ ኢያአምርዎ'"" ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ወዓዲ ፡ ገብአ ፡ 'ውስተ ፡ ዘቀዳሚ” ፡ ጻማሁ ፡ ወነበረ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይሴሰይ ፡ እምሥርወ ፡ ዕፅ ፡ ወእምአሕማላተ ፡ ገዳም ። ወእም አሜሃ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ነበረ ፡ ባሕቲቱ ፡ እምድኅረ ፡ ሞተ ፡ አቡሁ ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበ
43 The people of the monastery remained searching for him with great diligence and found him in the cave, dry as wood; they took him then and brought him into their monastery; after that, he fled, escaped, and went into a desert where people would not know (that) he (was there). He returned also to his previous austerities and remained feeding on tree roots and herbs of the desert. From that day, he remained alone, after his father had died. May his prayer and his blessing be with us, amen.
ረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ። ስምዑ ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ንንግርክሙ ፡ ዘገብረ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ ውስተ'" ፡ በዐት ፡ በውስተ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ገዳም ፡ እንዘ ፡ ያጻሙ ፡ ርእሶ ፡ በዐ ቢይ ፡ ገድል ። መዓልተ ፡ ይጸውም ፡ ወሰርከ ፡ ይጥዕም ፡ ንስቲተ ፡ ዘረከበ ፡ እመሂ ፡ እምሥርወ ፡ ገዳም ፡ ወእመሂ ፡ እምፍሬ ፡ ዕፀው ፡ ወእምኔሁ ፡ አኮ'" ፡ ዘይነሥእ ፡ እስከ ፡ ይጸግብ ፡ አላ ፡ ለእመ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ፻ ተፍእሞ ፡ ፪አው፳ ፡ ወማ ይኒ ፡ መጠነ ፡ ምልአ ፡ እድ፳ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ኢያበዝኅ ። ወከመዝ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይትጋ ደል ፡ ፈጸመ ፡ ፻ሻ''' ወላዕለ ፡ ዝኒ ፡ ወሰከ ፡ ካልአ'" ፡ ተሐርሞ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ይበይት'” ፡ ሰርከ ፡ 'በከመ ፡ እቤለክሙ ፡ ቀዳሚ!'"' ፡ እምድኅረ ፡ 'ዕርበተ ፡ ፀሐይ!'' ፡ ይቀውም ፡ እስከ ፡ ይፀብሕ ፡ 'ከመ ኢይምኦ ፡ ንዋም!" = ወሶበኒ ፡ መጽአ!” ፡ ንዋም ፡ እንዘ ፡ ቅውም ፡ ይፌጽም ፡ ወኢይነብርሂ ፡ ወኢይነውምሂ'”" = ወበዘከመዝ ፡ ገድል ፡ እንዘ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ እምድኅረ ፡ ፈጸመ ፡ ፻ጣወድቀ ፡ በገጹ'” ፡ የቢሶ ፡ ከመ ፡ ሣዕር ፡ እስመ ፡ ኃጥአት ፡ ሥጋ ፡ በዘትጸንዕ ። መብልዐ ፡ ወመ ስቴ ፡ ኀትአት ፡ ወንዋመ ፡ ለዕረፍት ፡ ኀጥአት ። ወበእንተዝ ፡ ዐጽም ፡ ባሕ ቲቱ ፡ ከመ ፡ ገልዕ ፡ ኮነት ። ወእስትንፋስሂ ፡ ኃጥአት ፡ ምብያተ ። ወበእንተዝ ፡ ወድቀ ፡ ከዊኖ ፡ ከመ ፡ ሣዕር ፡ ይቡስ ፡
44 Listen again, that I may tell you what this saint did while he was in a cave in this desert macerating himself in a great (ascetic) combat: by day, he fasted and, in the evening, tasted the little he had found — either roots of the desert or fruits of trees — and, of that, he did not take until he was satisfied, but if there were three mouthfuls, he (took only) two or one only; water also, (he) took only one hollow of the hand without (taking) more. By combating (ascetically) thus, he spent three (times) forty (days); to that he added another abstinence: when he was going to spend the night, in the evening, as I told you first, after the setting of the sun, he stood upright until morning so that sleep would not overcome him. If sleep had come while he was standing, he finished (praying) and did not sit down nor fall asleep. While he was in such an (ascetic) combat, after he had accomplished three (times) forty (days), he fell on his face, dried up like grass, for (his) body lacked what to be strong: it lacked food and drink, it lacked sleep for rest: because of that, he had become bone only, like a potsherd, and breath also lacked shelter. Because of that, he fell, having become like dry grass.


[p. 42] {#po.53.p42}
