45 While he was lying down, all the beasts came to him: lion, leopard, hyena, and serpent. The blessed one said: “Before all came to me a great serpent opening its mouth; when I saw it having opened its mouth, to me, in my thought, it seemed that it was going to swallow my cell. I said to God in my heart — for I could not utter a word and my throat was closed by thirst —, I said (therefore): ‘My Lord and my God, do not deliver me to this enemy of my father, for he seduced them and made them go out of the Garden; according to what You will have willed, by another death kill me.’ After that, (the serpent) turned toward me with clemency, closed its mouth, prostrated itself at my knees, kissed my feet, licked (them) with its tongue, and, then, returned to its lair. After that came a great leopard; when I saw it, my heart was frightened more than by the previous serpent. It too prostrated itself before me while wagging its tail, kissed my feet like the previous serpent, and went into its lair. After that came a hyena with a very ugly appearance; it too greeted me and went into its lair. After all came a great lion; it sat before me and began to lick my hands and my feet. As for me, I gathered my strength in my soul and seized the ear (of the lion) and its mouth; it leaned on me as if it had known me for a long time, and I too loved it more than all the beasts that had come to me. It lingered sitting before me, as if it were comforting me. I addressed the word to it by forcing myself, for my throat was closed and dried up by the thirst for water, and said to it: ‘Rise and go seek your food, for the evening has come.’ After that, it prostrated itself, greeted me, and went into its lair.”
እስመ ፡ መስየ ፡ ብሔር ። ወእምድኅረ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ሰገደ ፡ ወተአምሐኒ ፡ ወሖረ ፡ መካኖ ። ኦአባ ፡ ዜና ፡ ቲሩትከ ፡ ምንተ ፡ ይኒ ፡ ለሰሚዖቱ ፡ ያደምመኒ ። ወአራዊተ ፡ ትቀኒ ፡ ወአናብስተ ፡ ታገኒ ። መዐዛ ፡ ውዳሴከ ፡ ከመ ፡ መዓር ፡ ጥዕመኒ ፡ ወከመ ፡ ዕጣን ፡ ምዕዘኒ ።= በጽላሎተ ፡ ክነፊከ ፡ ከመ ፡ እጓለ ፡ ዖፍ ፡ ክድነኒ ። ወእምግርማ ፡ ጸላኢ ፡ አድኅነኒ ፡ ከመ ፡ ወልታ ፡ ሥሙር ፡ ክልለኒ ። በደመ ፡ ገቦሁ ፡ ለዘቤዘወኒ ፡ ለገቢረ ፡ ፈቃዱ ፡ አጽነዓኒ ። መክሊቶ ፡ ለዘወሀበኒ ፡ ረስየኒ ፡ ኣርብሕ ፡ ካዕበተ ፡ ዘምስለ ፡ ረባሑ ፡ ለዘይትኃሠሠኒ ፡ ነጠብጣበ ፡ አንብዕከ ፡ ቤዛ ፡ ነፍስየ'" ፡ ይኩነኒ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ።
46 Abba, the story of your virtue, what is more beautiful? Hearing it puts me in stupor. You subjugate the beasts and you dominate the lions. The perfume of your praise is sweet to me like honey and embalms me like incense. Under the shadow of your wings shelter me like a little bird (Ps 90[91]:4). From the terror of the Enemy save me. Like an excellent shield surround me. With the blood of the side of Him who redeemed me give me the strength to do His will. From the talent of Him who gave (it) to me make me draw a double profit which (will be) with the profit of Him who will ask me for an account (Mt 25:14-27). May the drops of your tears be for me the redemption of my soul, for ever and ever, amen.
ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ በጽሑ'” ፡ መነኮሳት ፡ እለ ፡ ውስተ ፡ መካን ፡ ወረከ ብዎ ፡ ግዱፈ ፡ ወነሥእዎ ፡ ወወሰድዎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ መካኖሙ ። ወእምድኅረዝኒ ፡ ተድህለ ፡ ካዕበ'" ፡ ወሖረ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገዳሙ!'"' ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ ተሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ።
47 After that, the monks who (were) in (this) place arrived and found him lying down; they took him and brought him into their monastery. After that, he escaped again and went into his desert. May his prayer and his blessing be with us, amen.

### Sämu’ël welcomes his first spiritual sons
ወእምዝ ፡ በውእቱ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ ረከቦ ፡ ለዘሩፋኤል ፡ ወረሰዮ ፡ ወልደ ፡ ዚአሁ ። ወእምድኅረ ፡ ነበረ ፡ ንስቲተ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ርእየ ፡ ጥብዓተ ፡ ልቡ ፡
48 After that, in those days, he met Za-Rufä’ël and made him his (spiritual) son. After (the latter) had remained a little, when (Sämu'el) saw the firmness of


[p. 44] {#po.53.p44}
ወጽንዓ ፡ ሃይማኖቱ ፡ ወበውእቱ ፡ ገዳም ፡ አልበሶ ፡ እስመ ፡ ሕፃን ፡ ውእቱ ፡ አሜሃ ፍ ወመሀሮ ፡ ሕገ ፡ ምንኩስና ፡ ወሐፀኖ ፡ በፈሪሀ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ። እስመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ በኩረ ፡ ኮኖ ፡ እምነ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ ደቂቁ ፡ በከመ ፡ ኮኖ ፡ በኩረ ፡ ለእንጦንዮስ ፡ አባ ፡ መቃርዮስ ፡ ዘየዐቢ ፡ እምአእላፍ ፡ ደቂቁ ። ወለአ ሐዱኒ ፡ ስሙ ፡ ኒቆዲሞስ ፡ ወልደ ፡ መምህሩ ፡ መጽአ' ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ ወነበሩ ፡ ሰለስቲሆሙ ፡ በፍቅር ፡ መንፈሳዊት ፡ ረሲዮሙ ፡ ኪያሁ ፡ አበ ፡ እሉ ፡ ክልኤቱ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ሖሩ ፡ ይኅስሱ ፡ በዓተ ፡ እሉ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ እስመ ፡ በጽሐ ፡ ሰዓተ!” ፡ ክረምት ። ወእንዘ ፡ የሐውሩ ፡ በፍኖት ፡ ረከቡ ፡ ዐቢየ ፡ ፈለገ ፡ ወቦሙ ፡ መርበብተ ፡ ወወገረ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቀላይ ፡ እስመ ፡ መሰግረ ፡ ዓሣ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወምሁር ፡ በኩሉ ፡ በከመ ፡ መሀሮ ፡ መን ፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ። ወሶቤሃ ፡ ተእኅዘ ፡ ዓሣ ፡ ወሰሐቡ ፡ መርበብተ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያው ፅኡ ፡ እምነ ፡ ቀላይ ፡ ወስእኑ ፡ አንቀልቅሎቶ ፡ ወሰርጉ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ለአውፅ ኦቶ ።፡ ወሶበ ፡ ተስእኖሙ ፡ አሰሩ ፡ ኀብለ ፡ መርበብት ፡ በጽንፈ ፡ ማይ ፡ ወቤቱ ፡ ህየ ፡ እንዘ ፡ አልቦ ፡ ዘይዴረሩ ። ወእምቅድመ ፡ አሜሃ” ፡ አልቦ ፡ ዘአስተጋብኡ ፡ ወኢዘአረሩ ። አላ ፡ በትውክልተ ፡ አምላክ ፡ የሐድሩ ። ወበሣ ኒታ ፡ ተንሥአ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡ ወቦአ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ፅመቀ ፡ ቀላይ ፡ ወአስተ
his heart and the solidity of his faith, he gave him the (monastic) garment in the desert; he was then a small child. He taught him the monastic law and raised him in the fear of God. (Za-Rufa'ël) was for him the eldest of all his (spiritual) children, as Abba Macarius the Great was for Anthony the eldest of his myriads of children. The name of a (monk was) Nigodimos, son of his master; he came to (Sämu'ël) and all three remained in a spiritual love, the two (monks) having made (Sämu'el) a father. After that, these saints went to look for a cave, for the time of winter had arrived. While they were traveling, they met a great river; they had a net and our father Sämu’el cast (it) into the depths, for he was a fisherman and instructed in everything, according to what the Holy Spirit had instructed him. Immediately a fish was caught, they pulled the net to take (it) out of the depths, but could not move it; they labored much to take it out. As they could not (do it), they tied the rope of the net to the edge of the water and spent the night there, without dining. Before this moment, they had not harvested nor reaped, but they remained in trust in God. The next day, our father Sämu’ël rose, entered to the bottom of the depths, gathered his net, and took it out by force.
ጋብአ ፡ መርበብቶ ፡ ወአውፅኦ ፡ በኀይል ።= ወአሜሃሰ ፡ ሀለዉ ፡ ህየ ፡ ፄ መነኮሳት ፡ ካልኣን ፡ ወኮኑ ፡ ዘ8ወሶበ ፡ ርእይዎ ፡ ግዘፎ ፡ ለውእቱ ፡ ዓሣ ፡ አንከሩ ፡ እስመ ፡ ዐቢይ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ጥቀ ። ወሶበ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ሰርከ ፡ ወገመድዎ ፡ ወአብሰልዎ ፡ ወበልዑ ፡ ወጸግቡ ፡ ወአትረፉ ፡ ብዙኀ ፡ እምውእቱ ፡ ዓሣ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ሖሩ ፡ እሉ ፡ ፪መነኮሳት ፡ ወተርፉ ፡ ሠለስቲሆሙ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ሖሩ ፡ ይኅሥሥ ፡ በዓተ ። ወእንዘ ፡ የሐ ውሩ ፡ በፍኖት ፡ ተአከየ ፡ ሰይጣን ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወልድ ፡ ዘስሙ ፡ ዘሩፋ ኤል ፡ ወተከለ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ዕፀ ፡ ኀበ ፡ እንተ ፡ የሐልፍ ፡ ወወግኦ ፡ እገሪሁ ፡ ወበረረት ፡ ከመ ፡ እንተ ፡ ሐጽ ፡ እስከ ፡ ተርእየት ፡ እንተ ፡ ላዕሉ ።= ወጻመወ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ለአውፅኦቱ ፡ ወሰሐበ ፡ በአስናኑ ፡ ወመሐዋ ፡ በኀይል ፡ እስከ ፡ ውሕዘ ፡ ደም ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገጹ ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ ረከቡ ፡ ንስቲተ ፡ ጾላዕተ'"፤ ፡ ዘአል ባቲ ፡ በቀዓ ፡ ወቦኡ ፡ ውስቴታ ፡ አመ ፡ ፲ ወ፪ለሰኔ ። ወነበሩ ፡ ህየ ፡ በዐ ቢይ ፡ መከራ ፡ እምብዝኃ ፡ ምር ፡ ወዝናም'” ፡ እስመ ፡ መዋዕለ ፡ ክረምት ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወአልቦ ፡ ዘይሴሰዩ ፡ ወኢምንተኒ ወእግዚአብሔር ፡ ይሬኢ ፡ ንዴ
49 Then there were six other monks there and they were nine. When they saw the size of the fish, they were astonished, for it was very large. When it was evening, they cut it up and cooked it; they ate, were satisfied, and left much of this fish. After that, the six monks went away and the three (others) remained. After that, they went to look for a cave. While they were traveling, Satan did harm to the adolescent by the name of Za-Rufä'ël: he planted for him a (piece of) wood where he was passing and (the latter) pricked his foot and pierced it like an arrow so that it appeared toward the top. Our father strove to take it out, he pulled it with his teeth and tore it out by force, so that blood flowed on his face. After that, they found a small unused cave and entered it on the twelfth of Sanë. They remained there in great torment because of the rigor of the cold and the rain, for these were the days of winter and there was nothing of which they could feed themselves. God saw their destitution and they fed themselves on what they found, either leaves or roots.
ቶሙ ፡ ወይሴሰዩ ፡ እምዘ ፡ ረከቡ ፡ እመሂ ፡ ቄጽለ ፡ ወእመሂ ፡ ሥርወ ።፡ ወሶበ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ሎቱ" ፡ በዕለተ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ጾላዕት ፡ ወሖረ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ያንሶሱ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ውሣቲተ ፡ ገዳም ፡ ምስለ ፡ ኒቆዲሞስ ፡ ወልዱ ። ወሶበ ፡ ሖሩ ፡ ንስቲተ ፡ ረከቡ ፡ 'ዐቢየ ፡ በዓተ'” ፡ ወተፈሥሑ ፡ ሶቤሃ ፡ ወገብሩ ፡ ውውዓ ፡ ከመ ፡ ዘረከቡ ፡ ጽርሐ ፡ ርሱየ ፡ ወአእኩትዎ ፡ ለእግዚአ ብሔር ። ወእምዝ ፡ ሖሩ ፡ ይንበሩ ፡ ውስቴታ ፡ ወቦኡ ፡ አመ ፡ ፲ ወ፪ ለሐምሌ ፡ ወይእቲ ፡ አኮ ፡ ፍጽምት ፡ ትርሲታ ፡ ወበፕኅታ ፡ ይውሕዝ ፡ ዐቢይ ፡ ፈለግ ፡
50 When he had been thirty days in this cave, our father went to walk in the interior of the desert with Nigodimos, his (spiritual) son. When they had advanced a little, they found a large cavern; they rejoiced then, made a great acclamation, as if they had found a furnished palace, and praised God. After that, they went to remain there and entered (it) on the twelfth of Hamlë. (Of) this one the layout was not complete: through its door flowed


[p. 46] {#po.53.p46}
ወአስራበ ፡ ማይኒ ፡ ይነፈንፍ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ውሣጤ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ በዓት ፡ 'እምአስ ዋጠ ፡ ውሒዝ ፡ ዘወረደ'" ፡ ታሕተ ፍ ወነበሩ ፡ ውስቴታ ፡ እሉ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ እንዘ ፡ ኢያአምሩ ፡ በአቶ ፡ ወጸአቶ ፡ ለክረምት ፡ እምግርማ ፡ ውእቱ'” ፡ ውቲዝ ፡ ዘይደምፅ ፡ ከመ ፡ ነጐድጓድ ፡ ወኮኖሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ማዕዖ ፡ ውስተ ፡ አፈ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ በዓት ።
a large torrent and cataracts of water also poured into the interior of this cave because of the downpours of the flood that had fallen. The saints remained in (the cave) without having knowledge of the beginning and the end of the winter because of the terrible (rumbling) that burst forth like thunder and was to them like a bolt at the opening of the cave.
ስምዑ ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ንዜኑክሙ”' ፡ ዘገብረ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ አቡነ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ በዐት ። ወበ ፳-እመዋዕል ፡ ሖረ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ዘሩፋኤል ፡ ወልዱ ፡ ይኅሥጮሠ ፡ ሲሳዮሙ ። ወእንዘ ፡ የሐውሩ ፡ በፍኖት ፡ ረከቡ ፡ ዐቢየ ፡ ፈለገ ፡ ወዐደውዎ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ኢይትጌየሎሙ ። ወይቤ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ረድእ ፡ ዘዜነወነ ፡ ዘንተ ። ወእንዘ ፡ ንትመየጥ ፡ ረከብኖ ፡ ምሉአ ፡ እምጽንፉ ፡ እስከ ፡ ጽንፉ ፡ እምብዝኃ ፡ ውቲዝ ፡ ወዝናም ። ወእቤሎ ፡ ለአቡየ ፡ ኦአባ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ማይ ፡ አኮኑ ፡ ዓዶኖ ፡ ይእዜ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ኢይትጎዔየለነ ፡ ናሁ ፡ መልአ ፡ እስከ ፡ ድንጋጉ ። ወይቤለኒ ፡ ተንሥእ ፡ ኦወልድየ ፡ ንጹሊ' ፡ ኀበ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ከመ ፡ ያክህለነ ፡ ዓዲወ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ውሒዝ ፡ ወስግድ ፡ ሥልሰ ፡ ቅድሜየ ፡ ወኢትሖር ፡ አንጻረ ። ወናሁ ፡ ሀለወት ፡ ቅድሜነ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ጽንፈ ፡ ማይ ፡ ኦም ፡ ዐቢይ ፡ ዘስሙ ፡ ዘግባ ፡ ማዕዶ ፡ ከሐ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ንፈቅድ ፡ ንኅነ ፡ ዐዲወ ። ወሶበ ፡ ፈጸምኩ ፡ ዘአኦዘዘኒ ፡ ረከብኩ ፡ ርእስየ ፡ ታሕተ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ኦም'” ዘግባ ፡ ምስለ ፡ መምህርየ ፡ ኅቡረ ፡ ወውእቱ ፡ ፈለግ ፡ ዘቅድሜነ ፡ ገብአ ፡ ድኅሬነ ። ወእቤሎ ፡ ኦአባ ፡ ዛቲ ፡ ኦም ፡ እንተ ፡ ርኢና ፡ 'በማዕዶ ፡ ከሐ”" ፡ መኑ ፡ አዕደዋ ፡ ዝየ ፡ ወዝንቱኒ ፡ ማይ ፡ ዘርኢኖ ፡ በቅድሜነ ፡ ናሁ ፡ ገብአ ፡ ድኅሬነ ። ወይቤለኒ ፡ አርምም ፡ ወልድየ ፡ ወባሕቱ ፡ ናእኩቶ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ በእንተ ፡ ሀብተ ፡ ጸጋሁ ፡ ዘረድአነ ። ወኢትክሥት ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ምሥጢረ ፡ እስከ ፡
51 Listen again, that we may tell you what this father of ours did while he was in the cave: one day, he went with Za-Rufä'ël his (spiritual) son to seek their food; while they were traveling, they met a great river and crossed it without it pressing them. The disciple, who told us this, says: “While we were returning, we found it full from one bank to the other because of the abundance of the current and the rain. I said to my father: “Abba, did we not cross this water a little while ago without it pressing us: now it is full to its banks?” He said to me: “Rise, my son; we are going to pray to God so that He may make us capable of crossing this flood. Prostrate yourself three times before me and do not go in front.” Behold, there was before us at the edge of the water a large tree by the name of zagbä, on the opposite bank where we wanted to cross. When I had finished what he had prescribed to me, I found myself under this zagbä-tree together with my master and the river that (was) before us had returned behind us. I said to him: “Abba, this tree that we saw on the opposite bank, who made it cross here? And this water that we saw before us, behold it has returned behind us!” He said to me: “Be silent, my son, only let us praise God for the gift of His grace that helped us; do not reveal this mystery until the day of my death.” I made the firm oath to him (about it).
ዕለተ ፡ ሞትየ ፡ ወአምሐሎ ፡ በጽኑዕ ፡ ማሕላ ። እስመ ፡ ግብረ ፡ እግዚአብሔርሰ ፡ ጥቀ ፡ ይትነከር ፡ ዘከመ ፡ ምንት ፡ ዐደወ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ድምፅ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ረድኡ ። ለእመኒ ፡ ተውህቦ ፡ክንፈ ፡ ኢያእመርነ ፡ 'ወለእመኒ ፡ ተጋውሐት ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ኢርኢነ”' ወለእመኒ ፡ ኬደ ፡ በእገሪሁ ፡ አሰረ ፡ ማይ ፡ ኢተረክበ ፡ ውስቴቱ ። ብፅዓን ፡ ለከ ፡ ኦአባ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ዘበኩልሄ ፡ ግብርከ ፡ ውዱስ ። ዘዐደውኮ ፡ ለውቲኅዘ ፡ ፈለግ ፡ ግሩም ፡ ምስለ ፡ ረድእከ ፡ በሐመረ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ከመ ፡ እንተ ፡ የብስ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ሰኩናክሙ ፡ ኢይርሕስ ። ወተመሰልኩ ፡ ለኤልሳዕ ፡ ዘዐደወ ፡ ማየ ፡ ዮርዳኖስ ፡ ዘቢጦ ፡ በሐሜለተ ፡ መምህሩ ፡ ኤልያስ ። ኀይለ ፡ ጸሎትከ ፡ ያድኅነነ ፡ እምአርዌ ፡ ርኩስ ፡ ነሀሉ ፡ ውስተ ፡ በአትከ ፡ በሀገር ፡ ሐዳስ ፡ 'እንተ ፡ ኢትፈልስ”"።" = ስብሐት ፡ ለሥሉስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ እስከ ፡ ደኀሪት ፡ እስትን ፋስ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ።
52 The work of God is altogether admirable, the manner in which this saint crossed without noise with his disciple! If he was given wings, we did not know (it); if (the river) was gathered for him, we did not see (it); if he trod (it) with his feet, the trace of the water was not found upon (them). Beatitude to you, holy abba, you whose work in everything (is) praised, you who crossed the course of the fearsome river with your disciple on the ship of the Spirit, like dry land, without the sole of your feet being wetted! You were similar to Elisha who crossed the water of the Jordan, having struck it with the mantle of his master Elijah (4R 2:14). May the power of your prayer save us from the impure serpent so that we may be in your cell in the new city that does not pass away. Glory to the holy Trinity until the last breath, for ever and ever, amen!

53 One day, while they were in this cave, they went out to seek their food — either roots or leaves; abba Sämu’ël found a root in a sloping place in which there was a large crevice. Our father Sämu’ël took a pickaxe and dug (around) the root until he


[p. 48] {#po.53.p48}
ዐቢይ ፡ ጸድፍ ። ወነሥአ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡ መክርየ ፡ ወከረየ ፡ ውእተ ፡ ሥርወ ፡ መጠነ ፡ ቦአ ፡ መትሕተ ፡ ምድር ። ወእምድኅረ ፡ ቀርበ ፡ ለፍጻሜሃ ፡ አኀዘ ፡ ይብትካ ። ወሶበ ፡ ተበትከ ፡ ወድቀ ፡ ድኅሪተ ፡ ወወረደ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ጸድፍ ። ወጐድኦ ፡ እብን ፡ ውስተ ፡ ናላሁ ፡ ወአስተርአየ ፡ ከመ ፡ ርምየተ ፡ ኩናት ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ከመ ፡ በድን ። ወስምዐ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ለሊሁ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡ አባ ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡ ወይቤ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ሀሎኩ ፡ ውዱቅየ ፡ መጽአ ፡ ኀቤየ ፡ ለሊሁ ፡ መድኀኒነ ፡ ተዐጺፎ ፡ ሞጣኅተ ፡ ብርሃን ፡ ወርዕድኩ ፡ እምግርማሁ ፡ ወገሠሠኒ ፡ ለንታየ ፡ ወጸበጠኒ ፡ ርእስየ”"" ፡ በእደዊሁ ፡ ማኅየዊት ፡ ወአጽንዓ””" ፡ ወኮ ንኩ ፡ ከመ ፡ ዘኢረከበኒ ፡ ሙስና ። ወእምአሜሃ ፡ ሀለወት ፡ ጽንእታ ፡ እስከ ፡
penetrated under the earth. After he had approached its end, he began to break (the root); when it broke, he fell backward and fell into this crevice. A stone struck his skull, it appeared like a spear wound and he was like a corpse. The blessed abba Sämu'ël was himself a witness and said: “While I was lying down, our Savior came Himself to me, clothed in a mantle of light; I trembled before His majesty. He touched me all over, took my head in His life-giving hand, fortified it, and I was as if perdition had not reached me. From that moment, it was solid until now.” He always told (this) to his (spiritual) children, while being alive.
ይእዜ ። ወከመዝ ፡ ይዜኑ ፡ ወትረ ፡ ለደቂቁ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ በሕይወቱ ። ብፅዓን ፡ ለከ ፡ ኦአባ ፡ ዓጸደ ፡ ወይን ፡ ሥሙር ፡ ዘምሉእ ፡ አስካለ ፡ በረከት ፡ ውስቴቱ ፡ ከመ ፡ መላእክት ፡ ዘኮነ”" ፡ ሕይወቱ ፡ ወከመ ፡ ርግብ ፡ የወሀቱ ። ውስተ ፡ ትርብዕታ ፡ ለምድርነ' ፡ ከመ ፡ ፀሐየ ፡ መርሞዳ ፡ ዘየ ሐቱ ። ፈለገ ፡ ሐሊብ ፡ ዘምሉእ ፡ ቅድሐቱ ። አስከሬነ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ውእቱ"”" ፡ ፅንቄ ፡ አንህዮ ፡ ዘሕቡእ ፡ ውስቴቱ”"”" ፡ ቃላተ ፡ ወንጌል ፡ ዘኮኖ ፡ ትዕርዝቱ ። ኀበ ፡ ንወፍር ፡ ወነአቱ ፡ ይትማኅፀነነ"” ፡ ጸሎቱ ፡ አሜን ወእምድኅረዝኒ ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ተአከየ ፡ ሰይጣን ፡ ወአምጽአ ፡ ጽኔሁ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወልድ ፡ ወሰበረ ፡ ብረኪሁ ። ወኮኖ ፡ ፅፁበ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ግብር ፡ ወፈድፋደሰ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ዘይፈቅዱ ፡ ይፍልሱ ፡ እምይእቲ ፡ በዐት ፡ እስመ ፡ ኃጥኡ ፡ ዘይሴሰዩ ፡ እመሂ”'' ፡ ሥርወ ፡ አው ፡ ፍሬ ፡ ሣዕር ። ወይ ቤሎ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ኦወልድየ ፡ ንበር ፡ ዝየ ፡ እስከ ፡ ትትፌወስ ፡ ከመ ፡ ንሖር”' ፡ ንሕሥሥ ፡ ሲሳየነ" ፡ እስከ ፡ ንርከብ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ኦሆ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ሖሩ ፡ ይኅሥሱ ፡ ሲሳዮሙ ፡ ወገደፍዎ” ፡ ህየ ። ወእምድኅረ ፡ ሰሉስ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ ኀለየ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወልድ ፡ ወይቤ" ፡ እፎኑ ፡ እነብር ፡ አነ ፡ ዝየ ፡ ባሕቲትየ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ምድረ ፡ ፈያት ፡ ወአራዊት ። እትነሣእ ፡ ወአሐውር ፡ ኀበ ፡ አቡየ ፡= ወዘንተ ፡ ብሂሎ ፡ ወፅአ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይድኅክ ፡ በበንስቲት ፡ ንስቲት ፡ ወበፈቃደ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ በጽሐ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ሀለዉ ።= ወእሙንቱሂ ፡ ረከቡ ፡ ኦመ ፡ እንተ ፡ ምልእት ፡ ፍሬ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ይቤሎሙ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ለደቂቁ ፡ ኦውሉድየ ፡ ንዑ ፡ ንሕሥሥ ፡ በዐተ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ንበይት ፡ ወገቢአነ ፡ ናስተጋብእ ፡ ፍሬ ፡ ዕፅ ፡ ለድራርነ ። ወሶበ ፡ ሖሩ ፡ ይኅሥሥሠ ፡ በዐተ ፡ ነሥአ ፡ ምሳረ ፡ ማር ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡ ወአሐዘ ፡ ይምትር ፡ ፅፀ ። ወመጽአ ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ጸላኤ ፡ ሠናያት ፡ ወነሥአ ፡ እምእዴሁ ፡ ውእተ ፡ ምሳረ ፡ ወወገረ ፡ ውስተ ፡ እገሪሁ ፡ ወአሕ መሞ ፡ ጥቀ ፡ እስከ ፡ ተመትረ ፡ ሥርወ ፡ እግሩ ፡ ወውሕዘ ፡ ደም ፡ ብዙኅ ፡ ወስእነ ፡ ሰጊረ ። ወይቤ ፡ ለሊሁ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡ ርኢክዎ ፡ በአዕይንትየ ፡ ለውእቱ ፡ ፀርነ ፡ ሰይጣን ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይወግር ፡ ውእተ ፡ ምሳረ ፡ ውስተ ፡ እገ ርየ ፡ ወይትሜሰል ፡ ኅብሩ ፡ ከመ ፡ ጢስ ፡ እምእቶን ። ወሶቤሃ ፡ ዐተበ ፡
55 After that, Satan committed evil again, made come ... upon the adolescent and broke his knees. This thing was painful to our father, especially because they wanted to leave this cave, for they had nothing with which to feed themselves — neither roots nor flowers of grass. Our father said to (the adolescent): “My son, remain here until you are healed, so that we may go seek our food until we find (it).” (The adolescent) said yes to him. After that, they went to seek their food and abandoned him there. After three days, the adolescent reflected and said: “How am I going to remain here alone in a land of bandits and wild beasts? I will rise and go to my father” (Lk 15:18). Having said this, he went out crawling little by little and, by the will of God, arrived where they were. As for them, they found a tree that (was) full of fruits. After that, our holy father said to his (spiritual) children: “My sons, come, let us first look for a cave where we will spend the night and, once returned, we will gather the fruits of the tree for our dinner.” When they had gone to look for a cave, mär Sämu'el took an axe and began to cut the tree. The enemy of good came again, took the axe from his hands and (made it) strike his foot; it wounded him greatly, so that the tendon of his foot was cut, that abundant blood flowed, and that he could not walk. Our father Sämu’ël himself said: “I saw with my eyes our enemy Satan (making) the axe strike my foot, its color was similar to the smoke of an oven.” Then he signed his face to


[p. 50] {#po.53.p50}
ገጾ ፡ ይምኖ ፡ ወጽግሞ ፡ ወጠነ ፡ ንስቲተ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሖር ፡ ወስእነ ፡ ወነበረ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ መካን ፡ ወኢተክህሎ ፡ ይትመየጥ ፡ ለፌ ፡ ወለፌ ፡ እምብ ዙኅ ፡ ሕማም ።
his right and to his left, he began to walk a little and could not; he remained in this place and it was not possible for him to turn from side to side because of the great pain.
ወእምዝ ፡ ይቤሎ ፡ ለውእቱ ፡ ወልድ ፡ ሊተሰ ፡ ተስእነኒ ፡ አንቀልቅሎ ፡ ሖር ፡ ወልድየ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ፍሬ ፡ ሰግላ ፡ ሐሉ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢያማስንዎ ፡ አራዊት ። ወተንሥአ ፡ ወሖረ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወልድ ፡ በከመ ፡ አዘዞ ፡ መምህሩ ። ወሶበ ፡ በጽሐ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ኦም ፡ ወርእየ ፡ በህየ ፡ በአምሳለ ፡ አንበሳ ፡ ወነምር ፡ ተመሲሎ ፡ ሰይጣን ፡ ወተመንንገነ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ቦዘየዐርግ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ኦም ፡ ወቦ ፡ እለ ፡ ያንሶስዉ ፡ ታሕቴሃ ፡ ወይትራወጹ ፡ ለፌ ፡ ወለፌ ፡ እስመ ፡ ተመይኖ ፡ ቀሊል ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ለሰይጣን ፡ ወይትሜሰል ፡ በአርአያ ፡ ኩሉ ። ወሶበ ፡ ርእዮሙ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወልድ ፡ ለእልክቱ ፡ አንበሳ ፡ ወነምር ፡ ሶበ ፡ አፍርህዎ ፡ ከመዝ”! ፡ ተመይጠ ፡ ኀበ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ 'ወጉየ ፡ በፍርሃት ፡ ወበጽሐ ፡ ኀበ ፡ አቡሁ፤'' ፥ ወይቤሎ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ምንተ ፡ ኮንከ ፡ ኦወልድየ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ 'ኦአባ ፡ እስመ"'' ፡ ርኢኩ ፡ አናብስተ ፡ ወአናምርተ ፡ በዲበ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ዕፅ ፡ ወይትሜሰሉ ፡ ከመ ፡ ዘበልዑ ፡ እምፍሬሁ”"' ፡ ወፈሪህየ ፡ ጐየ ይኩ ፡ ወመጻእኩ ፡ ኀቤከ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ቅዱስ”" ፡ ኦወልድየ ፡ ኢኮነ ፡ ነምረ”" ፡ አላ ፡ ሰይጣን ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ዘይትቃረነነ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ጊዜ ። ወይአዜኒ”' ፡ ነዓ ፡ ወልድየ ፡ አሐዝ ፡ እዴየ ፡ ወአብጽሐኒ ፡ ኀቤሁ ። ወሖረ ፡ በበ ፡ ንስቲት ፡ ንስቲት ፡ እንዘ ፡ የሐነክስ ፡ እስመ ፡ ጥቀ ፡ ቄስለ ፡ እገሪሁ ፍ ወሶበ ፡ በጽሑ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ኦም ፡ እንተ ፡ ባቲ ፡ ብዙኅ ፡ ፍሬ ፡ አልቦ ፡ ዘረከቡ ፡ ኢነ ምረ ፡ ወኢአንበሳ ፡ ወኢካልአ ፡ አራዊተ”" ወይቤሎ ፡ አባ”' ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡ ለረድኡ” ፡ ርኢ ፡ ኦወልድየ ፡ ከመ ፡ አልቦ ፡ ዝየ”" ፡ ኢነምረ”" ፡ ወኢካ ልአ ፡ አራዊተ ፡ አላ ፡ ጸላኤ ፡ ሠናያት ፡ ዘአፍረሀከ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይጌሊ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢንሴሰይ ፡ እምዛቲ ፡ ፍሬ ፡ ሰግላ”' ፡ ወከመ ፡ ኢንንበር ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዛቲ ፡ ገዳም ፡ ወንሖር ፡ በቀቢጸ ፡ ተስፋ ፡ በዘ ፡ ፈቀደ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያመክረነ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያእምር ፡ እመ ፡ ፈራህያን፤" ፡ ንሕነ ። ወእመ ፡ ሐደግነ ፡ ሎቱ ፡
56 After that, he said to the adolescent: “It is not possible for me to move: go, my son, near these sycamore fruits, stand guard so that the wild beasts do not spoil them.” The adolescent rose and went as his master had ordered him. When he arrived near this tree, he saw there Satan having taken the appearance of lions and leopards; (Satan) used false appearances toward him: one climbed on the tree, others walked under it and ran here and there, for deception is easy for Satan and he takes the aspect of everything. When the adolescent saw these lions and leopards, as they had frightened him thus, he returned to his (spiritual) father; he fled in fear and arrived at his father. Our father said to him: “What is the matter with you, my son?” (The adolescent) said to him: “Abba, I saw lions and leopards on the tree, they had the appearance of eating its fruit; frightened, I fled and came to you.” The saint said to him: “My son, these were not leopards, but it is Satan who attacks us at every moment. Now, come, my son, take my hand and bring me to it.” [He] went in small (steps) limping, for his foot was gravely wounded. When they reached this tree that had many fruits, they found neither leopards nor lions nor other wild beasts. Abba Sämu’ël said to his disciple: “See, my son, that there are here neither leopards nor other wild beasts, but the enemy of good who frightened you thinking that we would not feed ourselves on these sycamore fruits, that we would not remain in this desert and would leave having lost hope, while he wanted to test us to know if we (were) fearful. If we had abandoned this desert to him and had gone into another, there too he would have preceded us and would have deceived us with another deception.” They remained there until they had gathered the fruits of the tree.
ዛተ ፡ ገዳመ ፡ ወሖርነ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ካልእ ፡ በህየኒ ፡ ይቀድመነ ፡ ወይትሜየነነ ፡ በካ ልእ ፡ ሚን ፡። ወነበሩ ፡ ህየ ፡ እስከ ፡ ያስተጋብኡ ፡ ፍሬሃ ፡ ለይእቲ ፡ ዕፅ ።= ወእምድኅረ ፡ አረሩ ፡ ወአስተጋብኡ ፡ መጽአ ፡ ዐቢይ ፡ ዝናም ፡ ዘመ ፍርህ ፡ ጥቀ ። እስመ ፡ ኢኮነ ፡ ጊዜሁ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ዝናም ፡ አላ ፡ ወርኃ ፡ መገቢት ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወፈነዎ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ለመከራ ፡ እሉ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ በከመ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ሲራክ ፡ ፈልፈለ ፡ ጥበብ ፡ ወልድየ ፡ ለእመ ፡ ሖርነ ፡ ትትቀነይ ፡ ለእ ግዚአብሔር ፡ አስተዳሉ ፡ ርእሰከ ፡ ለሕማም ፡ ወአርትዕ ፡ ልበከ ፡ ወተዐገስ”” ፡ ወኢትትወለወል ፡ ለእመ ፡ ተመንደብከ ፡ ኢትናፍቅ ፡ ዳእሙ ፡ ትልዎ ፡ ለእግ ዚአብሔር ፡ ወኢትኅድጎ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትትባላሕ ፡ በደኃሪትከ ፡ ወተወከፍ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ እንተ ፡ በጽሐተከ ። ወተዓገስ ፡ በመዋዕለ ፡ ተፅናስከ ፡ እስመ ፡ በአሳት ፡
57 After they had harvested and gathered, a great rain occurred that (was) very frightening. Indeed it was not the season for this rain, but it was the month of Maggabit and God had sent it to test these saints, according to what Sirach, the source of wisdom, says: My son, if you go to serve God, prepare yourself for pain; make your heart straight, be enduring; do not be troubled when you are tormented and do not doubt. Only follow God, do not abandon Him so as to be liberated at your last (day) and accept everything that will happen to you. Be enduring in the days of your scarcity, for it is in fire that one tests gold and the just man, it is by the suffering of indigence


[p. 52] {#po.53.p52}
ያሜክርዎ ፡ ለወርቅ ። ወለብእሲኒ ፡ ጻድቅ ፡ በሕማመ ፡ ተጽናስ ፡ ያሜክርዎ ። ዳእሙ ፡ ተአመኖ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ወይረድአከ ፡ አርትዕ ፡ ፍናዌከ ፡ ወተወ ከል ፡ ቦቱ ።= ወዝናምሰ ፡ ዕዙዝ ፡ ጥቀ ፡ ወይወርድ ፡ ላዕሌሆሙ ፡ መዓልተ ፡ ወሌሊተ ፡ እምሰዓተ ፡ ቀዳም ፡ እስከ ፡ ሰዓተ ፡ ሰንበት ፡ ለጸቢሐ ፡ ሰኑይ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ፋጻ ፡ ወነፋስ ፡ ወአውሎ ። ወእምብዝኃ ፡ 'ሞር ፡ ወነፋስ" ፡ ኮኑ ፡ ክምቱታነ"! = ወክዳን ፡ ዘላዕሌሆሙ ፡ 'ከደኑ ፡ ቦቱ” ፡ ፍሬ ፡ ሰግላ ሆሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢያመስኖ ፡ ዝናም ፡ ወአልቦሙ ፡ ሲሳየ ፡ ዘእንበሌሃ ፡ በከመ ፡
let him be tested. Only have faith in God and He will help you; make your paths straight and trust in Him (Sir 2:1-6). As for the rain, it was very violent and fell upon them day and night, from the time of Saturday until the time of Sunday at the dawn of Monday, with whistling, wind, and storm. Because of the severity of the cold and the wind, they were constricted; the garment that was upon them, they used to cover the fruits of their sycamore so that the rain would not spoil them: they had no other food than them, just as we said previously.

58 We celebrate your greatness, glorious Abba, bound with the cord of the Gospel, in whom dwells the spirit of grace and power, who destroyed the cunning of the despicable devil when he frightened his disciple, having made himself like the leopard and the roaring lion, having heard your most prodigious story which is sweeter than honey and sugar. May your prayer be for us as an accepted shield, for ever and ever, amen. 59 One day, while our father Samu’el was walking with his (spiritual) son Za-Rufa’el, soldiers seized them, bound them, and led them into their cave. They remained there tearing to pieces everything they found and kicking like pigs and donkeys; the soldiers insulted them and mocked them. When it was morning, they took them with his disciples and led them away bound. While they were traveling, a great rain occurred, the sight of which was frightening; they were greatly afflicted, for it was a desert land. After that, our father prayed that the rain might withdraw; immediately it divided on both sides and moved away from them. When the soldiers saw this, they thanked him humbly, but they were hard of heart like the Jews in the days of old and, despite that, they did not let them go, took them to their city, and led them before their chiefs. When their chiefs saw them, they said to them: “Why have you brought these poor men: are they fighting (spiritually) for the love of their God?” Those who had brought them answered and said to them: “For [15 they are a help to our enemies.” Their chiefs said to them: “These are virtuous men, let them go where they want: they are without sin.” After that, they were unbound and they returned to their cave. May their prayer and their blessing be with us, amen.

60 These saints remained in this desert for three years without making a dwelling for themselves nor gathering food in storehouses, but seeking day by day what to eat—whether leaves, or roots, or flowers of grass, or dried sycamore fruits. With these fruits they lasted until Saturday and until Sunday. Even then, they did not find


[p. 54] {#po.53.p54}
እስከ ፡ ይጸግቡ ፡ አላ ፡ ቦአመ ፡ በበ ፡ ፳ፍሬ ፡ ዘይበልዑ ፡ ወቦአመ ፡ በበ፻ ይበልዑ ። ወሶበ ፡ የዐጽቦሙ ፡ ቦአመ ፡ በበ ፡ ይበልዑ ፡ እምውእቱ ፡ ፍሬ ፡ ሰግላ ።= ወከመዝ ፡ ነበሩ ፡ ፻ ዓመተ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይትራዓዩ ፡ በዐቂበ ፡ ሕጉ ፡ ለልዑል ። በከመ ፡ ይትራዓይ ፡ መርኤት ፡ እምሕዛእት ፡ ውስተ ፡ጺኦት"” ከመ ፡ ዘሀለዉ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገነተ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ በፍሥሐ ፡ ወበ ሐሜት”" ፍ
enough to be satisfied, but sometimes it was five by five that they ate the fruits; sometimes they ate them three by three. When it was necessary for them, sometimes they ate one by one of these sycamore fruits. They remained thus for three years grazing while keeping the law of the Most High—just as the flock grazes, far from enclosed pastures, in the meadow—as if they were in the Garden of God, in joy and gladness.
ኦአባ ፡ ዜና ፡ ጊሩትከ ፡ መንክር ፡ ዘኢይፌጽም ፡ ልሳነ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ፍጡር ። ሳሙኤል ፡ አቡነ ፡ አስካለ ፡ ወይን ፡ ሥሙር ፡ ዘይፈለፍል ፡ መዓርዒር ፡ ዘሰቀየቶ ፡ ዘአርሞን ፡ ጠል ። እብነ ፡ ሰንፔር ፡ ክቡር”' ፡ በጐድበ ፡ ጸላኢ ፡ ዘኢይትወቀር ። ማኅቶተ ፡ ሃይማኖት ፡ ጽዱል ፡ ዲበ ፡ ተቅዋመ ፡ መርዓት ፡ ንቡር ፍ ጸዓዳ ፡ ሐሊብ ፡ እሙሐዘ ፡ ወንጌል ፡ ዘይፈለፍል ፡ በነፍኒፎቱ ፡ ዘአ ብቄለ ፡ አእዋመ ፡ ሐቅል ፡ ወበውስቴቱ ፡ 'አፍረየ ፡ ስርናየ” ፡ እዙል ። ዘበሱ ራጌ ፡ ሥዑል ፡ ቀጸላተ ፡ ብርሃን ፡ ዘያንበለብል ፡ ይጹልላሁ ፡ ሠላስ ፡ አክ ሊል ። ወለለ እምኔነ” ፡ ሰፊሐከ ፡ እዴከ ፡ ለባርኮትነ ፡ ሥመር ። አስተ ምሕር ፡ በእንቲአነ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ትስልሥቱ ፡ ዘኢይትከፈል ፡ ዕዘዘ ፡ መንግሥቱ ፡ ልዑለ ፡ መንበር ። ይክፍለነ”" ፡ ነሀሉ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዓጸዱ ፡ በብርሃን ፡ ክሉል ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ፡=
61 Abba, prodigious is the story of your virtue that the tongue of created man cannot bring to its end! Samu’el our father, pleasant grape that flows sweetly, which the dew of Hermon has watered (Ps 133:3); precious sapphire stone that is not dented by the axe of the Enemy; sparkling lamp of faith placed on the lampstand of the Bride; white milk from the channel of the Gospel that flows in fine rain, which makes the trees of the fields grow in which it makes the robust grain bear fruit; he whom three crowns overshadow with the painted splendor of the diadems of light that blaze. Stretching out your hand toward each one of us, deign to bless us. Intercede for us before the Trinity that is not divided, to the powerful royalty, to the most high throne, so that it may grant us to be in its palace crowned with light, for ever and ever, amen.
ወበ ፳እምነ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ ከረየ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ግበ ፡ ዘዕሙቅ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ መጠነ ፡ ቿእመት ፡ አው ፡ ዘኢበዝኅ ።= ወይቤሎ ፡ ረድኡ ፡ ኦአባ ፡ ለምንት ፡ ትገብር ፡ ከመዝ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ አቡነ ፡ በትሕትና ፡ ከመ ፡ እትመሀለል ፡ ንስ ቲተ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ኀጢአትየ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወልድ ፡ ሕያው ፡ እግዚአብ ሔር ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢየኅድገከ ፡ እመኒ ፡ ሞትነ ፡ ኅቡረ ፡ ወእመኒ ፡ ሐየውነ ፡ ኅቡረ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዛቲ ፡ ግብ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ኦወልድየ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ዘጻመውከ ፡ ወዘጸለይከ ፡ ለከ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወአልቦ ፡ ዘይነሥአከ ። ወዘሰ ፡ እገ ብር ፡ አነ ፡ ህየንቴየ ፡ ወህየንቴከ ። ወዘንተ ፡ ብሂሎ ፡ ቦአ ፡ ውስቴቱ ፡ አመ ፡ ፲ወ፪ ለግንቦት ። ወነበረ ፡ ህየ ፡ እስከ ፡ ደብረ ፡ ታቦር ፡ አመ ፡ ፲ወ፻ ለነሐሴ ፡ ዘአልቦ ፡ ሲሳየ ፡ ወኢማየ ፡ ለጽምኡ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ኢይነብር ፡ ወኢ ይነውም ፡ አላ ፡ ይሴብሕ ፡ ቀዊሞ ፡ ማዓልተ ፡ ወሌሊተ ፡ ከመ ፡ ገብር ፡ ዘይቀውም ፡ ቅድመ ፡ ሊቁ ። እምአመ ፡ ቦአ ፡ ውስቴታ ፡ ኢያአምር ፡ መዓ ልተ ፡ ወሌሊተ ፡ ይስእል ፡ ወያስተምሕር ፡ ለኩሉ ፡ ፍጥረት ፡ ለሰብእ ፡ ወለእ ንስሳ ፡ ለነገሥት ፡ ወለመኳንንት ፡ ከመ ፡ ይዕቀቦሙ ፡ በድኅና ፡ ወበሰላም ። ለጳጳሳት ፡ ወለቀሳውስት ፡ ወለዲያቆናት ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢይማስን ፡ ክህነቶሙ ። ለመነ ኮሳት ፡ ወለደናግል ፡ ወለመዐስብ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይጸግዎሙ ፡ ንጽሐ ፡ ወየሀልዉ”" ፡ በትዕግሥት ። በእንተ ፡ ፍሬያት ፡ ወጽጌያት ፡ ወአዕጻዳተ”" ፡ ወይን ፡ አርኅዎ ፡ 'ክረምት ፡ ዘበበጊዜሁ”'' = ዘንተ ፡ ወዘይመስሎ ፡ ይጹሊ ፡ ለኩሉ ፡ ፍጥረት ።
62 One day, our father dug a pit that was very deep, eight cubits or more. His disciple said to him: “Why are you doing this?” Our father said to him with humility: “To supplicate a little concerning my sin.” The adolescent said to him: “By the life of God, I will not abandon you, whether we die together or we live together in this pit.” Our holy father said to him: “My son, all that you have suffered and that you have prayed, it is for you and no one will take it from you; as for what I am going to do, I, it is for me and for you.” Having said that, he entered (the pit) on the twelfth of Genbot; he remained there until (the feast of) Dabra Tabor on the thirteenth of Nahase without food or water for his thirst, without sitting or sleeping, but glorifying while standing day and night like a servant who is standing before his master. Since he had entered (the pit), he did not recognize day and night; he was soliciting and interceding for all creatures, for men and for animals, for kings and for governors, so that (God) might keep them in good health and in peace; for the bishops, for the priests, and for the deacons, so that their priesthood might not be corrupted; for the monks, for the virgins, and for the widows, so that He might grant them purity and that they might be in abstinence. As for the fruits, the flowers, and the vines, the year opened them for him, each at its moment. He made this prayer and others similar for all creatures.


[p. 56] {#po.53.p56}
ምንትኑ ፡ እሰምየከ ፡ ኦብእሴ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ መልአከኑ ፡ እስመ ፡ መልአክ ፡ ምድራዊ ፡ አንተ ። ወሚመ ፡ ብእሴኑ ፡ እስመ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ሰማያዊ ፡ አንተ ። በሚመጠን ፡ ጻማ ፡ ቀተልኮ ፡ ለሥጋከ ። በትጋሀ ፡ ጸሎት ፡ 'እንበለ ፡ ጽርዐት ፡ ቀተልኮ ፡ ለሥጋከ ። በሀፍ ፡ ወበድካም ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ዕረፍት”” ፡ ቀተልኮ ፡ ለሥጋከ ። 'በኀጢአ ፡ ንዋም ፡ ከመ ፡ መላእክት?" ፡ ቀተልኮ ፡ ለሥ ጋከ = በጥብጣቤ ፡ ኀብል ፡ ከመ ፡ ሰማዕት ፡ ቀተልኮ ፡ ለሥጋከ ። ኦብፁዓዊ ፡ ናሁ ፡ አርዌ ፡ ሕጐል ፡ ዘተመንገኖ ፡ ለአቡከ ፡ በሥሥዕት”" ፡ አግባእከ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ በቀሎ ፡ ወልድ ፡ ወሬዛ ፡ ወፈደይከ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይግባእ ፡ በሑፋሬ ፡ ዕድወ ፡ አቡከ ። ወበእንቲአከ ፡ ወበእንተ ፡ እለ ፡ ከማከ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ኢሳይያስ ፡ ኢትፍራህ ፡ ቀቆልኤየ ፡ ያዕቆብ ፡ ወእስራኤል ፡ ፍቁርየ" ፡ ዘሐረይኩ ፡ እስመ ፡ አነ ፡ እሁብ ፡ ማየ ፡ በውስተ ፡ ጽምእ ፡ ለእለ ፡ የሐውሩ ፡ በድወ ።፡ እሰይም ፡
63 How shall I call you, man of God? “Angel,” for you are an earthly angel; or else “man,” for you are a heavenly man. By how many trials have you killed your body? By the ardor of an uninterrupted prayer, you have killed your body; by sweat and by fatigues without rest, you have killed your body; by the lack of sleep like the angels, you have killed your body; with a whip of rope like the martyrs, you have killed your body. Blessed one, behold the serpent of perdition who deceived your father by greed, you have returned his vengeance to him, young man, and you have paid so that the debt of your father might return to him in ignominy. It is concerning you and concerning those who are like you that Isaiah says: Do not fear, my servant Jacob and Israel my beloved whom I have chosen, for I will put water in (the land of) thirst for those who walk in the desert; I will establish my spirit upon your offspring and my blessing upon your children (Is 44:2-3).
መንፈስየ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ዘርእከ ፡ ወበረከትየ ፡ ላዕለ”" ፡ ውሉድከ ።« ወእምድኅረ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ መጽኡ ፡ ሰብአ ፡ ገሊላ ፡ መነኮሳት ፡ አለ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ደሴት ፡ ከመ ፡ ይንሥእዎ-” ፡ ለተመጥዎ ፡ ሞርባን ፡ እስመ ፡ በዓለ ፡ ደብረ ፡ ታቦር ፡ ውእቱ ። ወይቤ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ረድእ ፡ ዘዜነወነ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ወሶ ቤሃ ፡ ሖርኩ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ ወአውፃእክዎ ፡ እምውእቱ ፡ ግብ ፡ ወረከብክዎ ፡ ከመ ፡ ዕፅ ፡ ዘየብሰ ። ወኀልቀ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ወአማዑቲሁ ፡ ጠበቀ ፡ ታሕተ ፡ ወተደመረ ፡ ምስለ”" ፡ ገበዋቲሁ ። ወገጹኒ ፡ ያበርህ ፡ ከመ ፡ ፀሐይ ፡ ወፍሠሥሕ ፡ ወሕ ጮሠይ ፡ ከመ ፡ ዘወፅአ ፡ እምቤተ ፡ በዓል ፍ ወሐቀፍክዎ"" ፡ ወሰዓምክዎ ፡ ወበ ከይኩ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ብካየ ፡ ዕፁበ ፡ ወአምጻእኩ ፡ ማየ ፡ ወሐፀብኩ ፡ እደዊሁ ፡ ወእገሪሁ ፡ ወርእሶ ፡ ወገጾ ፡ እምብዝኃ ፡ ሐመድ ፡ ዘላዕሌሁ ። ወአጽናዕኩ ፡ ሐቃሁ ፡ በቀጢን ፡ ልብስ ፡ እምቅድመ ፡ ንብጻሕ ፡ ኀበ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ አኀው ፥ ወእምዝ ፡ ነሥኡነ ፡ ወጾሩነ ፡ በአኅመር”'' ፡ ወወሰዱነ ፡ ውስተ ፡ መካኖሙ ።
64 After that came people from Galila, the monks who were on the island, to take him so that he might receive communion, for it was the feast of Dabra Tabor. The disciple who told us this says: “Immediately I went to him, made him come out of the pit, and found him like a tree that is withered: his body had been consumed, his entrails had stuck downward and adhered to his sides; his face shone like the sun, joyful and gay as if (Samu’el) had come out of a house in celebration. I took him in my arms, gave him a kiss, and shed vehement tears upon him. I brought water and washed his hands, his feet, his head, and his face from the abundant dust that was upon him; I girded his loins with a fine garment before we arrived at these brothers. After that, they took us, transported us by boat, and brought us to their monastery
ወሶበ ፡ ገባእነ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ማኅደርነ ፡ ተስእልክዎ ፡ ወእቤሎ ፡ በዘመ ጠነዝ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ ተቀነይከ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ፍቅረ ፡ አምላክከ ፡ አልቦኑ ፡ ዘአስተር አየከ ። ወአውሥአኒ ፡ ወይቤለኒ ፡ መጽአ ፡ ኀቤየ ፡ ለሊሁ ፡ እግዚእየ ፡ ኢየ ሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወይቤለኒ ፡ ሰላም ፡ ለከ ፡ ኦፍቁርየ ።= ወሶበ ፡ ርኢክዎ ፡ ወፈ ራህኩ ፡ እምሱራጌ ፡ ገጹ ፡ ወይበርቅ”” ፡ ከመ ፡ ፀሐይ ፡ ወወደቁ”” ፡ ኀበ ፡ እገሪሁ ፡ 'ወአሐዘኒ ፡ በእደዊሁ”" ፡ ወአንሥአኒ ፡ ወእምዝ ፡ ነሥአ ፡ ምራቆ ፡ ቅዱሰ ፡ ወኃተመኒ ፡ ቦቱ”! ፡ ኩለንታየ ፡ ወመጠወኒ ፡ መንኮብያሁ ፡ እንተ ፡ የማን ፡ ወአጥበወኒ ፡ ሥልሰ ። ወእምአሜሃ ፡ ኢያእመርኩ ፡ ረኀበ ፡ ወጽምአ ፡ ወኢድካመ ። ወተሐደስኩ ፡ ኩለንታየ ፡ ወወሀበኒ ፡ ዓዲ ፡ ዓሥራተ ፡ ዛተ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ወኩሎ ፡ አድያሚሃ ፡ ወተካየድኩ ፡ ምስለ ፡ አምላኪየ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ኃጥኣን ። ወይቤለኒ ፡ ዘጸውዓ ፡ ስመከ ፡ ወዘገብረ ፡ ተዝካረከ ፡ ወዘተአመነ ፡ በጸሎትከ ፡ ይኩን ፡ ማኅደሩ ፡ ምስሌከ ።
65 “When we returned to our dwelling, I questioned him and said to him: ‘For so many days, you gave yourself to adoration for the love of your God; did He not appear to you?’ He answered me and said to me: ‘He Himself my Lord Jesus Christ came to me and said to me: Hail to you, my beloved! When I saw Him, I was seized with fear before the radiance of His face—it shone like the sun—and I fell at His feet; He took me with His hands and raised me up, then He took His holy saliva, signed me with it all over, presented His right armpit to me, and suckled me three times. From that moment, I did not know hunger and thirst nor fatigue; I was renewed all over, He also gave me as a tithe this region and all its villages, and I concluded a pact with my God concerning the sinners. He said to me: Whoever will invoke your name, whoever will make your commemoration, whoever will have faith in your prayer, may his dwelling be with you.’”


[p. 58] {#po.53.p58}
አንክሮ ፡ አንክሩ ፡ ኦመሲሓዊያን ፡ ሕዝብ ፡ እለ ፡ ጉቡኣን ፡ ዮም ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዛቲ ፡ ተድባብ”" ለበዓለ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ክቡራዊ ፡ መክብብ ። ዘኀፀነቶ”' ፡ ጥበብ ፡ በመንፈሳዊት ፡ ሐሊብ ። ለጸዊረ ፡ ሕማማተ ፡ መስቀል ፡ ጥቡዐ ፡ ልብ ። ነጽሩ ፡ ዘከመ ፡ ኩነነ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ በጻማ ፡ ዕፁብ ። ቀዊሞ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዕሙቅ ፡ ግብ ።ፍ እንበለ ፡ ነቢር ፡ ወሰኪብ ። 8አውራኅኀ”” ፡ እስከ ፡ ሐወጾ ፡ ሰማያዊ ፡ ዘመንበሩ ፡ ኪሩብ ። ወተሐትመ ፡ በቅብአ ፡ ምራቁ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ለዓቃቤ ፡ ሥራይ ፡ ጠቢብ ። ዓሥራተ ፡ ምሕረት ፡ ይንሥአነ ፡ በሐዳስ ፡ ከብካብ ። አመ ፡ ይትካሀን ፡ ሎቱ ፡ በኩሩ ፡ ለአብ ፍ ወለእለ ፡ ከማሁ ፡ ጽዉዓን ፡ አርጋብ ። ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ።
66 Be filled with wonder, Christians who are gathered today in this pavilion for the feast of this glorious preacher whom wisdom has nourished with a spiritual milk to bear the sufferings of the cross with a firm heart. See how he has chastised his body in hard trials, standing in a deep pit, without sitting or lying down—four months until the Celestial One, whose throne is the cherubim, visited him. He was signed with the anointing of His holy saliva as a wise physician. May He take us as a tithe of mercy in the new nuptials when the firstborn Son of the Father acts as priest for him and for the chosen doves who are like him, for ever and ever, amen.

### Samu’el stays eight years at Wedaka
ወእምድኅረ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ወፅኡ ፡ እምይእቲ ፡ ገዳም ፡ ወበጽሑ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሀገር”" ፡ እንተ ፡ ትሰመይ ፡ ውዳካ ። ወወጠነ ፡ በውስቴታ ፡ ዐቢየ ፡ ገድለ ፡ ወረከበ ፡ በህየ ፡ ንስቲተ ፡ ማየ ፡ በዲበ ፡ ኩኩሕ ፡ ወኢኮነት ፡ ነቅዐ ፡ አላ ፡ ከመ ፡ ምዕቃለ ፡ ማይ ፡ እንተ ፡ ተጋብአት ፡ እምጠለ ፡ ሰማይ ። ወበላዕሌሃ ፡ ይወርድ ፡ ዐቢይ ፡ ፀሐይ ፡ ወሶበ ፡ አውዓያ ፡ ሐሩረ ፡ ፀሐይ ፡ ኢትየብስ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ማይ ፡ አላ ፡ ትጸይዕ ፡ ወኩሉ ፡ ዘርእያ ፡ ያስቆርር ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢትይበስኒ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ኩኩሕ ፡ ሀለወት ፡ ወከመ ፡ ኢትትሐደስኒ ፡ ኢየዐርግ ፡ ላዕሌሃ ፡ ነቅዐ ፡ ማይ ፡ ወበእንተዝ ፡ ኮነት ፡ ጽይእተ ። ወዘንተ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ረከበ ፡ ተፈሥሐ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ከመ ፡ ዘረከበ ፡ ምህርካ ፡ ብዙኃ ። ወነበረ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይሰቲ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ወይትባአሳ ፡ ለሥጋሁ ፍ ወበዘከመዝ ፡
67 After that, they left this desert and arrived in a region that was called Wedaka. (Samu’el) undertook there a great (ascetic) combat and found there a little water on a rock; it was not a spring, but like a reservoir of water that formed from the dew of heaven. A great sun fell upon it and when the ardor of the sun burned it, this water did not dry up, but became corrupted, and whoever saw it detested it, both because it did not dry up (while) it was on the rock and because it did not renew itself: a spring of water did not pour onto it, and because of that, it was corrupted. When he found that, this father of ours rejoiced as if he had found abundant spoil (Ps 119:162). He remained drinking it all the time and he did violence to his body. He remained eight years leading such a (ascetic) combat.
ገድል ፡ "እንዘ ፡ ይትጋደል” ፡ ነበረ ፡ ፳ ዓመተ ። ኦፍቁራንየ”' ፡- ንሕነሰ ፡ ለእመ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ስቴነ ፡ አሐተ ፡ ዕለተ ፡ 'ሕሠመ ፡ አው ፡ ረከብነ ፡ እምቱሳሔ” ፡ ካልእ ፡ ንብል ፡ እስመ ፡ ያሐም መነ ፡ ከብድነ ። ወዝንቱሰ ፡ ብእሴ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ በዝንቱ ፡ ማይ ፡ ኩነና ፡ ለሥጋሁ ፡ ጺአተ ፡ ማይ ፡ ኢያሕስሞ ፡ በፍቅረ ፡ አምላኩ ፡ ነዲዶ = ወካዕበ ፡ ይቀውም ፡ ውስተ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ማይ ፡ ኩላ ፡ ሌሊተ”” ፡ እብነ ፡ ጸዊሮ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ርእሱ ።= በዝንቱ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ሥራሕ ፡ ርእሶ ፡ አጻመወ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይርከብ ፡ መን ግሥቶ ፡ ድልወ ፡ ወገነቶ ፡ ርኅወ ።= ይምጻእ ፡ ይእዜ ፡ በሞገሰ ፡ ቃሉ ፡ ይቀ
68 My beloved, we, if our drink is foul for a single day or if we find a mixture of something else (in it), we say that our stomach hurts us; but this man of God chastised his body with this water; (he) burning with the love of his God, the foulness of the water did not harm him. He still stood upright in this water all night, carrying a stone on his head. With all these trials, he tired himself to obtain his prepared kingdom and his open garden. May he come now with the grace of his word to sanctify the brothers, amen.
ድስ ፡ አኅወ ፡ አሜን" ወበ ፳-እመዋዕል ፡ ሖረ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ፳ ግሑስ ፡ ዘስሙ ፡ ይባ ርከነ ፡ ክርስቶስ ። ወሶበ ፡ ርእዮ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ይቤሎ ፡ እፎ ፡ ትመጽእ ፡ ኅቤየ ፡ አንተ"'' ፡ ዓምደ ፡ ሰማይ ፡ ወምድር ፡ ድልወትየሰ ፡ ሊተ ፡ እመጻእኩ ፡ ኅቤከ ፡ ወእንሣእ ፡ በረከተከ ። ወአመ ፡ ትመጽእ ፡ እምድረ ፡ ፍጌን”" ፡ እንዘ ፡ ሀሎከ ፡ በማዕዶተ ፡ ወርዕ ፡ ከሠተ ፡ ሊተ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ግብረከ"” = እንዘ ፡ ሀሎኩ ፡ ቀዊምየ ፡ እጹሊ”" ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ፻ሰዓት ፡ ርኢኩከ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ታበርህ ፡
69 One day, our holy father went to a hermit by the name of Yebarekana Krestos. When (the latter) saw our father, he said to him: “How do you come to me, you pillar of heaven and earth? It would have been fitting that I should come to you and receive your blessing. When you were coming from the land of Fegen, while you were on the other side of the Ware‘, God revealed to me your whole way of acting. While I was standing praying at the third hour, I saw you


[p. 60] {#po.53.p60}
ውስተ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ከመ ፡ ሥርቀተ ፡ ፀሐይ ። ወሞጣሕት ፡ ዘዲቤየ ፡ አን በሩ ፡ ላዕሌከ ፡ ወኩሎ ፡ ክበደ ፡ ጻማ ፡ ዘላዕሌየ”' ፡ አወፈዩከ ፡ ኩሎ ። ወፍካ ሬሁሰ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ እስመ ፡ ቀርበ ፡ ፍልሰቱ ፡ እምዝንቱ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ለውእቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ። ወሞጣሕት ፡ ዘይቤ ፡ አሶተ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ እንተ ፡ ነበረት ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ጸገውዎ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡ ወኩሎ ፡ ክበደ ፡ ጻማየ ፡ አወፈዩከ ፡ ዘይቤሂ ፡ ወአእመሮ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይከውን ፡ ትጉሃ ፡ ለነፍሳት ፡ ከመ ፡ ጌር ፡ ኖላዊ ፡ ዘየዐቅብ ፡ መርዔቶ ፡ ወበጸሎቱ ፡ ያድኅን ፡ ነፍሰ ፡ ብዙኃን ፡ እም እደ ፡ ጸላኢ ፡ ዘይትቃረኖሙ ፍ ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ፥ ስምዑ ፡ ካዕበ ፡ አበዊየ ፡ ወአኀዊየ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ መንክረ ፡ ዘዕፁብ ፡ ለተና ግሮቱ ፡ ወመድምም ፡ ለሰሚዖቱ ፡ ዘገብረ ፡ አባስ?" ፡ በሕይወቱ ፡ ሥጋ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ለቢሶ ፡ ግብረ ፡ መላእክት ፡ ዘፈጸመ ፡ ቦቱ ። እንዘ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ገዳም ፡ ነበረ ፡ ፪ ዓመተ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ኢይነውም ፡ ፍጹመ ፡ አላ ፡ ይተ ግህ ፡ ኩላ ፡ ሌሊተ ። ወሶበ ፡ መጽኦ ፡ ንዋም ፡ እንዘ ፡ ቅውም ፡ ይፌጽም ፡ ወኢይነብር ። ወካዕበ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ከብዶ ፡ ንዋም ፡ ይገብር ፡ ግብረ ፡ እድ ፡ ወይሰ ርሕ ፡ መርበብተ ፡ ወያሴኒ ፡ ለኩሉ ፡ 'ለባዕልኒ ፡ ወለነዳይኒ"”'' ። ወአልቦቱ ፡ ዕረፍተ ፡ መዓልተ ፡ ወሌሊተ ፡ ወበዘከመዝ ፡ ግብር ፡ አድከማ ፡ ለሥጋሁ ፡ በሥራሕ ፡ ወበትጋህ ።
shining in the whole universe like the rising of the sun. The mantle that was upon me, they placed upon you, and all the burden of the trials that is upon me, they handed over to you entirely.” The explanation of this is that the departure of the saint out of this world was near. The mantle of which he spoke, medicine of the Holy Spirit, which was upon him, our father Samu’el was granted it. What he had also said—“they handed over to you all the burden of my trials”—indicated to (Samu’el) that he would be vigilant for souls like the good shepherd who guards his flock and that, by his prayer, he would save the soul of many from the hand of the Enemy who attacks them. May his prayer and his blessing be with us, amen. 70 Listen again, my fathers and my brothers, to this prodigy that is admirable to tell and astonishing to hear, which the abbot did during his lifetime, clothed in a body of man with which he accomplished a work of the angels: while he was in this desert, he remained seven years without sleeping at all, on the contrary he kept watch all night. When sleep had come to him, he finished (watching) while standing and did not sit down. And again, when sleep weighed upon him, he did manual work, made a fishing net, and did good to all—both to the rich and to the poor. He had no rest day and night; with such a way of acting, he weakened his body by labor and by watching.
ናስተበጽዓከ ፡ አባ ፡ 'ክቡራዊ ፡ ትጉህ"”” ፡ ከመ ፡ መላእክት ፡ ምዑዝ ፡ ከመ ፡ ዕፍረት ፡ ዘመነኖ ፡ ለዓለም ፡ ከመ ፡ ዕድዋት ፡ ተዘኪሮ ፡ ዘበሰማያት ። ሳሙኤል ፡ አቡነ ፡ ፈለገ ፡ መለኮት ፡ ዘይፈለፍል ፡ ለመድኀኒት ። እምንባበ ፡ ቃሉ ፡ ዘያረዊ ፡ ነፍሳተ ። ለኩሉ ፡ በከመ ፡ መፍቅዱ ፡ ይናዝዝ ፡ እንበለ ፡ ትዕ ቢት ፡ ወትዝህርት ። ትባርከነ ፡ ሰፊሐከ ፡ የማነ ፡ እዴከ ፡ ቅድስት”” ፥ 'ወኮነ ፡ በ5 እምነ”" ፡ መዋዕል ፡ ሖረ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡ ምስለ ፡ ረድኡ ፡ ዘሩፋኤል ፡ ውስተ ፡ ፈለግ”! ፡ ከመ ፡ ይኅሥሥሠ ፡ መፍቅዶሙ ፍሩ ወሶበ ፡ በጽሑ ፡ ረከቡ ፡ አንበሳ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይሰክብ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ጽንፈ ፡ ማይ ። ወቀደመ ፡ ረድእ ፡ ርእዮቶ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ኦአባ ፡ ትሬእዮኑ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ አን በሳ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ኦወልድየ ፡ ዝንቱሰ ፡ ሕሙም ፡ ውእቱ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ረድእ ፡ ኢኮነ ፡ ሕሙመ ፡ ሰትዮ ፡ ማየ ፡ ናሁ ፡ ይሰክብ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ አልቦ ፡ ሕሙም ፡ ውእቱ ። ወዘንተ ፡ ብሂሎ ፡ ቀርበ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ ወአኀዘ ፡ በእዴሁ ፡ ዘየማን ፡ ዘፍጽም ፡ እገሪሁ ፡ ለውእቱ ፡ አንበሳ ፡ ግሩም ። ወሶበ ፡ ነጸሮ”" ፡ ረከበ ፡ ውስተ ፡ እራ ፡ ሞቅስለ ፡ ዘምሉእ ፡ መግለ ፡ ወነሥአ ፡ መስፌ ፡ ወነቀሶ ፡ ወአርኀወ ፡ መካነ ፡ መግል ፡ ወውሕዘ”" ፡ ሶቤሃ ፡ ጸዓዳ ፡ መግል ፡ ምስለ ፡ ደም ፡ ቱሱሕ ። ወእምዝ ፡ አውፅአ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ሦከ ፡ አርዌ ፡ ዘስሙ ፡ ቅንፍዝ ። ወውእቱሰ ፡ አንበሳ ፡ ይትገነጳል ፡ ለፌ ፡ ወለፌ ፡ ከመ ፡ ዘንኡስ ፡ ከልብ ፡ ዘኢለመደ ፡ ተባእሶ ፡ ወከመ ፡ ማሕስአ ፡ በግዕ ፡ ዘአልቦ ፡ እከየ ። ወእምድኅረ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ መጽኡ ፡ ፄተኳሉት ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይሰድድዎ ፡ ለዓገዜን ፡
71 We celebrate you, glorious Abba, vigilant like the angels, embalming like perfume, who despised the world like refuse (Phil 3:8), remembering what is in the heavens. Samu’el our father, river of divinity that gushes for salvation, who invigorates souls by the expression of his word—he consoles each according to his need without pride or insolence. You bless us, having stretched out your holy right hand. 72 It happened one day that our father Samu’el went with his disciple Za-Rufa’el to the river to seek what they needed. When they arrived, they found a lion lying at the edge of the water: the disciple saw it first and said (to Samu’el): “Abba, do you see this lion?” Our father said to him: “My son, this one is suffering.” The disciple said to him: “He is not suffering: having drunk water, behold he is lying down.” (Samu’el) said to him: “No, he is suffering.” Having said that, he approached the (lion) and took in his hand the right front paw of the terrifying lion; when he looked at it, he found in its sole a wound that was full of pus. He took a needle, planted it, and opened the place of the pus; white pus mixed with blood flowed out immediately. After that, he removed from him a thorn of the animal by the name of porcupine. The lion gamboled from side to side like a little dog that is not accustomed to fighting and like a young lamb without malice. After that came six wolves chasing an antelope; they made it arrive where (the

monks) were. Our father said to the disciple: “Lower your head, my son, so that these wolves do not see us until we see the work of God that is going to happen.” There was there, beside them, deep water; the antelope entered into this depth and, not being able to (follow), the wolves went away. After that, our father made a sign to the lion: (the latter) went, took the antelope out of the water, and remained feeding on it until he was healed.
እስከ ፡ የሐዩ” ናስተብፅዖ ፡ ለብፁዓዊ ፡ ለዘ ፡ ንብረቱ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገዳም ፡ ሞስለ ፡ አራ ዊት ፡ ወእንስሳ” = ለፍትወተ ፡ ሥጋ ፡ በጻማ ፡ ዘአይበሳ ።= ዘኢለከፎ ፡ ለዓ ለም ፡ ደነሳ ። በአንሶስዎቱ”' ፡ ውስቴታ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ትምህርታት” ፡ ብዙኅ ፡ ለኀቅለ ፡ ዋሊ ፡ ዘቀደሳ ። ዓቃቤ ፡ ሥራይ ፡ ዘኮኖ ፡ ለአንበሳ ። ምስለ ፡ ተሣ ህሎ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ገሰሳ ፡ ለቀስለ ፡ እዱ ፡ ዘፈወሳ ። ትዌድሶ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲ ያን ፡ እንዘ ፡ ትብል ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡ ሐዊሳ”” = እምንእሱ ፡ ለንጽሕ ፡ ዘለብሳ ያንጽሐኒ ፡ በጸሎቱ”" ፡ እምነውረ ፡ አበሳ ፡= ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን =
73 Let us celebrate the blessed one, he whose life was in the desert with wild and domestic beasts, who withered the desire of the body by trial, whom the turpitude of the century did not touch, who sanctified the desert of Wali by traversing it with numerous teachings, who was a physician for a lion, who healed the wound of the paw of the latter when he touched it with clemency. The Church praises him by saying: “Samu’el who has been clothed in purity since his childhood!” May he purify us by his prayer from the stain of fault, for ever and ever, amen.
ወበ እመዋዕል ፡ ለአኮ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ለረድኡ ፡ ኀበ ፡ 8እጐ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሐውጾ = ወእንዘ ፡ ይትመየጥ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ረድእ ፡ ወሰይጣንሰ ፡ ተመንንገነ ፡ ካዕበ ፡ በከመ ፡ ልማዱ ። ወተመሰለ ፡ ከመ ፡ ዐቢይ ፡ ተመን ፡ ከመ ፡ ያብ ጥል ፡ ሩጸተ ፡ ፈቃዱ ፡ ለውእቱ ፡ ረድእ ፡ እስመ ፡ ቀሊል ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ለሰይ ጣን ፡ ተመስሎ ፡ በኩሉ ፡ ምትሀት ። ወአመግደመ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ማእከለ ፡ ፍኖት ፡ ኀበ ፡ እንተ ፡ የኀልፍ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ረድእ ። ወአእመረ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡ በመ ንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ዘኅዱር ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ በዓቱ ፡ ወጸ ለየ ፡ ኀበ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ከመ ፡ ያድኅኖ ፡ ለውእቱ ፡ ረድእ ፡ እመከራሁ ፡ ለጸላኢ ። ወውእቱሰ ፡ ረድእ ፡ ያፈጥን ፡ ሐዊረ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይፈጽም ፡ ትእዛዘ ፡ መምህሩ ። ወበጽሐ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ ተመን ፡ ወሶቤሃ ፡ ኬደ ፡ ዘባኖ ፡ በኀይለ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ። ወተከፍለ ፡ ለክልኤ” ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ከይሲ ፡ ወኮነ”” ፡ ለፌ ፡ ወለፌ ። ወሶበ ፡ በጽሐ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ረድእ ፡ ኀበ ፡ መምህሩ ፡ ወአቡነሰ ፡ ሮጸ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ ወሐቀፎ ፡ ወሰዓሞ ፡ ሥልሰ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ይገብር ፡ ለነ ፡ እግዚአብ
74 One day, our father sent his disciple to a brother to visit him. While the disciple was returning, Satan laid a trap for him again, according to his habit. He made himself like a great serpent to interrupt the journey that the disciple wanted to make; indeed it is easy for Satan to take any illusory form. He watched the disciple from the corner of his eye in the middle of the path by which (the latter) had to pass; our father Samu’el knew it by the Holy Spirit who dwelt upon him, while he was in his cell, and he prayed to God so that He might save the disciple from the temptation of the Enemy. As for the disciple, he was going quickly to execute the order of his master. (The latter) arrived where the serpent was and immediately trampled its tail by the power of the Holy Spirit; the serpent was divided in two and was on one side and the other. When the disciple arrived at his master, our father ran toward him, took him in his arms, gave him a kiss three times, and said to him: “God acts for us, my son.”
ሔር ፡ ወልደየ ። ይመስለክሙኑ ፡ ኦአበዊየ ፡ ሰኩና ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ይገብር ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ኀይለ ፡ ኀሰ ፡ ሕድግሰ ፡ ርእሰ ፡ ከይሲ ። ወለእመ ፡ ረከብነ ፡ ንስቲተ ፡ ሐሰካ ፡ መጠነ ፡ አጻብዕ ፡ ኢንክል ፡ ከፊሎቶ ፡ ንሕነ ፡ ደቂቀ ፡ እጓለ ፡ እመሕያው ። ወለተመ ንሰ ፡ እመኒ ፡ ሮድዎ ፡ በበትር ፡ 'ወዘበጥዎ ፡ በአእባን”" ፡ ኢይክሉ ፡ መቲሮቶ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ በዕፁብ ፡ ግብር ። አላ ፡ ከመሰ ፡ ታእምሩ ፡ ዕበዮ ፡ ለመድኀኒነ ፡ ዘይቤ ፡ በወንጌል ፡ ስቡሕ ። አባ ፡ ዕቀቦሙ ፡ በስምከ ፡ ለእለ ፡ ወሀብከኒ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይኩኑ ፡ አሐደ ፡ ከማነ ፡ በከመ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ ንሕነ ። 'ርኢኬ ፡ ኩሎሙ”፣ እለ ፡ ይገብሩ ፡ ፈቃዶ ፡ ይለብሱ ፡ መለኮቶ ። ወበእንተዝ ፡ ይረድኦሙ ፡ ኀይል ፡
75 Does it seem to you, my fathers, (that) the sole of a man’s foot exerts this power? Never in life! A fortiori a head of a serpent! If we find a small worm a finger long, we cannot divide it, we human beings. As for a great serpent, if one attacks it with a stick and hits it with stones, one cannot fell it except with great difficulty. But (this happened) so that you might know the greatness of our Savior who says in the glorious Gospel: “Father, keep in your name those whom you have given me so that they may be one as we (are) one” (Jn 17:11). See then: all those who do His will are clothed in His divinity; because of that, the power that dwells upon them

ዘኅዱር ፡ ላዕሌሆሙ ፡ በከመ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ነቢይ ፡ እመኒ ፡ ሖርኩ ፡ ማእከለ ፡ ጽለሎተ ፡ ሞት ፡ ኢይፈርሆ ፡ ለእኩይ ፡ እስመ ፡ አንተ ፡ ምስሌየ ። ወካዕበ ፡ ይቤ ፡ እምቅዱሳን ፡ እመ ፡ ተመሰሎሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ከይሲ ፡ ውእቶሙኒ” ፡ ይትሜሰልዎ ፡ ከመ ፡ ከራድዮን ፡ ወይሰርሩ ፡ በክንፈ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ መልዕልተ ፡ አርያም ፡ ወይትመኀፀኑ ፡ ኅበ ፡ መንበሩ ፡ ለልዑል ፡ ዘሎቱ ፡ ስብሐት ፡ ለዓ ለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ።
helps, according to what the prophet says: If I am in the midst of the darkness of death, I do not fear evil, for you are with me (Ps 23:4). And again, one of the saints says: “If it seems to them a serpent, they will seem to it a plover and they will fly away on the wing of the Spirit above the height of the heavens and will take refuge near the throne of the Most High—glory to Him for ever and ever, amen!”
ወበ ፳እምነ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ ሐመ ፡ ወልዱ ፡ ዘሩፋኤል ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ ሕማመ ፡ ወቀርበ” ፡- ለመዊት ። ወሖረ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ፈለግ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ፳መነኮስ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይኅሥሠ ፡ ሎሙ”' ፡ ዓሣ ፡ ወሶበ ፡ በጽሑ ፡ ህየ ፡ አው ፅኡ ፡ መጠነ ፡ ወሀቦሙ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ። ወሶበ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ምሴተ ፡ ወቤቱ ፡ ህየ ፡ፍ ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ መጽአት ፡ አንስትያዊት ፡ አንበሳ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ትጥህር ፡ ወትትመ ዓፅ ፡ ዐቢየ ፡ መዓተ ። ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዐ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወልድ ፡ ድምፀ ፡ አንበሳ ፡ ተመስወ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ወደንገፀ ፡ ልቡ ፡ ወየብሰ ፡ ከመ ፡ ገልዕ ። ወዝንቱሰ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ይጹሊ ፡ በከመ ፡ ልማድ ። ወይእቲኒ ፡ አንበሳ ፡ ቀርበት ፡ ኀቤ ሆሙ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ትጥኅር ፡ ከመ ፡ ቀዲሙ ፍ ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ መጽአ ፡ ዓቢይ ፡ አንበሳ ፡ ተባዕታዊ ፡ ዘገዚፍ ፡ ክሳዱ ፡ ወነዊኅ ፡ ቆሙ = አሐዘ ፡ ክሳዳ ፡ በአ ስናኒሁ ፡ ለይእቲ ፡ አንበሳ ፡ አንስትያዊት ፡ ወወረዋ ፡ ከመ ፡ እንተ ፡ እብን ፡ ወነጽሓ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ምድር ፡ አርሒቆ ፡ እምገጾሙ ። ወእምድኅረዝኒ ፡ ነሥኣ ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ ርሑቀ ፡ እምኔሆሙ = ወሶቤሃ ፡ መጠነ ፡ በእዴሁ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ብእሴ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ እምነ ፡ ዘቆመት ፡ ይእቲ ፡ አገበሳ ፡ እስከ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀለዉ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ፻ እመተ ። ወአንከረ ፡ ወተደመ ፡ ወሰብሖ ፡ ለረድ
76 One day, his (spiritual) son Za-Rufa’el fell gravely ill and was near to death. Our holy father went to the river with a monk to look for fish for them; when they arrived there, they took out as much as God gave them; when it was evening, they spent the night there. After that occurred a lioness roaring and seized with great anger; when the adolescent heard the noise of the lioness, his body liquefied, his heart was frightened and withered like a potsherd of clay. As for this father of ours, he was praying according to (his) custom. The lioness approached them roaring as before. After that occurred a great lion with a thick neck and a tall stature; he seized with his fangs the neck of the lioness, threw her like a stone, and cast her to the ground, (having) moved her away from the face (of the monks). After that also, he took her with him far from them. Then the man of God measured with his hand (the distance) from where the lioness was standing to where they were; there were three cubits. He was astonished, was stupefied, and glorified the help of God who does not abandon the one who entrusts himself to Him. May his prayer, his blessing, and his liberating help be with us, amen.
ኤተ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ዘኢየሐድግ ፡ ዘተወከለ ፡ ቦቱ”" ፥ ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ ወባላሒ”” ፡ ረድኤቱ ፡ ትኩን ፡ ምስሌነ"" ፡ አሜን ። ወበ 8፳ እመዋዕል ፡ ይቤሎ ፡ ረድኡ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ኦአባ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ 8አበ ፡ ምኔት ፡ ዘደብረ ፡ ለጋሶ ፡ ዘስሙ ፡ ገብረ ፡ መስቀል ፡ በአማን ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ፈራሄ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ። ወአውሥአ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ወአነ ፡ ርኢክዎ ፡ እምቅድመ ፡ ዮም ፡ ለውእቱ ፡ ብእሲ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ረድኡ ፡ በአ ይቴ ፡ ርኢኮ ።= ወይቤሎ ፡ አቡነ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ሀሎነ"''፤ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ምድረ ፡ ሳፍ ፡ አመ ፡ ኮንኩ ፡ እትመሀለል ፡ ውስተ ፡ እንታክቲ ፡ ግብ ፡ መጽአ ፡ ኀቤየ ፡ በሰ ረገላ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ወተራከበኒ ። ወእምድኅረ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ 'መጽኡ ፡ ልኡካነ”" ፡ አባ ፡ ገብረ ፡ መስቀል ፡ ኀበ ፡ አቡነ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብሉ”" ፡ እፈቅድአ ፡ እርአ ይአ ፡ ገጸከአ ፡ ወእንሣእአ ፡ በረከተከአ ። ወይቤ ፡ አቡነ"" ፡ እወ ፡ ይኩን ፡ ወአነ ፡ እመጽእ ፡ እስከ ፡ ቦርዛላ ፡ ወውእቱኒ ፡ ይምጻእ ፡ እስከ ፡ ህየ ፡= ወሶበ ፡ ነገርዎ ፡ ከመዝ ፡ ተንሥአ ፡ በድንጋፄ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ እቀድም ፡ አነ፡ መጺአ ፡ እምቅድመ ፡ ይምጻእ" ፡ ውእቱ ፡ አረጋዊ ፡ እማኅደሩ ። ወበፈቃደ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ተራከቡ ፡ በፍኖት ፡ ውስተ ፡ ፳መካን ። ወሶበ ፡ ተራከቡ ፡ በበይናቲሆሙ ፡ ተፈሥሑ ፡ ፈድፋደ ።
77 One day, his disciple said to our holy father: “Abba, there is a superior of Dabra Lagaso by the name of Gabra Masqal; in truth he is a God-fearing (man).” Our father answered and said to him: “I have seen this man before today.” The disciple said to him: “Where did you see him?” Our father said to him: “While we were in the land of Saf, when I was making supplications in the pit, he came to me on the chariot of the Spirit and met with me.” After that came envoys of Abba Gabra Masqal to our father, saying: “I want to see your face and receive your blessing.” Our father said: “Yes, may (that) be; I will come as far as Borzala, let him come as far as there.” When (the envoys) spoke to him thus, (Gabra Masqal) rose in trouble and said: “I am going to come first, I, before the old man comes from his dwelling.” By the will of God, they met on the way at a place. When they met, they rejoiced greatly.

ወሶበ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ሰርክ ፡ አቅረቡ ፡ ሎቱ" ፡ ማእደ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ አባ ፡ ገብረ ፡ መስቀል ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡ ኦአባ ፡ ጠዐም ፡ ንስቲተ ፡ እክለ ፡ ዘበእንተ ፡ ፍቅር ። ወይቤሎ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ስረይ ፡ ሊተ ፡ ኦእኅየ”' ፡ እስመ ፡ ኢበላዕኩ ፡ እክለ ፡ እምብዙኅ ፡ ዓመታት ። ወወድቀ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ አበ ፡ ምኔት ፡ ታሕተ ፡ እገሪሁ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ ኦአባ ፡ ኢትእበየኒ ፡ ተዘኪረከ ፡ ንግደትየ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ረድኡ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ኦአባ ፡ ኢትዜከርኑ ፡ ዘኀደገ ፡ ምኔቶ ፡ ወመጽአ ፡ ኀቤከ ። ወወዒአከ ፡ አፍአ ፡ ለእመ ፡ ቄእከ ፡ ምንተ ፡ ይነክየከ ፡ ወባሕቱ ፡ ኢታሕዝኖ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ፈራሄ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ኦሆ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ኢይ ፈቱ ። ወውእቱሰ""" ፡ ይጹሊ ፡ ኀበ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወይብል ፡ ኦእግዚእየ ፡ ትመትርኑ ፡ ዮም ፡ ኀኅብለ ፡ ፍቅርከ"" ፡ እንተ ፡ ኀደረት ፡ ውስተ ፡ ልብየ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ተባሀሉ”'"' ፡ ክልኤሆሙ ፡ አንተ ፡ ባርክ ፡ ወአንተ ፡ ባርክ ። ወሶበ ፡ ጐንደዩ ፡ ከመዝ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ረድእ ፡ ቡራኬ ፡ ይደሉ ፡ ለአበ ፡ ምኔት ፡ 'ወአበየ ፡ ውእቱሰ”'፤ ። ወአሐዘ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡ እዴሁ ፡ ለአባ ፡ ገብረ ፡ መስቀል ፡ ወአመዕተበ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ማእድ ፡ ወሶበ ፡ ዓረዩ ፡ ሃይማኖቶሙ ፡ ከመዝ ፡ በአሰረ ፡ ፍቅር ፡ አርአዩ"'' ፡ ጊሩቶሙ ፡ ዘመንክር ። ሶቤሃኬ ፡ ወረደ ፡ እምሰማይ ፡ ማእድ ፡ ሥሩዕ ፡ ወጽዋዕኒ ፡ ዘኢይትዓወቅ ፡ ኅበሪሁ ። ወውእቱሰ ፡ ረድእ”' ፡ ቆመ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ፕኅት ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢይባኡ ፡ አኀው!" ፡ ድንቀተ ፡ እለ ፡ ሀለዉ ፡ በአፍአ ፡ እስከ ፡ ይፌጽሙ ፡ ማእደ ። ወእምድኅረ ፡ ፈጸሙ ፡ ማእደ”' ፡ ቦአ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ረድእ ፡ ኀቤሆሙ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ አቡነ"'' ፡ ኦወልድየ ፡ ቅረብ ፡ ኀቤየ ፡ ወቀርበ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ ወሰዓሞ ፡ ወአውፅአ ፡ ልሳኖ ፡ ወአጥበዎ ። ወይቤ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ረድእ ፡ ምንተ ፡ ይኒ ፡ መዓዛሁ ፡ ኃጣእኩ ፡ በዘኣስተማስሎ ፡ ወይሰልብ ፡ አልባበ ፡ እምብዝኃ ፡ ጣዕሙ ፡ ፈድፋደ ። ወሐደሩ ፡ እሉ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ ዙላ ፡ ሌሊተ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይዘያነዉ ፡ ዕበያተ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ። ወሶበ ፡ ጸብሐ ፡ እንዘ''' ፡ የሐውሩ” ፡ ፍኖተ ፡ ወአቡነ ፡ ይተረጐም ፡ መጽሐፈ ፡ ኢዮብ ። ወአባ ፡ ገብረ ፡
78 When it was the time of evening, they brought them a table and Abba Gabra Masqal said to our father Samu’el: “Abba, taste a little food for love (for me).” Our father said to him: “Forgive me, my brother, I have not eaten food for many years.” The superior fell at the feet (of Samu’el) and said: “Abba, do not refuse me, remembering my journey.” The disciple of our father said to the latter: “Abba, do you not remember that he left his monastery and came to you? If, having gone outside, you spit out, what harm will that do you? Only do not afflict this God-fearing (man).” (Samu’el) said yes to him while not desiring (it). But he prayed to God and said: “My God, are you cutting today the bond of your love that dwells in my heart?” After that, (Samu’el and Gabra Masqal) both said to each other: “You, bless” and “you, bless.” When they had remained a long time thus, the disciple said: “The blessing befits an abbot.” But the latter refused. Our father Samu’el took the hand of Abba Gabra Masqal and signed the table. When they had thus made their faith equal by a bond of love, they made their prodigious virtue seen. Immediately descended from heaven a prepared dish and a cup whose color is not known. The disciple stood at the door so that the brothers who were outside would not enter unexpectedly before (the two men) had finished the dish. After they had finished the dish, the disciple entered to them and our father said to him: “My son, approach me,” and (the disciple) approached him. (Samu’el) gave him a kiss, stuck out his tongue, and made him suck (it). The disciple said: “How good is its taste! I have nothing to compare it to: it ravishes the hearts by its very great sweetness!” These saints remained all night conversing about the greatness of God. When it was morning, while they were traveling, our father was explaining the book of Job. Abba Gabra Masqal took paper and a pen and was writing while traveling; the rain was falling upon them.
መስቀል ፡ ነሥአ ፡ ክርታሰ ፡ ወቀለመ ፡ ወይጽሕፍ ፡ እንዘ ፡ የሐውር ፡ ፍኖተ ፡ ወዝናም ፡ ይወርድ ፡ ላዕሌሆሙ ። አንክሮ ፡ አንክሩ ፡ ኦፍቁራንየ ፡ ግብረ ፡ እሉ ፡ ቅዱሳን = ወዘሰ ፡ የሐ ውር ፡ ፍኖተ ፡ ይትዐቀብ”'' ፡ እገሪሁ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይጌሊ ፡ እመ ፡ ይረክብ ፡ እብነ ፡ አው ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢይውግኦ ፡ ሦክ ። ኦአበውየ ፡ ብፁዓዊያን ፡ ተፈጸመ ፡ በላዕሌክሙ ፡ ቃለ ፡ ዳዊት ፡ ነቢይ ፡ ዘይቤ ፡ እስመ ፡ ለመላእክቲሁ ፡ ይኤዝ ዞሙ ፡ በእንቲአከ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይዕቀቡከ ፡ በኩሉ ፡ ፍናዊከ ። ወበእደዊሆሙ ፡ ያነ ሥኡከ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢትትዐቀፍ ፡ በእብን ፡ እገሪከ ። ወአቡነ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይተረ ጐም ፡ ወውእቱ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይጽሕፍ ፡ ወገብሩ ፡ ከመዝ" ፡ እስከ ፡ አተዉ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ማኅደሩ ፡ ለአቡነ ። ጸሎቶሙ ፡ ወበረከቶሙ ፡ ተሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ።
79 Be filled with wonder, my beloved, before the action of these saints: he who travels, his foot is guarded, while he reflects, if he encounters an obstacle or so that a thorn does not prick him. My blessed fathers, it has been accomplished upon you, the word of David the prophet who says: For to his angels he will prescribe for you to guard you in all your paths; with their hands they will take you so that your foot does not stumble on a stone (Ps 91:11-12). Our father explaining and (Gabra Masqal) writing acted thus until they had returned to the dwelling of our father. May their prayer and their blessing be with us, amen.


[p. 68] {#po.53.p68}
ወበ እመዋዕል ፡ ተፈነወ ፡ ብድብድ ፡ ውስተ ፡ አሐቲ ፡ ሀገር ፡ ወእንዘ ፡ የሐውሩ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ፍኖት” ፡ ዘተአዘዙ ፡ ገብኡ” ፡ ፍኖተ ፡ መንገለ ፡ በዓቱ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡ ወሚካኤል ፡ ይመርሖሙ ፡ እስመ ፡ ሌሊተ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ አሜሃ ። ወሶበ ፡ በጽሑ ፡ ቅሩበ ፡ በዐቱ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ርእዮ ፡ ፳ እምሰራዊተ ፡ ብድብድ ፡ ወአውተረ ፡ ቀስቶ ፡ ወፈቀደ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሂጾ ፡ ለአቡነ ። ወከልኦ ፡ ሚካኤል ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ ኢታንሥእ ፡ እዴከ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ብእሴ ፡ እግ ዚአብሔር ፡ እስመ ፡ በጸሎቱ ፡ ይስዕር ፡ ኩሎ ፡ መናስወ ፡ ዘይመጽእ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዓለም”" ፡ ወበትንብልናሁ ፡ ያወርድ" ፡ ምሕረተ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ለኩሉ ፡ ፍጥረት ። ኢትሬእዮኑ ፡ ይቡሰ ፡ ከነፍር ፡ ዘአልቦ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ትር
80 One day, the plague was sent into a city and, while they were going, by the path that had been prescribed to them, (the soldiers of the plague) turned back toward the cell of our father Samu’el; (the archangel) Michael was leading them, for it was night then. When they arrived near the cell of our father, one of the soldiers of the plague saw him, drew his bow, and wanted to strike our father. Michael restrained him by saying: “Do not raise your hand against this man of God, for, by his prayer, he suppresses all the calamities that occur in the world and, by his intercession, he makes the mercy of God descend for every creature. Do you not see him, with parched lips, without a garment of flesh outside of skin and bones?”

81 There was there a monk, he was near to coming to our father. The demon took the word and said to Michael: “Allow me (to go) to this monk.” Michael said to him: “Devour this one so that it may be a penance for him, for he has not dedicated himself to God until now.” Then (the demon) struck him under his pectoral muscle, (the monk) fell immediately and began to writhe; the poison of the demon itched him all over and poured onto him like a cauldron of fire, his heart fainted, and he was like dead. Our father, standing, was praying according to his custom and saw everything that was happening; when he saw that they had struck this young (monk), he addressed a lamentation to God and said: “Do not kill, Lord, this (spiritual) son of mine with this evil sickness.” Michael addressed the word to him and said to him: “What makes you weep, servant of God?” (Samu’el) said to him: “Do you not see, angel of the power of God, how these demons have struck this (son) of mine?” Michael said to him: “Do not be afraid, your son will not die.” He ordered the demon to make the poison come out of the young (monk), (the demon) made the poison come out immediately, kept (it) for himself with his very fine arrow at the resemblance of a barley beard, and showed (it) to our father. The saint said to him: “Does this kill all people?” The demon answered our father and said to him: “In truth this kills them; if you look at it, small (is) its size, but its poison is great.”

82 Our father questioned Michael and said to him: “Where will you chase these destroyers?” Michael said to him: “Into a convent of monks.” Our holy father said to him: “What (is) their fault?” Michael said to him: “They do an action that irritates God, both in public and in secret, especially because the satanic serpent has deceived their heart and has dominated their spirit: they have begun to eat the flesh of their neighbor without a knife.” Having said that, he went away with these soldiers (of the plague). That which the angel said, listen to the explanation of it: there was a monk

in this convent, virtuous and God-fearing: he visited our father incessantly, his virtue (was) beautiful, (he) loved the brothers and loved his soul. After that, Satan was jealous of him and sowed in his heart an evil thought. (The monk) began to reveal the imperfections of his brothers and to produce for them a bad reputation; the heart of the brothers was darkened to hear his remarks. That day, the angel of God came, cut the tongue (of the monk), and threw (it) for him before him. The monk took in his hand his piece of tongue and went to our father Samu’el; arrived at him, he fell at his feet, began to writhe with groans and with abundant tears, and showed our father his piece of tongue. When our father saw, he was greatly afflicted, stretched out his hands, and prayed to God saying: “My Lord, do not be angry against your creature and do not make this brother perish with this painful punishment, for you (are) far from anger and full of mercy (Ps 145:8).” Having said that, he took (the piece of) the tongue that had been cut, glued it to the root of the tongue, and (the latter) was healed immediately; it had become black and remained thus until the day of the death (of the monk).

83 After that, (Samu’el) called his disciple Za-Rufa’el and said to him: “My son, come, go to such a monastery and say to its superior: ‘Make supplications, for the plague has been sent to you from God with a punishment of death, do not delay; from this fourth day that is coming, proceed to severe supplications in order to be saved from the punishment that is coming upon you.’ Tell them, only they will not listen to you at all, but they will blame us and will mock us. If they do not listen, what does it matter to us? It is better for us to accomplish the word of the Gospel that says: Love your neighbor as yourself (Mt 19:19; 22:39).” The disciple went and spoke to the head of the monastery, according to what his master had prescribed to him. The superior said to his (spiritual) children: “My sons, if God has willed (it), tomorrow gather in the church so that we may make supplications according to what our father Samu’el has prescribed to us.” His children answered him saying: “Our father, do not listen to the discourse of these holy solitaries, for such (is) their custom: because they have no worry, they give orders to people by saying: ‘Make supplications.’ Behold you see how the time to harvest has come for us, and our seeds have returned into the earth. As for you, do not listen to them, for the Enemy makes them speak here.”

death and of life, to his tongue: they came submitting to his holiness and said to him: “Forgive us, Abba; all this that has happened to us (happened) because we did not listen to your word. 85 Listen further, that I may tell you what happened one day while this saint was in the land of Wedäkä eating dinner: a vixen came to him and stood before him at the time of dinner; he would take crumbs from his food and offer them to her with his hand; she would lie in his lap and receive from his hand what he gave her. But one day, she did not come to him; he waited for her, having set aside her portion, and she was slow to come. After that, he called his (spiritual) son Za-Rufa’el and said to him: 'My son, this vixen who always comes to me, I have now waited for her and she is not here for me. Now go, my son, and call her for me.' The disciple said to him: 'Where shall I find her, abbä?' The saint of God said to him: 'You will find her nearby.' The disciple then arose, as his master had instructed him, went and found her nearby, without tiring himself to search, asleep under a flax tree. The disciple said to her: 'Arise, my father calls you.' || He took her, held her in his hands, and brought her to his master. (The latter) said to him: 'Send her so that she may come to me.' (The disciple) then sent her, (Sämu’el) gave her her food, and she received it from his hand, according to her custom. Glory to our God who honors his beloved ones at all times, for ever and ever, amen!

86 Listen further to what this saint mär Sämu'ël did, who loved sinners, brought the distant near, converted the one who had been lost, established peace, and reconciled them with their God. There was a monk, son of the sister of our father Samu’ël, by the name of Érmeyäs; he had learned as a manual trade to write books. Our father said to him: 'Do not work, abandon this writing.' (The monk) refused and worked in solitude so that our holy father would not know. When our holy father learned (of it), he said to him: 'Did I not say to you: “Do not work”? Why do you transgress my command?' Then the young (monk) withdrew far from him, went to a distant land, and returned to the world. When our father learned that he had returned to the world, he struck his breast and said: 'Lord, do not make your sheep the food of a wild beast and do not deliver (it) to the wolf.' After that, our father was shown the dwelling of the young (monk) in Sheol, where there is weeping and gnashing of teeth (Mt 8:12). Our father shed abundant tears and his heart suffered greatly. Michael the archangel came

to him covered in a mantle of light, a golden staff in his hand and, upon his head, a crown engraved with the sign of the cross; his wings (were) of fire. He said to him: 'Greetings to you, Samu’ël, chosen of God! Why do you grieve over this young (monk): he has neglected your word?' The old man said: 'Why should I not grieve: he is my blood and my bone?' Michael said to him: 'Do not be afraid, after a short time, he will come to you.'

87 After that, the man of God redoubled his supplications until he brought the young (monk) back to his (previous) life. God brought him to him before he died. (Sämu'ël) exhorted him with the word of the Scriptures, clothed him again in the monastic habit, and bound him with the rule of the holy Scriptures; he gave him many exhortations by which his soul would be saved. (The monk) was pleasing to God for all the rest of his life; he prostrated himself four thousand times each day and, at night, would go down to the river, enter the water, and stand there weeping, letting his tears flow like torrents of water: the drops of his tears resounded in the depths like the blows of hail; those who saw (him) were witnesses to this. He went into the desert, remained in great devotion to God, like the angels, and died in peace. May his prayer and his blessing be with us, amen.

88 To whom shall I compare this (chosen mär Sämu'el)? He was comparable to his God: he did not take vengeance on the one who transgressed his command; to save him, he solicited and begged; from the mouth of the wolf he snatched the sheep that was lost (Mt 18:12//); his virtues were greatly exalted; more than his predecessors he was strong and powerful. May he save us by his prayer from the hand of the serpent that used guile to attack us, for ever and ever, amen.

### The ascetic combat of Sämu’el

89 Listen further, that I may tell you what this saint mär Sämu'ël did; he remained leading a great (ascetic) combat, having placed a stone in his mouth so that it might be a lock for his lips. (Here is) this combat: 1) He increased silence upon himself; 2) He put chains on his feet; 3) He entered the water every night and recited the one hundred and fifty psalms of David five times each, standing in the water; 4) He wore a hairshirt under his garment; 5) He instituted a flagellation for his back, fifty (blows) each time; 6) He prostrated himself like a wheel, beginning from the time of sleep until the time of the sixth hour; 7) All these trials, he instituted (them) for himself at one and the same time without omitting any. From one end of the year to the other, he did thus. He also fasted forty (days) per year, four times: 1) the fast of the holy forty (days); 2) the fast

of the apostles; 3) the fast of nahasë; 4) the fast of tähsäs. Doing thus during these days, he passed (his life) from when he had begun until the moment of his departure from this world. Old age did not make him abandon (it) and youth did not overcome him, but he accomplished (this) according to what Paul says: I reach for what (is) behind me, I pursue what (is) before me (Ph 3:13-14). 90 Abbä, with all these trials you have killed your body. Is your nature not like the nature of Adam? Or have you put on a skin of stone? Indeed, stone, one breaks it and cuts it until it is leveled as one has willed. Your mother the Church praises you and says to you: 'Who (is) like you, who finished as he began and found as he pursued? You have plowed and your furrow has not deviated, you have sown and your grain has not perished; on the contrary, as you have sown, you have reaped; the whole of your production has become a heap of heaps; neither straw nor weed were found on your threshing floor, you concerning whom — and concerning those who (are) like you — your Lord said: “He who received the seed on good ground gave and produced fruit, a hundredfold and doubled, one thirty, one sixty, one hundred. He who has an ear (to) hear, let him hear this” (Mt 13:8-9//).' While all this (ascetic) combat was his, he did not fail and did not wither, but he appeared to remain in the bridal chamber and his face sparkled like a ray of sunlight. When he spoke, he had the taste of a honeycomb, all his members (were) crowned by the grace of God.
አባሉ ፡ ክሉል ፡ በጸጋ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ። ወዓዲ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ዝኒ ፡ ወሰከ ፡ ካልአ ፡ ገድለ ፡ ወአምጽአ ፡ ፍሬ ፡ ሣዕር ፡ ዘስሙ” ፡ ኩዕያ ፡ ወረሰየ ፡ ውእተ ፡ ሲሳዮ ፡ እስመ ፡ እምኩሉ ፡ ፍሬ ፡ ሣዕር ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ትመርር ። ወውእቱሰ ፡ ጥቀ ፡ አፍቀራ ፡ ለዝሉፉ ፡ ወትረ ፡ ሲሳዮ ፡ ረሰያ ፡ አኮ ፡ ኢያእሚሮ ፡ ለይእቲ ፡ 'ዕፅ ፡ ምረታ"" ፡ አላ ፡ ያስተማስላ ፡ ንብረተ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ዓለም ። ይቤ ፡ እስመ ፡ ተአኪ ፡ ዛቲ ፡ ዓለም ፡ እምዛቲ ፡ ፍሬ ፡ ሣዕር ። በዝንቱ ፡ ሕሊና ፡ ኩነና ፡ ለሥጋሁ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ መዋ ዐለ ፡ ሕይወቱ ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ ወጻማ ፡ ትሕርምቱ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ለባሴ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ያድኅነነ ፡ እምተኩላ"'' ፡ ርኩስ ፡ ወእምተቃርኖቱ ፡ ለዲያብ
91 To this he added another combat: he brought the flower of an herb by the name of ke'yä and made it his food because, of all the flowers of the herb, this one is the bitterest. But he always loved it strongly, he constantly made it his food not without knowing the bitterness of this vegetable, but he assimilated it to the way of being of this world; he would say: 'This world is worse than this flower of the herb.' With this thought, he macerated his body all the days of his life. May the prayer, the blessing, and the trial of abstinence of this saint clothed with the Spirit save us from the impure wolf and the attacks of the devil forever, amen.
ሎስ ፡ ለዓለም ፡ አሜን ።= ወበ8እምነ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ ሖረ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ፳እጐ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ፈለገ ፡ ተከዜ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይኅሥሥ!"”' ፡ ዓሣ ፡ ለድራሮሙ ። ወበጺሖሙ ፡ ህየ ፡ ወገረ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡ መርበብተ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቀላይ ፡ ወተእኅዘ ፡ መርበብት ፡ እንተ ፡ ውስጥ ፡ በአንቀረ ፡ እብን ። ወጻመዉ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ለአው ፅኦቱ ፡ ወወዐሉ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይስኅቡ ፡ ወሶበ ፡ ተስእኖሙ ፡ አሰሩ ፡ ኃብለ ፡ መር በብት ፡ በጽንፈ ፡ ማይ ፡ ወአምጽኡ ፡ ሕለተ ፡ ረያመ ፡ ይመጥኑ ፡ ዕመቆ ፡ ወስእኑ ፡ ረኪቦቶ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ተንሥአ ፡ ብፁዓዊ'”'' ፡ ወሰፍሐ ፡ እደዊሁ ፡ ወጸለየ ፡ ኀበ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወፈጺሞ ፡ ጸሎቶ ፡ ባረከ ፡ በእዴሁ ፡ ዲበ፡ ማይ ፡ ወአማዕተበ ፡ መንገለ ፡ ባሕር ፡ በትእምርተ ፡ መስቀል ። ወሶቤሃ ፡
92 One day, our holy father went with a brother to the Takkazë river in order to look for fish for their dinner. When they had arrived there, our father Sämu'ël cast a net into the depths; the net caught there in a crevice of the rock. They struggled much to get it out and spent the day pulling. As they did not succeed, they tied the rope of the net to the edge of the water and brought a long reed to measure the depth; they could not find it. After that, the blessed one arose, stretched out his hands, and prayed to God; having finished his prayer, he blessed the water with his hand and made a sign of the cross toward the river. Immediately it parted — on one side and the other — and

stood up like a rampart wall so that the sandy bottom appeared below; as for the net, it appeared like a spread-out garment. (Sämu'ël) ordered the brother to go down into the depths and bring up the net there. The brother made his way into the middle of the river and began to walk, as on dry land, with joy and gladness; when he looked upward, he saw the water like a high cliff on one side and the other. He praised God, being astonished, stupefied, and marveling at the greatness of God who performs miracles and wonders by the hand of his saints. When he had gathered the net, he could not lift it, for two large fish were in it. Our father said to him: 'My son, one alone suffices for us: throw back the one that remains.' (The disciple) did as our father had ordered him. When he had lifted (the net) onto his shoulders, (it) touched the ground because of the considerable size of the very large fish. When the brother had come out, the water reunited as before and (the two monks) returned to their houses.
