precious stone that shines seven times more than the sun, it is the beauty of your (spiritual) combat; this that (my) left (hand) carries (is) pure incense in the likeness of your holy priesthood. It is I who have come to you to announce to you what (will happen) after, for you have loved me much. This prayer of mine that you say over the water and also my anaphora that you add over it, this, do not abandon (it) during the days of your life, and to your children also prescribe that after you they do not abandon (it), for whoever invokes me with this anaphora, I will love him with all my heart. Now, listen, let me speak to you: just as you have not ceased to make mention of me, neither do I cease to make mention of you before my beloved Son.' Having said this, she blessed him and ascended to heaven. May her prayer, her intercession, and the mercy of her Son be with us, amen.
ወስእለታ ፡ ወምሕረተ ፡ ወልዳ ፡ 'የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ"" ፡ አሜን ።= ስምዑ ፡ ካዕበ ፡ በእዝነ ፡ ልብ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ መንክረ ፡ ወዕፁበ ፡ ወመድምመ ፡ ዘይከውን ፡ በቀዔተ ፡ ለነፍስክሙ ። ወሖረ ፡ 'ውእተ ፡ አሚረ”" ፡ ፳እምነ ፡ አኀው ፡ ኅበ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ኦአባ ፡ መጻእኩ ፡ ኀቤከ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትፈቅር ፡ ሊተ ፡ አሐተ ፡ ነገረ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ግብሮሙ ፡ ለመነኮሳት ፡ ዘይከውን ፡ በደሐሪ ፡ መዋዕል ፍ ሰማዕኩ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብሉ ፡ ቦዘ ፡ ጸሊም ፡ ክንፉ ፡ ወቦ እለ ፡ ሕብረ ፡ ሐመድ ፡ ክንፎሙ ፡ ወቦ ፡ ጻዓዳ ፡ ወቦ ፡ ዘእሳት ፡ አማንኑ ፡ ዘይብሉ ፡ ከመዝ ።= ወአውሥአ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ ኦወልድየ ፡ አንሰ ፡ ኢይትናገር ፡ ወኢምንተኒ ፡ በጐስዐተ ፡ ልብየ ፡ ኅበ ፡ አይሂ ፡ ግብር ፡ ዘኢከ ሠተ ፡ ሊተ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ። ወይእዜኒ ፡ ሖር ፡ ውስተ ፡ ማኅደርከ ፡ እስከ ፡ እጴጹሊ ፡ ኀበ ፡ አምላኪየ ፡ ወበግብአትከ ፡ ኣጤይቀከ ። ወውእቱሰ ፡ እጐ ፡ ጦረ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ማኅደሩ ። ወእምድኅረ ፡ ሕዳጥ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ ተመይጠ ፡ ኅቤሁ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ኦአባ ፡ መጻእኩ ፡ ኀቤከ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትፈጽም ፡ ሊተ ፡ 'ኀሚሦትየ ፡ ዘሰአልኩከ”" ፡- አሜሃ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ሠናየ ፡ ገበርከ ፡ ኦወልድየ ፡
144 Listen again with the ear of the heart to this prodigy, a marvelous and astonishing thing, which will be profitable to your souls: that day, one of the brothers went to our father Sämu’ël and said to him: 'Abbä, I have come to you so that you might explain to me a thing, concerning the way of being of the monks, which will be in the last days. I have heard it said: “There is one whose wings (are) black, there are some whose wings (are) the color of ash, there are (some who are) white, and there are some of fire.” Is it truthful, what is said thus?' Our holy father answered and said: 'My son, I say nothing by the effusion of my heart, on anything whatsoever, that the Holy Spirit has not revealed to me. Now, go to your dwelling until I have prayed to my God; upon your return, I will inform you.' The brother went to his dwelling. A few days later, he returned to (Sämu’ël) and said to him: 'Abba, I have come to you so that you might grant for me the request that I presented to you then.' Our father said to him: 'You have done well, my son, only you have delayed coming to me. Now, listen, let me tell you.
ወባሕቱ ፡ ጐንደይከ ፡ ምጽአተከ ፡ ኀቤየ ።= ወይእዜኒ ፡ ስማዕ ፡ እንግርከ ። ወአመ ፡ 'ይእቲ ፡ ሌሊት”" ፡ እንዘ ፡ ሀሎኩ ፡ ቀዊምየ ፡ እጹሊ” ፡ መጽአ ፡ ኀቤየ ፡ እግዚእየ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ በስብሐት ፡ ዐቢይ ፡ ወይቤ ለኒ ፡ ሰላም ፡ ለከ ፡ ኦፍቁርየ ። ወሶበ ፡ ርኢክዎ ፡ ወደቁ ፡ ውስተ ፡ እገሪሁ ። ወአኀዘኒ ፡ በእደዊሁ ፡ ማሕየዊት ፡ ወአንሥአኒ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ ተንሥእ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትነጽር ፡ ዘሰአልከኒ ፡ በእንቲአሁ ፡ ግብሮሙ ፡ ለመነኮሳት ፡ ዘይከውን ፡ በደኀሪ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ አመ ፡ ትንሣኤ ፡ ምውታን ። ወቆምኩ ፡ ሶቤሃ ፡ ቅድመ ፡ እግዚእየ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ።፥ 'መቅድመ ፡ ኩሉ"' ፡ መጽኡ ፡ ኅብረ ፡ ጸሊማን ፡ ወድኩም ፡ ክነፊሆሙ ፡ ወሥእኑ ፡ ሰጊረ ፡ ወንስቲትክመ ፡ ሐለፉ ፡ እንተ ፡ ቅድሜየ ፡ ወወደቁ ፡ በጊዜሃ ፡ ወሰገድኩ ፡ ስልሰ ፡ ወእቤሎ ፡ እግዚእየ ፡ እለ ፡ መኑ ፡ እሙንቱ ። ወአውሥአኒ ፡ ወይቤለኒ ፡ እሉ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ እለ ፡
145 “That night, while I was standing praying, my Lord Jesus Christ came to me in great glory and said to me: ‘Peace to you, my beloved!’ When I saw him, I fell at his feet. He seized me with his life-giving hands and raised me up, saying: ‘Rise to look at that about which you questioned me: the way of being of the monks that will be in the last days, at the resurrection of the dead.’ I then stood before my Lord Jesus Christ. Before all came (those) whose wings (were) black in color and weak; they could not walk and only a few passed before me. I fell on the spot, prostrated myself three times, and said to him: ‘My Lord, who are these?’ He answered me and said to me: ‘These are those whom the century has swallowed and who have been chained by its

146 “After that came those whose wings were the color of ash. I prostrated myself three times and said to (my Lord): ‘Who are these?’ He said to me: ‘These fulfilled their monastic duty with hypocrisy so that people would praise them and say they feared God. They gave the impression of being just on the outside, like a pretty woman: she adorns herself on the outside and has a sterile womb, which has no fruit, she groans and suffers always in her heart because she has not obtained children. Likewise, therefore, the souls of the sinners groan in their bodies.’ Came also those with wings similar to the moon. I prostrated myself three times and said to (my Lord): ‘Who are these?’ He said to me: ‘These are monastic superiors who have not committed sin nor practiced justice; indeed they are pure from sin and loved justice in their thought. As for works, they have none, according to what the apostle made a comparison for them, saying: The faith that has no good works is dead itself’ (Jas 2:26).

147 “Came also those (with wings) in the likeness of the stars. I prostrated myself three times and said to him: ‘Who are these?’ He said to me: ‘The children of those who remained in their teaching.’ I said to him: ‘My Lord, how have they been inferior to their fathers?’ He said to me: ‘Because they could not be raised by their fathers to a higher level; these did not have the power to enlighten their children, just as the moon has no heat and cannot warm its stars; on the contrary, when the morning arrives, its light diminishes.’ Came also those with wings in the likeness of the sun and whose whole person shone. I prostrated myself three times and said to (my Lord): ‘Who are these?’ He answered me and said to me: ‘These (are) monastic superiors who have borne the yoke of my cross, having heard my word that I said in the Gospel: Enter through the narrow and very constricted gate that leads to eternal life’ (Mt 7:13-14). Came also those whose wings (were) of fire. I prostrated myself three times and said to him: ‘Who are these?’ He said to me: ‘The disciples of those who (are) in the likeness of the sun.’ I said to him: ‘My Lord, are the disciples greater than their masters?’ He said to me: ‘Yes, they are greater, for they shine much; like (their masters), it is not possible for them to abandon what pleases them.’

ንጹሓን ። ኢሰማዕከኑ ፡ ዘይቤ ፡ ዳዊት ፡ ነቢይ ፡ በእንቲአሆሙ ። ወኮነ ፡ ከመ ፡ አባግዕ ፡ ዘይጠብሑ ። ከመዝኬ ፡ ኮኑ ፡ ድልዋነ ፡ ወመጠዉ ፡ ሥጋ ሆሙ!" ፡ ለኩነኔ ፡ ወለመቅሠፍት ። እቶነ ፡ እሳት ፡ ኢያፍርሆሙ ፡ በልሐ ፡ አስይፍት ፡ ኢያደንገፆሙ ፡ ሐውዛተ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ኢያስፈጦሙ?"' ፡ ፍትወታተ ፡ ከንቱ ፡ ኢሞኦሙ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ያደልዉ ፡ ሊተ ፡ በኩሉ ፡ መልእክቶሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይርከቡ ፡ ብፅዓ ፡ ዘአንበርኩ ፡ ሎሙ ፡ በውስተ ፡ ወንጌል ። ወእቤሎሙ ፡ ወአንትሙሰ ፡ ባሕቱ ፡ እለ ፡ በእንቲአየ ፡ ተዐገስክሙ ፡ ምስሌየ ፡ በሕማ ምየ ፡ በከመ ፡ ሊተ ፡ ወሀበኒ ፡ አቡየ ፡ መንግሥተ ፡ ከማሁ ፡ አስተዳሎኩ"" ፡ መንግሥተ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትብልዑ ፡ ወትስተዩ ፡ ምስሌየ ፡ በማእድየ ፡ በውስተ ፡ መንግሥትየ ፡ ወትነብሩ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ፲ ወ ፪ መናብርት ፡ ወትኬኔንኑ ፡ ፲ወ ፪ ሕዝበ ፡ እስራኤል ። በከመ ፡ ለበስኩ ፡ አነ ፡ ሜላተ ፡ ብርሃን ፡ ሰማያዊተ ፡ ከማሁ ፡ ይለብሱ ፡ ሐዋርያትየ ። ወበከመ ፡ አነ ፡ ዐረጉ ፡ በደመና?” ፡ ዘእሳት ፡ ከማሁ ፡ የዐርጉ ፡ እሙንቱሂ ፡ በአፍራሰ ፡ እሳት ። ከመዝኬ ፡ ይትወሀቦሙ ፡ ወይነ ሥእ ፡ ዕሜቶ ፡ ረድእኒ ፡ ወመምህርኒ ፡ ዘፍጹም ፡ ምግባሩ ፡ ወዘንተ ፡ ብሂሎ ፡ ዐርገ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሰማይ ። ወኩሉ ፡ ስግደታትየ ፡ ቅድመ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ኮነ ፡
heard what David the prophet says about them: And we are like sheep that are slaughtered (Ps 43[44]:23)? Likewise, therefore, they have been ready and have offered their bodies to torture and to flagellation. The furnace of fire did not frighten them, the edge of the swords did not make them tremble, the voluptuousness of the century did not seduce them, the vain desires did not conquer them, while they satisfied me in all their ministry in order to obtain the beatitude that I established for them in the Gospel. I say to them: “Only you (are) those who, for me, have remained constant in my trials; just as, for me, my Father gave me a kingdom, likewise I have prepared a kingdom so that you may eat and drink with me at my table in my kingdom; you will sit on twelve thrones and judge the twelve tribes of Israel” (Lk 22:28-30). Just as I have put on a luminous celestial garment (Ps 103[104]:2), likewise are my apostles clothed. Just as I ascended with a cloud of fire, likewise they ascend, they, with horses of fire. Thus, therefore, it is given to them: the disciple receives his reward, and the master also whose work is accomplished.’ Having said this, he ascended to heaven. All my prostrations before my Lord were twenty-four,” said the saint.
፳ወ፪ይቤ ፡ ቅዱስ ። ኦእለ ፡ ትሰምዑ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ኢትኩኑ ፡ ድንዙዛነ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትድኃኑ ፡ እም ወይሌ ፡ ዘይረክቦሙ ፡ ለሃካያን ፡ እለ ፡ ከማየ ፡= አላ ፡ ተዘከሩ ፡ ቃሎ ፡ 'ዘይቤ ፡ እግዚእነ'” ፡ ዘነበበ ፡ በውስተ ፡ ወንጌሉ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ ዑቁ ፡ ኢታክብዱ ፡ ልበክሙ ፡ በስታይ ፡ ወበፈጊዕ ፡ ወሐውዝ ፡ መንበርት ፡ ወትበጽሐክሙ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ግብተ ፡ ከመ ፡ መስገርት ፡ እንተ ፡ ትወርድ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ እለ ፡ ይነብሩ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ምድር ፡ ወባሕቱ ፡ ጸልዩ ፡ በኩሉ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትክ ሀሉ ፡ አምሥጦ ፡ በጸሎትክሙ ። ወለክሙሰ”'' ፡ ኦፍቁራንየ ፡ በከመ ፡ ብገኃ ፡ ምሕረቱ ፡ ይግበር ፡ ለክሙ ። ወይረሲክሙ ፡ ድልዋነ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ሕሩያኒሁ ፡ ወቅ ዱሳኒሁ ፡ በይእቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ግርምት ፡ ወዕፅብት ። ወየሀብክሙ ፡ ምግባራተ ፡ ብሩሀተ ፡ ወይረሲክሙ ፡ ድልዋነ ፡ አምልኮቱ ፡ ወያቅምክሙ ፡ ለዐቂበ ፡ ምግባ ራተ ፡ ሠናይ ፡ ለአፈድፍዶ ፡ ውስቴታ ፡ ወሰኒቀ ፡ እምኔሃ ፡ ለዘይመጽእ ፡ ዓለም ። ወያርጉ ፡ ለክሙ ፡ መዝገበ ፡ በረከቱ ፡ ወያድኅንክሙ ፡ እምግርማ ፡ መዓቱ ፡ ወይረሲክሙ ፡ ሱቱፋነ ፡ ለመንግሥቱ ። በጸሎተ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ከመ ፡ መላእክት ፡ ዘኮነ ፡ ሕይወቱ ። ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ።
149 You who hear this, do not be indolent, so that you may be saved from the misfortune that touches the lazy who (are) like me, but remember the word that Our Lord says, which he issued in his Gospel, saying: “Take care, do not weigh down your hearts in drink, in lust, and in the delights of life, and that Day would arrive upon you suddenly like a net that descends upon all those who dwell on earth. But pray at all times to be able to escape by your prayer” (Lk 21:34-36). As for you, my beloved, may he act for you according to the greatness of his mercy; may he make you ready with his elect and his saints on that terrifying and formidable day; may he give you luminous works, may he make you ready to adore him, and may he set you standing to keep the beautiful works, to redouble them, and to draw from them a viaticum for the world to come; may he open for you the treasure of his blessing, may he save you from the terror of his anger, and may he make you associates for his kingdom, by the prayer of this father of ours, the saint whose life has been like (that) of the angels, in the centuries of centuries, amen.
ወኮነ ፡ ካዕበ ፡ በ ፳-እመዋዕል ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይጹሊ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡ ቀዊሞ ፡ ማእከለ ፡ ባሕር ፡ በከመ ፡ ያለምድ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይደግም ፡ መኃልየ ፡ መኃልይ ፡ ዘሰሎሞን ፡ መጽአ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ መልአከ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወአምጽአ ፡ ሎቱ” ፡ መጽሐፈ ፡ 'ኅትምተ ፡ ዘእኅዝት'” ፡ በአኅባለ ፡ ወርቅ ፡ 'ወጽሕፍታኒ ፡ በቀለመ ፡ ወርቅ ፡ ወመስተረ ፡ ርእስሂ ፡ ዘወርቅ ፡ ወእቱዝ ፡ በአ ኅባለ ፡ ወርቅ” = ወይቤሎ ፡ መልአክ ፡ ነጽር ፡ ዘንተ ፡ መጽሐፈ ፡ ዘጥብጥብት”' ፡
150 || It happened again one day, while this saint, our father Sämu’el, was praying standing in the middle of the river, according to his habit, by (reciting) twice the Song of Songs of Solomon, that an angel of God came to him and brought him a closed book which (was) held by golden cords and written with a golden reed; the case (7) also (was) of gold and held by golden cords. The angel said to him: 'Look at this book which (is) expertly made with a


[p. 124] {#po.53.p124}
በቀለመ ፡ ወርቅ ፡ እንተ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ጸሎታቲከ ፡ ውክፍተ ፡ በቅድመ ፡ እግዚአብ ሔር ፡ ወአሕባላቲሃ ፡ ዘወርቅ ፡ እንተ ፡ 'ኢትትበተክ ፡ እንተ ፡ ይእቲ” ፡ አም ሳለ ፡ ሕሊናከ ፡ ጽሪት ፡ እንተ ፡ ኢትትበተክ ፡ እምድር ፡ እስከ ፡ ሰማይ ። ወዝኒ ፡ መስተረ ፡ ወርቅ ፡ አምሳለ ፡ ውዳሴከ ፡ በአምሳለ ፡ መርዓዊ ፡ ወመ ርዓት ። ዘንተ ፡ ይቤሎ ፡ መልአክ ፡ ለብፁዕ ፡ ወቅዱስ ፡ ሳሙኤል ። ስብሐት ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ 'ዘቀደሰ ፡ ነፍሶ” ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ። አኩቴት ፡ ለወልድ ፡ ዘአንጽሖ ፡ እምደነስ ። ዕበይ ፡ ወክብር ፡ ለመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ዘዐቀቦ ፡ ዘእን
golden reed, which is your prayers accepted before God, and its infrangible golden cords, which are in the likeness of your chosen thought, infrangible from the earth to the heaven; as for this golden case (7), (it is) in the likeness of your praise, in the likeness of the Bridegroom and the Bride.' The angel said this to the blessed and holy Sämu’el. Glory to God (the Father) who has sanctified the soul of this saint! Praise to the Son who has purified him from defilement! Greatness and honor to the Holy Spirit who has kept him without impurity, in the centuries of centuries, amen.
በለ ፡ ርኩስ ። ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ።« ወኮነ ፡ በ8 እመዋዕል ፡ መጽኡ ፡ ኅቤሁ ፡ ፪ፈያት ፡ ኀበ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገዳሙ ፡ ወይቤልዎ ፡ ለእመሰ ፡ ኮንከ ፡ ብእሴ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ አንተ ፡ አርእየነ ፡ ትእምርተ ፡ ወእመ ፡ አኮ ፡ ንቀት ለከ ። ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ 'አነ ፡ አአምን" ፡ ከመ ፡ ገብረ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ አነ ፡ ወባሕቱ ፡ ምግባርየሰ ፡ ምግባረ ፡ ኃጢአት ፡ ምሉእ ፡ ወኢይትከሀለኒ ፡ አርእዮ ፡ ትእምርተ ፡ እስመ ፡ ግብረ ፡ ጻድቃን ፡ ውእቱ" = ወይቤልዎ ፡ ንሕ ነሰ ፡ ሰማዕነ ፡ ከመ ፡ ጻድቅ ፡ አንተ ፡ ወአራዊተ ፡ ገዳምኒ ፡ ትቀኒ ። ወይቤ ሎሙ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አንሰ ፡ ኢኮንኩ ፡ ጻድቀ ፡ ዘፈቀድክሙ ፡ ግበሩ ፡ ላዕሌየ = ወይቤልዎ ፡ ንሕነሰ ፡ ኢሐደግናከ ፡ ለእመ ፡ ኢያርአይከነ ፡ ትእምርተ ። ወይቤ ሎሙ ፡ ኅድጉኒ ፡ እጸሊ ፡ ኅበ ፡ አምላኪየ ፡ ወተመይጠ ፡ መንገለ ፡ ምሥራቅ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ እግዚእየ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ እስመ ፡ ኢኮነ ፡ በትዕቢት ፡ ዘአኅስስ ፡ እምኔከ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ነገረ ፡ አላ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያእምሩከ ፡ እሉ ፡ አብዳን ፡ ከመ ፡ ቅሩብ ፡ አንተ ፡ ለእለ ፡ ይጹውዑከክከ ፡ በጽድቅ ። አኮ ፡ ዘእፈርህ ፡ ቅትለተ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ አላ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢይበሉከ ፡ ድኩመ ፡ ኀይል ፡ አንተ ።= አኮ ፡ ሊተ ፡ እግዚኦ ፡ ለስመ ፡ ዚአከ ፡ ሀብ ፡ ስብሐተ ። ወዘንተ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ መጽ አት ፡ ደመና ፡ ብርህት ፡ ወተጽዕነ ፡ ዲቤሃ ፡ መሠጠቶ ፡ እምኔሆሙ ፡ እንዘ ፡
151 || It happened one day that two brigands came to this saint while he was in his desert and said to him: 'If you are a man of God, make us see a miracle; otherwise we will kill you.' The saint said to them: 'I confess that I am a servant of God, but my actions (are) full of acts of sin and it is not possible for me to make a miracle seen, for it is the work of the just.' [15] They said to him: 'As for us, we have heard that you were just and that you subjected the animals of the desert.' The saint said to them: 'I am not just, do to me what you want.' They said to him: 'We will not leave you if you do not make us see a miracle.' He said to them: 'Let me pray to my God.' And he turned toward the east and said: 'My Lord Jesus Christ, certainly it is not out of pride that I ask you for this thing, but so that these senseless ones may know about you that you are near to those who invoke you in justice; not that I fear that these (will) kill me, but so that they do not say of you that you are of weak power; not to me, Lord, to your name, give the glory (Ps 113:9 LXX).' While he was saying this, a luminous cloud arrived; he ascended upon it (and it) carried him far from them, they seeing him with their eyes, took him far away, and they did not know where it had taken him. May his blessing come to us, amen.
ይሬእይዎ" ፡ በአዕይንቲሆሙ ፡ ወአስተራሐቀቶ”' ፡ ወኢያእመሩ ፡ ኀበ ፡ አብጽ ሐቶ ። በረከተ ፡ ዚአሁ" ፡ ይብጽሐነ ፡ አሜን ። ወካዕበ ፡ ስምዑ ፡ ንንግርክሙ” ፡ ዘገብረ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ማር ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡ ወተሰምዐ ፡ ዜና ፡ ጊሩቱ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ዓለም ። ወእምድኅረ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ መጽኡ ፡ አረሚ ፡ እለ ፡ ይብልዎሙ ፡ ሐራ ፡ ጸሊማን ፡ እለ ፡ ኢይትፈለጡ ፡ እምአራዊት ፡ ፍጹመ ፡ በምንተኒ ፡ በኩሉ ፡ ግዕዞሙ ። ወመጽኡ ፡ እሉ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ ነሚኦሙ ፡ ውሉዶሙ ፡ ሕሙማነ ፡ ወይቤልዎ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ አምጻእነ ፡ ኀቤከ ፡ ውሉደነ ፡ ድውያን ፡ ከመ ፡ ትውርቅ ፡ ምራቀከ ፡ ላዕሌሆሙ ፡ ነአምን ፡ ከመ ፡ በምራቅከ ፡ የኀይዉ ፡ እሉ ፡ ውሉድነ”" = ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ብእሴ ፡ እግዚ አብሔር ፡ ይኩንክሙ ፡ በከመ ፡ ሃይማኖትክሙ ። ወወረቀ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ውሉዶሙ ፡ ወቀብኦሙ ፡ ሶቤሃ ፡ ወመጠዎሙ ፡ ለአበዊሆሙ ፡ ወሐይዉ ፡ እምደዌሆሙ ፡ በጸጋ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወስእለቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡
152 Listen again, let me tell you what our father mär Sämu’el did—and the fame of his virtue was heard in all the universe. After that came pagans who are called “black soldiers” who do not separate from animals absolutely in anything, in all their way of being. These came to our father, having taken their suffering children, and said to him: 'We have brought you our sick children so that you might spit your saliva upon them: we believe that, by your saliva, these children of ours will be healed.' The man of God said to them: 'May it be for you according to your faith.' He then spat upon their children, anointed them (with saliva), and returned them to their parents: they were healed of their illness by the grace of the Holy Spirit. May the prayer, the intercession, and the blessing of this saint save us

from the aggression of our enemy who wants to destroy us, and may he pay our debt by his pain so that, on the day of retribution, we may stand with him on the right, amen.
ወካዕበ ፡ ስምዑ ፡ ዘኮነ ፡ እምዝንቱ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አባ ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡ መጽአ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ 8እጐ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ሀበኒ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ኀጢአትየ ፡ ንስሐ ። ወሶቤሃ ፡ አግኀሦ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ለውእቱ ፡ እጐ ፡ እንተ ፡ ባሕቲቱ ፡ ኀበ ፡ አልቦ ፡ ዘይሬ እዮ = ወፈትሐ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ እምሐቋሁ ፡ ሐብለ ፡ በዘቦቱ ፡ ይዘብጥ ፡ ወይቀስፍ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ወቀልዐ ፡ ልብሶ ፡ እስከ ፡ ብረኪሁ ፡ ውሕዘ ፡ ደም ፡ ብዙኅ ። ወሶበ ፡ ርእየ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ እጐ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይውሕዝ ፡ ደም ፡ እምሥጋሁ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ዘበጠ ፡ አቡነ ፡ አንከረ ፡ ወተደመ ።= ወይቤሎ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ለውእቱ ፡ እጐ ፡ ከመዝኬ ፡ ግበር ፡ ኦወልድየ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትድኀን ፡ እመከራ”" ፡ ጸላኢ ፡ ዘመጽአ ፡ ላዕሌከ ፡ ወአሰረ ፡ ሐብለ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሐጋቃሁ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢያእምሮ ፡ ሰብእ ። ወኩሉ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ዘይመጽእ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ እመሂ ፡ ካህን ፡ አው ፡ ዲያቆን ፡ አው ፡ መነ ኮስ ፡ አው ፡ ሕዝባዊ ፡ ወይከሥት ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ኀጢአቶ ፡ እመሂ ፡ ንኡስ ፡ አው ፡ ዐቢይ ፡ ለኩሉ ፡ ለለ8 መጠነ ፡ ወሀቦሙ ፡ ይነሥእ ፡ ለርእሱ ፡ ከማሁ ፡ እመሂ ፡ ወሀቦ ፡ ጥብጣቤ ፡ አው ፡ አርምሞ ፡ አው ፡ ጸመ ፡ ሌሊት ፡ አው ፡ በኩሉ ፡ ዘኮነ ፡ ዘከመ ፡ ወሀቦ ፡ ይትቄበል ፡ ውእቱኒ ፡ ወያጻምዋ ፡ ለነፍሱ ፡ እስከ ፡ ይትፌጸም ፡ ዕድሜ ፡ ንስሐሁ ፍ ሰሚዖ ፡ ቃለ ፡ እግዚኡ ፡ ዘይቤ ፡ ብፁዕ”” ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ዘይሜጦ ፡ ነፍሶ ፡ ቤዛ ፡ ቢፁ ። ወበእንተዝ ፡ ቃል ፡ ተሰቅለ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ መዋዕለ ፡ ሕይወቱ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይኬቤንን ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ቤዛ ፡ ቢጹ ።= ወዓዲ ፡ አም ጽአ ፡ ካልኣነ”" ፡ ትሕርምታተ ፡ ለሥጋሁ ። ወሶበ ፡ ይሁብዎ ፡ ሲሳዮ ፡ ይነ ሥእ ፡ ከመ ፡ ዘይበልዕ ፡ ወየሐውር ፡ በጽምሚት ፡ ወይወግር ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዐዘ ቅት ፡ ወጽዋዕኒ ፡ ይክዑ ፡ መጠነ ፡ ፈቀደ ፡ ወይፌጽም ፡ ጓአንዘ ፡ ኢይ ጥዕም ፡ ምንተኒ ፡ ወአልቦ ፡ ዘያአምሮ ።= ወላዕለ ፡ ዝኒ ፡ ወሰከ ፡ ዓዲ ፡ ካልአነ ፡ ቅሥፈታተ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ሥጋሁ ። ወያመጽእ ፡ ማየ ፡ በኮራ ፡ ወይሰ ቅል ፡ መልዕልተ ፡ ርእሱ ፡ ኀበ ፡ እንተ ፡ ይቀውም ፡ ወይሰሞሮ ፡ ንስቲተ ፡ ለውእቱ ፡ ኮራ ፡ ወይሰቅያ ፡ ለሥጋሁ ፡ በነጠብጣበ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ማይ ፡ ቄሪር ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ኑኃ ፡ ሌሊት ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢይምጽኦ ፡ ንዋም ። ከመዝ ፡ አስመሮ ፡ ለአ
153 Listen again to what happened through this saint abbä Sämu’el: a brother came to him and said to him: 'Give me a penance because of my sin.' Immediately the saint took this brother aside, alone, where no one would see him. The saint undid from his loins the rope with which he struck and flagellated his body and lowered his garment to his knees; abundant blood flowed. When the brother saw the blood flowing from the body of our father when (the latter) struck, he marveled and was stupefied. The saint said to the brother: 'Do thus, my son, to save yourself from the temptation of the Enemy that has come upon you.' And he tied the rope to his loins so that people would not know. Every person who came to him—whether priest, or deacon, or monk, or layman—and revealed his sin—whether small or large—as much as he had given to each one he took for himself likewise. Whether he gave a flagellation, or (a penalty of) silence, or a night fast, or whatever it was, just as he had given (it) to him, he himself took (it) and mortified himself until the time of his penance was accomplished, having heard the word of his Lord who says: “Happy is the man who gives his life as a ransom for his neighbor” (Mt 20:28//). Because of this word, he crucified himself all the days of his life by punishing his body as a ransom for his neighbor. He also inflicted other macerations on his body: when they gave him his food, he took (it) as if he were going to eat, went in secret, and threw (it) into a well; as for the cup (of water), he spilled it as he wanted, and he spent forty (days) without tasting anything and no one knew it. Besides that, he also added other mortifications against his body: he brought water in a jar and suspended (it) above his head, where he stood; he made a small hole in the jar and it watered his body with the drops of cold water, during the whole duration of the night, so that sleep would not come upon him. Thus he pleased his God by a beautiful (ascetic) combat. May his prayer and his blessing be with us, in the centuries of centuries, amen.

154 One day, all the disciples of our father Sämu’el fell ill and had (no one) who served them. The old man took a large waterskin and went to the river to bring back water, there was a large slope there. Having drawn water, while he was taking out the waterskin, he could not lift it; he stretched his hands toward God and said: 'Lord, make your miracle seen today.' Immediately, an angel, one of the seraphim, came, took the waterskin for him, and returned

carrying (it), our father going behind him; having returned, the angel emptied the water for our father into a jar, handed him the waterskin, and ascended into the heavens. 155 One day, our father went to a monastery of monks to visit them. While he was there, envoys of those who (were) in the monastery of his master abbä Adhani came to him saying: 'A thief has stolen all the goods of the convent of your (spiritual) father and has left nothing of the sacred vessels; he entered, having broken a window of the church. Now, pray to God and he will hear you.' They spoke to him while he was standing on the threshold of the church in order to enter inside to receive the eucharist at the moment of the ninth hour. When they spoke to him thus, he was greatly afflicted and said: 'Lord, may your eyes look upon all the earth, do not let the tomb of your servant abbä Adhani, the man of God, be plundered; do not hide, Lord, this brigand, whether he has gone far to the east, to the west, to the north, or to the south. If you do not bring me this brigand with the property of my father and if you do not make him stand before me today, you will have rejected me, Lord, from before your face.' After the end of the mass, that day, when he had spoken while leaving the church, a monk came to him, gripping the thief, and made (the latter) stand before our father while he was in the courtyard of the church. He said to him: 'Abbä, behold, I have brought you this brigand who stole the property of your father; I found him on the way, having tightened (the vessels) in a bag. Now, take (him), do what you want.' When the bag was opened, all the vessels of the church were found intact; our father blessed the monk who had brought (the brigand) to him. After that, the brigand was taken with the vessels of the church and brought to Dabra Bankwal; when the monks saw him, they marveled and glorified God, especially when they were told everything that our father had done. May his prayer, his blessing, and the gift of his help be with us, amen. Before dying, Sämu’el recounts his celestial vision

156 Listen again, my fathers and my brothers, who (are) gathered in this holy church, who have come to make the commemoration of this saint (and) blessed Sämu’el. When the moment of his departure from this world was near, one day, he called his disciples and said to them: 'My children, listen to me, let me tell you what is profitable to your souls: while I was standing and praying in my dwelling, the holy archangel Michael appeared to me and said to me: “Peace to you, elect of God! ወኣርእይከ ፡ ወኣለቡከ ፡ መካነ ፡ ዘድልው ፡ ለከ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትትፈሣሕ ፡ ወትትሐ ሠይ ፡ ውስቴቱ ፡ እምድኅረ ፡ ንግደትከ ፡ እምዝንቱ ፡ 'ዓለመ ፡ ሕይወት"" ፡ ሐላፊ ። ወአንሰ ፡ ደነንኩ ፡ ርእስየ ፡ ወሰገድኩ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ እንዘ ፡ እብል ፡ አነ ፡ መኑ ፡ አነ ፡ እስከ ፡ ያስተርኢ ፡ ሊተ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ መላእክት ፡ አቢይ ፡ ሚካ ኤል = ወእምዝ ፡ ወሰደኒ ፡ ወነሥአኒ ፡ ወአዕረገኒ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሰማያት ። ወአብ ጽሐኒ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዐቢይ ፡ ሀገር ፡ ዘሕንጽት ፡ አዕማዲሃ"" ፡ በወርቅ ፡ ወበያክ ንት ።= ወአውደ ፡ መርኅባ ፡ ስርግው ፡ በዕንቄ ፡ ጳዝዮን ። ወዐቢይ ፡ አረፍታ ፡ ወነዋኅ ፡ ጥቅማ ። ወይእቲ ፡ ሀገር ፡ ኢትፈቅድ ፡ ፀሐየ ፡ ወወርኀ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያብርሁ ፡ ላቲ ፡ ወትጸድል“' ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ ምክዕቢተ ፡ ፻፻እምብርሃነ ፡ ፀሐይ ።= ወአርአየኒ ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ጽርሐ ፡ ዐቢየ ፡ ዘሕኑጽ ፡ በአምሳለ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ወግብረታሰ ፡ ኢይትከሀል ፡ ልሳነ ፡ እጓለ ፡ እመሕያው ፡ ይፈክራ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ሥነ ፡ ራእያ ፡ ሐዋዝ ፡ ሕንጸታ ፡ ወአዳም ፡ ትርሲታ ፡ ወመራኅብቲሃኒ ፡ ጽፍ ጹፍ ፡ በወርቅ ፡ ወበዕንቀኑ ። ወእምአእባኒሃ ፡ ይወጽእ ፡ ብርሃን ፡ ዘይፈደፍድ ፡ እምቀዳሚ ፡ ምክዕቢተ ፡ እምአእላፍ ፡ ፀሐይ ። ወርኢኩ ፡ ውስቴቱ ፡ እንተ ፡
(is) prepared for you so that you may rejoice and exult there after your departure from this world of transitory life.” I lowered my head and prostrated myself before God, saying: 'Who am I that the great archangel Michael should appear to me?' After that, he took me, lifted me up, and made me ascend into the heavens; he introduced me into a great city whose columns (were) built of gold and hyacinth, the circumference of its squares (was) adorned with topaz, its wall (was) great and its rampart high. And this city did not need the sun nor the moon to light it (Rev 21:12, 23), and it shone ten thousand times more than the light of the sun. He showed me also a great palace that (was) built in the likeness of a church. As for its workmanship, it is not possible for the tongue of men to expose it because of the beauty of its aspect; pleasant (was) its construction and pleasing its adornment; its streets (were) paved with gold and precious stones. From its stones came a light that surpassed the first, (stronger) than ten thousand suns. I saw on the (wall), written inside, the names of men, on all its (surface) from top to bottom.
አፍአሃ ፡ ጽሑፍ ፡ አስማተ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ኩለንታሁ ፡ እምላዕሉ ፡ እስከ ፡ ታሕቱ ። ወሶበ ፡ አርኀወ ፡ ኃዋኅዊሃ ፡ ደንገፅኩ ፡ ወወደቁ ፡ በገጽየ ፡ ወስእ ንኩ ፡ ንጻሬሃ ። ወይቤለኒ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ መልአክ ፡ ዘይመርሐኒ ፡ ተንሥእ ፡ ወኢት ፍራህ ፡ እስመ ፡ አምጽአከ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ከመ ፡ ትነጽራ ። ወሶቤሃ ፡ ተንሣ እኩ ፡ ወወሰደኒ ፡ መንገለ ፡ ኀዋኅዊሃ ፡ ወአቀመኒ ፡ ህየ ፡ ወይቤለኒ ፡ ስግድ ፡ ወሰገድኩ ፡ በከመ ፡ አዘዘኒ ። ወእምዝ ፡ አብአኒ'"" ፡ ውስተ ፡ ውሳጤ ፡ ጽርኅ ወሀሎ ፡ ህየ ፡ ደብተራ ፡ ዘእሳት ፡ ወርኢኩ ፡ አዕማዲሃ ፡ ዘብርሃን ፡ በአምሳለ ፡ መጽሄት ፡ ዘያስተርኢ ፡ ገጸ ፡ ሰብእ ። ወነጸርኩ ፡ ለፌ ፡ ወ"ለፌ ፡ ወርኢኩ ፡ በህየ ፡ መልክዐ ፡ ገጸ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ሥዑላን ፡ በኩለሄ ፍ ወለለ ፳-መልክዕ ፡ ጽሑፍ ፡ ስሙ ፡ መልዕልተ ፡ ርእሱ ፍ ወስዕሎሙሰ ፡ ግሉፍ ፡ በእሳት ፡ ወውስቴቱ ፡ መልክዐ ፡ ገጾሙ ፡ ወጽሕፈተ ፡ አስማቲሆሙ ፡ በቀለመ ፡ ብርሃን"”" ፡ ጥብ ጡብ ። ወርኢኩ ፡ ካዕበ ፡ መናብርተ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ዘዘዚአሆሙ ፡ ኅብሮሙ ፡ ነዋ ኅት ፡ ጥቀ ፡ በፕልቄ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ መልክዐ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ወአክሊለ ፡ ብርሃን ፡ ንቡር ፡ በዲበ ፡ እሉ ፡ መናብርት ፡ ግሉፋት ፡ በአዕናቀኑ ። ወርኢኩ ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ማእከለ ፡ እሉ ፡ መናብርት ፡ መንገለ ፡ ምሥራቅ ፡ መንበረ ፡ ዐቢየ ፡ ወልዑለ ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ ወቦቱ ፡ ፲ ወ፪ መዐርገ ፡ ወየአውዶ ፡ ብርሃን ፡ ዐቢይ ፡ ወዲቤሁ ፡ ንቡር ፡ ፻ አክሊል ፡ ወውስቴቱ ፡ ጌራ ፡ መንግሥት ፡ ርሱያት ፡ በአዕናቀኑ ። ወርኅበታሰ ፡ ለይእቲ ፡ መንበር ፡ ይመስለኒ ፡ እምአግመረት ፡ ፪ ታዕካ ፡ ነገሥት ፡
157 “When he opened the doors (of the city), I trembled, fell on my face, and could not look at it. The angel who was leading me said to me: ‘Rise and do not be afraid, for God has made you come so that you might look at it.’ Immediately I rose and he led me toward its doors, made me stand there, and said to me: ‘Prostrate yourself.’ I prostrated myself as he had ordered me; after that, he made me enter into the interior of the palace. There was there a tent of gold and I saw its columns of light in the likeness of a mirror that makes the face of men appear. I looked from side to side and I saw there the portrait of faces of man entirely painted and, for each portrait, the name (was) written above the head. Their paintings (were) engraved with fire and, inside, (there was) the portrait of their faces and the inscription of their names well executed with a reed of light. I saw also many thrones of different colors, very high; they (were) in (the same) number as the portraits of man and crowns of light (were) placed on these thrones, encrusted with precious stones. I saw also in the midst of these thrones, toward the east, a throne very great and very high, it had twelve steps and a great light surrounded it; three crowns (were) placed on it and there (was) a royal diadem, (the whole) adorned with precious stones. The size of this throne seemed to me (to be able) to contain two royal camps with their armies.
ምስለ ፡ ሠራዊቶሙ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ተስእልክዎ ፡ ለውእቱ ፡ መልአክ ፡ ዘይመርኀኒ ፡ ወእቤሎ ፡ እግዚእየ ፡ እፈቅድ ፡ ከመ ፡ ታጠይቀኒ ፡ ምንትኑ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ መናብርት ፡ ትኩ ላን ፡ በዝየ ፡ 'ወአክሊለ ፡ ብርሃን'"'' ፡ መልዕልቴሆሙ ፡ ንቡር ፡ ወምንትኑ ፡ ካዕበ ፡ እሉ ፡ መልክዐ ፡ ገጸ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ሥዑላን ፡ በኩልሄ ፡ ወለለ መልዕልተ ፡
158 “After that, I questioned the angel who was guiding me and said to him: ‘My lord, I want you to teach me what (are) these thrones placed here and the crowns of light placed on them, what (are) also these portraits of faces of man entirely painted, above the head of each

ርእሱ ፡ ጽሕፈተ ፡ ቦቱ ወአውሥአኒ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ መልአክ ፡ ወይቤለኒ ፡ ኦአባ ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡ እሉ ፡ መናብርት ፡ ወዝኩሉ ፡ አክሊላት ፡ ዘትሬእዮሙ ፡ አለ ፡ ንቡራት ፡ መልዕልቴሆሙ ፡ ዘአስተዳለወ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ለሰብእ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ ለእለ ፡ ይደልዎሙ ። ወእሉ ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ዘትሬእዮሙ ሥዑላን ፡ መልክዐ ፡ ገጾሙ ፡ ዓሥራተ ፡ ዚአከ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ እለ ፡ ይገብሩ ፡ ተዝካረከ ፡ ወእለ ፡ ይትአ መኑ ፡ በጸሎትከ ፡ ወእለ ፡ የሐንጹ ፡ መርጡለከ ፡ ወእለ ፡ ይጽሕፉ ፡ መጽሐፈ ፡ ገድልከ ፡ ዝየ ፡ ርስቶሙ ፡ ምስሌከ ። ወሶበ ፡ ትወፅእ ፡ አሐቲ ፡ ነፍስ ፡ እም ሥጋሃ ፡ መጽአ ፡ ዝየ ፡ በፍሥሐ ። ወይኤዝዝ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ለመላእክቲሁ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ ከመ ፡ ያጥምቅዋ ፡ በማየ ፡ ሕይወት ፡ ወያለብስዋ ፡ ሞጣኅተ ፡ ብር ሃን ፡ ወያነብርዋ ፡ ዲበ ፡ አሐቲ ፡ መንበር ፡ እምእሉ ፡ መናብርት ፡ ወይከል ልዋ ፡ በአክሊል ፡ ዘኢይማስን ። ወትትፈሣሕ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ኩሎሙ"! ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡
of which there is an inscription.’ The angel answered me and said to me: ‘Abbä Sämu’el, these thrones and all these crowns that you see, which (are) placed on them, (are) what God has prepared for the holy men who will be worthy of them. As for these painted portraits of faces that you also see, it is your tithe: those who will make your commemoration, those who will have faith in your prayer, those who will build your sanctuary, and those who will write the book of your life—here (will be) their inheritance with you. When a soul leaves its body, it will come here in joy and God will order his holy angels to baptize it with the water of life, they will clothe it with a mantle of light, install it on a throne among these thrones, and crown it with a crown that does not wither; it will rejoice with all the saints and will live the eternal life.”
ወተሐዩ ፡ ሕይወተ ፡ ዘለዓለም ። ወይቤለኒ ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ መልአክ ፡ ዘይመርሐኒ ፡ ርኢከኑ ፡ ማእ ከለ ፡ እሉ ፡ መናብርት ፡ እንተ ፡ መንገለ ፡ ምሥራቅ ፡ መንበረ ፡ ዐቢይ ፡ 'ወልዑል ፡ ፈድፋደ'"'' ፡ ወዲቤሁ ፡ ፻ አክሊል ፡ ንቡራት ፡ ወእቤሎ ፡ እወ ፡ እግዚኦ ። ወይቤለኒ ፡ ዚአከ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወዝንቱ ፡ ፻ አክሊል ፡ ዘትሬእዮሙ ፡ መልዕልተ ፡ መንበር ፡ ዘአስተዳለወ ፡ ለከ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ኦአባ ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡ 8በእንተ ፡ ድንግልናከ ፡ ወካልኡ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ጻማ''' ፡ ገድልከ ፡ ዘተጋደልከ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ፍቅረ ፡ አምላክከ ፡ ወሣልስ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ዘመነንከ ፡ ዓለመ"'' ፡ ወኩሎ ፡ ዘውስቴቱ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ስሙ ፡ ቅዱስ ። ወአንሰ ፡ እቤሎ ፡ አኮ ፡ ድልወት ፡ ሊተ ፡ ለዘእመስል ፡ ፃእፃአ ፡ እንዘ ፡ አነ ፡ መሬት ፡ ወሐመድ ፡ እፎኑ ፡ ይደ ልወኒ ፡ ዘከመዝ ፡ ክቡራት ። ወይቤለኒ ፡ መልአክ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ትሕት ናከ ፡ አዕበየከ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ። ወእቤሎ ፡ አይቴኑ ፡ ወሰና ፡ ለዛቲ ፡ ጽርሕ ወይቤለኒ ፡ እምአመ ፡ አርኃውኩ ፡ ለከ ፡ አናቅጺሃ ፡ ወኬድከ ፡ እስከ ፡ ይእዜ ፡ ወርኢከሃ ፡ ስርጉት ፡ በወርቅ ፡ ወበዕንቀኑ ፡ ወበጳዝዮን ፡ ዝየ ፡ ማእከላ ፡ ወእ ምዝ ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ኣርእየከ ፡ ፍጻሜሃ ። ወሶቤሃ ፡ ወሰደኒ ፡ ወአብጽሐኒ ፡ እስከ ፡ ፍጻሜሃ ።= ወሊተሰ ፡ በሕሊናየ ፡ ይመስለኒ ፡ እምኮነ ፡ ርኅበታ ፡ ለይእቲ ፡ ቤት ፡ መጠነ ፡ ፲ ወ፪ምሕዋረ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ተአክል ፡ እንዘ ፡ ኣንእሳ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ቅዱስ ። ወአልቦ ፡ ዘርኢኩ ፡ በህየ ፡ ማኅቶተ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያብርሁ ፡ ሊተ ፡ አላ ፡ አዕማ ዲሃ ፡ ብርሃን ፡ ወመሳክዊሃ ፡ ብርሃን ፡ ወጠፈራ ፡ ብርሃን ፡ ወእምኩሉ ፡ ግብ ረታ ፡ ይወጽእ ፡ ብርሃን ። ወህየ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ ምሉእ ፡ መፀዛ ፡ ዕፍረት ፡ ወአልቦ ፡
159 "The angel who guided me said to me again: ‘Have you seen in the midst of these thrones, toward the east, the great and very high throne and the three crowns placed upon it?’ I said to him: ‘Yes, my lord.’ He said to me: ‘It is yours, and these three crowns that you see above the throne (are) what God has prepared for you, Abbä Sämu'ël: one for your virginity, another for the trials of your (ascetic) combat that you have waged for the love of your God, the third for having despised the world and all that (is) in it for his holy name.’ I said to him: ‘I am not worthy of them, I who am like one untimely born (1 Cor 15:8); while I am dust and ashes, how could such honors come to me?’ The angel said to me: ‘Because of this humility of yours, God has magnified you.’ I said to him: ‘What (is) the limit of this palace?’ He said to me: ‘Since I opened its doors to you and you trod (it) until now, and you saw it decorated with gold, precious stones, and topazes, here (is) its middle. After this, I will make you see its end as well.’ Immediately, he led me and brought me to its end. To me, in my thought, it seemed that the size of this house was twelve days' journey, making it small — said the saint. I did not see lamps there that would light me, but its columns (were) light, its windows light, its ceiling light, and from its whole structure, light came forth. There, there was a profusion of the scent of balm — nothing compares to its odor; it ravished hearts by its very great sweetness."
ዘይትማሰል ፡ ጹናሁ('' ፡ ወይሰልብ ፡ አልባበ ፡ እምብዝኃ ፡ ጣዕሙ ፡ ፈድፋደ ። ወእምድኅረ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ወሰደኒ ፡ መንገለ ፡ ደብር ፡ ልዕልት ፡ ወአርአየኒ ፡ እንተ ፡ መትሕቴሃ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ገነታተ ፡ ዘአልቦ ፡ ምስፈርተ ፡ ለርኅቡ ፡ ወስፉሕ ፡ ጥቀ ፡ ወኢርኢኩ ፡ ተፍጻሜቶ ፡ ወውስቴቱ"'' ፡ ምሉእ ፡ እምኩሉ ፡ አትክልት ፡ ዘዘዚአሁ ፡ ፍሬሁ ፡ ወዘዘዚአሁ ፡ ጽጌሁ''' ፡ ወዘዘዚአሁ ፡ ጣዕመ ፡ መዓዛሁ ። ወአቀ-ጽጽሊሆሙ ፡ ኢየሐብሩ ፡ ፳ምስለ ፡ ካልኡ ፡ ወጣዕመ ፡ መዓዛሆሙ ፡
160 "After this, he led me to a high mountain and made me see below it a whole garden without limit to its size and very extensive; I did not see its end. It was filled with all plants with diverse fruits, diverse flowers, and diverse sweetnesses of perfume; their foliage did not mingle with one another and the sweetness of their perfume sated the belly before one


[p. 134] {#po.53.p134}
ያጸግብ ፡ ከርሠ ፡ እምቅድመ ፡ ይብልዕዎ ። ወእቤሎ ፡ ለውእቱ ፡ መልአክ ፡ ዘይመርኀኒ ፡ እግዚእየ ፡ ዘመኑ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ገነት ፡ ወይቤለኒ ፡ ለከ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ርስትከ ። ቀዳሚ ፡ እንተ ፡ ርኢካ ፡ መንግሥተ ፡ ሰማያት ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ምዕራፍከ ። ወዛቲኒ ፡ ገነት ፡ ይእቲ ፡ መካነ ፡ ተድላከ ። እስመ ፡ ለኩሎሙ ፡ ጻድቃን ፡ እለ ፡ ከማከ ፡ ይትወሀቦሙ ፡ ከመዝ ፡ መንግሥተ ፡ ሰማያት ፡ ለዕረ ፍት ፡ ወገነተ ፡ ተድላ ፡ ለትፍሥሕት ። ወይቤ ፡ አቡነ ፡ እምፈተውኩ ፡ አንሰ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢይትመየጥ ፡ ወኢይግባእ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ዘቀዳሚ ፡ ንብረትየ ። ወይቤለኒ ፡ ኢትፍራህ ፡ ናሁ ፡ ፈጸምከ ፡ ጻማ ፡ ተጋድሎትከ ፡ ወትገብእ ፡ ዝየ ፡ ፍጡነ ፡ እስመ ፡ ቀርበ ፡ መዋዕሊከ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትፍልስ ፡ እምውእቱ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ዘምሉእ ፡ ብከይ ፡ ወሐዘን ፡ ወትመጽእ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዛቲ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ፍሥሐሃ ፡ ዘኢየሐልቅ ፡
ate of them. I said to the angel who guided me: ‘My lord, to whom does this garden belong?’ He said to me: ‘To you, it is your inheritance. The kingdom of heaven, which you saw first, is your place of rest; as for this garden, it is the place of your delights, for thus is it given to all the righteous who (are) like you: the kingdom of heaven for rest, the garden of delights for joy.’” Our father said: “I would like, for my part, not to return and come back to my former condition.” “He said to me, (continued Sämu’el:) ‘Do not be afraid, behold you have accomplished the trials of your (ascetic) combat and you will return here quickly, for it is near, your day (fixed) for you to leave this world which (is) filled with tears and affliction; you will come to this place whose joy does not perish in the ages of ages.’”
ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ወእምድኅረ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ወሰደኒ ፡ ውስተ ፡ አርያም ፡ መንበሩ ፡ ለል ዑል ፡ ወአብጽሐኒ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ይሰግዱ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ፍጥረት ። በህየ ፡ ርኢክዎ ፡ ለወ ልድ ፡ ፍቁር ፡ በየማነ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ ነቢሮ ፡ ዲበ ፡ መንበረ ፡ ስብሐቲሁ ። ወበ ህየ ፡ ርኢክዎሙ ፡ ለ፳ወሀ፪ካህናተ ፡ ሰማይ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ንቡራን ፡ ዲበ ፡ መናብ ርት ። ህየ ፡ ሀለዉ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ ማኅበረ ፡ ቅዱሳን ። ወበህየ ፡ ርኢክዋ ፡ ለእግዝ እትነ ፡ ማርያም ፡ በየማነ ፡ ወልዳ ፡ ፍቁር ፡ ወሐራ ፡ ሰማይ ፡ ይሴብሑ ፡ ቅድሜሃ ። ወበህየ ፡ ርኢክዎሙ ፡ ለአበው ፡ ቀደምት ፡ አብርሃም ፡ ይስሐቅ ፡ ወያዕቆብ ። ወበህየ ፡ ርኢክዎሙ ፡ ለነቢያት ፡ በበነገዶሙ ፡ 'ወለሐዋርያት ፡ በበነገዶሙ!"' ፡ ሰማዕት ፡ በበማኅበሮሙ ፡ መላእክት ፡ በበጾታሆሙ ። አእላፈ ፡ አእላፋት ፡ ወትእልፊተ ፡ አእላፋት ፡ ይዜምሩ ፡ ቅድሜሁ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብሉ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ጸባኦት ፡ ፍጹም ፡ ምሉአ ፡ ሰማ ያት ፡ ወምድር ፡ ቅድሳተ ፡ ስብሐቲከ ። ወይቤለኒ ፡ ስግድ ፡ ወሰገድኩ ፡ ቅድመ ፡ ገጹ ፡ ለአምላክ ፡ ሕያው ። ወተነበበኒ ፡ ወልድ ፡ ፍቁር ፡ ወይቤለኒ ፡ ሰላም ፡ ለከ ፡ ኦፍቁርየ ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡ ተፈሣኅ ፡ ኦገብር ፡ ጎር ፡ ዘበሕዳጥ""” ፡ ምእመን ፡ ኮንከ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ብዙኅ ፡ እሰይመከ ። 'እስመ ፡ አንተ'”" ፡ ጻመውከ ፡ ወተጋደልከ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ በበይነ ፡ አፍቅሮትከ ፡ ሊተ ፡ ወተመሰልከ ፡ ኪያየ ፡ በጸዊረ ፡ ሕማማተ ፡ መስቀልየ ። ወአነ ፡ ይእዜ ፡ እጹግወከ ፡ ዐስበ ፡ ጻማከ ፡ ጌራተ ፡ መንግሥትየ ። ወናሁ ፡ አስተዳሎኩ ፡ መንበረከ ፡ ወአክሊለከ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ፳-ወ፪ካህናተ ፡ ሰማይ ፡ ዕሩየ ፡ ወትነብር ፡ ደርገ ፡ ምስሌሆሙ ፍ ወክፍልከኒ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ፲ ወ፪ ሐዋርያት ፡ ቅዱሳን ። ኀበ ፡ ተቀብረ ፡ አዕጽምቲከ ፡ ወኀበ ፡ ተነበ ፡ መጽሐፈ ፡ ገድልከ ፡ ወኀበ ፡ ተገብረ ፡ ተዝካርከ ፡ ህየ ፡ ይኩን ፡ መድኃኒት ፡ ወሣህል ፡ ወምሕረት ። ወኩሉ ፡ ዘይትአመን ፡ በጸሎትከ ፡ ወዘይገ ብር ፡ ተዝካረከ ፡ ወሀብኩከ ፡ ዓሥራተ ፡ ለከ ። ወለእመኒ ፡ ኀኅጥእ ፡ ዘኮነ ፡ ውፁአ ፡ እምሕግ ፡ እሰሪ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ በእንቲአከ ። ወኩሎ ፡ ድካማተ ፡ ዘተወከ ፍከ ፡ በእንቲአየ ፡ ኢይከውን ፡ መጠና ፡ ለፍሥሓ ፡ ለአሐቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ እንተ ፡ ነሣእከ ፡ ይእዜ ። ወናሁ ፡ ቀርበ ፡ ፍልሰትከ ፡ ወትመጽእ ፡ ዝየ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሕይወት ፡ ዘለዓለም ። ወዘንተ ፡ ነገሮ ፡ አመ ፡ ፲ ወፄ ለወርኃ ፡ ነሐሴ ፡ በበዓላ ፡
161 "After this, he led me into the very high heavens, the throne of the Most High, and brought me to where every creature prostrates itself. There, I saw the beloved Son at the right hand of his Father, seated on the throne of his glory; there, I saw the twenty-four priests of heaven seated on thrones; there, there was the whole assembly of the saints; there, I saw Our Lady Mary at the right hand of her beloved Son, the soldiers of heaven glorifying before her; there, I saw the patriarchs Abraham, Isaac, and Jacob; there, I saw the prophets each according to his family, the apostles each according to his family, the martyrs each according to his assembly, the angels each according to his order. Thousands of thousands and myriads of myriads were chanting before (God) saying: ‘Holy, holy, holy (is) the God of hosts! The heavens and the earth are completely full of the holiness of your glory (Is 6:3).’ (The angel) said to me: ‘Prostrate yourself.’ I prostrated myself before the face of the living God. The beloved Son spoke to me and said: “Greetings to you, my beloved Sämu’el! Rejoice, good servant: you who were with a few faithful, I will set you over many (Mt 24:21), for you have labored and fought much because of your love for me and you have made yourself like me by the fact of bearing the sufferings of my cross; I now, I am going to reward you, in recompense for your labors, with the goods of my kingdom. Behold I have prepared your throne and your crown with the twenty-four priests of heaven, on an equal footing, and you will sit together with them (Rev 4:4 etc.), and your share (will be) with the twelve holy apostles. Where your bones will be buried, where the book of your life will be, where your commemoration will be made, let there be salvation, clemency, and mercy. I will give you as a tithe, for you, whoever has faith in your prayer and makes your commemoration; even if (it is) a sinner who has departed from the Law, I will forgive him for your sake. All the trials that you have accepted for me will not be the equivalent of the joy, which you have received now, of a single day. Behold your departure is near and you will come here into eternal life.” (Our Lord) said this to him on the sixteenth of the month of Nahasë, during the feast of Our Lady

Mary. After this, (Sämu’ël) returned to his dwelling. May his prayer and his blessing be with us, amen. 162 How shall I praise you, blessed one of illustrious family? The account of your virtue (is) prodigious and astonishing, it will not be brought to its end by my tongue, weak in speech. You who are clothed with humility and love without measure or limit; shining lamp of the faith which (is) placed on a lampstand; whose splendor of radiance shines in all the ends of the universe; pearl that was found in the expanse of the desert; basin of pure gold that was given for the glory of the peoples, placed beside the river of the Gospel; rain of mercy which, having poured out from there, spreads into the valley of tears; who received a palace of the construction (of which) the beauty (is) very pleasant, of the wall of flames (of which) the enclosure (is) imposing, of which the pavement (is) the color of embers! I would desire, for my part, where you have walked, to kiss the place of the soles of your feet. Sämu'el, my father, doctor of the Law, doctor of peace! May the drops of your tears and the flood of your purity be for me the redemption of my sins in the world that will come. Where your name has been invoked, where your commemoration has been made, where the book of your life has been read, on the day of your feast, today I walk there with a word of peace. This bride, guard her like the apple of the eye with your imposing radiance, where is the casket of your precious stone, installed in its sanctuary in the middle of the tomb. Having drawn from the sea of divinity, pour upon us downpours of mercy that will fatten the bones. Take us as a tithe of mercy, gift of the imposing King. Having removed the tent of fire, make us enter through the door of the high heavens, where is our high priest Jesus, the Savior of the world, who exists from the beginning with his Father and with his Spirit, so that we may stand in his joy openly, so that we may sing the glory of his reign with a glorification, from now until forever, amen. New praise of Sämu’el

163 Let us return again to the evocation of the praise of the chosen one of God, Sämu'ël our father, that we may tell you his wonders: he (is) a source of blessing whose wealth of the peoples that is accumulated there is not exhausted, for God has blessed him and opened to him the door of blessing with the dew of heaven and with the expanse of the earth (Gen 27:28), according to what he said in the Law at the head of the book: what (has been) reserved for him in order to be upon his head, (as) to each what was fitting for him (Gen 49:28) — thus he says. If you have listened well to the word of the Lord your God and have taken care to put into practice his commandment today, God will place you above the nations of the earth. Blessed will you be in the fields and blessed will you be in the city! Blessed will be the fruit of your womb and blessed will be the wheat of your land, and the stalls of your cows

and the pasture of your sheep and the blessing of your granaries! Blessed will you be when you enter and blessed will you be when you go out! God will send his blessing into your houses and into everything to which you put your hand. God will open for you the treasure of the blessing of heaven to give you rain in its time for the land and he will bless for you every work of your hands. You, you will lend to many nations, but you, you will not borrow. You, you will command many nations, but you, no one will command you. God will place you at the head and not at the tail, you will be on top and you will not be on the bottom (Dt 28:1, 3-6, 8, 12-13).

164 Having been gratified with this blessing from the Most High, (Sämu’ël) began to spread (it) upon the poor and the destitute. God performed through his hands great miracles and numerous healings; indeed, those sick in body, he prayed over them and they recovered their health. Those who had flagellations and blows from the demons, he healed them; those who had fallen into the lust of sin, he brought into purity and made them remain in penance, in lamentation, and in tears. Those who remained in laughter and in games, took pleasure in food, and frolicked in debauchery, he taught them submission to God and to remain in solitude and in isolation. Those who were lazy and hated manual labor, he awakened them to effort and prescribed for them to work with their hands and to have pity on their neighbors who were in distress. The violent and the brutal, he made them abandon rapine and the taking by force of what was not theirs in order, on the contrary, to give of their goods both to strangers and to the afflicted, with a content face and a joyful heart. (Those whose) ears were deaf to listening to the commandments of life and those who were slow to put them into practice in the thickness of their hearts, he awakened them to the path of life and nourished their hearts with the milk of his teaching; from that moment, they were insatiable to listen to the holy Scriptures. Those who had weakened before the temptation of the Enemy and whom calamities and misfortunes had touched, he strengthened their hearts, consoled them in their affliction, delivered them, rejoiced them by his sweet voice, and awakened them to a beautiful (spiritual) combat. Those who were poor, strangers, and destitute, with the lack of food and clothing, and wanted to turn back, he helped them and strengthened them so that they might bear their poverty in praise. He said to them: “My children, let us be a little patient in order to obtain rest for our souls; the Garden of delights (is) open for us, it awaits us so that we may enter it if we have gone through the narrow gate and its constricted path (Mt 7:14). Let us abstain now from entering through the spacious gate and the wide path (Mt 7:13), to frolic in the delights of the goods that will come; now, let us fight with all our will.”
ትመጽእ ፡ ሠናይት ። ወይእዜኒ ፡ ንትጋደል ፡ በኩሉ ፡ ፈቃድነ ። ወለእለ ፡ ኮኑ ፡ ውዱቃነ ፡ በሕማመ ፡ ኃጢአት ፡ ወገቢረ ፡ ዝሙት ፡ ወከብደ ፡ ላዕሌሆሙ ፡ ዐበይተ ፡ ኃጣውእ ፡ ወኢይከሥቱ ፡ ለመምህራኒሆሙ ፡
165 Those who had fallen into the suffering of sin and the practice of lust, upon (whom) had weighed the greatness of sins, (who) did not reveal (them) to their masters and were ashamed to reveal them, when they arrived

before him, he knew them by the mirror of his intelligence and pierced them by the sharpness of his eyes; with a single one of his looks they were liquefied in fear, they stirred and trembled. After this, he took them alone, embraced them, kissed them, and consoled them; he sprinkled them with the sweet water (coming) from the inspiration of his word, tore from the roots of their heart what was corrupt and what was putrid, purified them, and washed them. He himself, our father, was thus a witness and said: “When I arrived in a (certain) place, I restored to life seventy souls whose sin (had been) thus hidden.” After this, he had said to them: “My children, it is better for us to hold ourselves far from such a habit and to turn away from evil so as not to receive punishment in the depths of the darkness that has no communion with the light. It is fitting for us to weep, to lament, and to pour out our tears before God with humility so that we may enter the wedding of the heavenly Bridegroom with our holy penitent fathers.” By the greatness of the grace of Christ that (was) upon him, the fire of the Holy Spirit had begun to burn in their heart; with the burning of this brazier in them toward the thoughts of the Spirit, they abandoned all that (is) of flesh and forgot the works of desire.

166 To those who had anger and fury in the likeness of beasts, insatiability in the likeness of animals, quarrel and dispute, pride of heart, and every sin that opposes the fear of God, to them he taught to abandon that and to remain in abstinence, in the commandments of the holy Gospel, in a humble thought all the time, in hope and in complete faith; he said to them: “Guard yourselves, my children, from vindictiveness, from the spirit of vengeance, and from fury. The foundation, the structure, and the good of perfection (Col 3:14) of all (are) love, humility, and the fear of God; he who has acquired these sees himself alone, lesser than every creature, and just as he is beneath everything, he in truth will be a sacrifice chosen and accepted for God if he walks in the gentleness of the Lord and his humility, according to what (is) written: ‘The sacrifice of God (is) a gentle spirit (Ps 50[51]:19), that is to say, fruits and perfect love. Then (the man) becomes the dwelling of the holy Trinity, the kingdom of heaven (is) open for him, and he (is) worthy of the beatitude (pronounced) by the mouth of the Lord (Mt 5:3).” Those whose faith (was) deficient in relation to the great mystery, he turned their heart toward the knowledge of the right faith and said to them: “My children, the beginning of everything is to guard the faith from within, the fear of God, the humility of heart, and what (makes one) tremble before his word. Let us fight therefore now to inherit the life that does not perish; if we have guarded our faith in righteousness and our thought in sincerity, then

then we will receive the reward that he has promised us by the heavenly calling to sit at table with our first fathers in the kingdom of heaven.” 167 With such (words) he taught them and awakened them all by the light of his holy teaching, according to what Isaiah the prophet says: “Behold I have made you the light of the nations so that you may straighten the earth and give light to those who (are) in darkness” (Is 49:6, 8-9). Our Lord also said: “You, you are the salt of the earth and the light of the world” (Mt 5:13-14). With such (words) he taught them and drew them toward a truthful knowledge: he guided them in the likeness of the star that went before the magi in those days until it had brought them to Our Lord Jesus Christ — glory to him (Mt 2:1-11)! Who is he who heard the sweetness of his word and was not sated? Who is he who saw his face and did not rejoice? The grace of Christ surrounded him and the glory of God, like fire, burned in him; he made the whole universe shine with the lightning of his teaching which surpassed the light of the sun in the middle of the sky. God rendered him like a pure crystal which (is) full of an odorous perfume that exhales fragrances and aromas; not like the vial of Sämu’ël the prophet in which one has gathered all the scent of perfumes, which the arm of man has prepared for the glorious anointing of royalty, but the perfume of this one (is) something that (comes) from on high, which was made from the union with the divinity. When this crystal broke, mercenaries took it, placed it in the vineyard that (is) in Engaddi (Song 1:14), and put it under the cover of the basket of grapes which is the tomb; they could not hide it. He was glorified and highly exalted up to the heaven of heavens; a shoot of the Garden appeared in its midst, it sprouted, grew, and became a tree of life. When it was morning, it added branches and the odor of its perfume spread into all the ends of the universe. Sämu’ël tree of life


[p. 142] {#po.53.p142}
ወወፅአ ፡ ፄና ፡ መዓዛሁ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ አጽናፈ ፡ ዓለም ። ስምዑኬ ፡ ኦአበዊየ ፡ ወአኀዊየ ፡ ፍካሬሃ ፡ ለዛቲ ፡ ዕፀ ፡ ሕይወት ፡ ይቤ ፡ እምቅዱሳን ፡ ርኢኩ ፡ ኦመ ፡ ዐባየ ፡ ዘምልእት ፡ ፍሬ ፡ እንተ ፡ ትበ ጽሕ ፡ እምድር ፡ እስከ ፡ ሰማይ ። ወከደነ ፡ አድባረ" ፡ ጽለሎታ ፡ ወሰፍሐ ፡ አዕፁቂሃ ፡ እስከ ፡ አጽናፈ ፡ ዓለም ፡ 'ምሥራቀ ፡ ወምዕራበ ፡ ሰሜነ'"፤ ፡ ወደ ቡበ ። ወከብደት ፡ ጥቀ ፡ በጸዊረ ፡ ፍሬ ። ወበታሕቴሃ ፡ ያጸሉ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ፍጥ ረት ፍ ወወድቀት ፡ በጊዜሃ ፡ እንዘ ፡ አልቦ ፡ ዘይገዝሞ ፡ እምብገዝኃ ፡ ፍሬ ፡ ዘላዕሌሃ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ጸለይኩ ፡ ኀበ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ከመ ፡ ይክሥት ፡ ሊተ ፡ ግብሮ ፡ ለውእቱ ፡ ኦም ፡ እፎ ፡ ወድቀ ፡ እንዘ ፡ አልቦ ፡ ዘይገዝሞ ። ወመ ጽአ ፡ ቃል ፡ እምሰማይ ፡ ዘይብል ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ኦም ፡ ዘትሬኢ ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ምጽላሎሙ ፡ ለአባግዕ ። ወሶበ ፡ በዝኃ ፡ ፍሬሁ ፡ ወድቀ ፡ ለሊሁ ፡ በፈቃዱ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ይግዝምዎ ፡ ወብዙኃን ፡ አሕዛብ ፡ ይጸሉ ፡ ታሕተ ፡ ጽላ ሎተ ፡ ጸሎቱ ። ወውእቱ ፡ አምሳሊሁ ፡ ለጴጥሮስ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ዘተውህበ ፡ ሎቱ ፡
168 Listen therefore, my fathers and my brothers, to the explanation of this tree of life; one of the holy (monks) says: “I saw a great tree that (was) filled with fruits, which from the earth reached up to heaven. Its shadow covered the mountains and its branches extended to the ends of the universe, to the east and to the west, to the north and to the south; it became very heavy with bearing fruits — under it sheltered every creature — and it fell suddenly, without anyone having cut it down, because of the abundance of the fruits that (were) upon it. After this, I prayed to God that he might reveal to me the matter of this tree, how it had fallen without anyone having cut it down. A voice came from heaven that said: ‘This tree that you see is Sämu’el, the shade of the sheep. When his fruits became numerous, he fell himself, by his will, without anyone having cut it down; many peoples will shelter under the shadow of his prayer. He is in the likeness of Peter because it was given to him the dominion over the spirits


[p. 144] {#po.53.p144}
ስልጣነ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ መናፍስት ፡ ርኩሳን ። ናሁኬ ፡ አለበውናክሙ ፡ ኦሕዝብ ፡ ምእምናን” ፡ አምሳሊሁ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ እስራኤላዊ ፡ ዘአልቦ ፡ ጽልሑተ ። ወጸ ገዎ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ በረከተ ፡ ሰማይ ፡ እምላዕሉ ፡ ወበረከተ ፡ ምድር” ፡ እንተ ፡ ባቲ ፡ ኩሎ ።= ወኮነ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ፍሥሓ ፡ ለዘይሬኢ ፡ ወለዘይሰምዕ ፡ ዘርሑቅ ፡ ወዘቅሩብ ፡ ወተጋብኡ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ እምኩሉ ፡ ዘመደ ፡ እጓለ ፡ እመ ሕያው ፡ እስከ ፡ ኮነት ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ምድረ ፡ በዳ ፡ አህጉረ ፡ ለክርስቶስ ፡ ወምድር ፡ እንተ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ፍሬ ፡ አፍረየት ፡ ህየንተ ፡ ፳88ወቋ ወ፻ ።= ወኮነ ፡ ምኔታት ፡ በአውደ ፡ በዐቱ ። እምጽንፈ ፡ ሲሬ ፡ እስከ ፡ ጽንፈ ፡ ዋሊ ፡ መልኡ ፡ አር ጋብ ፡ ውስተ ፡ አግዋሊሃ ፡ ለመርዓት ፡ እለ ፡ ያዐርጉ ፡ መሥዋዕተ ፡ ነባቤ ፡
unclean.’” Behold therefore that we have made you understand, believing people, the analogy of this Israelite without guile (Jn 1:47). God gratified him with the blessing of heaven, from on high, and with the blessing of the earth that has everything. He was joy for him who saw and for him who listened, distant or near; one gathered to him from all the races of man until this land of desolation became a city for the Christ, that the land that (was) without fruit bore fruit — for one, thirty and sixty and a hundred (Mt 13:8//) — and that there were convents around the cave of (Sämu’ël). From the limit of the Chiré to the limit of the Wäli, the doves filled the stalls of the Bride, they who offered the intelligent sacrifice to the Lamb.

169 Concerning this, Isaiah the prophet says: Shine, shine, Jerusalem! Your light has come and the glory of God has risen upon you. Kings walk in your light and the nations in your radiance. Lift up your eyes, look around you and see that your children have gathered around you; all your sons and your daughters come from afar. Kings bring your gifts and offer a chosen sacrifice on my altar and glorify in my house of prayer (Is 60:1, 3-4, 6-7). They will graze on all the paths, they will not be hungry nor thirsty, the heat of the sun will not torment them. (Sämu’ël) brought them to springs of water (Is 49:9-10) and announced (to them) the salvation from God. God made of him a refuge for all creatures, just as in ancient time Moses had established for Israel three cities so that they might be a refuge for every stranger — for Reuben in Gilead, for Manasseh in Bashan, at Ayyalôn for Gad —, so that (the murderer) might take refuge beside one of these three cities so that the avenger of blood might not kill him (Dt 4:41-43). Just so therefore God made a refuge of this saint, and after his death and in his lifetime; the Christians were not the only ones healed, but the pagans also. It is not even the children of men alone who were saved by his prayer, but the animals of the desert also were saved by it, and for all creatures he was a refuge. All the fornicators and impure, those who committed defilement and lying, all those who had stains and were swallowed by the seas of sin and painful diseases, when they came and took refuge beside him, he was for them a help by his holy prayer and delivered them from the avenger of blood, that is to say, the devil, and (from) his demons. Thus was he a refuge for all creatures. Sämu’ël sends a disciple to the land of the dogs

this, our father said to him: “Come, my son, serve me in the land of the dogs; there is over there a young girl who (is) worthy of the kingdom of heaven, bring her to me here.” The man said to him: “Abbä, how shall I know the path and where is the land: I have not heard of it until now?” Our holy father said to him: “Go, you, toward the north and God will guide you, only let (this) be a sign for you: when you arrive there, you will meet (the young girl) carrying a pitcher, in the process of going to draw water, and she will say to you: ‘For the prayer of Abbä Sämu’ël, take me with you.’” The man obeyed and left (Sämu’el). While he was going on the path, when he was in the middle of the land of the pagans, he met two anchorites from among the ancient fathers; their beard descended onto their chest and their hair and the hair of their back covered them like a cloak; their height was tall and (they were) very imposing in their appearance. (The man), who saw them, said: “When I measured the footprint of their feet, (I found that) it was a cubit and a span; immediately, I fell at their feet, greeted them, and said to them: ‘My fathers, from where (do you come) and where are you going?’” They said to him: “We, we are going to the desert of Barakä, according to what Abbä Sämu’el has prescribed for us; he said to us: ‘Do not stay in the land of the pagans, it is better that you practice prayer in the land of the Christians.’ Because of that, we are going over there, where the man of God has prescribed (it) for us.” “I said to them, (continued the man:) ‘Do you know Abbä Sämu’el?’ They said to me: ‘Yes, we know him: is there a Christian who invokes the name of Christ who does not know Sämu’el?’ Having said that, they passed by.
ሙኤል ፡ ወዘንተ ፡ ብሂሎሙ ፡ ኀለፉ ፡፥ ወአነኒ ፡ ሖርኩ ፡ 'ውስተ ፡ ብሔረ"”" ፡ ከላባት ፡ ኀበ ፡ ዘተአዘዝኩ « ወሶበ ፡ በጻሕኩ ፡ ህየ ፡ ወቦእኩ ፡ ሀገረ ፡ ወበፈቃደ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ረከብ ክዋ ፡ ለይእቲ ፡ ወለት ፡ እንዘ ፡ ተሐውር ፡ ትቅዳሕ ፡ ማየ ።= ወትቤለኒ ፡ ኦብ እሲ ፡ እምአይቴ ፡ እንግዳ ፍሩ ወእቤላ ፡ አንሰ ፡ ክርስቲያናዊ ፡ እምብሔረ ፡ ጽንብላ ፡ ወብየ ፡ መፍቅደ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዛቲ ፡ ሀገር ፡ ወበእንተዝ ፡ መጻእኩ ፡ ዝየ ። ወትቤለኒ ፡ ንሥአኒ ፡ ምስሌከ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ጸሎቱ ፡ ለአባ ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡ እምዛቲ ፡ ብሔረ ፡ ነኪር ። ወእቤላ ፡ ታአምርዮኑ ፡ ለአባ ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡ ወትቤ ለኒ ፡ እወ ፡ አአምሮ ፡ ወአነ ፡ ርኢክዎ ፡ በሕልምየ ፡ ፻ ለያልየ ፡ ወመጽአ ፡ ኀቤየ ፡ ወይቤለኒ ፡ ኢትፍርሂ ፡ ወአነ ፡ 'እነሥአኪ ፡ ኀቤየ'"" ፡ ወትከውኒ ፡ ክርስቲያናዊተ ። ኦፍቁርየ ፡ ስማዕ ፡ እንግርከ ፡ እስመ ፡ ንሕነ ፡ በሕሱም ፡ ፍጥረት ፡ ተፈጠርነ ፡ እምኩሉ ፡ ደቂቀ ፡ እጓለ ፡ እመሕያው ፡ ወከመዝ ፡ ሕግነ ፡ ውስተ ፡ 'ዛቲ ፡ ሀገር'” ፡ ለእመ ፡ ኮኑ ፡ ተባዕተ ፡ ይትወለዱ ፡ ከላባት ፡ ወለእመ ፡ ኮና ፡ አንስተ ፡ ይትወለዳ ፡ ሰብእ ። ወሊተኒ ፡ ሀለዉኒ ፡ ፪ከላባት ፡ አኀውየ ፡ እለ ፡ ተወለድነ ፡ እምአሐቲ ፡ እም ፡ ወከመዝ ፡ ይትዋሰቡ ፡ ፍጥ ረተ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ፍጥረተ ፡ ከላባት ፡ ወበእንተዝኬ ፡ እፈቅድ ፡ ትንሥ አኒ ፡ ምስሌከ ።
171 “As for me, I went into the land of the dogs, where I had received the order. When I arrived there, I entered the city and, by the will of God, found the young girl in the process of going to draw water. She said to me: ‘Man, from where do you come?’ I said to her: ‘I am a Christian from the land of Senbelä and I have business in this city, that is why I have come here.’ She said to me: ‘Take me with you out of this foreign land, for the prayer of Abbä Sämu’ël.’ I said to her: “Do you know Abbä Sämu’el?’ She said to me: ‘Yes, I know him; I, I saw him in my dream three nights: he came to me and said to me: Do not be afraid, I, I am going to take you to me and you will become a Christian. My beloved (continued the woman), listen, that I may tell you: we, we were created with a nature more disgraced than (that of) all the children of men. Thus (is) our status in this city: if (they) are males, they are born dogs; if (they) are females, they are born human beings. As for me, I have two dogs, my brothers, we who were born by the same mother; thus is married the nature of men with the nature of dogs, and because of that I want you to take me with you.’”


[p. 148] {#po.53.p148}
ወሶበ ፡ መስየ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ንዋም ፡ ወፃእነ ፡ እምሀገር ፡ ወሖርነ ፡ ኩላ ፡ ሌሊተ ፡ እስከ ፡ ይጸብሕ ። ወሶበ ፡ ጸብሐ ፡ ዐረግነ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዐቢይ ፡ ሰግላ ። ወእሙንቱሰ ፡ ከላባት ፡ በጽሑ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይረውጹ ፡ ከመ ፡ አንስርት ፡ ወድምፀ ፡ እገሪሆሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ነጐድጓድ ፡ ወብዝጥፕሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ አንብጣ ፡ ወከ በብዋ ፡ ለይእቲ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ሀሎነ ፡ ኦም ፡ ንሕነ ። ወአሐዙ ፡ ይሒክዋ ፡ በአስናኒ ሆሙ ፡ ለይእቲ ፡ ኦም ፡ ወይክርዩ ፡ በአጽፋሪሆሙ ፡ በተባርዮ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ 'እም ድኅረ ፡ ፳"" ወይገንሑነ ፡ ኩሎሙ'" ፡ በመዓት ፡ ወበቀ'ጥዐ ፡ ወሶበ ፡ አኮ ፡ ኀይለ ፡ ጸሎቱ ፡ ለአባ ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡ ዘአጽንዓነ ፡ እምወደቅነ ፡ በድንጋፄ ፍ ወወ ዓሉ ፡ ከመዝ ፡ እስከ ፡ ሰርክ ፡ ወንስቲትክመ ፡ ተረፎሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይወግርዋ ፡ ለይእቲ ፡ ኦም ። ወሶበ ፡ መስየ ፡ ተመይጡ ፡ ውስተ ፡ አብያቲሆሙ ። ወከመዝ ፡ ሕጎሙ ፡ ኢይወጽኡ ፡ በሌሊት ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ በመዓልት ። ወንሕነሂ ፡ ነሐውር ፡ በሌሊት ፡ ወሶበ ፡ ጸብሐ ፡ ነዓርግ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ኦም ፍ ወለለጽባሑ ፡ ይረክቡነ ፡ ከመ ፡ ቅጽበት ። ወከመዝ ፡ ገበርነ ፡ እስከ ፡ ወፃእነ ፡ እምደወሎሙ ፡ ወባሕቱ ፡
172 “When evening fell and it was the time of sleep, we left the city and went all night until the morning rose. When the morning rose, we climbed into a large sycamore. As for the dogs, they arrived by swooping like eagles; the noise of their paws (was) like thunder and their multitude, like locusts, they surrounded the tree where we were. They began to gnaw with their fangs at this tree and to dig with their claws in turn, one after the other; they all barked against us with anger and with fury. If the power of the prayer of Abbä Sämu’ël had not strengthened us, we would have fallen from fright. They passed the day thus until evening, it lacked little that they threw (down) this tree. When evening fell, they returned to their houses; thus is their status: they do not go out at night, only by day. As for us, we went at night and, when the morning rose, we climbed into a tree; each morning, they found us in an instant. We did thus until we had left their territory, but they did not cross the land of another.”
ብሔረ ፡ ባዕድሰ ፡ ኢየዐድዉ ። ወእምዝ ፡ በጻሕነ ፡ ኀበ ፡ አባ ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡ ወአወፈይክዋ ፡ ለይእቲ ፡ ወለት ፡ ወአብኣ ፡ ክርስትና ፡ ወኮነት ፡ መነኮሳይተ ። ወተምህረት ፡ ፻ወ ዛመዝ ሙረ ፡ ዳዊት ፡ ወ፲ወ ፳ነቢያተ ፡ ወመኃልየ ፡ መሓልይ ፡ ወዓንቀጸ ፡ ብርሃን ፡ ወኩሎ ፡ ውዳሴሃ ፡ ለማርያም'"'' ፡ ወዘንተ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ በ ዕለት ፡ ፈጸመት ።= ወነ በረት ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገዳም ፡ እንዘ ፡ ትትጋደል ፡ በብዙኅ ፡ ጾም ፡ ወጸሎት ። ወእ ምድኅረ ፡ ሕዳጥ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ ተሰወረት ፡ ወተደመረት ፡ ምስለ ፡ ስዉራን ። ጸሎታ ፡ ወበረከታ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ፡
173 “After this, we arrived beside Abbä Sämu’ël and I handed (him) the young girl; he brought her into Christianity and she became a nun. She learned the hundred and fifty psalms of David, the fifteen (canticles of the) prophets, the Song of Songs, the Angasa berhän, and all the Weddäsë Märyäm; she accomplished all that in thirty days. She remained in the desert to fight (ascetically) in a great fast and prayer. After some days, she disappeared and joined anchorites.” May his prayer and his blessing be with us, amen.

### A monk who wants to go to Jerusalem
ወካዕበ ፡ መጽአ ፡ ኅበ ፡ አባ ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡ ፳መነኮስ ፡ ወነገሮ ፡ ሕሊ ናሁ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ፍኖተ ፡ *" ኢየሩሳሌም ። ወአውሥኦ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ተዓገስ ፡ ወልድየ ፡ ይቴይሰከ ፡ ትንበር ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዛቲ ፡ ሀገር ፡ ቅድስት ወሶበ ፡ ሖርከ ፡ ህየ ፡ ተሐድግ ፡ ክርስትናከ'" ፡ አባሕኩከ ፡ ታውስብ ፡ ብእ ሲተ ፡ ክርስቲያናዊተ ፡ እምትክሐድ ፡ ስመ ፡ ክርስቶስ ። ወውእቱሰ ፡ አበዮ ፡ ወሖረ ፡ ተጸሚሞ ።= ወእንዘ ፡ የሐውር ፡ በፍኖት ፡ ረከብዎ ፡ ተንበላት ፡ ወወሰ ድዎ ፡ ወነሥእዎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ብሔሮሙ ፡ በግብር ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ፈቃዱ ፡ ወሜ ጥዎ''" ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሕጎሙ ፡ ወወሀብዎ ፡ ብእሲተ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ከማሆሙ ። ወውእ ቱሰ ፡ ይበኪ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ዘኢሰምዐ ፡ ቃለ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ሌሊተ ፡ ወመዓልተ ፡ ወይት መኀፀን ፡ ኀበ ፡ ጸሎቱ ፡ ለአባ ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ ኦአባ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ዘረከበኒ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ዘኢሰማዕኩ ፡ ቃለከ"'' = ኦአባ ፡ ትብጽሐኒ ፡ ጸሎትከ ፡ ወበሊሕ ፡ ረድኤትከ ፡ ይምጻእ ፡ ፍጡነ ፡ ወይርድአኒ ፡ ወያውፅአኒ ፡ እምዛቲ ፡ መስገርት ፡ እንተ ፡ ተተክለት ፡ ሊተ ። ወሖረ ፡ አቡነ ፡ በሰረገላ ፡ መንፈስ ፡
174 And again, a monk came to Abbä Sämu’ël and told him his thought concerning the journey to Jerusalem. Our father answered him and said to him: “Contain yourself, my son, it is better for you to remain in this holy region: if you went over there, you would abandon your state as a Christian; I have permitted you to marry a Christian woman rather than that you should renounce the name of Christ.” But (the man) refused him and went away turning a deaf ear. While he was making his way, Muslims met him, took him, and brought him into their land by force, against his will; they converted him to their law, gave him a wife, and he was like them. As for him, he wept that he had not listened to the word of our father, night and day, and committed himself again to the prayer of Abbä Sämu’ël saying: “Abbä, all this that has touched me (has happened) from the fact that I did not listen to your word. Abbä, may your prayer arrive upon me and may your prompt help come quickly, help me, and make me leave the trap that has been set for me.” Our father went on the chariot of the Spirit, arrived beside
ወበጽሐ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ ወነሥኦ ፡ እማእከለ ፡ አረሚ ፡ ወወጸፎ ፡ እምህየ ፡ ከመ ፡ ዘይወጽፍ ፡ እብነ''' ። ወበአሐቲ ፡ ምድርባይ ፡ አብጽሖ ፡ ዝየ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ምድረ ፡ ሲሬ ፡ ወአግብኦ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ጥንተ ፡ ምንኩስናሁ ። ወአስመሮ ፡ ለእግዚ አብሔር ፡ ወአዕረፈ ፡ በሰላም ።
(the man), took him from the middle of the infidels, and threw him from over there like one who throws a stone. With a single throw, he made him arrive here, in the land of Chiré, and brought him back to the beginning of his monastic state. (The man) pleased God and died in peace.


[p. 150] {#po.53.p150}

175 Abba, the story of your virtue (is) prodigious and cannot be brought to its end by the account nor be completed by the tongue of created man. Only intercede for us before the good God so that we may not be excluded from his banquet of blessing, but may be with those who are seated at table in the house of the pure Bridegroom, in the ages of ages, amen. The death of Sämu’ël 176 Now, we are going to inform you of the manner in which took place the departure of this chosen and holy (man) from this transitory world: when he was weakened by the greatness of the trial and the vigil and his eyes had become troubled because of the abundance of tears, his body withered from the fact of the greatness of the trial in fasting; during all the days of his life, he did not change his rule, until he had spent eighty years in this labor. Of this (time) his abstinence lasted sixty-five years, (he) not eating wheat; on the contrary he remained to feed himself on shoots of the desert — some leaves, or else flowers of herbs, or roots — with much effort. He considered each day as if he were to die that day. Then after he had lived a hundred years, God, the friend of the human race, wanted to make him rest from these heavy fatigues, trials, and (ascetic) struggles that he had undergone in his body, in the dwelling on high with the righteous and those who do what pleases him, according to what John says in his Vision: “Happy are those who are called to the wedding of the Lamb” (Rev 19:9). He says again: “They enter through its gates into this city” (Rev 22:14). He says again: “He who will have conquered will inherit this, and I will be for him his God and he will be for me my son” (Rev 21:7).
ወሶበ ፡ ሠረቀ ፡ ወርኃ ፡ ታኅሣሥ ፡ ተዓውቆ ፡ ሕማም ፡ ወገደፈ ፡ ነፍሶ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ምድር ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ ፈጸንተ ፡ ወዕፁብ ፡ ነዳድ ። ወአ ኀዘ ፡ ይሥርዖሙ ፡ ለደቂቁ ፡ ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ኦውሉድየ ፡ ዘታፈቅሮሙ ፡ ነፍስየ ፡ ስምዑ ፡ እምኔየ ፡ ዘእዜዘክሙ ፡ ዮም ፡ አነ ፡ አቡክሙ ። እስመ ፡ ዛቲ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ተፍጻሜተ ፡ ትእዛዝ ፡ ዘእሁበክሙ ፡ ዮም ፡ መቅድመ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ፍርህዎ ፡ ለእግ ዚአብሔር ፡ ወዕቀቡ ፡ ትእዛዛቲሁ ። ኦፍቁራንየ ፡ ወደቂቅየ ፡ ተፋቀሩ ፡ በበይና ቲክሙ ፡ በምልአ ፡ ልብክሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይኅድር ፡ በረከትየ ፡ ላዕሌክሙ ። ወዘ ንተ ፡ ብሂሎ ፡ ባረኮሙ ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ ቡራኬ ። አመ'"'?' ፡ ፲ወ፪ለወርኃ ፡ ታኅ ሣሥ ፡ ከብደ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ደዌ ፡ ወአኀዊሁኒ ፡ የአውድዎ ፡ በትርአሱ ፡ ወበትር ጋፁ ።= ወያነብቡ ፡ ወንጌለ ፡ ዘዮሐንስ ፡ ወሶበ ፡ በጽሑ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ሠምራዊት ፡
177 When the month of Tähsäs began, a pain declared itself in him; he threw himself to the ground, there was a great fever and a painful febrile state. He began to dictate a rule for his (spiritual) children, he said to them: “My beloved sons whom my soul loves, listen to what I prescribe to you today, I your father; indeed this is the completion of the commandment, which I give to you today. Before all, fear God and guard his commandments. My beloved and my children, love one another from the fullness of your heart so that my blessing may remain upon you.” Having said that, he blessed them with a great blessing. The twelfth of the month of Tähsäs, the sickness weighed heavily upon him and his brothers surrounded him, at his head

አውሥአ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ኦደቂቅየ ፡ ታአምሩኑ ፡ ምንት ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ኃይለ ፡ ቃል ፡ ዘይትነበብ ፡ ላዕሌየ ፡ ዮም ፡ እምዝንቱ ፡ ቃለ ፡ ወንጌል ። ወአ ውሥኡ ፡ አኃው ፡ ወይቤልዎ ፡ አልቦ ፡ ዘአእመርነ ፡ ኦአባ ፡ አምረነ ፡ ምንት ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ፍካሬሁ ። ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ናሁ ፡ መልአከ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ቅድሜየ ፡ ይትረዳእ ፡ ውስተ ፡ አንብቦ ፡ ወንጌል ፡ እንዘ ፡ አንትሙሰ ፡ ትሰግዱ ፡ ለዘ ፡ ኢታአምሩ ፡ ወንሕነሰ ፡ ንሰግድ ፡ ለዘናአምር ። ወዘንተ ፡ ከመዝ ፡ ይብል ፡ ኢት ሰምኡ ፡ ወጐንደየ ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ንስቲተ ፡ ወይቤ ፡ ስምዑ ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ዘይብል ፡ መል አክ ፡ 8በየማኑ ፡ ወ ፳በፀጋሙ ፡ ወማእከሌሆሙ ፡ ኢየሱስ ። ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዑ ፡ አኃው ፡ አንከሩ ፡ እምነገሩ ። ወሶበ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ሰዓት ፡ አዕረፈ ።
and at his feet. They were reading the Gospel of John; when they arrived at (the episode of) the Samaritan woman (Jn 4:4-42), our father took the word and said to them: “My children, do you know what is the power of the word that has been pronounced over me today among these words of the Gospel?” The brothers answered and said to him: “We do not know, Abbä; indicate to us what is its explanation.” He said to them: “Behold the angel of God before me helped with the reading of the Gospel (which says:) ‘While you, you are prostrated before what you do not know, we, we prostrate before what we know’ (Jn 4:22).” He said this thus: “You do not listen.” He waited again a little and said: “Listen again to what (the angel) says: “One at the right and one at the left; in the middle of them, Jesus’ (Mt 20:21// or Mt 27:38, the two thieves?).” When the brothers heard, they marveled at his remarks. When it was the time of the ninth hour (Mt 27:46//), he

died. 178 His death took place on Tuesday and behold there exhaled smoke of incense with a sweet perfume, to the point that this place was filled. (There were) seven angels holding a parasol and, on each parasol, an inscription that said: “Sämu’el chosen of God”; (there were) seven others holding a crown; they crowned him, upon his head, saying: “You have placed a crown that (is) of precious stone upon his head” (Ps 20[21]:4). They surrounded him, at his right and at his left. Lights descended to the point of illuminating the whole place around the monastery; the soul of Sämu’el, white as snow, went out then, the Lord took it, embraced it with his holy hands, kissed it, and said to it: “Come in peace, my beloved pure soul.” The twenty-four priests of heaven descended with censers, the angels welcomed (Sämu’ël) in joy and in gladness, and the clamor of the angels was heard in the doors of the airs, the Lord being seated on a chariot of light, the pure soul in his bosom. The whole assembly of the angels, of the martyrs, of the righteous, of the prophets, and of the apostles and all the saints glorified with a sweet glorification — until (the soul) remained under the shadow of the right hand of the Most High, in the light of life and in the holy city where there is no death, in eternal joy. May his prayer and his blessing be with us in the ages of ages, amen.

179 On the fortieth day, there was again a great miracle: just as the saints had gathered on the day of his death, just so gathered then and descended the angels, each according to his tribe, the children of Adam, each according to his order; the twenty-four priests of heaven descended with their censers; Our Lord descended with his myriads of angels and the little children sang before him a sweet canticle. Gathered again all the anchorites from all the ends of the universe, those who were in (a life of) flesh among the first fathers, thus (are) their names: Aronyos, the prefect of Serha Seyon; Endreyänos, the deacon of Waylagä; Yohannes, the monk of the land of Talazag; Za-Mikä’ël and Endreyänos of Gadäma Bur and many others, innumerable. Thus gathered the

(beings) heavenly and earthly in order to make nuptial feasts for the honor of his marriage, for he was a virgin and pure. From God was sent a blessing that sufficed for the generations of generations; before this blessing spread according to what had been prescribed, the brothers raised a dispute: immediately everything withdrew into heaven, for where there is dispute, God does not give his approval, just as it is said in Nabyud: Worse than everything (are) the altercation and the dispute between brothers; the ways of those who (act) thus are not straight and the Holy Spirit moves away from them. But God did not reject his people who had gathered, from far and near, to receive the blessing of this saint publicly at the moment of the (eucharistic) sacrifice: all were sealed with the gift of the grace of Our Lord and Savior Jesus Christ — glory to him in the ages of ages, amen! Final exhortation

180 My fathers and my brothers, anointed Christian people, behold therefore that we have narrated to you a little of the wonders (of Sämu’ël) that cannot be counted; even if I had remained all the days of my life working night and day to write his (spiritual) combat, I would not have been able to (narrate) completely the virtuous acts of this saint. Just as the sand of the sea cannot be counted nor the depth of the seas measured, just so his miracles cannot be told totally nor the trials of his (ascetic) combat be put into writing. But to the wise a few words suffice, to him who has the belief in hope and the faith, according to what the Wisdom of Sirach says: (If there is) a beautiful assembly in the wall of a construction, the wall will never be opened, just so therefore for a firm heart and for the design of the wise (Sir 22:16). He who teaches a fool (is) like one who glues together shards and he who speaks to a fool (is) like one who speaks to a sleeper: when you have finished speaking to him, he says to you: “What did you say?” Weep for a fool, for his heart has carried him away (Sir 22:9-11). 181 My beloved, awaken your heart and do not fall from your firmness, on the contrary walk in the holiness (of Sämu’el) while being carried toward his grace; approach now to draw from the abyss of his mercy. Behold here flows the river of life that flowed from the dew of Hermon (Ps 132[133]:3), not like the well of Bethlehem where one draws by combat and by the spear (2 Sam 23:15-16), nor like the fountain of Midian from which the Hebrew drew (Ex 2:15-17). On the contrary, behold the chief of the shepherds of sheep calls you, standing at the edge of the troughs, saying: “Let him who is thirsty come to me and drink” (Jn 7:37). Behold here (there is) a precious stone that (is) placed on the table; is there a traveler

wise who has a gift (Mt 13:45-46)? Let him come now with the tears (?) of his talent, (pour them) into the basket of the Bride and receive this pearl of great price without laboring for the gain of his journey. Behold here (there is) gold purified that (is) pure and refined, which has not been adulterated; call the poor, that without paying they may receive, gratuitously. Behold here (there is) a diadem of gold adorned in the name of the three Names that cover it. Is there a wise young man who hopes for the coming of the Bridegroom? Let him come now and receive this royal crown so that it may be for him the adornment of his head for the moment of his wedding. Are there fishermen who cast the net into the abyss of the sea? Let them come now to lower their nets to the right of the ship and bring up from there the white fish that came out first from the sea of the world, which has in its mouth a stater (Mt 17:27) that has been marked with the seal of the Lamb who was killed outside the tent (Heb 13:12).

182 Now, my beloved, let us fight all the time in the search for the approval of God with fear and with trembling and let us be zealous with all our strength to guard his faith; let us examine these teachings with a pure eye to obtain clemency and mercy before him; let us be ready for his blessing; let us run quickly toward his beauties and he will prepare us for the communion with this holy father of ours when, in the crowns of justice, he will receive the reward of the victory and of the calling that (comes) from on high — so that we may obtain the joy, the mercy, the voluptuousness, and the delights in the kingdom of heaven. In Our Lord and Savior Jesus Christ before whom every nature prostrates itself, whom every tongue glorifies, and to whom every creature makes submission in heaven and on earth (Phil 2:10). Glory and praise to him at all times, now and always, in the ages of ages, amen!

183 Here is completed the (account of the) (spiritual) combat of the holy and blessed, great, spiritual, truly Christian, merciful and just—the illuminator, the shining star—blessed father, Abbä Sämu’ël of Gadäma Wali. May his prayer, his blessing, and the gift of his help be with us, amen. 184 You who have gathered today, on this holy day, the day of the commemoration of our holy father, you who read and you who listen, may Christ our God make you worthy (to be) with his saints and his elect, on this formidable day of violent terror, by the grace of Our Lord Jesus Christ who came for our salvation, endured the crucifixion, died for us, and shed his holy blood with which he redeemed us (Rev 1:5). May he welcome your prayers, your alms, and your penance; may he welcome your gifts and your offering; may he help you in your (spiritual) combat; may he hear your supplication; may he give you luminous deeds and crowns of victory; may he wash away your defilement and grant you

constancy and thanksgiving; may he make you worthy to adore him and grant you your blessings; may he cause his grace to dwell upon you and bring you near to what is pleasing to him; may he save you from terror, you who tremble before all dangers, perils, evils, and illnesses; may he save you from all misfortunes and the seas of carnal thoughts; may he purify your defilement with the water of life; may he invigorate you with the oil of faith; may he pour upon you cataracts of mercy; may he protect you with his strong right hand and his outstretched arm; may his adoration enter like water into your bowels and like oil into your bones; may he cause you to sit at the heavenly banquet in the community of the martyrs and virgins who pleased him during their lives, and may he cause you to enter into the Jerusalem which (is) in heaven—its foundations (are) of nuggets of pure gold and its twelve gates, each of twelve pearls that have no need of the sun and the moon; may he make you hear the voice of joy and the voice of the zithers of the little children who sing a spiritual canticle, who are the one hundred and forty-four thousand who have washed their garments in the blood of the Lamb (Rev 14:2-3); may he not separate you from the pomp of his marriage so that you may rejoice when he says (to the sheep): 'Blessed of my Father, come (Mt 25:34) in the grace and intercession of Mary our Lady who is the dwelling of the divinity.' May he crown you with his grace and cover you with his right hand; may he give you every good (spiritual) combat, an increase in it, and a viaticum (drawn) from it for the world to come; may he cause the one among you who is lost to return; may he bring back in safety the one among you who has departed, and may he heal your sick. May the Lord give you consolation in the sorrows that befall you, by the power of the cross of Our Lord Jesus Christ—glory to him with his heavenly Father and the life-giving Holy Spirit now, always, and unto the ages of ages, amen!

(MIRACLES) 185 ... believing in this faith that our fathers the apostles taught us in their Sinodos, we write with the help of the holy Trinity a few of the miracles and wonders (of Sämu’ël) in proportion to what we can hear and not in proportion to what this blessed father of beautiful memory did, a just and pure Israelite, priest and virgin, clothed in the monastic habit, affliction of the desert, follower of the footsteps of his Lord, bearer of the Cross,
