አሰረ ፡ እግዚኡ ፡ ጸዋሬ ፡ መስቀል ፡ መምህረ ፡ ሕግ ፡ ራትዕ ፡ አበ ፡፡ ካህናት ፡ ወመነኮሳት ፡ ምጽላለ ፡ ሕዝብ ፡ ወአሕዛብ ፡ ታቦተ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ምስካየ ፡ ኀጥኣን ፡ ፈዋሴ ፡ ድውያን ፡ ወሰዳዴ ፡ መናፍስት ፡ ርኩሳን ፡ ሐራዊ ፡ ጽኑዕ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቃትል ፡ በሥራሐ ፡ ገድል ፡ ወበኩናተ ፡ ወንጌል ፡ ምስለ ፡ አበ ፡ ደም ፡ ዘውእቱ ፡ ዲያብሎስ ፡ ፀራ ፡ ለጽድቅ ፡ ምስለ ፡ አጋንንቲሁ ፡ ጸዋ ጋን ፡ እለ ፡ ይተግሁ ፡ ወይሰካተዩ ፡ ለአስግሮ ፡ ነፍሳተ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ፀሐይ ፡ ብሩህ ፡ ጽዱል ፡ ኮከብ ፡ ለምስማከ ፡ ርእሳ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ዘሐነፀቶ ፡ ጥበብ ። በግዕ ፡ ጸዓዳ ፡ ወየዋህ ፡ ርግብ ። ዘተሐፅነ ፡ ወልህቀ ፡ በመንፈሳዊ ፡ ሐሊብ ። በሥመ ፡ ስላሴ ፡ ዕቱብ ። ምናናተ ፡ ርዴሁ ፡ ክዑብ ። ምዕቃለ ፡ ቅድስና ፡ ወንጽሕ ፡ ዘአእምሮ ፡ መዝገብ ። ሱታፌ ፡ ነቢያት ፡ ወሐዋርያት ፡ ዘውገ ፡ ኪሩብ ። አራሬ ፡ ጽድቅ ፡ ከመ ፡ መስተገብርት ፡ ንህብ ። ዘወፅአ ፡ ስሙዓቱ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ትርብ ዕተ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ርቲብ ። ማር ፡ ሳሙኤል ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ ወሀብተ ፡ ረድ ኤቱ ፡ ይዕቀበነ ፡ በተሣህሎ ፡ ለእለ ፡ ተጋባእነ ፡ ዮም ፡ ለሰሚዐ ፡ መንክራቲሁ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዛቲ ፡ ተድባብ ። እምአፈ ፡ አርዌ ፡ ወዝእብ ። ወእምተኩላተ ፡ ዓረብ ። ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ።
doctor of the Law, upright father of priests and monks, shelter of the people and the nations, ark of the Holy Spirit, refuge of sinners, healer of the sick and expeller of impure spirits, vigorous soldier, fighting with the splendor of the (spiritual) combat and with the lance of the Gospel against the father of blood who is the devil, the enemy of the truth (Jn 8:44), against his ferocious demons who watch and conspire to trap the souls of men, shining sun, sparkling star, house that Wisdom built (Pr 9:1) to rest her head, white lamb and gentle dove, who was nourished and grew up with spiritual milk, marked with the name of the Trinity, whose mines of interest have doubled (the capital) (Lk 19:13-19), cistern of holiness and purity (and) treasure of knowledge, associate of the prophets and apostles (and) companion of the cherubim, harvester of justice like the industrious bee (Pr 6:8), whose reputation has gone out into the four corners of the vast world, mär Sämu’ël. May his prayer, his blessing, and the gift of his help guard us with compassion, we who have gathered today to listen to his wonders under this canopy, far from the mouth of the serpent and the hyena and far from the wolves of the sunset (Hab 1:8; Zeph 3:3), unto the ages of ages, amen.
ኦአበውየ ፡ ወአኀዊየ ፡ ጸልዩ ፡ በእንቲአነ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሩጽ ፡ ለነ ፡ ቃለ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ከመ ፡ ናስምዕክሙ ፡ መንክራቲሁ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡ ማር ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡ ዘይትነበብ ፡ አመ ፡ ለወርኃ ፡ መስከረም ፡ በሰላመ ፡ እግዚ አብሔር ፡ አብ ፡ አሜን ።
186 My fathers and my brothers, pray for us so that the word of God may hasten toward us so that we may make you hear the wonders of this father of ours, the blessed mär Sämu’èl, which are read on the sixth of the month of maskaram, in the peace of God the Father, amen.

### First miracle: the fish-filled water that gushed from the rock
ተአምራቲሁ ። ስምዑኬ ፡ ከመ ፡ ንንግርክሙ ፡ ኦሕዝብ ፡ መሲሓዊያን ፡ በልቡና ፡ ወበጥበብ ፡ ዘገብረ ፡ በ ፳ እመዋዕል ፡ አቡነ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡ ማር ፡ ሳሙ ኤል ፡ እንዘ ፡ ያንሶሱ ፡ ምስለ ፡ አርዳኢሁ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ውሳጤ ፡ ገዳም ፡ በዕ ለተ ፡ ዐርብ ። ወሶበ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ፍና ፡ ሰርክ ፡ ቤቱ ፡ ማእከለ ፡ ኩኩሕ ፡ ወሐጥኡ ፡ ዘይበልዑ ፡ ወተግሕሠ ፡ አቡነ ፡ በባሕቲቱ ፡ ወጸለየ ፡ ኀበ ፡ አም ላኩ ፡ ሰፊሖ ፡ እደዊሁ ፡ ወአንቃዕዲዎ ፡ አዕይንቲሁ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ ኦእግዚኦ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ መፍቀሬ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ አምላከ ፡ ምሕረት ፡ ወመሴስየ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ፍጥረት ፡ ዘወሀብኮሙ ፡ ሲሳየ ፡ ለሕዝብከ ፡ እስራኤል ፡ በውስተ ፡ ገዳም ፡ ግ ዓመተ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ይዝርኡ ፡ ወይእርሩ ። ወይእዜኒ ፡ ሀቦሙ ፡ ሲሳዮሙ ፡ ለእሉ ፡ አግብርቲከ ፡ ነዳያን ፡ እስመ ፡ አልቦሙ ፡ ዘይበልዑ ። ወእምድኅረ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ከመዝ ፡ መጽአ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀለዉ ፡ አርዳኢሁ ፡ ነሚኦ ፡ መባሕተ ፡ እም ኀበ ፡ እግዚኡ ፡ ወባረከ ፡ ሥልሰ ፡ መልዕልተ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ኩኩሕ ፡ ዘቤቱ ፡ ዲቤሁ ፡ ወነቅአ ፡ ማይ ፡ እምኔሃ ፡ በአምሳለ ፡ ሐሊብ ፡ ጻዓዳ ፡ ዘምስለ ፡ ዓባይት ፡ ዓሣት ። ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡ ለደቂቁ ፡ ንሥኡ ፡ እምነ ፡ ዛቲ ፣፡
187 1st miracle of (Sämu’ël). Listen then so that we may tell you, Christian people, with intelligence and wisdom what our father, the blessed mär Sämu’el, did one day while he was walking with his disciples in the interior of the desert on a Friday. When it was evening, they settled for the night in the middle of a rock and lacked food. Our father went aside, alone, and prayed to his God, having stretched out his hands and lifted his eyes, saying: 'Lord God, friend of men, God of mercy and nourisher of every creature, you who gave food to your people Israel in the desert for forty years, without them sowing or reaping, now give their food to these poor servants of yours, for they have nothing to eat.' After he had said this, he came to where his disciples were, having received power from his Lord; he made a blessing three times over the rock on which they had settled for the night; water gushed from it in the likeness of white milk, which (was) with large fish. The blessed one said to his (spiritual) children: 'Take of this water and of this fish what will

ማይ ፡ ወእምነ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ዓሣ ፡ ዘየአክለነ ፡ ሲሳየ ፡ ለሰንበት ፡ ወለእድ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ታአኩትዎ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ በእንተ ፡ ዘወሀበነ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ በብዝኃ ፡ ሣህሉ ፡ እስመ ፡ መሓሪ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወመፍቀሬ ፡ ሰብእ ። ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዑ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ነገረ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ወርእዩ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ተአምረ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡ ማር ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡ ተፈሥሑ ፡ ወተሐሠዩ ፡ ወሰብሕዎ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ወገብሩ ፡ በከመ ፡ አዘዞሙ ፡ ወነበሩ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይሴሰዩ ፡ እምነ ፡ ዘአስተጋብኡ ፡ እልክተ ፡ ክልኤተ ፡ መዋዐለ ። ወሶበ ፡ ጸብሐ ፡ ዕለተ ፡ ሰኑይ ፡ ካዕበ ፡ አዘዞሙ ፡ ለአርዳኢሁ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያስተጋብኡ ፡ እምዘተርፈ ፡ ሲሳዮሙ ። ወባረከ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ማይ ፡ ወላዕለ ፡ እልክቱ ፡ ዓሣ ፡ እለ ፡ ትእኅዙ ፡ ወተበልዑ ፡ ወኮኑ ፡ ሕያዋነ ፡ ከመ ፡ ቀዳሚ ፡ ወቦኡ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ኩኩሕ ፡ እንተ ፡ ነቅዓት ፡ ወማይሂ ፡ ገብአት ፡ ከማሁ ። ወዓዲ ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ባረከ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ኩኩሕ ፡
suffice as food for Saturday and Sunday, giving thanks to God for what he has given us in the greatness of his clemency; indeed he is merciful and a friend of men.' When they heard this discourse from him and saw this miracle of our father, the blessed mär Sämu’el, they rejoiced, exulted, and glorified God; they did as he had prescribed and remained to feed themselves those two days on what they had gathered. When Monday began to dawn, he prescribed again to his disciples to gather what remained of their food; he made a blessing over this water and these fish that had been taken and eaten: they became alive again as before and entered into the rock that had been split, and the water returned (to it) likewise. And again he made a blessing over the rock: (it) closed up and became as it was before.
ወተሐትመት ፡ ወኮነት ፡ ከመ ፡ ቀዳሚ ኦአባ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡ ሠናየ ፡ ዝክር ፡ እስራኤላዊ ፡ ሱታፌ ፡ ተልሜዳን ፡ ዘውገ ፡ ንግሥታት ፡ ስሳ ። ዘአንቃዕከ ፡ ማየ ፡ እምኩኩሕ ፡ ይቡስ ፡ ለሲ ሳየ ፡ ደቂቅከ ፡ ዘምስለ ፡ ዓሣ ። ይእዜኒ ፡ ይኩነነ ፡ ትንብልና ፡ ጸሎትከ ፡ ሰራዬ ፡ ኃጢአት ፡ ወደምሳሴ ፡ አበሳ ፡ ዘምስለ ፡ ወልድከ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ወልደ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ። ፪ ተአምሪሁ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡ ማር ፡ ሳሙኤል ። ወበ እመዋዕል ፡ ተንሥአ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ መንፈቀ ፡ ሌሊት ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሖር ፡ ኀበ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ምስለ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ መነኮስ ፡ ወበጽሑ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ ዐቢይ ፡ ጽድፍ ፡ ወኃጥኡ ፡ እንተ ፡ ኀበ ፡ የሐልፉ ፡ ፍኖተ ፡ እስመ ፡ ሰወሮሙ ፡ ጽልመተ ፡ ሌሊት ፡ እስመ ፡ መዋዕለ ፡ ክረምት'" ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወርኃ ፡ ነሐሴ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ጸለየ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡ ማር ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡ ኀበ ፡ አምላኩ ፡ በከመ ፡ ያለምድ ፡ አእ ሚሮ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይትወሀብ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ዘፈቀደ ፡ እምእግዚኡ ። ወሶበ ፡ ፈጸመ ፡ ጸሎቶ ፡ አብርሀ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ሱራጌ ፡ መለኮት ፡ ዘይጸድል ፡ ፈድፋደ ፡ እምነ ፡ ፀሐይ ፡ ወሶበ ፡ ርእዩ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ መድምመ ፡ ዘገብረ ፡ ሎሙ ፡ እግ ዚአብሔር ፡ ሰገዱ ፡ ወሰብሕዎ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ገብኡ ፡ ውስተ ፡ እንታክቲ ፡ ፍኖት ፡ እንተ ፡ ስሕቱ ፡ ወእንዘ ፡ የኀውሩ ፡ አውሥአ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ መነኮስ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ለብፁዓዊ ፡ ኦአባ ፡ አንሰ ፡ እምአመ ፡ ተወለድኩ ፡ ኢርኢኩ ፡ ዘከ መዝ ፡ ብርሃነ ። ተሰጥወ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ኦወልድየ ፡ ርቱዕ ፡ ይደሉ ፡ ከመ ፡ ናእኩቶ ፡ ለአምላክነ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ዘገብረ ፡ ለነ ፡ በዕበየ ፡ ጸጋሁ ። ወእንዘ ፡ ይዜያነዉ ፡ ከመዝ ፡ በዕበያተ ፡ እግዚአብሔ”ር ፡ በጽሑ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ዘፈቀዱ ፡
188 Blessed Abbä of beautiful memory, Israelite, associate of the Disciples, companion of the sixty queens (Song 6:8), you who made water gush from a dry rock which (was) for the food of your children with fish! Now, may the intercession of your prayer be for us, (to) you who forgive sin and erase fault, which (may also be) with your son, our father Walda Krestos, unto the ages of ages, amen. Second miracle: the light in the night 189 2nd miracle of our father, the blessed mär Sämu’el. One day, he rose at the time of the middle of the night to go to church with a monk; they arrived where there was a great precipice and did not find the path by which to pass, for the darkness of the night hid (it) from them: it was the days of winter, the month of nahasë. After that, our father, the blessed mär Sämu’ël, prayed to his God as he was accustomed, knowing that everything he wanted would be given to him by his Lord. When he had finished his prayer, the splendor of the divinity which sparkles more than the sun emitted light for him: when they saw this astonishing thing that God had done for them, they prostrated themselves and glorified him. After that, they returned to that path they had lost; as they were going, the monk spoke and said to the blessed one: 'Abbä, I, since I was born, have not seen such a light.' Our father answered and said to him: 'My son, it is right that we praise God for what he has done for us in the greatness of his grace.' While conversing thus about the greatness of God, they arrived at the place they

wanted and entered the church. After that, the monk went out to look for the light and did not find (it); he returned wondering that there was the darkness of the night as before.
ኦአባ ፡ ሠናየ ፡ ዝክር ፡ ማር ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡ ዘውገ ፡ ነቢያት ፡ ወሐዋር ያት ፡ አስተምሕር ፡ በእንቲአነ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ዘአስተርአየ ፡ ለከ ፡ ብርሃነ ፡ ሱራ ፡ መለኮት ፡ ጊዜ ፡ መንፈቀ ፡ ሌሊት ። ከመ ፡ ያድኅነነ ፡ እሞተ ፡ ኃጢአት ወይ ጸግወነ ፡ መክፈልተ ፡ ወርስተ ፡ ምስሌከ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሀገሪትከ"" ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ፡=
190 Abbä of beautiful memory, mär Sämu’ël, companion of the prophets and apostles, intercede for us before the splendor of the divinity that made the light appear at the time of the middle of the night, so that he may save us from the death of sin and grant us a portion and an inheritance with you in your heavenly city, unto the ages of ages, amen.

### Third miracle: a large elephant on the path
፻ ተአምሪሁ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡ ማር ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡ ወበ፳እምነ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ እንዘ ፡ የሐውር ፡ ባሕቲቱ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገዳም ፡ ረከበ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ፍኖት ፡ ጸባብ ፡ ዐቢይ ፡ ነጌ ። ወሶበ ፡ ነጸሮ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ነጌ ፡ ቆመ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ፍኖት ፡ ጸባብ ፡ ወኢክህለ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይትመየጥ ፡ ድኅሬሁ ፡ እስመ ፡ ገዚፍ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ምሉአ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ፍኖት ። ወሶበ ፡ ፈቀደ ፡ ይሖር ፡ ቅድሜሁ ፡ ፈርሀ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢያደንግዖ ፡ ብእሴ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወዝንቱኒ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡ ሠናየ ፡ ዝክር ፡ ምሉአ ፡ ሃይማኖት ፡ ዘኅዱር ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ጸጋ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ቆመ ፡ ቅድመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ነጌ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ፍርሀት ። ወአውሥኦ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ሰሰል ፡ ሊተ ፡ ሀበኒ ፡ ፍኖተ ፡ እንተ ፡ ኀበ ፡ አኀልፍ ፡ ከመ ፡ እሖር ፡ ኀበ ፡ ዘፈቀድኩ ። ወውእቱ ፡ ነጌ ፡ ዘይት በሀል ፡ ሐርማዝ ፡ በነገረ ፡ ግዕዝ ፡ አስመከ ፡ መንገለ ፡ አሐቲ ፡ ገቦሁ ፡ ከመ ፡ ዘይነውም ፡ ውስተ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ፍኖት ፡ ጸባብ ፡ ወባቲ ፡ ሀለዉ ፡ አአባን ፡ ዓበይት ፡ ዘአስመከ ፡ ቦሙ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ነጌ ። ወእምዝ ፡ አንሥአ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ አሐተ" ፡ አገዳሁ ፡ እንተ ፡ ቅድም ፡ ወአሐደ"”' ፡ እገሪሁ ፡ ዘድኅር ። ወአቡ ነሰ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡ ሠናየ ፡ ዝክር ፡ ማር ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡ አድነነ ፡ ርእሶ ፡ ወኅለፈ ፡
191 3rd miracle of our blessed father mär Sämu’el. One day, while he was going alone in the desert, he met a large elephant on a narrow path. When the elephant saw him, it stopped on this narrow path and could not turn back, for it was massive and the path was filled (by it). When the man of God wanted to go in front of it, he feared frightening (the elephant); this blessed father of ours of beautiful memory, full of faith, upon whom the grace of the Holy Spirit dwelt, stood before the elephant without fear. He addressed it and said to it: 'Move aside for me, give me the path by which I am to pass so that I may go where I want.' As for the elephant, which is called harmäz in the Ge'ez language, it leaned on one of its sides as if it were sleeping on this narrow path; in it, there were large stones on which the elephant was leaning. After that, it lifted for Sämu’ël one of its front thighs and one of its back legs. Our blessed father, of beautiful memory, mär Sämu’ël bowed his head and passed under the two legs that the elephant had lifted for him and went where he wanted.
ማእከለ ፡ ክልኤሆሙ ፡ አእጋሪሁ ፡ ዘአንሥአ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ነጌ ፡ ወሖረ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ዘፈቀደ ኦአበዊየ ፡ ወአኀውየ ፡ አንክሮ ፡ አንክሩ ፡ ግብረ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ብፁ ዓዊ ፡ ሠናየ ፡ ዝክር ፡ ገባሬ ፡ መንክራት ፡ ዘይትኤዛዙ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ አናብስት ፡ አናምርት ፡ ወነጌያት ፡ ከመ ፡ እንተ ፡ ገብር ። ይእዜኒ ፡ ይክድነነ ፡ ትንብልና ፡ ጸሎቱ ፡ ከመ ፡ ወልታ ፡ ስሙር ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ፡
192 My fathers and my brothers, be in wonder at the action of this blessed father of ours, of beautiful memory, the wonder-worker to whom lions, leopards, and elephants obeyed like a servant. Now, may the intercession of his prayer cover us like a proven shield, unto the ages of ages, amen. Fourth miracle: the lions and the leopards obey Sämu’ël

193 4th miracle of the man of God mär Sämu’ël. One day, while he was inside the desert in the midst of lions and leopards, being in friendship with them like a companion, a man came to him to meet with him. As for our blessed father, he ordered these lions and leopards to move away from him so that the man who had come to him would not be frightened when he saw them. After that, mär Sämu’el spoke to him about the greatness of God for the benefit of his soul. When the man refused to listen to his word that he was making known to him, the man


[p. 166] {#po.53.p166}
ጸውዖሙ ፡ ብእሴ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ለእሙንቱ ፡ አናብስት ፡ ወአናምርት ፡ እለ ፡ አዘዞሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይትጋኀሠ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ወመጽኡ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ ፍጡነ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይጥኅሩ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይግበሩ ፡ ፈቃደ ፡ ዚአሁ ፡ ወዐገትዎ ፡ ለውእቱ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይትጌረሙ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ። ወሶበ ፡ ርእዮሙ ፡ ለእልክቱ ፡ ወድቀ ፡ በገጹ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ምድር ፡ ወጸልመ ፡ ልቡናሁ ፡ እምብዝኃ ፡ ፍርሀት ፡ ወረዓድ ፡ ወኢያእ መረ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ዘሀሎ ፡ መካን ። ወካዕበ ፡ አዘዞሙ ፡ ብእሴ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሖሩ ፡ እልክቱ ፡ አናብስት ፡ ወአናምርት ፡ እምኔሁ ። ወአንሥኦ ፡ ለውእቱ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ዘወድቀ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ኦወልድየ ፡ ምንተ ፡ ኮንከ ፡ ወኢክ ህለ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያግብእ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ቃለ ፡ እምብዝኀ ፡ ድንጋፄ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ገብአ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ አእመረ ፡ ልቡናሁ ፡ ወሰገደ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ለብፁዓዊ ፡ ማር ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይትጋነይ ፡ ለቅድስናሁ ፡ አውሥኦ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ መሰልከኒ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ዘከማየ ፡ ወኢያእመርኩ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ግብረከ ፡ ዘከመ ፡ ይትኤዘዙ ፡ አራዊተ ፡ ገዳም ፍ ወይእዜኒ ፡ ኦሆ ፡ ለቃልከ ፡ ወእገብር ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ዘአይዳዕከኒ
of God called the lions and the leopards to whom he had ordered to move away from him; they came to him quickly roaring, to do his will, and surrounded the man, spreading terror upon him. When (he) saw them, he fell on his face to the ground; his intelligence was darkened because of the greatness of the fear and trembling and he did not know the place where he was. The man of God ordered these lions and leopards again to go far from him. He raised the man who had fallen and said to him: 'My son, what is the matter with you?' He could not answer him because of the greatness of the fright. After that, when (the man) came to himself, his spirit knew and he prostrated himself before the blessed mär Sämu’ël, paying homage to his holiness. He answered him and said to him: 'You seemed to me a man like me and I did not know all this way of yours: how the beasts of the desert obeyed you. Now, yes to your word, I will do everything you indicate to me.' He was blessed by (Sämu’el) and went away admiring his wonders.
ወተባረከ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ወሖረ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ያስተዓፅብ ፡ መንክራቲሁ ኦአባ ፡ ሠናየ ፡ ዝክር ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ወብፁዕ ፡ ማሕየዌ ፡ ነፍሳተ ፡ ሰብእ ። አስተምሕር ፡ በእንቲአነ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ርእሰ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ እግዚእ ፡ ለእለ ፡ ተጋባእነ ፡ ለሰሚዐ ፡ ተኣምራቲከ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዛቲ ፡ ምሥዋዕ ። ከመ ፡ ይጸግወነ ፡ ቁመተ ፡ መንገለ ፡ የማን ፡ ምስለ ፡ ቡሩካን ፡ አባግፅ ። ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ።
194 Abbä of beautiful memory, holy and blessed, life-giver of the souls of men, intercede for us before the Head of days, the Lord, for (us) who have gathered to listen to your miracles in this sanctuary, so that he may grant us the place on the right with the blessed sheep, unto the ages of ages, amen.

### Fifth miracle: the furious rhinoceros
፳ ተአምሪሁ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡ ሠናየ ፡ ዝክር ፡ እስራኤላዊ ፡ ገባሬ ፡ መንክራት ፡ ማር ፡ ሳሙኤል ። ወበ ፳-እምነ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ እንዘ ፡ ያንሶሱ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገዳም ፡ ምስለ ፡ ረድኡ ፡ ወሶበ ፡ ርእዮሙ ፡ አርዌ ፡ ሐሪስ ፡ ጐየ ፡ ኀቤሆሙ ፡ በመዓት ፡ ከመ ፣፡ ይቅትሎሙ ።= ወሶበ ፡ ቀርበ ፡ ኀቤሆሙ ፡ አንሥአ ፡ ብእሴ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ እደዊሁ ፡ ቅዱሳተ ፡ ወባረከ ፡ ዲቤሁ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ ለይማእከ ፡ ኀይለ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወይንጻሕከ ፡ ፍጡነ ። ወበጊዜሃ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ፍጻሜ ፡ ቃሉ ፡ ወድቀ ፡ ድኅሪተ ፡ ረስዮ ፡ አርባዕተ ፡ እገሪሁ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ወሞተ ፡ ግብተ ። ወዝ ንቱሰ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡ ርኅሩኃ ፡ ልብ ፡ ለኩሉ ፡ ፍጥረት ፡ ከመ ፡ አም ላኩ ።= ወካዕበ ፡ ባረከ ፡ ዲቤሁ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ተንሥእ ፡ በኀይለ ፡ እግዚእየ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ። ወበጊዜሃ ፡ ተንሥአ ፡ ወሐይወ ፡ ፍጡነ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ አርዌ ፡ ሐሪስ ፡ ዘሞተ ፡ ወሰገደ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ለማር ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡ ከመ ፡ ዘይትጋሃነይ ፡ ገብር ፡ ለእግዚኡ ። ወአብሰ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡ ይቤሎ ፡ ሖር ፡ ውስተ ፡ መካንከ ፡ እምይእዜሰ ፡ ኢታሕስም ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ሰብእ ። ወካዕበ ፡ ሰገደ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ አርዌ ፡
195 5th miracle of this blessed father of ours of beautiful memory, Israelite, wonder-worker, mär Sämu’ël. One day, while he was walking in the desert with his disciple, a rhinoceros, when it saw them, ran toward them in fury to kill them. When it was near them, the man of God lifted his holy hands and made a blessing over it, saying: 'May the power of God overcome you and strike you down quickly.' At that very moment, at the end of his words, (the rhinoceros) fell backward, having put its four legs in the air, and died suddenly. But this blessed father of ours, with a heart compassionate toward every creature, like his God, made a blessing again over (the rhinoceros) and said to it: 'Rise by the power of my Lord Jesus Christ.' At that very moment, the rhinoceros that was dead rose and came back to life quickly; it prostrated itself before mär Sämu’el like a servant paying homage to his lord. The blessed father said to it: 'Go to your lair, henceforth do not do harm to people.' The rhinoceros prostrated itself again before the man of God and did as he had ordered it.
ሐሪስ ፡ ለብእሴ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወገብረ ፡ በከመ ፡ አዘዞ ፥ ኦአባ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡ በዑታቤ ፡ እዴከ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ፈቀደ ፡ ፀቢኦተከ ፡ ለአ ርዌ ፡ ሐሪስ ፡ ዘቀተልኮ ። ወካዕበ ፡ በተሣሀሎ ፡ እምድኅረ ፡ ሞተ ፡ ዘአንሣእኮ ።« አስተምሕር ፡ በእንቲአነ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ዘኩልነ ፡ ናመልኮ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያስምዐነ ፡ አመ ፡ ፅለተ ፡ ቅስት ፡ ቃለ ፡ ፍሥሓ ፡ ወባርኮ ። ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ።
196 Blessed Abbä, you who, when it wanted to attack you, with a sign of the cross of your hand, killed the rhinoceros and who by clemency, after it was dead, also resurrected it, intercede for us before the one whom we adore, so that he may make us hear, on the day of judgment, a word of joy and blessing, unto the ages of ages, amen.

ተኣምሪሁ ፡ ለብፁዓዊ ፡ ማር ፡ ሳሙኤል ። ወበ ፳እምነ ፡ መዋዕል"" ፡ እንዘ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገዳሙ ፡ ለአከ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ ዳዊት ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ መፍቀሬ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ መኩንን ፡ ራትዕ ፡ ምሉአ ፡ ሃይማ ኖት ፡ ሰጊዶ ፡ ምሥራቀ ፡ ወምዕራበ ፡ ሰሜነ ፡ ወደቡበ ። እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ ኦአባ ፡ ተማኅፀንኩአ ፡ በጸሎትከ ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ጸሊ ፡ ሊተ ፡ ኀበ ፡ አምላክከ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይጽብኦ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወያውድቆ ፡ ፍጡነ ፡ በትንብልናከ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ዓላዌ ፡ ሕግ ፡ እስማኤላዊ ፡ እስመ ፡ ፀረ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወከ ሓዴ ፡ ክርስቶስ ። ወአእመረ ፡ አብ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡ ሠናየ ፡ ዝክር?” ፡ ዘወፅአ ፡ ስሙዓቱ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ትርብዕታ ፡ ለምድር ፡ ማር ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ነገረ ፡ በመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ዘሕዱር ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ እምቅድመ ፡ ይብጻሕ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ ላእክ ፡ ዘተፈነወ ፡ እምኅበ ፡ ዳዊት ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ርቱዓ ፡ ሃይማኖት ። ወሖረ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ በሰረገላ ፡ ብርሃን ፡ እንዘ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ መራድ ፡ ማር ፡ ሳሙ ኤል ፡ ወተራከቦ ፡ ለዳዊት ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ወአይድዖ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ሞቱ ፡ ለዓላዊ ፡ ዘከመ ፡ ይፀብእ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ፍጡነ ። ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዐ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ነገረ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ተፈሥሐ ፡ ወተኀህሠየ ፡ ዳዊት ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ዘኀወዖ ፡ ማር ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡ ወበእንተ ፡ ዜና ፡ ሞቱ ፡ ለዓላዊ ፡ ዘይከውን ፡ ፍጡነ ። ወካዕበ ፡ ተናገሩ ፡ በበይናቲሆሙ ፡ ዕበያተ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ ተዋሀቡ ፡ ሰላመ ፡ ወነሥኦ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ሰረገላ ፡ ብርሃን ፡ ከመ ፡ ቀዳሚ ፡ ወአብጽሖ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገዳሙ ፡ እምቅድመ ፡ ጎሐ ፡ ጽባሕ ፡ እንበለ ፡ ያእምሮ ፡ ሰብእ ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ በጽሐ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ላእክ ፡ ዘተፈነወ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ ወአይ ድዖ ፡ መልእክቶ ። አውሥአ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ኦወልድየ ፡ ወአነሂ ፡ አእመርኩ?'' ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ነገረ ፡ እምቅድመ ፡ ትብጻሕ ፡ አንተ ፡ ኀቤየ ፡ ወከሠተ ፡ ሊተ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ እስመ ፡ ፈነወከ ፡ ኀቤየ ፡ ዳዊት ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ሰዓት ፡ ሰጊዶ ፡ ለአምላኩ ፡ ምሥራቀ ፡ ወምዕራበ ፡ ሰሜነ ፡ ወደበቡበ ፡ እስመ ፡
Sixth miracle: Sämu’ël goes on a chariot of light to King Däwit 197 6th miracle of the blessed mär Sämu’ël. One day, while he was in his desert, Däwit the God-loving king, the upright judge full of faith, dispatched to him, having prostrated himself to the east and the west, to the north and the south, saying: 'Abbä, I have committed myself to your holy prayer; pray for me before your God so that the Lord may fight and strike down quickly, by your intercession, this transgressor of the Ismaelite law, for he is an enemy of the Church and a denier of Christ.' The blessed father of beautiful memory, whose reputation had gone out into the four corners of the earth, mär Sämu’ël, knew this affair by the Holy Spirit who resided upon him, before the messenger who had been sent to him from Däwit, the king of right faith, reached him. Mär Sämu’ël went on a chariot of light to Däwit the king while (he) was on campaign and met with him; he let him know about the death of the impious one, how God would fight quickly for him (Däwit). When Däwit the king heard these words from him, he rejoiced and exulted that mär Sämu’el had visited him and at the news of the death of the impious one that would happen quickly. They also conversed about the greatness of God; after that, they gave each other the greeting and the chariot of light took (Sämu’ël) as before and brought him into his desert before the day dawned, without people knowing it. After that, the messenger who had been sent to him arrived and made his message known to him. Our blessed father spoke and said to him: 'My son, I knew this affair before you arrived at me and God revealed to me that Däwit the king had sent you to me at the time of the third hour, having prostrated himself before his God to the east and the west, to the north and the south; indeed he is a friend of the saints and of right faith. Now, my son, return to him and he will tell you everything before you have informed him.'
መፍቀሬ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወርቱዓ ፡ ሃይማኖት ፡ ወይእዜኒ ፡ ኦወልድየ ፡ ግባእ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ ወውእቱ ፡ ይዜንወከ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ እምቅድመ ፡ ታይድዖ ። ወይቤ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ላእክ ፡ ዘዜነወነ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ አንሰ ፡ ተባረኩ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ለብእሴ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወሖርኩ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ኣነክር ፡ መንክራቲሁ ። ወሶበ ፡ በጻሕኩ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ አይደዓኒ ፡ እምቅድመ ፡ እንግሮ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ እስመ ፡ መጽአ ፡ ኅቤየ ፡ ብእሴ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ በሰረገላ ፡ ብርሃን ፡ እምቅድመ ፡ ይኩን ፡ ጊዜ ፡ መንፈቀ ፡ ሌሊት ፡ ወተራከበ ፡ ምስሌየ ፡ ወአነ ፡ ርኢክዎ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ትብጻሕ ፡ አንተ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ ከመዝ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ርእየተ ፡ ገጹ ፡ ወጽሕሙ ፡ ወአካለ ፡ ቆሙ ፡ ወዓዲ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ላእክ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ሰማዕኩ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ነገረ ፡ አንከርኩ ፡ ወሰባሕክዎ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡
198 The messenger who told us this says: 'I was blessed by the man of God and went away wondering at his wonder. When I arrived at the king, he informed me before I spoke to him, saying: “The man of God came to me on a chariot of light before it was the time of the middle of the night and met with me; I saw him before you arrived at him: such is the appearance of his face, his beard, and his stature.”' The messenger also says: 'When I heard these words, I wondered and glorified God who does miracles and wonders for those who love him.'
ዘይገብር ፡ ተኣምረ ፡ ወመንክረ ፡ ለእለ ፡ ያፈቅርዎ ። ኦአባ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡ መዝገበ ፡ ንጽሕ ፡ ማኅደረ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ተጽ ዒነከ ፡ በሰረገላ ፡ ብርሃን ፡ ዘኀወጽኮ ፡ ለንጉሥ ። አስተምሕር ፡ በእንቲአነ ፡ ቅድመ ፡ ግጻዌ ፡ ሥሉስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ መኩንነ ፡ ሥጋ፡ ወነፍስ ፥ ከመ ፡
199 Blessed Abbä, treasure of purity, dwelling of the Holy Spirit, you who, mounted on a chariot of light, visited the king, intercede for us before the personality of the holy Trinity, the judge of the body and the soul, so that


[p. 170] {#po.53.p170}
ይጸግወነ ፡ ምስሌከ ፡ መክፈልተ ፡ ወርስተ ፡ በዓለም ፡ ሐዲስ ። እንተ ፡ ኢትበሊ ፡ ወኢትፈልስ ። ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን
it may grant us with you a portion and an inheritance in the new world that neither ages nor passes away, unto the ages of ages, amen. Seventh miracle: water gushed in the desert
ኒ ተኣምሪሁ ፡ ለብእሴ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ማር ፡ ሳሙኤል ።፡ ወበ-፳እምነ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ ወፅአ ፡ ምስለ ፡ አርዳኢሁ ፡ እምውሳጢተ ፡ ገዳም ፡ ከመ ፡ ይትመጦ ፡ ምሥጢራተ ፡ ቅድሳት ፡ ወእንዘ ፡ የሐውሩ ፡ በፍኖት ፡ ተመ ንደቡ ፡ አርዳኢሁ ፡ ወጸምኡ ፡ በኃጢአ ፡ ማይ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ዘረከቦሙ ፡ ዋዕየ ፡ ፀሐይ ። ወሶበ ፡ ርእየ ፡ ምንዳቤሆሙ ፡ ማር ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡ ሰአለ ፡ ኅበ ፡ እግዚ አብሔር ፡ አምላኩ ፡ ከመ ፡ የሀቦሙ ፡ ዘፈቀዱ ፡ ወሶቤሃ ፡ ተወክፈ ፡ እግዚአብ ሔር ፡ ጸሎቶ ፡ ወዓንቅዓ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ማየ ፡ ሕይወት ፡ ከመ ፡ ይስተዩ ፡ ደቂቁ ። ወውእቱ ፡ ምድር ፡ ይቡስ ፡ ዘሀለዉ ፡ ዲቤሁ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ከመ ፡ እንተ ፡ ገነት ፡ ወወፅኡ ፡ ዓበይት ፡ አእዋም ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ወጸለልዎሙ ። ወሶበ ፡ ርእዩ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ተፈሥሑ ፡ ወተኀሠዩ ፡ ወሰብሕዎ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ወአውሥአ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡ ብእሴ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ማር ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡ ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ለአርዳኢሁ ፡ ንሥኡ ፡ ስተዩ?'' ፡ እምዝንቱ ፡ ማይ ፡ ዘወሀበክሙ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ መፍቀሬ ፡ እጓለ ፡ እመሕያው ፡ ሶበ ፡ ርእየ ፡ ምንዳቤክሙ ። ወገብሩ ፡ እልክቱ ፡ አኀው ፡ በከመ ፡ አዘዞሙ ፡ አብ ፡ መንፈሳዊ ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ ይቤሎሙ ፡ ተንሥኡ ፡ ንሖር ፡ ደቂቅየ ፡ ከመ ፡ ንብጻሕ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ዘኀለይነ ። ወሶቤሃ ፡ ተሰወረ ፡ እምአ
200 7th miracle of the man of God mär Sämu’ël. One day, he went out with his disciples from the interior of the desert to receive the holy mysteries; while they were traveling, his disciples were tormented and were thirsty from lack of water because the heat of the sun touched them. When he saw their torment, mär Sämu’ël petitioned the Lord his God so that he would give them what they wanted; immediately God welcomed his prayer and made the water of life gush for him so that his (spiritual) children might drink. As for the dry land on which they were, it became like a garden, large trees came out of it and shaded them. When they saw this, they rejoiced, exulted, and glorified God. Our blessed father, the man of God, mär Sämu’ël, spoke and said to his disciples: 'Take, drink of this water that God, the friend of the human race, has given you when he saw your torment.' These brothers did as the spiritual father had prescribed to them. After that, he said to them: 'Rise, let us go, my children, to arrive where we decided.' Immediately the water of life disappeared from their eyes and also the trees that had shaded them.
ዕይንቲሆሙ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ማየ ፡ ሕይወት ፡ ወእልክቱኒ ፡ አእዋም ፡ እለ ፡ ጸለ ልዎሙ ። ኦአባ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡ ሠናየ ፡ ዝክር ፡ ኣነክር ፡ አንሰ ፡ ቲሩተ ፡ ዚአከ ፡ ዘከመ ፡ አንቃዕከ ፡ ማየ ፡ እምድር ፡ ይቡስ ፡ ለስቴ ፡ አርዳኢከ ። ይእዜኒ ፡ እስእለከ ፡ እንበለ ፡ አስትቶ ፡ ትባርክ ፡ እሊአከ ። ወትንሥአነ ፡ ዓሥራተ ፡ ምሕረት ፡ በኪዳንከ ። ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ፡=
201 Blessed Abbä of beautiful memory, I wonder at your goodness, at the way you made water gush from the dry earth as a drink for your disciples. Now, I petition you: without negligence bless yours and take us as a tithe of mercy into your covenant, unto the ages of ages, amen.

### Eighth miracle: Sämu’ël predicts the departure of the renegade monk
፳ ተአምሪሁ ፡ ለብፁዕ ፡ ወኅሩይ ፡ ማር ፡ ሳሙኤል ። በ8እምነ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ ተጋብኡ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ካህናት ፡ ወዲያቆናት ፡ ወመነኮሳት ፡ ደቂቀ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ መካን ፡ ወባዕዳንሂ ፡ ብዙኃን ፡ መጽኡ ፡ እምካልእ ፡ መካን ፡ ሶበ ፡ ሰምዑ ፡ ዜናሁ ፡ ለአብ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡ መስተገብረ ፡ ጽድቅ ፡ እንበለ ፡ አጽርዖ ፡ ማር ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ጉባኤ ፡ ዓቢይ ፡ ወአገበርዎ ፡ ለአባስ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይተርጐም ፡ ሎሙ ፡ መጻሕፍተ ፡ ሕግ ፡ ዘይከውን ፡ ለበቀዔተ ፡ ነፍሶሙ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብሉ ፡ ኦአባ ፡ ንስእለከ ፡ ከመ ፡ ታይድዓነ ፡ ወትፈክር ፡ ለነ ፡ እመጻሕፍተ ፡ ሕግ ፡ ዘኢናአምሮ ፡ ንሕነ ፡። ወአቡነሰ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡ ምሉአ ፡ ትሕትና ፡ ተሰጥወ ፡ ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ኅድጉኒ ፡ ኦአኀውየ ፡ አንሰ ፡ ኢያአምር ፡ አላ ፡ እፈቅድ ፡ ከመ ፡ እስማዕ ፡ እምኔክሙ ። ወእሙንቱኒ ፡ አኀው ፡ አገበርዎ ፡ በአጽሕቦ ፡ እስመ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ ይፈቅዱ ፡ ይስምዑ ፡ እምጣዕመ ፡ ቃሉ ፡ አእሚሮሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ጽጉበ ፡ መጻሕፍት ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ወምሉአ ፡ ልቦና ። ወሶበ ፡ ርእየ ፡ ጻህቀ ፡ ልቦሙ ፡ ማር ፡ ሳሙ ኤል ፡ አሐዘ ፡ ይተርጐም ፡ ሎሙ ፡ እመጻሕፍተ ፡ ሐዲስ ፡ ወብሉይ ፡ በበመት ልው ፡ እስከ ፡ ያነክሩ ፡ ሰማዕያን ፡ ሣእሣአ ፡ ቃሉ ፡ ወመክሥተ ፡ አፉሁ ፡፥
202 8th miracle of the blessed and elect mär Sämu’el. One day, the priests, the deacons, and the monks, children of this place, gathered in the holy church. Many others came from a second place when they heard the story of the blessed father, practicing justice without interruption, mär Sämu’ël. It was a great assembly and they forced the abbot to explain to them the books of the Law that were profitable to their soul, saying: 'Abbä, we pray you to make known to us and to explain to us, from the books of the Law, what we do not know.' But our blessed father, full of humility, answered and said to them: 'Leave me, my brothers, I do not know, but I want to hear from you.' The brothers forced him by pressing (him), for all wanted to hear the sweetness of his word, knowing that he was imbued with the Scriptures and full of intelligence. When he saw the zeal of their heart, mär Sämu’ël began to interpret for them the books of the New and Old (Testament) in succession, so that the listeners wondered at the eloquence of his word and (his way of) opening his mouth. reckless, of little knowledge, rose and rose up against the abbot with insolence, (uttering) vain, useless things that did not come from the word of the Scriptures. When the assembly of the holy (attendees) saw the error of this man, it reprimanded him so that he would be silent, but he refused to listen to their word and they wanted to remove him from the midst of the assembly. He still refused to go away from there, for he was (a man) with a stiff neck and a blind heart. He who saw hidden things since his childhood, mär Sämu’ël, said to them: 'Leave him and do not quarrel with him; I saw with my eyes the Holy Spirit withdraw from him. Immediately the spirit of Satan entered into him and the demons clothed him in an impure hyena skin. Behold, you will see what will happen to this man tomorrow morning.' When it was the next day, (the man) abandoned his monastic habit there, went away, joined the pagans, and became a denier of Christ. The (people) gathered, having seen what had happened to him, wondered, glorified God, and celebrated mär Sämu’ël.

203 Blessed Abbä of beautiful memory, full of intelligence like Ezra, companion of the Ecclesiastes, cistern of holiness and treasure of the Scriptures, intercede for us before the Ancient of days (Dan 7:9), head of wisdom, you who clothed with a hyena skin the one who rose against you with insolence before the assembly, so that he may make of us, by your intercession, guests with the fledglings of knowledge at the new wedding, unto the ages of ages, amen. Ninth miracle: Sämu’ël predicts the fate of the seven children of a plowman 204 9th miracle of the man of God mär Sämu’ël. May the blessing of his prayer be with us, amen. He was returning to his desert from the place where he had received the holy mysteries when a plowman saw him; (he) abandoned his oxen, came to him, was blessed by him, took (their) burden from his disciples, and escorted him. The blessed father mär Sämu’ël said to him: 'Return, my son, to your oxen and do not abandon the work of your hands.' But the plowman, hoping for his blessing, escorted them all the more. When the man of God saw the strength of his faith, he spoke and said to him: 'My son, may God bless you and may he cause the blessing of all the saints to rest upon you; return to the work of your hands.' After that, he led him aside, alone, and said to him: 'My son, God has revealed to me secret things about you, which will be profitable to you: behold, there are seven children in your loins who (will be) worthy of the kingdom of heaven; you too will inherit (eternal) life with them.' When the plowman heard this remark from him, he prostrated himself, glorified God, and, having kissed the hands and feet of mär Sämu’el, returned to the task of his hands while rejoicing. After that, the plowman begot, as the man of God had made known to him, seven children in a row; at the time of their entry into the baptism
ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ ወለደ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ሐረሳዊ ፡ በከመ ፡ አይድፆዖ ፡ ብእሴ ፡ እግዚአብ ሔር ፡ ሕፃናተ ፡ በበ ፡ መትልው ፡ ወበጊዜ ፡ በአቶሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ጥምቀተ ፡ ክርስትና ፡ ኮነ ፡ ዕረፍቶሙ ፡ ለኩሎሙ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ይልሀቁ ። ወውእቱኒ ፡ ሐረሳዊ ፡ ኢየኀዝን ፡ በእንተ ፡ ሞተ ፡ ደቂቁ ፡ አላ ፡ ይትፌሣሕ ፡ ወይመስሎ ፡ ከመ ፡ ዘተጸውዑ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ መርዓ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ዘአይድዖ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ብፁ ዓዊ ፡ እምቅድመ ፡ ይኩን ። ወውእቱኒ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ እምድኅረ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ ደቂቁ ፡ ወቦአ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገነተ ፡ ተድላ ፡ ሕይወት ፡ ዘለዓለም ። ኦአባ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡ ሠናየ ፡ ዝክር ፡ ኅሩይ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትስብያዊ ። ካህን ፡ ምዑዝ ፡ ዘውገ ፡ ሌዊ ። ብእሲ ፡ ሰማያዊ ፡ ወመልአክ ፡ ምድራዊ ። ዘአይዳዕኮ ፡ እምቅድመ ፡ ይኩን ፡ ኅቡኣተ ፡ ለሐረሳዊ ። አስተምሕር ፡ በእንቲአነ ፡ ቅድመ ፡ ሥሉስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ማሕየዊ ። ከመ ፡ ይቤዘወነ ፡ እምአፈ ፡ ተኩላ ፡ ነዓዊ ። ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ፡
of the Christian, their death occurred to all, before they had grown up. The plowman did not grieve at the death of his children, on the contrary he rejoiced and it seemed to him that they had been called into the bridal house, because our blessed father had made (it) known to him before (that) happened. He himself died after all his children and entered into the Garden of delights, the eternal life. 205 Blessed Abbä of beautiful memory, elect like (Elijah) the Tishbite, benevolent priest, companion of Levi, heavenly man and earthly angel, you who, before they happened, made known hidden things to the plowman, intercede for us before the life-giving holy Trinity, so that it may redeem us from the mouth of the hunting wolf, unto the ages of ages, amen. Tenth miracle: Sämu’ël heals a pagan child who had swallowed snake venom

206 10th wonder of the blessed of beautiful memory mär Sämu’ël. May the blessing of his prayer be with us unto the ages of ages, amen. There was a small child among the children of the pagans; when he saw his parents eating a snake, having cut its head so that the venom that was in it would not kill them—they had such a habit—this small child, one day, caught a snake, ate (it) with its head without knowing, and died suddenly from the venom of this snake. His parents gathered, made a lamentation, and wept over him. After that, they said among themselves: 'Come, let us bring the small child who is dead to a man who dwells in the desert, for he is a prophet of the Christians; he is called by the name of Sämu’ël.' Having said this, they lifted the small child who was dead and brought him to where mär Sämu’el was; they begged him while weeping to make their son who was dead live again, by interceding before his God. When the blessed father saw the tears and the groans of these pagans, he spoke and said to them: 'By what cause did the death of this small child occur?' The pagans answered and said to him: 'When he ate the head of the snake with its venom, immediately his spirit went out.' After that, the blessed one, wonder-worker, mär Sämu’ël ordered them to silence their lamentation and to move away from him; then he stretched out his hands and prayed to his God, saying: 'Lord God far from anger and full of mercy, God of the living and resurrecter of the dead, now show the richness of your grace upon this small child who is dead and grant him life by your power, for you (are) clement to all the human race and to you belong honor and glory unto the ages of ages, amen.' After having said this, he made a blessing over the corpse, saying: 'By the power of my Lord Jesus Christ, rise from your sleep of death, little child.' Immediately, at the moment of the end of his words, the corpse awoke and his spirit returned upon him. The man of God called his

ወሖሩ ፡ እልክቱ ፡ አረሚ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ብሔሮሙ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይትፌሥሑ ፡ ወያነክሩ ፡ ርእዮሙ ፡ ዘኮነ ፡ ተአምረ ፡ ወመንክረ ፡ እምብእሴ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ፈዋሴ ፡ ድውያን ፡ ወመንሥኤ ፡ ምውታን ፡ ማር ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡ ለባሴ ፡ ንጽሕ ፡ ዘውገ ፡ ተልሜዳን ።
parents and returned their son to them alive. These pagans went to their country rejoicing and wondering, having seen the miracle and wonder that had happened through the man of God, healer of the sick and resurrecter of the dead, mär Sämu’ël, clothed in purity, companion of the Disciples.
መዝገበ ፡ ቅድስና ፡ ሱታፌ ፡ ትጉሃን ፡ አስተምሕር ፡ በእንቲአነ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ብሉየ ፡ አዝማን ፡ አምላከ ፡ ብርሃን ። ከመ ፡ ያስምዐነ ፡ ፍሥሐ ፡ ወሐሜተ ፡ አመ ፡ ዕለተ ፡ ኩነኔ ፡ ወደይን ።= ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ።
207 Treasure of holiness, associate of the Watchers, intercede for us before the Ancient of times, God of light, so that he may make us hear joy and gladness on the day of judgment and punishment, unto the ages of ages, amen.

### Eleventh miracle: Sämu’ël exorcises an obstinate monk
፲ ወ8 ተአምሪሁ ፡ ለብእሴ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ሠናየ ፡ ዝክር ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡ ማር ፡ ሳሙኤል ። ትንብልና ፡ ጸሎቱ ፡ ተሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ። ወበ ፳እምነ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ ገብሩ ፡ ባእሰ ፡ ፪ምኔተ ፡ መነኮሳት ፡ ወስእኑ ፡ ሰብአ ፡ ሀገር ፡ ከመ ፡ ይዕርቅዎሙ ፡ ወነበሩ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይትጋአዙ ፡ ጐንዱየ ፡ መዋዕለ ። ወለአኩ ፡ ሰብአ ፡ ሀገር ፡ እለ ፡ አእመሩ ፡ ጋእዞሙ ፡ ለእልክቱ ፡ መምህራን ፡ ኅበ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡ ማር ፡ ሳሙኤ”ል ፡ ከመ ፡ ይዕርቆሙ ። ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዐ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ነገረ ፡ ጸውዖሙ ፡ ለክልኤሆሙ ፡ ወመጽኡ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ ወተባረኩ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ወሶቤሃ ፡ ከሠተ ፡ አፉሁ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይገሥጾሙ ፡ በኃለ ፡ መጻሕፍት ፡ ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ ይቤሎ ፡ ለ ፳ እምኔሆሙ ፡ በፀበል ፡ አንተ ፡ አባ ሰከ"" ፡ ለከ ፡ እስመ ፡ አንተ ፡ ትዔምዖ ። ወውእቱሰ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ጸዋግ ፡ ኢተ ወክፈ ፡ ነገሮ ፡ ወአበየ ፡ ሰሚዖ ፡ ቃሎ ፡ ወተምዐ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡ ማር ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ለእመ ፡ ኢገበርከ ፡ ዮም ፡ ዘአዘዝኩከ ፡ ይወርድ ፡ ላዕሌከ ፡ መቅሠፍተ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ። ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዐ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ነገረ ፡ ፈርሀ ፡ ወርዕደ ፡ ወገብረ ፡ በከመ ፡ ይቤሎ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡ አት ሐተ ፡ ርእሶ ፡ ወተጋነየ ፡ ለካልኡ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ አበስኩ ፡ ላዕሌከ ፡ እስመ ፡ አነ ፡ አሕዘንኩከ ፡ በከንቱ ። ወሶበ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ከመዝ ፡ ርእየ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡ ማር ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይወፅእ ፡ እምአፉሁ ፡ በአምሳለ ፡ ከይሲ ፡ ዘየአክል ፡ መጣኑ ፡
208 11th miracle of the man of God of beautiful memory, the blessed mär Sämu’ël. May the intercession of his prayer be with us unto the ages of ages, amen. One day, two convents of monks entered into conflict; the people of the country could not reconcile them and they remained in conflict for long days. The people of the country, who knew the conflict of the (two) superiors, dispatched to our blessed father mär Sämu’ël so that he might reconcile them. When he heard this affair, he called the two (men); they came to him and were blessed by him. Immediately he opened his mouth to exhort them with the word of the Scriptures; after that, he said to one of them: 'Say, you, your fault: you are committing an injustice toward him.' But this perverse man did not accept his remark and refused to listen to his word; our blessed father mär Sämu’ël became angry at the man and said to him: 'If you do not do today what I have prescribed to you, the punishment of God will descend upon you.' When he heard this remark, (the man) became afraid, trembled, and did as the blessed one had told him; he bowed his head and asked for forgiveness from his companion, saying: 'I have faulted against you, I have afflicted you without reason.' When he said this, the blessed mär Sämu’ël saw Satan come out of the mouth (of the man) in the aspect of a serpent whose size was seventy cubits. From that day, the man was gentle, patient, and a friend of the brothers.
፳በእመት ። ወእምይእቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ኮነ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ የዋሀ ፡ ወመሥተዓ ግሠ ፡ ወመፍቀሬ ፡ አኀው ፡፥ ኦአባ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡ ሠናየ ፡ ዝክር ፡ ገባሬ ፡ መንክራት ፡ ከመ ፡ ቃለ ፡ አዋዲ ፡ ጸጋዌ ፡ ሰላም ። ኩኩሐ ፡ ሃይማኖት ፡ ከመ ፡ ጴጥሮስ ፡ ወከመ ፡ ጳው ሎስ ፡ መምህር ፡ ዘአውፃእከ ፡ እምአፈ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ በትእዛዘ ፡ ቃልከ ፡ አበስኩ ፡ ላዕሌከ ፡ ለካልኡ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ይብል ፡ ሰይጣን ፡ በአምሳለ ፡ ከይሲ ፡ ርጉም ፍ ይእ ዜኒ ፡ ይኩነነ ፡ ትንብልናከ ፡ ማዕዶት ፡ እምሰርም ፡ ከመ ፡ ተንከተም = ለዓ ለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ።
209 Blessed Abbä of beautiful memory, wonder-worker like the voice of the herald dispenser of peace, rock of faith like Peter and like Paul, (monastic) superior, you who made (these words addressed) to his companion come out of the mouth of the man by the order of your word: 'I have faulted against you,' as Satan was speaking under the aspect of a cursed serpent, now, may your intercession be for us a ferry out of the abyss, like a bridge, unto the ages of ages, amen.

### Twelfth miracle: the cross on Sämu’el’s staff drives away Satan
፲፪ ተአምሪሁ ፡- ለብፁዓዊ ፡ ኅሩይ ፡ ማር ፡ ሳሙኤል ። በረከቱ ፡ ትኩን ፡ ላዕሌነ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ፡
210 12th miracle of the blessed elect mär Sämu’ël. May his blessing be upon us unto the ages of ages, amen.


[p. 178] {#po.53.p178}
ወበ ፳እምነ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ እንዘ ፡ የሐውር ፡ አቡነ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ፍኖት ፡ ተመጢዎ ፡ ምሥጢራተ ፡ ቅድሳት ፡ ቆመ ፡ ሰይጣን ፡ ቅድሜሁ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡ ግድፍ ፡ በትረከ ፡ እምውስተ ፡ እዴከ ፡ ከመ ፡ ንትጋ ደል ፡ አንተ ፡ ወአነ ፡ ዮም ።= አውሥአ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ኢይገድፍ ፡ በትርየ ፡ እምእዴየ ፡ እስመ ፡ ትእምርተ ፡ ሕማም ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ላዕሌከ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ ዲቤሁ ። ወተግኅሠ ፡ ንስቲተ ፡ እምህየ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ሰይጣን ፡ ወቆመ ፡ ከመ ፡ ቀዳሚ ፡ ተመሲሎ ፡ ከመ ፡ እንተ ፡ ጋኔን ፡ ዓቢይ ፡ ዘይበጽሕ ፡ ቆሙ ፡ እስከ ፡ ደመና ፡ ወተረገመ ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያፍርሆ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ አሰስል ፡ በትረከ”" ፡ እምኔከ ፡ ከመ ፡ ንትባአስ ። ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዐ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ብእሴ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ እምጸላኤ ፡ ሠናያት ፡ ይቤ ፡ አኮ ፡ ዘእገድፎ ፡ እምኔየ ፡ አላ ፡ እስዕሞ ፡ ወእሳ ለሞ ፡ ወአዓትብ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ ገጽየ ። ወእምድኅረ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ከመዝ ፡ ማር ፡ ሳሙ ኤል ፡ አንሥአ ፡ እደዊሁ ፡ ወባረከ ፡ በውእቱ ፡ በትር ፡ ዘሀሎ ፡ ዲቤሁ ፡ ትእምርተ ፡ መስቀል ፡ አንጺሮ ፡ መንገለ ፡ ሰይጣን ፡ ወሶቤሃ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ከመ ፡ እንተ ፡ ጢስ ፡ ወነፍጸ ፡ እምኔሁ ። ወአቡነሰ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡ ሰገደ ፡ ሥልሰ ፡ ወሰብሖ ፡ ለአምላኩ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ዘጸገዎ ፡ መዊአ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ጸላኢሁ ፡ ወዐረ ፡
One day, while our blessed father was traveling, having received the holy mysteries, Satan stood before him and said to him: 'Sämu’ël, Sämu’ël, throw your staff far from your hand so that we may wrestle, you and I, today.' The blessed one answered and said to him: 'I will not throw my staff far from my hand, for the sign of the pain (that comes) upon you is upon it.' Satan moved a little way from there and stood (before him) as before, having made himself like a great demon whose height reached the clouds; he had taken on a terrifying appearance for (Sämu’ël) in order to frighten him. He said to the latter: 'Remove your staff from you so that we may fight.' When the man of God heard this from the enemy of good, he said: 'I will by no means throw it far from me; on the contrary, I embrace it, I greet it, and I sign my face with it.' After mär Sämu’el had said this, he raised his hands and made a blessing with this staff upon which was the sign of the cross, looking toward Satan; immediately (the latter) became like smoke and dispersed far from him. Our blessed father prostrated himself three times and glorified his God for having granted him victory over his enemy and the adversary of his Father.
አቡሁ ፡ ኦአብውየ ፡ ወአኀውየ ፡ ደቂቀ ፡ መርጡል ፡ አንክሮ ፡ አንክሩ ፡ ግብረ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡ ሠናየ ፡ ዝክር ፡ ገባሬ ፡ ተኣምር ፡ ማር ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡ ሱታፌ ፡ አውግረ ፡ ስቲን ፡ ወአድባረ ፡ ከርቤ ። ዘአንጸፈ ፡ ፀረ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ ዘፈ ቀደ ፡ ተቃትሎ ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ በዑታቤ ። ያስተምሕር ፡ በእንቲአነ ፡ አመ ፡ ዕለተ ፡ ደይን ፡ ወምንዳቤ ። ወዓዲ ፡ ይንሥአነ ፡ ዓሥራተ ፡ ለኪዳኑ ፡ ንዑ ፡ ኀቤየ ፡ ቡሩካኑ ፡ ለአቡየ ፡ ትረሱ ፡ መንግሥተ ፡ ሰማያት ፡ ዘድልው ፡ ለክሙ ፡ እምቅድመ ፡ ይትፈጠር ፡ ዓለም ፡ እግዚኡ ፡ አመ ፡ ይቤ ፍ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ።
211 My fathers and my brothers, children of the sanctuary, be filled with wonder at the action of this blessed father of ours of beautiful memory, worker of miracles, mär Sämu’ël, associated with the hills of incense and the mountains of myrrh (Song 4:6), who caused the enemy of his Father, who wanted to fight with him, to disperse by the sign of the cross. May he intercede for us on the day of punishment and misfortune, and may he also take us as a tithe for his covenant when his Lord shall have said: 'Come to me, you blessed of my Father, to inherit the kingdom of heaven which (has been) prepared for you before the world was created' (Mt 25:34). In the ages of ages, amen.

### Thirteenth miracle: Sämu’ël reprimands an elephant

212 13th prodigy of our blessed father, the man of God, the second prophet, mär Sämu’el. One day, a large elephant came to the abbot’s dwelling and took a little grass there to eat, while our father was outside. When (the latter) heard the noise of the elephant, he turned his face toward his dwelling to see what was happening. When he saw the elephant eating the grass, (having) taken it from his dwelling, he became angry with it; immediately the elephant fell to the ground and rolled there; it could not rise from there until the man of God mär Sämu’ël permitted (it). After that, the elephant went away, hurrying and turning its eyes from side to side behind it, seized with fear and trembling before the blessed one who had made it fall with a glance of his eyes like (the) throwing of an arrow.

ኦአባ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡ ኣስተዐጽብ ፡ አንሰ ፡ ዕበየ ፡ ጸጋከ ፡ በአንክሮ ፍ ሶበ ፡ ነሥአ ፡ ሣዕረ ፡ እማኅደርከ ፡ በነጽሮ ፡ አዕይንቲከ ፡ ለዓቢይ ፡ ነጌ ፡ ዘትዌግሮ ። አስተምሕር ፡ በእንቲአነ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ርእሰ ፡ ጥበብ ፡ ወአእምሮ ። ከመ ፡ ይረስየነ ፡ ፅሥሠራነ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ነፍሰ ፡ ዚአከ ፡ ንትዋረስ ፡ ሀገሮ ። ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ፡
213 Blessed Abba, I am astonished, in wonder at the greatness of your grace, you who, when an elephant takes grass from your dwelling, (throws to the ground) with a glance of your eyes a great elephant. Intercede for us with the Chief of wisdom and knowledge, so that he may make us guests with your soul so that we may inherit his city, in the ages of ages, amen. Fourteenth miracle: Sämu’ël knows the sin of a monk who fornicated
፲8መንክራቲሁ ፡ ለብእሴ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ማር ፡ ሳሙኤል ወበ ፳እምነ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ ፈጺሞ ፡ ጸሎተ ፡ ፻ ሰዓት ፡ ወፅአ ፡ እማኅደሩ ፡ ወሖረ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ሀለዉ ፡ አርዳኢሁ ፡ ወእንዘ ፡ ይነግሮሙ ፡ በእንተ ፡ መድኃኒተ ፡ ነፍሶሙ ፡ ቃለ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወዓዲ ፡ እምነ ፡ ዜና ፡ ገድሎሙ ፡ ለቅዱሳን ፡ ወዘእንበለ ፡ ይፈጽም ፡ ነጊሮቶሙ ፡ አስተጣፍሐ ፡ እደዊሁ ፡ ወሖሰ ፡ ርእሶ ፡ ወአርመመ ፡ እንዘ ፡ የሐዝን ፡ ወይቴክዝ ። ወሶበ ፡ ርእዩ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ አርዳኢሁ ፡ አውሥኡ ፡ ወይቤልዎ ፡ ኦአባ ፡ ምንተኑ ፡ ኮንከ ፡ ዘመጻእከ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ትት ፌሣሕ ፡ ኀቤነ ፡ ወትትኀሠይ ።ፍ አንተ ፡ በምንት?'' ፡ ትቴክዝ ፡ ግብተ ፡ ወተሐ ዝን ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ትፈጽም ፡ አይድፆተነ ፡ ዘአኀዝከ ፡ ነገረ ። ወዘንተ ፡ ብሂ ሎሙ ፡ ተስእልዎ ፡ በአገብሮ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያጠይቆሙ ፡ ዘኮነ ። ወእምዝ ፡ አው ሥአ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡ ዳግማዊ ፡ ነቢይ ፡ ነጻሬ ፡ ኅቡኣት ፡ ማር ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡ ወይ ቤሎሙ ፡ እስመ ፡ በዛቲ ፡ ሰዓት ፡ አማሰነ ፡ ቅድስናሁ ፡ በዝሙት ፡ ፳ መነኮስ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ብእሲት ፡ አረማዊት ፡ በምድረ ፡ ሮም ፡ ተኅይጦ ፡ እንበይነ ፡ መብ ልዕ ፡ ዘአቅረበት ፡ ሎቱ ። ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዑ ፡ አርዳኢሁ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ነገረ ፡ እም ኔሁ ፡ ደንገፁ ፡ ወፈርሁ ፡ ወተባሀሉ ፡ በበይናቲሆሙ ፡ እፎ ፡ ያአምር ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ከመ ፡ አምላክ ፡ ኀጢአተ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ዘይትገበር ፡ ውስተ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ አጽናፈ ፡ ዓለም ። ወዘንተ ፡ ብሂሎሙ ፡ አርመሙ ፡ ወኢተኅበሉ ፡ ዳግመ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይስአልዎ ፡ ለብእሴ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ እንዘ ፡ ያነክሩ ፡ ንጽሐ ፡ ቅድስ
abroad. 214 14th prodigy of the man of God mär Sämu’el. One day, having finished the prayer of the third hour, he went out of his dwelling and went to where his disciples were. While he was telling them, concerning the salvation of their soul, the word of God and also a story of the (spiritual) combat of the saints, before he had finished speaking to them, he clapped his hands, shook his head, and fell silent, becoming afflicted and saddened. When they saw this, his disciples spoke and said to him: 'Abbä, what is the matter with you, you who came to us rejoicing and exulting? Why do you suddenly become sad and afflicted before finishing making known to us the subject you began?' Having said this, they asked him insistently to teach them what had happened. After that, the blessed one, the second prophet, the spectator of hidden things, mär Sämu’ël answered and said to them: 'At this hour, a monk has spoiled his holiness by fornication with a Greek woman in the land of Rome, having been seduced because of the food she presented to him.' When his disciples heard this statement from him, they were shaken and said among themselves: 'How does this man know like God the sin of men which is committed in all the ends of the universe?' Having said this, they fell silent and did not dare to question the man of God again, while marveling at the purity of his holiness. As for our blessed father, he remained praying to his God for this monk so that (the latter) would not destroy his soul futilely without penance.
ናሁ ። ወአቡነሰ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡ ነበረ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይጹሊ ፡ ኀበ ፡ አምላኩ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ መነኮስ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢያጥፍኣ ፡ ለነፍሱ ፡ በከንቱ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ንስሐ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ተወከፈ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ስእለቶ ፡ ለማር ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡ ወአምጽኦ ፡ ለውእቱ ፡ መነኮስ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ ነቢሮ ፡ ማእከለ ፡ አርዳኢሁ ፡ ወተባረከ ፡ እምብእሴ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ። ወሶቤሃ ፡ አውሥአ ፡ አብ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡ ዘኅዱር ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ጸጋ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ማር ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ ኦወልድየ ፡ ለምንትኑ ፡ አማሰንከ ፡ አልባሰ ፡ ቅድስናከ ፡ በዝ ሙት ፡ ምስለ ፡- ብእሲት ፡ አረማዊት ፡ በብሔረ ፡ ሮሜ ፡ ተሐይጠከ ፡ በፍ ቅረ ፡ መብልዕ ፡ በዕለተ ፡ ረቡዕ ፡ እምድኅረ ፡ ኅለፈ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ፻ ሰዓት ፡ ተዓዲ ወከ ፡ ትእዛዘ ፡ ሐዋርያት ፡ ዘሠርዑ ፡ ለመሃይምናን ።= ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዐ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ነገረ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ፈርሀ ፡ ወርዕደ ፡ ወአኃዞ ፡ ዐቢይ"'" ፡ ድንጋፄ ፡ ወኢክህለ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያግብእ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ቃለ ፡ ዘሰምዐ ፡ እምኔሁ ። ወተንሥአ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ እምነ ፡ አርዳኢሁ ፡ አውሥአ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ኦአባ ፡ ለምንትኑ ፡ ትከ
215 After that, God accepted the request of mär Sämu’ël and brought this monk to (the latter) while he was sitting in the midst of his disciples; he was blessed by the man of God. Then the blessed father, upon whom dwelt the grace of the Holy Spirit, mär Sämu’ël, spoke, saying: 'My son, why have you spoiled your garment of holiness by fornication with a Greek woman in the land of Rome, having been seduced by the love of food on Wednesday after the moment of the third hour had passed, you having transgressed the commandment of the apostles which they established for the believers?' When the man heard this statement, he became afraid and trembled; a great trouble seized him and he could not answer the word he had heard from (Sämu’ël). One of the disciples of (the latter) stood up, spoke, and said to (the latter): 'Abbä, why do you reveal the sin of this man before the
ሥት''' ፡ ኀጢአቶ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ በቅድመ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ወትዘልፎ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ጌጋዩ « አውሥአ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ኦወልድየ ፡ ኢትትሐዘብኬ ፡ ወኢተኀኅሊ ፡ ወኢ ይምሰልከ ፡ ዘተናገርኩ ፡ በገስዐትየ ፡ በከ ፡ ፅሩዐ ፡ ዘከመ ፡ ረከብኩ ፡ አላ ፡ ተበዋኅኩ ፡ እምኀበ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ከመ ፡ እዝልፎ ፡ ወእክሥት ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ኃጢአቶ ፡ ማእከለ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ እስመ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ፍርሃት ፡ ወኃፍረት ፡ ዘዐገቶ ፡ ሰረየ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ አበሳሁ ። ወእምድኅረ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ከመዝ ፡ ለረድኡ ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ይቤሎ ፡ ለውእቱ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ኦወልድየ ፡ ናሁ ፡ ተዐደዋ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ለጌጋይከ ፡ ወአእትት ፡ ፍርሀተ ፡ እምኔከ ፡ ተዐቀብ ፡ እምይ እዜ ፡ ወኢተአብስ?" = ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዐ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ቃለ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ አይድዓ ፡ ዥሎ ፡ ኃጢአተ ፡ ዘረከቦ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ኃፍረት ፡ በቅድመ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ እለ ፡ ሀለዉ ፡ ህየ ፡ በከመ ፡ ርእየ ፡ ብእሴ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ። ወእምዝ ፡ ተባ ረከ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ወአማኅፀነ ፡ ነፍሶ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ ወሖረ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ያነክር ፡ ወያስ
people and reprimand him concerning his fault?' (Sämu’ël) answered and said to him: 'My son, do not therefore imagine, do not think, and do not suppose that I have uttered useless and idle things such as I found (them) in my agitation; on the contrary, I have received a mandate from God to reprimand him and to reveal his sin for him in the midst of the people, for, because of this fear and shame which has invested him, God has forgiven his fault.' After having said this to his disciple, he said again to the man: 'My son, behold, God has remitted your fault and I remove the fear from you; guard yourself henceforth and do not sin.' When he heard this word from him, (the man) made known all the sin that had touched him, without shame, before all the people who were there, just as the man of God had seen. After that, he was blessed by the latter, entrusted his soul to him, and went away marveling and celebrating his holiness.

216 Blessed Abba of beautiful memory, spectator of hidden things, second prophet, you who by the intercession of your prayer saved from the death of sin the man who had defiled his holiness by fornication with a Greek woman in the land of Rome, on the advice of the Serpent, intercede for us with your God, so that he may place our share of inheritance in your holy city, in the ages of ages, amen. Fifteenth miracle: the saliva of Sämu’ël heals a blind child

217 15th miracle of the blessed one, worker of prodigies, dwelling of the Holy Spirit, mär Sämu’ël. May his prayer, his blessing, and the gift of his help be with us, in the ages of ages, amen. When the story of his prodigies had been heard everywhere, namely that he healed the sick and resurrected the dead by the power of his God, after that, one day, the parents of a young boy whose eyes had been blind since his childhood took him; they brought him to where the man of God mär Sämu’ël was and begged the latter, saying: 'Abbä, grant light by your prayer to this blind man whom we have brought to you.' As for the blind man, he cried out and said: 'Abbä, when I heard your story, I came to you rejoicing; now, I have entrusted myself to the name of your God so that you might remove the darkness from my eyes and grant them light so that they might see.' When he heard this statement from the blind man and the supplication of his parents, our blessed father mär Sämu’ël stood up and prayed to his God, for he was tender of heart and interceded for every creature so that (God) would send his clemency and his mercy upon all his works. After that, he received the blind man from his parents and made him enter his dwelling; he took (of) his holy saliva in his hands and anointed his eyes (with it), saying: 'May your eyes open by the name of Jesus Christ, the dispenser of life, the Lord of the luminaries.' Immediately, the blind man looked and saw everything that was before him; immediately, he glorified


[p. 184] {#po.53.p184}
ዕበየ ፡ ቅድስናሁ ። ወነሥኦ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡ ለውእቱ ፡ ዘሐይወ ፡ አዕይንቲሁ ፡ እምነ ፡ ማኅደሩ ፡ ወአወፈዮሙ ፡ ለአዝማዲሁ ፡ ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ኦደቂቅየ ፡ ዑቁ ፡ ወተዐቀቡ ፡ ወኢትንግሩ ፡ ወኢለመኑሂ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢይትመዓዕ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ላዕ ሌክሙ ። ወእሙንቱሂ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ርእዩ ፡ ወልዶሙ ፡ ዘሐይወ ፡ አዕይንቲሁ ፡ በት ንብልናሁ ፡ ለብእሴ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ገብኡ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ብሔሮሙ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይትፌሥሑ ፡ ወይትኃሠዩ ፡ ወያነክሩ ፡ እምነ ፡ ተአምሪሁ ፡ ለብፁዓዊ ፡ ማር ፡
God, celebrating the greatness of his holiness. The blessed one removed from his dwelling the one whose eyes had been healed, returned (him) to his parents, and said to them: 'My children, pay attention, guard yourselves, and do not speak to anyone so that God may not become angry with you.' As for them, when they saw their son whose eyes had been healed by the intercession of the man of God, they returned to their country rejoicing, exulting, and marveling at the miracle of the blessed mär Sämu’ël.
ሳሙኤል ። ኦአበዊየ ፡ ወአኃውየ ፡ ሕዝብ ፡ መሲሓዊያን ፡ አንክሮ ፡ አንክሩ ፡ ተኣምራተ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡ ዘኢይትከሀል ፡ ለፕልቆ ፡ ዘይጹጉ ፡ ብርሃነ ፡ ለዐይነ ፡ ዕዉር ፡ ቀቢኦ ፡ ምራቆ ። ወይእዜኒ ፡ ያስተምሕር ፡ በእ ንቲአነ ፡ ኀበ ፡ አምላኩ ፡ ጽሂቆ ። ከመ ፡ ናግብእ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ዘምስለ ፡ ረባሑ ፡ አመ ፡ ይትሐሰበነ ፡ ዘአወፈየነ ፡ ወርቆ ።= ለዓለመ?" ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ። ፲5መንክራቲሁ ፡ ለብእሴ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ኅሩይ ፡ ወንጹሕ ፡ ካህን ፡ ወድንግል ፡ ማር ፡ ሳሙኤል ። ትንብልና ፡ ጸሎቱ ፡ ትኩን ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ።= ወበ ፳እምነ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ እንዘ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገዳሙ ፡ ጽህቀ ፡ በነፍሱ ፡ ከመ ፣፡ ይትመጦ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ወደሙ ፡ ለእግዚእነ ፡ ወመድኀኒነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ወሶቤሃ ፡ በይእቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ተንሥአ ፡ ወሖረ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ረድኡ ፡ ወበጽሐ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ፳መካን ፡ ወሶበ ፡ ርእይዎ ፡ ደቂቃ ፡ ለይእቲ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ተመጠውዎ ፡ በፍሥሐ ፡ ወበኀዔት ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብሉ ፡ እስመ ፡ አምጽአ ፡ ለነ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ብእሴ ፡ ጻድቀ ፡ መናኔ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ዘይነብር ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገዳም ፡ ከመ፡ ንትመጦ ፡ በእደዊሁ ፡ ምሥጢራተ ፡ ቅድሳት ፡ ጌሠመ ፡ በበዓለ ፡ ዕረፍታ ፡ ለማርያም ። ወሶበ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ሳኒታ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ አዕርጎ ፡ ምሦርባን ፡ አስተብዮዕዎ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ ደቂቀ ፡ መካን ፡ ከመ ፡ ይባእ ፡ ለተክህኖ ።= ወአቡነሰ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡ ምሉአ ፡ ትሕትና ፡ ማር ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡ አውሥአ ፡ ወይቤሎሙ ፡ አኦአኀዊየ ፡ አንሰ ፡ ኢኮንኩ ፡ ድልው ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ግብር ፡ ዘትብሉ ፡ አንትሙ ፡ አላ ፡ መጻእኩ ፡ ተሰፊውየ ፡ ከመ ፡ እሳተፍ ፡ ምሥጢራተ ፡ ቅድሳት ፡ እምኔክሙ ።= ወሶበ ፡ ርእዩ ፡ ትሕትናሁ ፡ ለብፁዓዊ ፡ ማር ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡ ገባሬ ፡ መንክራት ፡ አገበርዎ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይስእልዎ ፡ በአጽሕቦ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይትለአክ ፡ ለአዕርጎ ፡ ሦርባን ።= ወውእቱኒ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ርእየ ፡ ጽንዐ ፡ ሃይማኖቶሙ ፡ ቦአ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቅድሳተ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ ለተክህኖ ፡ ወሰ ገደ ፡ ሥልሰ ፡ ቅድመ ፡ ምሥዋዕ ። ወእምዝ ፡ አንሥአ ፡ አዕይንቲሁ ፡ ወጸለየ ፡ ኀበ ፡ አምላኩ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ ኦእግዚእየ ፡ ወአምላኪየ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስ ቶስ ፡ ደምስስ ፡ አበሳየ ፡ ወስረይ ፡ ሊተ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ኃጢአትየ ፡ ወተወከፍ ፡ እምኔየ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ዘኣቄርብ ፡ ለከ ፡ ምርባነ ።= ወለእሉሂ ፡ ሕዝብ ፡ ረስዮሙ ፡ ድልዋነ ፡ ወንጹሓነ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይትመጠዉ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ እስመ ፡ ለከ ፡ ስብሐት ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ።
218 My fathers and my brothers, Christian people, be filled with wonder at the miracles which it is not possible to count of this blessed father of ours, he who grants light to the eyes of the blind, having anointed (them) with his saliva. Now, may he intercede for us with his God, being diligent, so that we may bring back to him with its gain his gold which he has entrusted to us (Mt 25:14-30), at the moment when he will ask us for an account, in the ages of ages, amen. Sixteenth miracle: the heavenly court descends at the mass of Sämu’ël. 219 16th prodigy of the man of God, chosen and pure, priest and virgin, mär Sämu’ël. May the intercession of his prayer be with us in the ages of ages, amen. One day, while he was in his desert, he desired in his soul to receive the body and blood of Our Lord and Savior Jesus Christ. Immediately, that day, he stood up, went with his disciple, and arrived at a (certain) place; when the children of that church saw him, they received him with joy and with gladness, saying: 'God has brought us this just man, despiser of the world, who dwells in the desert so that we might receive from his hands the holy mysteries tomorrow, during the feast of the dormition of Mary.' When it was the next day, at the moment of the offering of the (Eucharistic) sacrifice, all the children of the place begged him to enter to act as priest. But our blessed father, full of humility, mär Sämu’ël spoke and said to them: 'My brothers, I am not worthy of this thing that you say; on the contrary, I came hoping to receive from you the holy mysteries.' When they saw the humility of the blessed mär Sämu’ël, the worker of prodigies, all pressed him, praying to him insistently so that he would officiate at the offering of the sacrifice. As for him, when he saw the strength of their faith, he entered into the holy of holies to act as priest and prostrated himself three times before the altar. After that, he raised his eyes and prayed to his God, saying: 'My Lord and God Jesus Christ, erase my fault, forgive all my sin, and accept from me this offering that I present to you. Make these people ready and pure so that they may receive of it, for to you (belongs) the glory in the ages of ages, amen.'


[p. 186] {#po.53.p186}
ወሶበ ፡ ፈጸመ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ጸልዮ ፡ ካህን ፡ ሣውዕ ፡ ማር ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡ ወረደ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ሰረገላሁ ፡ ኪሩባዊ ያን ፡ ርቡዓነ ፡ ገጽ ። ወመጽአት ፡ ካዕበ ፡ እግዝእትነ ፡ ማርያም ፡ እሙ ፡ ለብ ርሃን ፡ ምስለ ፡ ኩሎን ፡ ደናግል ፡ ንጹሓት ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይተልውዋ ፡ ብዙኃን ፡ ኃራ ፡ ሰማይ ። ወይእቲኒ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ወኮነት ፡ ርብብተ ፡ ከመ ፡ ሰማይ ፡ ተለዓለ ፡ እምድር ፡ ወተወለጠ ፡ አርአያሃ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ኅብረ ፡ እሳት ። ወእ ምድኅረዝ ፡ ሶበ ፡ አኀዘ ፡ ይቀድስ ፡ በበመትልው ፡ አቡነ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡ ማር ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡ አስተጋብአ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ብዙኃን ፡ ነፍሳተ" ፡ ሰብእ ፡ እለ ፡ ይከውኑ ፡ ዓሥራተ ፡ ዚአሁ ፡ እምነ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ በሓውርት ። ወመጽኡ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይሰሩ ፡ ከመ ፡ እንተ ፡ አርጋብ ፡ ጽዕድዋን ፡ ወሶበ ፡ በጽሑ ፡ ህየ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ጉባኤ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ዘኢይትጐለቀኑ ።= ወእምድኅረ ፡ ፈጸመ ፡ ብእሴ ፡ እግዚአ ብሔር ፡ አዕርጎ ፡ ምርባን ፡ ይቤሎ ፡ መድኅን ፡ ኦሳሙኤል ፡ ኅሩይየ ፡ ኅትም ፡ በእደዊከ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ፍጽሞሙ ፡ ለእልክቱ ፡ ነፍሳት ፡ እለ ፡ አስተጋባእክዎሙ ፡ እምነ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ በሓውርት ፡ ከመ ፡ ይኩኑ ፡ ዓሥራተ ፡ ዚአከ ። ወውእቱኒ ፡ ገብረ ፡ በከመ ፡ አዘዞ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይትፌሣሕ ፡ ወይትኃሠይ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ዘጸገዎ ፡ ዓሥራተ ፡ ብዙኃን ፡ ንፍሳተ ፡ ሰብእ ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ ነሥኦ ፡ እግዚ እነ ፡ ወአዕረጎ ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ኢየሩሳሌም ፡ ሰማያዊት ፡ ወጸገዎ ፡ በህየ ፡
220 When the priest sacrificer mär Sämu’ël had finished this prayer, Our Lord Jesus Christ descended to him with his chariot, the cherubim with four faces. Also came Our Lady Mary, the mother of the Light, with all the pure virgins, many soldiers of heaven following her. As for the church, it was extended like the sky; it rose above the earth and its appearance was changed to the color of fire. After that, when our blessed father abbä Sämu’ël began to consecrate step by step, God gathered for him many human souls—who would be his tithe—from all countries; they came flying like white doves; when they had arrived there, the assembly was numerous, innumerable. After the man of God had finished the offering of the sacrifice, the Savior said to him: 'Sämu’ël my chosen one, put a seal of your hand on the forehead of these souls that I have gathered from all countries so that they might be your tithe.' (Sämu’ël) did according to what Our Lord had ordered him, rejoicing and exulting that he had granted him, as a tithe, many souls of men. After that, Our Lord took him and made him ascend with him into the heavenly Jerusalem and granted him there a share and an inheritance, him and all those who had received a seal from his hand. Of this was witness one of the disciples of the abbot, upon whom dwelt the Holy Spirit.
መክፈልተ ፡ ወርስተ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ወለኩሎሙ ፡ እለ ፡ ተኃትሙ ፡ በእደዊሁ ። በዝ ንቱ ፡ ነገር ፡ ኮነ ፡ ስምዐ ፡ ፳እምነ ፡ አርዳኢሁ ፡ ለአባስ ፡ ዘኅዱር ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ጸጋ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ። ኦአባ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡ ሠናየ ፡ ዝክር ፡ እንዘ ፡ ኣስተኣረይ ፡ መንክራቲከ ፡ ለዕበየ ፡ ጸጋከ ፡ ውዳሴ ፡ እዌሩ ። ሶበ ፡ ቦእከ ፡ ለአዕርጎ ፡ ሞ'ርባን ፡ ዘወረደ ፡ ኀቤከ ፡ ለአብ ፡ በኩሩ ። ምስለ ፡ ርቡዓነ ፡ ገጽ ፡ ኪሩባዊያን ፡ መንበሩ ። አስ ተምሕርኬ ፡ ይእዜኒ ፡ በእንቲአነ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይጸግወነ ፡ መክፈልተ ፡ ወር ስተ ፡ ለተኀትሞ ፡ በእደዊከ ፡ ቅዱሳት ፡ ምስለ ፡ እለ ፡ ተዓሠሩ ። ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን
221 Blessed Abba of beautiful memory, in collecting your prodigies, to the greatness of your grace I cast praise, you to whom, when you entered to offer the sacrifice, descended the First-Born of the Father with the cherubim with four faces, his throne; intercede therefore now for us with him so that he may grant us a share and an inheritance with those who have been invited to be marked with a seal by your holy hands, in the ages of ages, amen.

### Seventeenth miracle: Sämu’ël crosses a watercourse on a lion
፲ጻ፪ ተአምሪሁ ፡ ለብፁዓዊ ፡ ሠናየ ፡ ዝክር ፡ ማር ፡ ሳሙኤል ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ ተሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ። ወበ-፳እምነ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ እንዘ ፡ የሐውር ፡ ምስለ ፡ ረድኡ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ውሳ ጢተ ፡ ገዳም ፡ በከመ ፡ ልማዱ ። ሶበ ፡ በጽሐ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ዐቢይ ፡ ፈለግ ፡ ተን ሥአ ፡ እምጽንፈ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ማይ ፡ ዐቢይ ፡ ፳አንበሳ ፡ ወመጽአ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ ብእሴ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወሶበ ፡ ርእዮ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡ ማር ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡ ለውእቱ ፡ አንበሳ ፡ አሐዘ ፡ በእደዊሁ ፡ ወተጽዕነ ፡ ዲቤሁ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ረድኡ ፡ ወዐደወ ፡ ውእተ ፡ ፈለገ'" ፡ ወእምዝ ፡ አብጽሖሙ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ አንበሳ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ውሳ
222 17th miracle of the blessed one of beautiful memory mär Sämu’ël. May his prayer and his blessing be with us in the ages of ages, amen. One day, while he was going with his disciple into the interior of the desert, according to his habit, as he arrived at a great river, a lion rose from the edge of this great watercourse and came to where the man of God was. When the blessed mär Sämu’ël saw the lion, he grabbed it with his hands, mounted it with his disciple, and crossed the river. After that, the lion brought them into the interior of the desert, to where they had decided to go.
ጢተ ፡ ገዳም ፡ ኀበ ፡ ዘሐለዩ ፡ ኀዊረ ። ኦአበዊየ ፡ ወአኀዊየ ፡ አንክሮ ፡ አንክሩ ፡ ግብረ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡ ማኅፈደ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ለባሕረ ፡ ንጽሕ ፡ ምእላዱ ፡ ተጽዒኖ ፡ ዲበ ፡ አንበሳ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ወልዱ ፡ ባሕረ ፡ ግሩመ ፡ ዘየዐዱ ፡ ዖፆፈ ፡ አእምሮ ፡
223 My fathers and my brothers, be filled with wonder at the action of this blessed father of ours, citadel of the Holy Spirit, cistern of the river of purity, he who, mounted on a lion with his (spiritual) son, crossed a fearsome river; winged being of knowledge, affliction of the desert


[p. 188] {#po.53.p188}
ንጽራረ ፡ ገዳም ፡ ምስለ ፡ አናምርት ፡ ወአናብስት ፡ ወነጌያት ፡ ዘይሬሲ ፡ ሀልዎ ፡ ምስሌሆሙ ፡ ወትረ ፡ ልማዱ ። ይእዜኒ ፡ ይባርከነ ፡ ለእሊአሁ ፡ በንጽ ሕት ፡ እዱ ። ወይንሥአነ ፡ ዓሥራተ ፡ ለኪዳኑ ፡ እምከብካበ ፡ መርዓ ፡ ዓላዊ ያነ ፡ ሕግ ፡ አመ ፡ ይሰድዱ ። ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ፡
with the leopards, the lions, and the elephants, whose habit has established (his) constant dwelling with them. Now, may he bless us, (us) his own, with his pure hand and may he receive us as a tithe for his covenant when the people of impious faith will be expelled from the nuptial feasts, in the ages of ages, amen. Eighteenth miracle: Sämu’ël pays a visit to Endreyäs…
፲፳ መንክራቲሁ ፡ ለብእሴ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ማር ፡ ሳሙኤል ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ። ወበ ፳-እምነ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ እንዘ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ውሳጤ ፡ ገዳም ፡ ይቤሎ ፡ ለረድኡ ፡ ተንሥእኬ ፡ ኦወልድየ ፡ ከመ ፡ ንሖር ፡ ወነሐውጾ ፡ ለእንድርያስ ፡ ፍቁርነ ፡ እስመ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዐቢይ ፡ ደዌ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ ይእዜ ። ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዐ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ነገረ ፡ እምነ ፡ መምህሩ ፡ አውሥአ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ረድእ ፡ ወይ ቤሎ ፡ ኦአባ ፡ በአይቴ ፡ ንክል ፡ በጺሐ ፡ ዮም ፡ ኅቤሁ ፡ እስመ ፡ ርሑቅ ፡ ብሔር ፡ ወየአክል ፡ መጠነ ፡ ምሕዋረ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ አርባዐ ፡ ዕለተ ። ወአቡነ ፡ ብፁ ...”
224 18th prodigy of the man of God mär Sämu’ël. May his prayer and his blessing be with us in the ages of ages, amen. One day, while our blessed father was in the interior of the desert, he said to his disciple: 'Rise therefore, my son, so that we may go and pay a visit to Endreyäs our beloved, for he is in a grave illness now.' When he heard this statement from his master, the disciple answered and said to him: 'Abbä, how can we go today to him, for (his) country (is) far and it requires in fact four days of human stages.' Our blessed father…

### Nineteenth miracle: Sämu’ël heals the sick on the way
ስዕየ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ወነገፈ ፡ ቅራፈ ፡ ሞጐሰሊሁ ፡ ጥዕየ ፡ እምጻማሁ ፡ ወሐይወ ፡ እምደዌሁ ፡ ወአርአያሁኒ ፡ ተወለጠ ፡ በይእቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ሠናየ ፡ ላሕይ ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ ሰገደ ፡ ለብእሴ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ በእንተ ፡ ዘፈወሶ ፡ እምደዌሁ ፡ ወተባረከ ፡ እምኔሁ ። እንዘ ፡ ይገብእ ፡ ብሔሮ ፡ ተራ ከበ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ፳ድዉይ ፡ በውስተ ፡ ፍኖት ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ዘሐይወ ፡ አይቴኑ ፡ ተሐውር ፡ ኦእጥየ ። ተሰጥወ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ኅበ ፡ አባ ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡ ከመ ፡ ይፈውሰኒ ፡ እስመ ፡ ብየ ፡ ዐቢየ ፡ ደዌ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ከርሥየ ።= ወአው ሥአ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ዘሐይወ ፡ እስመ ፡ አነሂ ፡ እምኀቤሁ ፡ ተፈዊ ስየ ፡ መጻእኩ ፡ በትንብልና ፡ ጸሎቱ ፡ እምሕማመ ፡ ዝልጋሴ ፡ ዘነበረ ፡ ዲቤየ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ገሰሰኒ ፡ በእደዊሁ ፡ ቅዱሳት ። ተሰጥወ ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ድዉይ ፡ ወይ ቤሎ ፡ ኦእትየ ፡ አምረኒኬ ፡ ኅበ ፡ አንበረ ፡ እደዊሁ ፡ ዲቤከ ፡ ብእሴ ፡ እግዚአ ብሔር ። ወአውሥአ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ገሠሠኒ ፡ ኩለንታየ ። ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዐ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ድዉይ ፡ ሰፍሐ ፡ እደዊሁ ፡ ወገሰሰ ፡ ኩለንታሁ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይትአመን ፡ ትንብልና ፡ ጸሎቱ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡ ማር ፡ ሳሙኤል ። ወካዕበ ፡ ገሰሳ ፡ ለመካነ ፡ ሕማሙ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ ተማኅፀንኩ ፡ በጸሎቱ ፡ ለአባ ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡ ፈዋሴ ፡ ድዉያን ፡ ወሶቤሃ ፡ ሐይወ ፡ ፍጡነ ፡ እምጻማ ፡ ደዌሁ ፡ ዘነበረ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ እምብዙኅ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ ወእምዝ ፡ አይድዖ ፡ ለካልኡ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ዘተፈ ወሰ ፡ እምደዌሁ ። ወውእተ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ሰገዱ ፡ ክልኤሆሙ ፡ ኅቡረ ፡ ወሰብሕዎ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ወገብኡ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ብሔሮሙ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ያስተዓፅቡ ፡ መንክራ
225 … from him and cut the crust of his wounds; he healed from his ailment and recovered from his illness. His appearance was changed on that day and he was of beautiful appearance. After that, he prostrated himself before the man of God because the latter had healed him of his illness and he was blessed by him. While he was returning to his country, he met a sick man on the way; he who had been healed said to the latter: 'Where are you going, my brother?' (The other) answered and said to him: 'To abbä Sämu’ël so that he may heal me, for I have a great ailment in my belly.' He who had been healed answered and said to him: 'I too am coming from him, having been healed by the intercession of his prayer of the leprosy that dwelt upon me, when he touched me with his holy hands.' The sick man answered again and said to him: 'My brother, show me therefore where the man of God put his hands on you.' (The other) answered and said to him: 'He touched me all over.' When he heard this, the sick man extended his hands and touched (the other) all over, having confidence in the intercession of the prayer of our blessed father mär Sämu’ël. He touched again the place of his ailment, saying: 'I have entrusted myself to the prayer of abbä Sämu’ël, the healer of the sick.' Immediately he healed quickly from the pain of the illness that had dwelt in him for many days. After that, he informed the other that he was healed of his illness. At that moment, both prostrated themselves together, glorified God, and returned to their country celebrating the prodigies of the man of God mär Sämu’ël.
ቲሁ ፡ ለብእሴ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ማር ፡ ሳሙኤል ። ኦአበውየ ፡ ሕዝብ ፡ መሲሓውያን ፡ አንክሮ ፡ አንክሩ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ብፁ ዓዊ ፡ ሰሚዐክሙ ፡ ተአምራቲሁ ። ዘይፌውስ ፡ ዝልጉሰ ፡ ገሲሶ ፡ በእደዊሁ ።= ወዓዲ ፡ ዘያሐዩ ፡ ድዉየ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ በፍኖት ። ዘእንበለ ፡ ይብጻሕ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ለከፈ ፡ በተአምኖ ፡ ትንብልናሁ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ዘአንበረ ፡ እዴሁ ። ይእ ዜኒ ፡ ያስተምሕር ፡ በእንቲአነ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ፻ ገጽ ፡ ዘኢይትባዓድ ፡ ህላዌሁ ። ከመ ፡ ይጸግወነ ፡ መክፈልተ ፡ ምስሌሁ ። ኀበ ፡ ዘተወፈየ ፡ መካነ ፡ ተድላሁ ። ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን
226 My fathers, Christian people, be filled with wonder, having heard the miracles of our blessed father, he who heals the leper by touching (him) with his hands, who also heals the sick man who was on the way, without approaching him, when (the latter) touched, with confidence in his intercession, (the place) where he put his hand. Now, may he intercede for us with the Three Persons whose nature is not different so that they may grant us a share with him, where he has received the place of his delights, in the ages of ages, amen. Twentieth miracle: Sämu’ël blesses all of Ethiopia mounted on a chariot


[p. 190] {#po.53.p190}
፳ ምንክራቲሁ ፡ለአቡነ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡ መዝገበ ፡ ጥበብ ፡ ምሉአ ፡ ልቡና ፡ ኦፈ ፡ አእምሮ ፡ ንጽራረ ፡ ገዳም ፡ ፍጻሜ ፡ ሕግ ፡ ወዐቃቤ ፡ ትእዛዝ ፡ ማር ፡ ሳሙኤል ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ፡ ወበ ፳እምነ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ ይቤ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ዘይነብር ፡ ውስተ ፡ ምድረ ፡ ዳሞት ፡ ዘኅዱር ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ጸጋ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ። መጽአ ፡ ኅቤየ ፡ አባ ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡ ዘገዳመ ፡ ዋሊ ፡ ተጽዒኖ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ሰረገላ ፡ ብርሃን ፡ እቲዞ ፡ በእ ደዊሁ ፡ መስቀለ ፡ ዘያጸድል ፡ ከመ ፡ ፀሐይ ፡ ዘይበጽሕ ፡ ቆሙ ፡ እምድር ፡ እስከ ፡ ሰማይ ። ወሐተመኒ ፡ ፍጽምየ ፡ ሥልሰ ፡ በውእቱ ፡ ትእምርተ ፡ መስ ቀል ። ወእምዝ ፡ ነሥአኒ ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ ወኦድነ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ኩለንታሃ ፡ ለምድረ ፡ ኢትዮጵያ ፡ እምጽንፋ ፡ እስከ ፡ ጽንፋ ፡ ወአልቦ ፡ እንከ ፡ መካን ፡ ዘኢ በጻሕነ ፡ ኀቤሁ ። ዘበበ ፡ ምዕራፉ ፡ እንዘ ፡ የሐትማ ፡ በውእቱ ፡ መስቀል ፡ ብሩህ ፡ ዘሀሎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ እደዊሁ ። ወከመዝ ፡ ኦደ ፡ ሰለስተ ፡ ዕለተ ፡ መክ ብባ ፡ ለኢትዮጵያ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይባርክ ፡ አድባሪሃ ፡ ወአውግራቲሃ ፡ አዕጻዳቲሃ ፡ ወአሕቁላቲሃ ። ወበጊዜ ፡ ፍጻሜ ፡ ኡደቱ ፡ አውሣእኩ ፡ ወእቤሎ ፡ ኦአባ ፡ ለምንትኑ ፡ ትገብር ፡ ከመዝ ።፡ ተሰጥወ ፡ ወይቤለኒ ፡ ኦወልድየ ፡ እስመ ፡ ከመዝ ፡
of light. 227 20th prodigy of our blessed father, treasure of wisdom, filled with intelligence, winged being of knowledge, affliction of the desert, completion of the Law and guardian of the commandment, mär Sämu’ël. May his prayer and his blessing be with us in the ages of ages, amen. One day, a man who resided in the land of Dämot, upon whom dwelt the grace of the Holy Spirit, said: 'Abbä Sämu’ël of Gadäma Waäli came to me, mounted on a chariot of light, holding in his hands a cross that shone like the sun, whose height reached, from the earth, to the sky. He marked my forehead three times with the sign of this cross. After that, he took me with him and we traveled the entirety of the land of Ethiopia, from one end to the other—there is no place where we did not go—, (he) marking (Ethiopia) at each of his stops with this luminous cross that was in his hands. He traveled thus for three days the entirety of Ethiopia, blessing its mountains and its hills, its villages and its fields. At the moment of the end of his circuit, I spoke and said to him: “Abbä, why do you do thus?” He answered and said to me: “My son, because thus has ordered me my Lord Jesus Christ: that I bless and mark the entirety of Ethiopia, for it will make my commemoration after my death.”'
አዘዘኒ ፡ እግዚእየ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ከመ ፡ እባርክ ፡ ወእኅትም ፡ መክ ብባ ፡ ለኢትዮጵያ ፡ እስመ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ ይገብሩ ፡ ተዝካርየ ፡ እምድኅረ ፡ ሞትየ ፡። ኦአባ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡ ሠናየ ፡ ዝክር ፡ እስራኤላዊ ፡ ለባሴ ፡ ንጽሕ ፡ ከመ ፡ ዮሐንስ ፡ ሐዋርያ ፡ ዘኦድካ ፡ ስልሰ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ተዓትባ ፡ በትእምርተ ፡ መስቀል ፡ ለብሔረ ፡ ኢትዮጵያ ። አስተምሕር ፡ በእንቲአነ ፡ ኀበ ፡ አምላከ ፡ አማልክት ፡ ለምድር ፡ ዘይቀንያ ። ከመ ፡ ይጸግወነ ፡ መክፈልተ ፡ ወርስተ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሀገ ሪትከ”" ቅድስት ፡ ዓይነ ፡ መዋቲ ፡ ዘኢርእያ = ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ፡=
228 Blessed Abbä of beautiful memory, Israelite clothed in purity like John the apostle, you who have traveled three times, marking it with the sign of the cross, the land of Ethiopia, intercede for us with the God of gods who dominates the earth so that he may grant us a share and an inheritance in his holy city that the eye of a mortal has not seen, in the ages of ages, amen.

### Twenty-first miracle: Sämu’ël transforms water into beer
፳፳ ተአምሪሁ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡ ርግብ ፡ የዋህ ፡ ወበግዕ ፡ ጻዓዳ ፡ ዓምደ ፡ ሃይማኖት ፡ ወማኅቶተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ማር ፡ ሳሙ ኤል ። ትንብልና ፡ ጸሎቱ ፡ ትኩን ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ። ወበ 8፳እምነ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ መጽኡ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ ክልኤቱ ፡ መሃይምናን ፡ ከመ ፡ ይንሥኡ ፡ በረከቶ ፡ ወከመ ፡ ይተርገም ፡ ሎሙ ፡ እምቃለ ፡ ቅዱሳት ፡ መጻሕ ፍት ፡ በዘትድኅን ፡ ነፍሶሙ ። ወሶበ ፡ ሐወዞሙ ፡ ቃሉ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡
229 21st miracle of our blessed father, sweet dove and immaculate lamb, column of the faith and lamp of the holy Church, mär Sämu’ël. May the intercession of his prayer be with us, in the ages of ages, amen. One day, two believers came to (Sämu’ël) to receive his blessing and so that he might explain to them, from the word of the holy Scriptures, that with which their soul would be saved. When the word of our blessed father

ሣፊ ማር ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡ ቤቱ ፡ ህየ ፡ ወሶበ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ፍና ፡ ሰርክ ፡ አዘዞ ፡ ብእሴ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ለረድኡ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያምጽእ ፡ ማየ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ ወገብረ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ረድእ ፡ በከመ ፡ አዘዞ ፡ አቡሁ ። ወአቡነሰ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡ ገባሬ ፡ መንክ ራት ፡ ባሪኮ ፡ ውእተ ፡ ማየ ፡ በስመ ፡ ሥሉስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ወፈነወ ፡ ሎሙ ፡ ለእሙንቱ ፡ መሃይምናን ፡ ከመ ፡ ይስተዩ ። ወሶበ ፡ ሰትዩ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ እልክቱ ፡ መሃይምናን ፡ አንከሩ ፡ እምጣዕመ ፡ መዓዛሁ ። ወውእቱ ፡ ማይ ፡ ዘኮነ ፡ ምዝረ ፡ በትንብልና ፡ ጸሎቱ ፡ ለብእሴ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፍ ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ መጽአ ፡ ኀቤ ሆሙ ፡ ካልእ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ዘሀሎ ፡ ምስሌሆሙ ፡ ወእሙንቱሂ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ መሃይ ምናን ፡ ገብሩ ፡ ፍቅረ ፡ ቢጽ ፡ ወወሀብዎ ፡ እምነ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ምዝር ፡ ዘይሰ ትዩ ወሶበ ፡ ጥዕመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ አውሥአ ፡ ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ኦአኀወየ ፡ እምአይቴኑ ፡ ረከብክሙ ፡ በውስተ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ገዳም ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ምዝረ ፡ ሰገም ፡ ዘመንክር ፡ ጣዕሙ ።= ወተሰጥውዎ ፡ መሃይምናን ፡ ወይቤልዎ ፡ እስመ ፡ ፈነወ ፡
mär Sämu’ël had charmed them, they spent the night there. When it was the time of evening, the man of God ordered his disciple to bring water to him; the disciple did as his (spiritual) father had ordered him. Our blessed father, the worker of prodigies, having blessed this water in the name of the holy Trinity, sent (it) to these believers so that they might drink. When these believers drank of it, they were astonished at the flavor of its taste: the water had become beer by the intercession of the prayer of the man of God. After that came to them another man, who was with them; these believing people practiced the friendship of the neighbor and gave him of this beer that they were drinking. When the man had tasted (it), he spoke and said to them: 'My brothers, where did you find in this desert this barley beer whose taste (is) wonderful?' The believers answered him and said to him: 'The man of God sent (it) to us; we did not know from where it came.'

230 Blessed Abba, I marvel, having heard your miracle; you who, having blessed, made water into beer by the intercession of your prayer by following the trace of your Lord (Jn 2:1-10). Now, intercede always for us with your God so that he may grant us an inheritance in the city that you have received, in the ages of ages, amen. Twenty-second miracle: Sämu’ël reveals the thought of a monk with a parable. 231 22nd prodigy of the man of God, companion of the apostles and the prophets, worker of prodigies, spectator of hidden things, mär Sämu’ël. One day, while a monk was coming to the abbot, he did not find the path that led to the latter. He entered into the midst of a desert where there were many forests and brambles. When the monk was tormented by the harshness of the desert, he thought in his heart and said: 'It seems to me, for my part, that no one among men, and since the world was created, has arrived here, where I am today.' After having said this, he arrived by the will of God to where mär Sämu’ël was with his disciples and was blessed by him. After that, our blessed father spoke to his (spiritual) children, making a parable saying: 'A man came to a monk to pay him a visit. As he did not find the path because of the harshness of the desert, he thought and said: “No one has arrived here, where I am today, since the world was created.” The monk to whom the man who had thought this had wanted to pay a visit heard, being in his dwelling, an angel of God saying to (the man): “Behold, you are standing above the tomb of seven people.”' When the man heard this statement from the mouth of the blessed mär Sämu’ël, he was troubled, was afraid, and said to our father: 'It is I who thought this when I was tormented by the fact of not finding the path;

abbä, forgive me because I thought thus in my ignorance.' After that, he was blessed by the man of God and left him, being astonished at this affair.
አንክሮ ፡ አንክሩ ፡ ኦአበውየ ፡ ወአኃውየ ፡ መንክራቲሁ ፡ ለብፁዓዊ ፡ ዘያአምር?፤ ፡ ኅቡኣተ ፡ ዘይትገበር ፡ ውስተ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ በሓውርት ፡ ሠናይ ፡ ወእኩይ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ህየ ፡ ከመ ፡ ዘሰምዐ ፡ ወርእየ ። ይምጻእኬ ፡ ይእዜ ፡ መንገሌነ ፡ ኪያነ ፡ ለባርኮ ፡ ብሂሎ ፡ እሊአየ ። ዓሲሮ ፡ ነፍሳተ ፡ ዚአነ ፡ ያርፍቀነ ፡ ምዕራፈ ፡ ተድላሁ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ዘተወፈየ ።፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ።
232 Be filled with wonder, my fathers and my brothers, at the prodigy of the blessed one who knew the hidden things that were done in all countries, good and bad, without him being there, as if he had heard and seen. May he come therefore now toward us to bless us, saying: '(You are) mine.' Having invited our souls, may he make us sit at table in his dwelling of delights that he has received, in the ages of ages, amen. Twenty-third miracle: Sämu’ël learns from the heavenly priests the twelve
፳፻ ተአምሪሁ ፡ ለብፁዓዊ ፡ ሠናየ ፡ ዝክር ፡ ሐመረ ፡ ብዕል ፡ ዘኢለከፎ ፡ ባሕረ ፡ ኃጢአት ፡ ማዕበል ። አርዘ ፡ ሊባኖስ ፡ ጥሉል ፡ ለአርጋብ ፡ ምጽላል ። ካህን ፡ ወድንግል ፡ መምህረ ፡ ሕግ ፡ መልንግል ። ፈጻሜ ፡ ቃለ ፡ ወንጌል ።= ማር ፡ ሳሙኤል « ወበ፳-እምነ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ ይቤ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡ መሠጠኒ ፡ መልአከ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወአብጽሐኒ ፡ መልዕልተ ፡ አርያም ። ኀበ ፡ ሀለዉ ፡ ካህናተ ፡ ሰማይ ፡ ሱራፌላ ዊያን ። ወነበርኩ ፡ ደርገ ፡ ምስሌሆሙ ፡ እንዘ ፡ አአጥን ፡ መንበረ ፡ ስሉስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ በዐቢይ ፡ ስብሐት ፡ ወጸሎት ፡ ዘኢተምህርኩ ፡ ዝየ ። ወዓዲ ፡ ተከ ሥተ ፡ ሊተ ፡ ፲ወ፪ቅዳሴ ፡ ወ፲ወ፪በእንተ ፡ ብፁዕ ፡ ወካዕበ ፡ አእመርኩ ፡ ወተከሥተ ፡ ሊተ ፡ ድወ፪ ልሳናት ፡ ዘተካፈለ ፡ አመ ፡ ሐነፁ ፡ አሕዛብ ፡ ማኅፈደ ፡ በባቢሎን ። ኦፍቁራንየ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ነገር ፡ ኮነ ፡ ስምዐ ፡ መጽሐፈ ፡ ገድሉ ፡ ዘከመ ፡ ነበረ ፡ ፲ወ ፪ ዓመተ ፡ እንዘ ፡ የዐጥን ፡ መንበረ ፡
anaphoras. 233 23rd miracle of the blessed one of beautiful memory, vessel of wealth that the sea of sin—a flood—has not touched, cedar of Lebanon verdant, shade for the doves, priest and virgin, doctor of the Law, exegete, completion of the word of the Gospel, mär Sämu’ël. One day, the blessed one said: 'An angel of God took me and brought me above the height of the heavens, where the seraphic priests of heaven are. I remained together with them to incense the throne of the holy Trinity with a great glorification and prayer that I had not learned down here. There were also revealed to me the twelve anaphoras, and the twelve “(prayers) for the blessed,” I knew (them) also: there were revealed to me the seventy-two languages (among) which the peoples were divided when they built the tower at Babylon (Gn 11:7-8).' My beloved, concerning this affair the book of his life is witness that he remained twelve years incensing the throne (of the God) of hosts with the twenty-four priests of heaven.
ጸባኦት ፡ ምስለ ፡ ፳ ወ፪ካህናተ ፡ ሰማይ ። ኦአባ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡ ሠናየ ፡ ዝክር ፡ ደግማዊ ፡ ነቢይ ፡ ዘውገ ፡ ሙሴ ፡ ወአውሴ ። ኣስተበፅዕ ፡ አንሰ ፡ ዕበየ ፡ ጸጋከ ፡ በውዳሴ ። ሰሚዕየ ፡ ዘከመ ፡ ተከሥተ ፡ ለከ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ብፁዕ ፡ ወቅዳሴ ። ይእዜኒ ፡ አስተምሕር ፡ በእንቲአነ ፡ ምሕረተ ፡ ወሣህለ ፡ ቀዊመከ ፡ ቅድመ ፡ ስላሴ ።= ከመ ፡ ይጸግወነ ፡ መክፈልተ ፡ ወርስተ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሀገሪትከ'" ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ዘኢትፈቅድ ፡ ብርሃነ ፡ ፀሐይ ፡ ወጸ ዳለ ፡ ከዋክብት ፡ ወብናሴ ። ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ።
234 Blessed Abba of beautiful memory, second prophet, companion of Moses and Joshua, I celebrate, for my part, the greatness of your grace in praise, having heard how a revelation was made concerning the blessed one and the anaphoras. Now, implore for us mercy and clemency, standing before the Trinity, so that it may grant us a share and an inheritance in your holy city which does not need the light of the sun and the brightness of the stars and the moon, in the ages of ages, amen.
፳8 ተአምሪሁ ፡ ለብእሴ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ምዕቃለ ፡ ቅድስና ፡ መዝገበ ፡ ንጽሕ ፡ ማኅጸረ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ደብር ፡ ድኅ ፡ አሰረ ፡ ፀሐይ ፡ ዘይ ተሉ ፡ ኮከብ ፡ ብሩህ ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ።= አሜን ። ወበ ፳እምነ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ እንዘ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ውሣጢተ ፡ ገዳም ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይትራዓይ ፡ በዐቂበ ፡ ሕጎ ፡ ለልዑል ፡ መጽኡ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ ሠራዊተ ፡ መስቴማ ፡ ጽልሙታን ፡ ተመሲሎሙ ፡ አራዊተ ፡ ገዳም ፡ ሕሠማነ ፡ ራእይ ፡ ፀዋጋን ፡ ወመፍርህ ፡ ጥቀ ፡ ግርማሆሙ ፡ ወዐገቱ ፡ አውደ ፡ በዓቱ ፡ ለብእሴ ፡ እግዚአ ብሔር ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይጥህሩ ፡ ወይትናቀዉ ፡ በበ ፡ ቃሎሙ ፡ ፈቂዶሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይሖር ፡ እምውስተ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ውሳጢተ ፡ ገዳም ፡ እንበይነ ፡ አፍርሆቶሙ ፡ በቀ ቢጸ ፡ ተስፋ ።= ወአቡነሰ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡ ሠናየ ፡ ዝክር ፡ ማር ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡ ሶበ ፡ አእመረ ፡ ትምይንተ ፡ ሚኖሙ ፡ ወፅአ ፡ ኀቤሆሙ ፡ ተወኪሎ ፡ በኀይለ ፡ አም ላኩ ፡ ወባረከ ፡ በትእምርተ ፡ መስቀል ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይጹውዕ ፡ አስማተ ፡ ስላሜ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ቀዊሞ ፡ ማእከሌሆሙ ። ወሶቤሃ ፡ ተዘርዉ ፡ እምቅድመ ፡ አዕይንቲሁ ፡
Twenty-fourth miracle: Sämu’ël dissipates the demons by the sign of the Cross. 235 24th miracle of the man of God, cistern of holiness, treasure of purity, enclosure of the Holy Spirit, mountain dug out (Song 2:17), trace of the sun that the bright star follows. May his prayer and his blessing be with us, in the ages of ages, amen, amen. One day, while he was in the interior of the desert taking his pasture while keeping the law of the Most High, came to him black soldiers of the Aggressor, having made themselves like beasts of the desert, ugly of appearance, cruel—and their terrifying appearance (was) very frightening. They surrounded the surroundings of the cave of the man of God, roaring and shouting, each according to his voice, wanting him to go away from the interior of the desert because (he) had frightened them by the loss of (their) hope. As for our blessed father of beautiful memory, mär Sämu’ël, when he knew the trickery of their deception, he went out to them, having placed his trust in the power of his God, and made a blessing with the sign of the Cross, invoking the names of the holy Trinity, standing in their midst. Immediately, they dissipated before his eyes and were like smoke that is driven away by the force of the wind.


[p. 196] {#po.53.p196}
ወኮኑ ፡ ከመ ፡ እንተ ፡ ጢስ ፡ ዘተሰደ ፡ እምኀይለ ፡ ነፋስ ። ኦአባ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡ መልአክ ፡ ዘበምድር ፡ ወብእሲ ፡ ዘራማ ። ዓምደ ፡ ሃይማኖት ፡ ጽኑዕ ፡ ለቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ተቅዋማ ። ዘአንፈጽከ ፡ በዑታቤ ፡ እዴከ ፡ እለ ፡ መጽኡ ፡ ለተፃብኦትከ ፡ ጸላያነ ፡ ሠናያት ፡ ሠራዊተ ፡ ማስቴማ ፡ ይእዜኒ ፡ ትንብልና ፡ ጸሎትከ ፡ ያርፍቀነ ፡ ምስለ ፡ አፍሕርተ ፡ ጥበብ ፡ ለኢየ ሩሳሌም ፡ ሰማያዊት?” ፡ ውስተ ፡ አድያማ ።= ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ፡=
236 Blessed Abba, angel who (is) on earth and man of the heavenly heights, solid column of the faith, foundation of the Church, you who put to flight by the sign of the cross of your hand the enemies of good, the soldiers of the Aggressor, who had come to attack you, now, may the intercession of your prayer make us sit at table with the fledglings of wisdom in the lands of the heavenly Jerusalem, in the ages of ages, amen. Twenty-fifth miracle: the intercession of Sämu’ël heals a monk of the
