venom of a serpent. 237 25th miracle of our blessed father of beautiful memory, solid rock of the faith, victor of the Enemy, priest sacrificer, refuge of the abandoned sheep, mär Sämu’ël. May his prayer, his blessing, and the gift of his help be with us in the ages of ages, amen. One day, a monk said: 'I rose from my sleep, at the moment of the middle of the night, to pray and I went to where I always stand, according to my habit. A great serpent was waiting there, asleep; as for me, without seeing it, I walked on it: immediately, the serpent raised its head in anger, took in its mouth one of my feet entirely and instilled its venom into it; then it began to move away from me after having done so. I, when I saw it moving away from me, I spoke and said to it: “Return now and go back to me to take back your venom that you have put in my foot. I adjure you by the intercession of the prayer of abbä Sämu’ël.”' Immediately, the serpent returned to the monk,
ውስተ ፡ እገሪየ ። አምሐልኩከ ፡ በትንብልና ፡ ጸሎቱ ፡ ለአባ ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡ ወሶቤሃ ፡ ገብአ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ከይሲ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ ወተፍእመ ፡ እገሪሁ ፡ ለውእቱ ፡ መነኮስ ፡ ወነሥአ ፡ ሕምዞ ፡ ዘወደየ ፡ ዲቤሁ ፡ ወውእተ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ተነፍሐ ፡ ኩለ ንታሁ ፡ ወተቀብወ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ከይሲ ፡ ነቅዐ ፡ ከርሱ ፡ ወተክዕወ ፡ አማዑቱ ፡ ወሞተ ፡ ግብተ ። ወውእቱሰ ፡ መነኮስ ፡ ጸውዖሙ ፡ ለአብያጺሁ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይም ጽኡ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ ነሚኦሙ ፡ ማኅትወ ፡ ወሶበ ፡ በጽሑ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ አይድፆሙ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ዘኮነ ፡ በበ ፡ መትልው ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ ርእይኩ ፡ ኦአኀውየ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ከይሴ ፡ ዘሞተ ፡ ግብተ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ነሰከ ፡ እግርየ ፡ ወአነሂ ፡ አምሐልክዎ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይንሣእ ፡ ሕምዞ ፡ እምኔየ ፡ በኀይለ ፡ ጸሎቱ ፡ ለአባ ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡ ወገብአ ፡ ኀቤየ ፡ ወገብረ ፡ በከመ ፡ እቤሎ ፡ ወአሕጐለ ፡ ርእሶ ፡ ይእዜ ፡ ዘፈቀደ ፡ ሊተ ፡ ይቅትለኒ ። ወአኃውሰ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ሰምዑ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ነገረ ፡ አን ከሩ ፡ ወሰብሕዎ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ዘገብረ ፡ ተአምረ ፡ በጸውዖ ፡ ስሙ ፡ ለአ ቡነ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡ ሠናየ ፡ ዝክር ፡ ገባሬ ፡ መንክራት ። ወእምዝ ፡ ነሥኡ ፡ በትረ ፡ ሕለት ፡ ረያም ፡ ወሰፈርዎ ፡ ለውእቱ ፡ ከይሲ ፡ ወኮነ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ እመት ፡
took in its mouth the foot of the latter and withdrew its venom that it had put on him. At that very moment, the serpent swelled all over and puffed up; its belly burst, its entrails flowed out, and it died suddenly. The monk called his companions so that they might come to him, having taken a lamp. When they had arrived at him, he taught them everything that had happened, in order, saying: 'I saw, my brothers, this serpent which died suddenly when it bit my foot; I adjured it to withdraw from me its venom by the power of the prayer of abbä Sämu’ël: it returned to me, did as I had told it, and destroyed itself then, it which had wanted to kill me, me.' The brothers, when they heard these statements from him, marveled and glorified God who had performed a miracle by the invocation of the name of our blessed father of beautiful memory, the worker of prodigies. After that, they took a long reed stalk and measured the serpent: it was four cubits and a half-cubit.


[p. 198] {#po.53.p198}
፪ወመንፈቀ ፡ እመት ፡ ኦአበዊየ ፡ ወአኀውየ ፡ አንክሮ ፡ አንክሩ ፡ ተአምራተ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡ ፅዱለ ፡ ሥን ። ለባሴ ፡ ንጽሕ ፡ ዘውገ ፡ ትጉሃን ። ዘአድኅኖ' ፡ ለብእሲ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ተማኅፀነ ፡ በትንብልና ፡ ጸሎቱ ፡ እምሕምዘ ፡ ተመን ። ያስተም ሕር ፡ ይእዜኒ ፡ በእንቲአነ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ብሉየ ፡ አዝማን ። ከመ ፡ ይጸግወነ ፡ ርስተ ፡ ምስለ ፡ አርጋብ ፡ የዋሃን ። ወምስለ ፡ አባግዕ ፡ ጸዓድዒዳን ፍ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ።
238 My fathers and my brothers, be filled with wonder at the miracle of this blessed father of ours, splendor of beauty, clothed in purity, companion of the Watchers, who saved man from the venom of the serpent when he entrusted himself to the intercession of his prayer. May he now intercede with the Ancient of Days, so that He may grant us an inheritance with the sweet doves and with the immaculate sheep, for ever and ever, amen.

### Twenty-sixth miracle: the archangel Michael carries Sämu’ël to King Däwit
፳፪ ተአምሪሁ ፡ ለብፁዓዊ ፡ ሠናየ ፡ ዝክር ፡ ኦመ ፡ ርጌ ፡ ምዑዝ ። መሠረተ ፡ ከኒሳ ፡ ጽኑዕ ፡ ዘሃይማኖት ፡ በለዝ ። ነጻሬ ፡ ኅቡኣት ፡ ከመ ፡ ወልደ ፡ ቡዝ ። ማር ፡ ሳሙኤል ። ትንብልና ፡ ጸሎቱ ፡ ትኩን ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ፡ ወበ ፳እምነ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ እንዘ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ውሳጢተ ፡ ገዳም ፡ ምስለ ፡ አርዳኢሁ ፡ መጽአ ፡ ኅቤሁ ፡ ሚካኤል ፡ ሊቀ ፡ መላእክት ፡ እንዘ ፡ ያንበለ ብል ፡ ንደተ ፡ እሳት ። ወሶቤሃ ፡ አዋከየ ፡ ከመ ፡ ብርሃነ ፡ ፀሐይ ፡ ወተወለጠ ፡ ኩለንታሁ ፡ ወለብሰ ፡ ግርማ ፡ መፍርህ ፡ ወተንሥኡ ፡ አርዳኢሁ ፡ ወጉዩ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ በዓቢይ ፡ ፍርሃት ፡ ወድንጋፄ ፡ እስመ ፡ ኢተክህሎሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይቁ ሙ ፡ ህየ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ዘተወለጠ ፡ ሠሁራጌ ፡ ገጹ ፡ ለብፁዓዊ ፡ ማር ፡ ሳሙ ኤል ። ወሶቤሃ ፡ ተንሥአ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ መልአክ ፡ ዘመጽአ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ ወጾሮ ፡ በክነፊሁ ፡ ወአብጽሖ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ዳዊት ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ መፍቀሬ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ርቱዓ ፡ ሃይማኖት ፍ እንዘ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ሠራዊቱ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ፍኖት ፡ ፈቂዶ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይምጻእ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ ወይንሣእ ፡ በረከቶ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ብፁዳዊ ፡ ማር ፡ ሳሙኤል ። ወሶበ ፡ ርእዮ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ተፈሥሐ ፡ ወተኀሠየ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ያነክር ፡ ዕበየ ፡ ጸጋሁ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ዘበጽሐ?” ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ ተኀዚሎ ፡ በክንፈ ፡ መልአክ ።« አእሚሮ ፡ ሕሊናሁ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይፈቅድ ፡ ይምጻእ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ ለተባርኮ ። ወእምዝ ፡
239 26th miracle of the blessed one of beautiful memory, fragrant embalmed tree, solid foundation of the Church, pillar of the faith, spectator of hidden things like the son of Buz, mär Sämu'ël. May the intercession of his prayer be with us for ever and ever, amen. One day, while he was in the interior of the desert with his disciples, Michael the archangel came to him, a flame of burning fire. Immediately, (Sämu’el) shone like the light of the sun, he was transformed entirely and clothed in a terrifying majesty; his disciples rose and fled far from him in great fear and trembling, for they could not remain there because the face of the blessed mär Sämu’ël had been transformed (into) a glory. Then the angel who had come to him rose, carried him on his wings, and brought him to Däwit, the king of upright faith who loves God, while (the latter) was on the road with his soldiers, wishing to come to our blessed father mär Sämu'ël and receive his blessing. When the king saw him, he rejoiced and exulted, marveling at the greatness of his grace: that he had arrived to him carried on the wings of an angel, having known his thought, namely that he wanted to come to him to be blessed. After that, they spoke of the greatness of God and the

king made known (to Sämu’el) all his thought, for which he had wanted to meet with him. After that, he begged the man of God to bless his royalty, his soldiers, and the whole of Ethiopia. Our blessed father did as the king had asked him, then they gave each other the greeting and the man of God returned to the interior of the desert, mounted on the wings of the angel. As for the king, he turned back, having the herald proclaim these words: “I have met by the will of God with abbä Sämu'ël of Gadäma Wali, to whom I had thought to go.”

240 Blessed Abbä, I marvel at the greatness of your grace, having heard how the glory of your face was transformed like the light of the sun, so much so that your own fled in fear before your majesty; (of) you also who visited the king, mounted on the wings of the angel, knowing, before he informed you of it, that he wanted to meet with you. May your intercession now keep us with clemency from the mouth of the cruel beast, for ever and ever, amen. Twenty-seventh miracle: Sämu’ël recites the Our Father and receives bread from heaven.
፳፪ ተአምሪሁ ፡ ለብእሴ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ዓምደ ፡ ሃይማኖት ፡ ማኅቶተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ፅዱለ ፡ ሥን ፡ ከመ ፡ ፀሐይ ፡ ወወርኅ ፡ ወከዋ ክብት ፡ ማር ፡ ሳሙኤል ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ወበ ፳እምነ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ እንዘ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገዳም ፡ ምስለ ፡ አርዳኢሁ ፡ ወሶበ ፡ ኀጥኡ ፡ ሲሳየ ፡ ዘይበልዑ ፡ እመቅማኅ ፡ ገዳም ፡ ኢፈረያተ ፡ ወኢቀ ጽለ ፡ ወኢአስራወ ፡ አርዳኢሁ ፡ ወሶበ ፡ ርእየ ፡ ብእሴ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ተፅና ሶሙ ፡ ለአርዳኢሁ ፡ ሰፍሐ ፡ እደዊሁ ፡ ሰአለ ፡ አስተብቀዓ ፡ ኀበ ፡ አምላኩ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ አቡነ ፡ ዘበሰማያት ፡ ይትቀደስ ፡ ስምከ ፡ ትምጻእ ፡ መንግሥ ትከ ፡ ወይኩን ፡ ፈቃድከ ፡ በሰማይ ፡ ከማሁ ፡ በምድር ። ሲሳይነ ፡ ዘለለዕለትነ ፡ ሀበነ ፡ ዮም ። ወኅድግ ፡ ለነ ፡ አበሳነ ፡ ወኢታብአነ ፡ እግዚኦ ፡ ውስተ ፡ መን ሱት ። አላ ፡ አድኅነነ ፡ ወባልሐነ ፡ እምኩሉ ፡ እኩይ ፡ እስመ ፡ ዚአከ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ መንግሥት ፡ ኀይል ፡ ወስብሐት ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ። ወሶ ቤሃ ፡ አውረደ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ መልአከ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ኅብስተ ፡ እምሰማይ ፡ ዘን ኩር ፡ አርአያሁ ፡ ወጥዑም ፡ ጹና ፡ መዓዛሁ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ጸውዖሙ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡ ለደቂቁ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይምጽኡ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ንሥኡ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ኅብስተ ፡ ዘወሀበክሙ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ መፍቀሬ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ርእዮ ፡ ንዴተክሙ”" ፡ ወተፅናሰ ፡ ምንዳቤክሙ ፡ በውስተ ፡ ዛቲ ፡ ገዳም ። ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዑ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ አርዳኢሁ ፡ ተፈሥሑ ፡ ወሰብሕዎ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ በእንተ ፡ ዘወሀቦሙ ፡ መፍ
241 27th miracle of the man of God, column of the faith, lamp of the holy Church, shining with beauty like the sun, the moon, and the stars, mär Samu’ël. May his prayer and his blessing be with us, for ever and ever, amen. One day, while he was in the desert with his disciples, as his disciples lacked food to eat among the plants of the desert — neither fruits, nor leaves, nor roots —, when the man of God saw the destitution of his disciples, he stretched out his hands, solicited (and) begged his God saying: “Our Father who (art) in heaven, hallowed be thy name, thy kingdom come, thy will be done on earth as it is in heaven, give us this day our daily bread; forgive us our trespasses and lead us not, Lord, into temptation, but save us and deliver us from all evil, for thine is the kingdom, the power, and the glory for ever and ever, amen” (Mt 6:9-13). Immediately, the angel of God made descend for him from heaven a bread of wonderful appearance and sweet aroma. After that, our blessed father called his (spiritual) children so that they might come to him and said to them: “Take this bread that God, the friend of men, has given you, having seen your poverty and your painful destitution in this desert.” When his disciples heard this, they rejoiced and glorified God that He had given them what their body needed. After that, they ate the bread with which their God had gratified them through the intercession of the prayer of our blessed father, mär Sämu’ël.
ቅደ ፡ ሥጋሆሙ ፡= ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ በልዑ ፡ እምነ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ኅብስት ፡ ዘጸገዎሙ ፡ አምላኮሙ ፡ በትንብልና ፡ ጸሎቱ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡ ማር ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡ ኦአበዊየ ፡ ወአኀውየ ፡ አንክሮ ፡ አንክሩ ፡ መንክራቲሁ ፡ ለብፁዓዊ ፡ ልደ ፡ መርዓት ፡ ደነሰ ፡ ኀጢአት ፡ ዘኢተቀብአ ፡ አባሉ ። ነሚኦ ፡ መስቀለ ፡
242 My fathers and my brothers, be filled with wonder at the prodigy of the blessed offspring of the Bride, whose body has not been anointed with


[p. 202] {#po.53.p202}
ሞቱ ፡ አሰረ ፡ መርዓዊ ፡ ዘይተሉ ፍ ዘአውረደ ፡ ስኢሎ ፡ እምኅበ ፡ አምላኩ ፣፡ ለእሊአሁ ፡ ኅብስተ ፡ እምላዕሉ ። ይእዜኒ ፡ ያስተምሕር ፡ በእንቲአነ ፡ ኀበ” ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ሐዋርያነ ፡ እምቅድሜነ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ ነሀሉ ። ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ።
the turpitude of sin, who, having taken the cross of the death of the Bridegroom, follows the trace of the latter, who, having solicited his God, made bread from on high descend for his own. Now, may he intercede for us with Jesus Christ our apostle, so that we may be where, before us, he has been, for ever and ever, amen. Twenty-eighth miracle: Sämu'ël rejoices in his numerous monastic offspring.
፳፳ ተአምሪሁ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡ ሠናየ ፡ ዝክር ፡ መዘምር ፡ እስራኤ ላዊ ፡ ዘውገ ፡ ዳዊት ፡ ሰንቃዊ ፡ ማር ፡ ሳሙኤል ። ሀብተ ፡ ረድኤቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ። ወበ ፳እምነ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ ለአከ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ ይስሐቅ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ መፍቀሬ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ ኦአባ ፡ ጸሊአ ፡ ሊተ ፡ ወበእንተ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ሕዝብየ ፡ ኀበ ፡ አምላክከ ፡ እስመ ፡ አንሰ ፡ ሖርኩ ፡ ለመራደ ፡ ነኪር ፡ ከመ ፡ እፅባእ ፡ ፀረ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ። ወበጽሐ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ላእክ ፡ ኅበ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገዳም ፡ ወአይድዖ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ በከመ ፡ ይቤሎ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡= ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዐ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ብእሴ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ማር ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡ ተፈሥሐ ፡ ወተሐሠየ ፡ ወሰገደ ፡ ብዙኀ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ለአምላኩ ። ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ ይቤሎ ፡ ለውእቱ ፡ ላእክ ፡ ሖርኬ ፡ ኦወልድየ ፡ በሰላም ፡ ወበሎ ፡ ለንጉሥ ፡ ዘለአከከ ፡ ኀቤየ ፡ በተአምኖ ። እግዚአብሔር ፡ ይኩንከ ፡ ኀይለ ፡ ወጽንዐ ፡ ወየሀብከ ፡ መዊአ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ፀርከ ። ወይዕቀብ ፡ ታዕካከ ፡ ወሰራዊተከ ፡ ወያግብ እከ ፡ በዳኅና ፡ ወበሰላም ፡ ምስለ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ ሕዝብከ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ትትፌሣሕ ፡ ወትትሐሠይ ። ወውእቱኒ ፡ ላእክ ፡ አማኅፀነ ፡ ርእሶ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ ወተባሪኮ ፡ ሖረ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ፍኖቱ ። ወእምዝ ፡ አውሥኡ ፡ አርዳኢሁ ፡ ወይቤልዎ ፡ ኦአባ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ምንትኑ ፡ ተፈሣሕከ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ትሰግድ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ቦበ ፡ ለአከ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ኀቤከ ። ወዘንተ ፡ ብሂሎሙ ፡ አስተብቀዕዎ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይንግ ሮሙ ፡ ወሶበ ፡ ርእየ ፡ ብእሴ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ተኀሥሦቶሙ ፡ ለአርዳኢሁ ፡ ተሰጥወ ፡ ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ኦደቂቅየ ፡ አማንኬ ፡ ተፈሣሕኩ ፡ ወተኀሠይኩ ፡ እንዘ ፡ እሰግድ ፡ ለአምላኪየ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ዘጸገወኒ ፡ ዓሥራተ ፡ ብዙኃን ፡ ነፍሳተ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ እምይእቲ ፡ ሀገር ፡ ዘሖረ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይፅብኦሙ ። ወሶበ ፡
243 28th miracle of our blessed father of beautiful memory, the Israelite psalmist, companion of David the musician, mär Sämu'el. May the gift of his help be with us for ever and ever, amen. One day, Yeshadq, the king who loves God, sent (a messenger) to him saying: “Pray for me and concerning all my people to your God, for I have gone on campaign against foreigners in order to fight the enemy of God.” The messenger arrived to our blessed father while (the latter) was in the desert and informed him of everything, according to what the king had told him. When he heard this, the man of God rejoiced, exulted, and prostrated himself many times before his God. After that, he said to the envoy: “Go then in peace, my son, and say to the king who sent you to me with confidence: ‘May God be for you power and strength and give you victory over your enemy; may He guard your camp and your soldiers and make you return safe and in peace with all your people, (you) rejoicing and exulting.’” The envoy returned to (Sämu’ël) and, having been blessed, went his way. After that, the disciples of (Samu'ël) spoke and said to him: “Abbä, for what reason did you rejoice by prostrating yourself many times when the king sent to you?” Having said this, they begged him to tell them; when the man of God saw the questioning of his disciples, he answered and said to them: “My children, in truth I rejoiced and exulted by prostrating myself before my God, because He has gratified me with a tithe of many human souls from the region where the king has gone to fight (the inhabitants).” When they heard this from the mouth of the abbot, his disciples were silent, being astonished that he knew hidden events before they happened.
ሰምዑ ፡ አርዳኢሁ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ እምአፉሁ ፡ ለአባስ ፡ አርመሙ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ያስተዓ ፅቡ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ዘያአምር ፡ ኅቡኣተ ፡ እምቅድመ ፡ ይኩን ። አማንኬ ፡ ኦአበዊየ ፡ ወአኀውየ ፡ ሕዝብ ፡ መሲሓውያን ፡ መጽኡ ፡ ብዙኃን ፡ አግብርተ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ እምይእቲ ፡ ሀገር ፡ እምድኅረ ፡ አዕረፈ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡ ሠናየ ፡ ዝክር ፡ ማር ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡ ወነሥኡ ፡ አልባሰ ፡ ምንኩስና ፡ እመካነ ፡ ዚአሁ ፡ ወኮኑ ፡ መስተጋድሳላነ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ፍቅረ ፡ አምላ ኮሙ ።= ወእምድኅረ ፡ ፈጸሙ ፡ በድሮሙ ፡ ቦኡ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሕይወት ፡ ዘለዓ ለም ፡ በከመ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ብእሴ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ማር ፡ ሳሙኤል ። አባ ፡ ብፁ ዓዊ ፡ ተፈጸመ ፡ በላዕሌከ ፡ ዘይቤ ፡ ሰሎሞን ፡ በምሳሌ ፡ ከናፍረ ፡ ጻድቃን ፡ ያአምራ ፡ ነዋኀ ።= ይእዜኒ ፡ አስተምሕር ፡ በእንቲአነ ፡ ኅበ ፡ አምላከ ፡ ጽድቅ ፡
244 In truth, my fathers and my brothers, Christian people, many servants of God came from that region after our blessed father of beautiful memory, mär Sämu’ël, had died; they received the monastic garment in his establishment and became (spiritual) combatants for the love of their God. After finishing their career, they entered into eternal life, according to what the man of God, mär Sämu’ël, had said. Blessed Abbä, what Solomon said in the Proverbs has been fulfilled in you: The lips of the righteous know high things (Pr 10:21). Now, intercede for


[p. 204] {#po.53.p204}
ዘይቀንት ፡ ንጽሐ ። ከመ ፡ ይጸግወነ ፡ በአድኅኖ ፡ ፍሥሓ ። ወአመ ፡ ዕለተ ፡ ደይን ፡ ወፍዳ ፡ ዕሜተ ፡ ብዙኃ ። ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ፡=
us with the God of justice who is girded with purity, so that He may gratify us with the salvation of joy and, on the day of judgment and retribution, with an important reward, for ever and ever, amen.

### Twenty-ninth miracle: Sämu'el makes water gush from the rock
፳ ወ8 ተአምሪሁ ፡ ለብእሴ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ካህን ፡ ሣውዕ ፡ ዘውገ ፡ ወልደ ፡ ኢዮሴዴቅ ። ምስማከ ፡ ሃይማኖት ፡ ጽኑዕ ፡ ዓምደ ፡ በላቅ ፍ ከመ ፡ ቃለ ፡ አዋዲ ፡ ንጽራረ ፡ ገዳም ፡ መስተገብረ ፡ ጽድቅ ። ንጽሐ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ጽሩይ ፡ እምብሩር ፡ ወእምነ ፡ ፍቱን ፡ ወርቅ ። ማር ፡ ሳሙኤል ። ትንብልና ፡ ጸሎቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ፍ፥ ወበ፳እምነ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ መጽኡ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ መነኮሳት ፡ ከመ ፡ የሐውጽዎ ፡ ወይንሥኡ ፡ በረከቶ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ አፍአ ፡ እማኅደሩ ፡ ነቢሮ ፡ መልዕልተ ፡ ኩኩሕ ፡ ምስለ ፡ አርዳኢሁ ። ወበጽሑ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ እልክቱ ፡ መነኮሳት ፡ ወተባረኩ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ወወዓሉ ፡ ህየ ፡ ምስሌሁ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይሴአልዎ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያይድፆሙ?” ፡ ትእዛዛተ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ዘሀሎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ መጻሕፍት ፡ ሐዲስ ፡ ወብሉይ ። ወሶበ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ምሴት ፡ ፈቀዱ ፡ እሙንቱ ፡ መነኮሳት ፡ ከመ ፡ ይስተዩ ፡ ማየ ፡ ወሐደግዎ ፡ ህየ ፡ ባሕቲቱ ፡ ለብእሴ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወሖሩ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይኅሥሥሠ ፡ ወአቡነሂ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡ ስኢሎ"” ፡ ኀበ ፡ አምላኩ ፡ አንቅዐ ፡ ማየ ፡ እምይእቲ ፡ ኩኩሕ ፡ ዘነበረ ፡ ዲቤሃ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ጸውዖሙ ፡ ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ኦደቂቅየ ፡ ናሁ ፡ ብስረትክሙ ፡ እስመ ፡ ረከብኩ ፡ ማየ ፡ እምነ ፡ ዛቲ ፡ ኩኩሕ ፡ ዘነበርነ ፡ ዲቤሁ ። ወእሙንቱሰ ፡ አኀው ፡ ሶበ ፡ ርእ ይዎ ፡ ለውእቱ ፡ ማይ ፡ ዘአንቅዐ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡ ስኢሎ ፡ እምኅበ ፡ አም ላኩ ፡ እምነ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ኩኩሕ ፡ ዘነበረ ፡ ዲቤሃ ፡ ተፈሥሑ ፡ ወተኀሠዩ ፡ ወሰትዩ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይሴብሕዎ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ በእንተ ፡ ዘይትዌከፍ ፡ ስእለቶሙ ፡ ለእለ ፡ ያፈቅርዎ ፡ ወበእንተ ፡ ዘይገብር ፡ ኀይለ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ለእለ ፡ ይትዌከሉ ፡ ኀቤሁ ። ወእምድኅረ ፡ ሰትዩ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ተሰወረ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ማይ ፡ እምአዕይንቲሆሙ ፡ ወኢተዐውቀ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ኩኩሕ ፡ አሰረ ፡ ንቅዐት ወአቡነሰ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡ ምሉአ ፡ ትሕትና ፡ አምሐሎሙ ፡ በስመ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ለእሙንቱ ፡ አኀው ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢይንግሩ ፡ ምስጢሮ ፡ ለሰብእ ፡ እስከ ፡ ዕለተ ፡
245 29th miracle of the man of God, sacrificing priest, companion of the son of Yoçadag (Zec 6:11), support of the faith, solid column of marble like the voice of the herald, affliction of the desert, artisan of justice — the purity of his body (is) purer than silver and more than fine gold —, mär Sämu’el. May the intercession of his prayer be with us for ever and ever, amen. One day, monks came to him to visit him and receive his blessing, while he was outside his dwelling, sitting on a rock with his disciples. These monks arrived to him, were blessed by him, and spent the day there with him, asking him to make known to them the commandments of God that are in the new and old Scriptures. When it was the evening hour, these monks wanted to drink water, left the man of God there alone, and went to look (for it). As for our blessed father, having solicited his God, he made water gush from the rock on which he had been sitting. After that, he called (these monks) and said to them: “My children, here is good news for you: I have found water (coming) from this rock on which we have been sitting.” As for these brothers, when they saw the water that our blessed father had made gush, having solicited his God, from the rock on which he had been sitting, they rejoiced, exulted, and drank of it, glorifying God because He welcomes the solicitation of those who love Him and because He performs many miracles for those who put their trust in Him. After they had drunk of it, this water disappeared from their eyes and there appeared, on the rock, no trace of gushing. As for our blessed father full of humility, he adjured these brothers in the name of God not to tell his mystery to people until the day of his death. These monks, having received a blessing from the man of God, returned to their dwelling, admiring the prodigy of him who performed miracles by the name of his God.
ሞቱ ። ወእልክቱ ፡ መነኮሳት ፡ ነሚኦሙ ፡ ቡራኬ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ለብእሴ ፡ እግ ዚአብሔር ፡ ገብኡ ፡ ውስተ ፡ መካኖሙ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ያስተዐፅቡ ፡ መንክራቲሁ ፡ ለገባሬ ፡ ተኣምር ፡ በስመ ፡ አምላኩ ። ኦአበዊየ ፡ ወአኀውየ ፡ አንክሮ ፡ አንክሩ ፡ ለብፁዓዊ ፡ መዝገበ ፡ ብዕል ፡ ዕበየ ፡ ጸጋሁ ፍ ዘአንቅዐ ፡ ማየ ፡ እምኩኩሕ ፡ ይቡስ ፡ በትንብልናሁ ፍ ከመ ፡ ያስትዮሙ ፡ ለእለ ፡ መጽኡ ፡ ኀቤሁ ። ይእዜኒ ፡ ያስተምሕር ፡ በእንቲአነ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ሥሉስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ዘኢይትባዓድ ፡ ህላዌሁ ፍ ከመ ፡ ይጸግወነ ፡ መክፈልተ ፡ ወርስተ ፡ ምስሌሁ ። ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ፡=
246 My fathers and my brothers, be filled with wonder at the greatness of the grace of the blessed treasure of wealth that made water gush from a dry rock, by his intercession, in order to give drink to those who had come to him. Now, may he intercede for us with the holy Trinity whose nature is not diverse, so that It may gratify us with a share and an inheritance with him for ever and ever, amen.

Thirtieth miracle: Sämu'’ël becomes like an eagle to bring many souls to him. 247 30th prodigy of the man of God, guardian of the Law, executor of the commandments, companion of the prophets and apostles, healer of the sick, savior of souls, mär Sämu'ël. May his prayer and his blessing be with us for ever and ever, amen. One of his disciples upon whom the grace of the Holy Spirit dwelt, who saw hidden things similarly, said: “On the twelfth of the month of nahasë, I saw my (spiritual) father being renewed like an eagle (Ps 103:5), having arranged above him luminous wings like (those) of the angels, which shone more than the sun. While, watching this, I marveled, he rose like an eagle, flew into the air, and traveled three times around the whole world. After that, he returned spreading his wings, fell asleep where his tomb would be, before the church was built, and cried three times with a loud voice, launching an invitation to the souls of the people (so that they might come) from the (places) he had traveled. Immediately, many human souls gathered, in the image of white bees, from the east and the west, from the north and the south, from the septentrion and the southern regions, without rest or interruption, night and day: they neither diminished nor became sparse. On the contrary, after the morning had come, they increased and multiplied by gathering at the place where he had fallen asleep; they surrounded the man of God entirely by piling up on him and it was like a great mound. After I had seen this, my blessed father died in a green old age on the fourth month, the twelfth of tähsäs. Then, again, the holy, righteous, and virtuous people gathered from all the countries of the world; Our Lord descended from the seventh heaven with all his angels, thousands of thousands and myriads of myriads that were without number, the souls of the righteous following him according to their order: the prophets and the apostles, the righteous and the martyrs, the virgins and the little children, with the queen of the whole universe, the Virgin Mary, mother of the Light of lights. It was a great grace and a considerable honor that the heart of man cannot explain and the tongue of a mortal describe. Of the honor of the death of our blessed father of beautiful memory, Israelite, mär Sämu’ël and of this affair, one of the (spiritual) children of the abbot, by the name of Tomäs, was a witness.”
ዝክር ፡ እስራኤላዊ ፡ ማር ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡ ወበእንተ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ነገር ፡ ኮነ ፡ ስምዐ ፡ ፳እምነ ፡ ደቂቁ ፡ ለአባስ”" ፡ ዘስሙ ፡ ቶማስ ። ኦአባ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡ ተፈጸመ ፡ በላዕሌከ ፡ ዘይቤ ፡ ዳዊት ፡ ነቢይ ፡ በመዝ ሙር ፡ ክቡር ፡ ሞቱ ፡ ለጻድቅ ፡ በቅድመ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ። ወሰሎሞንሂ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ሰሐቅ ፡ ሞቱ ፡ ለኃጥእ ፡ ወጻድቅሰ ፡ መዊቶ ፡ ኃደገ ፡ ተስፋ ። ይሔይስ ፡ ፅለተ ፡ ሞት ፡ እምዕለተ ፡ ልደት ። ኦፍቁራንየ ፡ ሕዝብ ፡ መሲሓዊያን ፡ አን
248 Blessed Abbä, what David the prophet said in the Psalms has been fulfilled in you: Precious (is) the death of the righteous before God (Ps 116:15). Solomon also says: An object of mockery is the death of the sinner, but the righteous, dying, has abandoned hope (Pr 11:3, 7). The day of death is better than the day of birth (Qo 7:1). My beloved, Christian people, be

ክሮ ፡ አንክሩ ፡ ብዕለ ፡ ጸጋሁ ፡ ለብፁዓዊ ፡ በውዳሴ ፡ ወበውውዓ ። ሰሚዐ ክሙ ፡ መንክራቲሁ ፡ ዘከመ ፡ ኦደ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ኩሉ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አክናፈ ፡ ብር ሃን ፡ ነሚኦ ፡ ከመ ፡ የዐሥር ፡ ነፍሳተ ፡ ብዙኃን ፡ በጸውዖ ። ይእዜኒ ፡ ይተን ብል ፡ በእንቲአነ ፡ ምሕረተ ፡ ወሣህለ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ አጽርዖ ። ኀበ ፡ ብሉየ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ በግዕ ፡ አልፋ ፡ ወኦ ። ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ።
filled with wonder at the richness of the grace of the blessed one with praise and with acclamation, having heard his prodigy: he traveled the whole world, having taken wings of light, to invite the souls of many by a call. Now, may he ask in intercession for us mercy and clemency without interruption from the Ancient of Days, the Lamb, the Alpha and the Omega (Rev 1:8), for ever and ever, amen. Thirty-first miracle: the offering of the hopeless woman who did not

know Sämu’el. 249 31st miracle of our blessed father of beautiful memory, Israelite, white dove, column of the Church, the equal of the mountains of myrrh (Song 4:6) of Samaria and Judah. May the gift of his grace, which was given to him, be with us for ever and ever, amen. When the man of God rested, far from the fatigues of the (spiritual) combat and the pains of vigilance, in eternal life, the news of his death was heard in all the regions of Ethiopia; Yeshagq, the king who loves God, of upright faith, had a herald speak saying: “Make the commemoration of abbä Sämu'êl the hermit, so that he may take you as a tithe for his pact and also so that all your goods and the product of your hands may be blessed.” When they heard this word of the herald that the king had prescribed, all the people of Ethiopia made the commemoration of the man of God mär Sämu’êl, in joy and in exultation, having faith in his blessing.

250 But a woman without good action, of reduced knowledge, took bread and beer from her house and went, saddening, grieving, and murmuring in a place that is called Dabra Sinä that Däwit the king had had built. A man met this woman while she was traveling; when he saw her, he spoke and said to her: “Woman, what is the matter with you? Why are you grieving?” She answered and said to him: “My brother, because I have been ordered, by constraint, without my wanting it, to make the commemoration of a man whom I do not know, whom they call Sämu’el the hermit.” When she arrived at the church, she handed (her offering) to the one who was receiving the cakes from the part of the people who had brought (them) for the commemoration of this blessed father of ours. After that, when the offertory was finished, one saw the bread that the woman had brought become like dry cow dung; as for the beer, one found it had become blood. Immediately, the priests ordered the woman who had brought this bread and this beer to be brought. They spoke and said to her: “Woman, reveal now and tell your sin, for behold God has made a miracle appear through you.” When she heard this from the mouth of the priests, the woman was alarmed, a great fear took her, and she could not hide her

action; on the contrary, she informed those who had questioned her, saying: “I brought this without wanting to, saddening and grieving that I had been ordered, by constraint, to make the commemoration of a man whom I did not know.” When the priests heard this statement from the woman, they said among themselves: “The man of God has made a miracle seen through his prayer. Rise then now, let us pray.” They said many times: “Lord, have mercy on us.” After that, the bread that had become dry cow dung returned as before and the beer that had become like blood returned as before, in the state of beer.


[p. 210] {#po.53.p210}
ኦአበዊየ ፡ ወአኃውየ ፡ አንክሮ ፡ አንክሩ ፡ ተኣምራቲሁ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡ ሶበ ፡ አምጽአት ፡ ብእሲት ፡ ለተዝካሩ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ተስፋ ፡ መብ ልዐ ፡ መስቴ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ትቴክዝ ፡ ወተሐዝን ፡ ኀበ ፡ ከዊነ ፡ ጽፋዐ ፡ ላህም ፡ ይቡስ ፡ ለኅብስት ፡ ዘሜጦ ። ወኅበ ፡ ከዊነ ፡ ደም ፡ ለምዝር ፡ ዘወለጦ ። ይእ ዜኒ ፡ ያስተምሕር ፡ በእንቲአነ ፡ ቅድመ ፡ ፻ አካል ፡ ዘኢይትባዓድ ፡ በተፈልጦ ከመ ፡ ይዕቀበነ ፡ እምክረ ፡ መስቴማ ፡ ስት ፡ በዕፍረተ ፡ አእምሮ ፡ ጽዒጦ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ፡=
251 My fathers and my brothers, be filled with wonder at the miracle of our blessed father, he who, when the woman brought food (and) drink without hope for his commemoration while saddening and grieving, modified the bread into the state of dry cow dung and changed the beer into the state of blood. May he now intercede for us before the Three Persons who are not made different by separation, so that they may keep us from the design of the seductive Aggressor, (they) seasoning with the aromatics of knowledge, for ever and ever, amen. Thirty-second miracle: the dust of the tomb of Sämu’ël heals
ወ፪ ተኣምሪሁ ፡ ለብእሴ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ሠናየ ፡ ዝክር ፡ ስሙዓ ፡ ዜናሁ ፡ ዘይትበደር ፡ እምወርቅ ፡ ወእምብሩር ፡ እምጳዝዮን ፡ ወሰንፒር ፡ ማር ፡ ሳሙኤል ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን « ወበ 8እምነ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ ሶበ ፡ ፈቀዱ ፡ አኀው ፡ ከመ ፡ ይንግዱ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ኢየሩሳሌም ፡ ነሥኡ ፡ ጸበለ ፡ እምነ ፡ ዝኅሩ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡ ማር ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡ ወእምዝ ፡ ተንሥኡ ፡ ወሖሩ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ዘኀለዩ ፡ ወሶበ ፡ በጽሑ ፡ ብሔረ ፡ አረሚ ፡ ቦኡ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ባዕል ፡ ዘኢአምነ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይቢቱ ፡ ህየ = ወውእቱሰ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ አረማዊ ፡ ቦቱ ፡ ዓቢየ ፡ ደዌ ፡ እምነ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ ብዙኅ ። ወሶበ ፡ ርእዮሙ ፡ ለእሙንቱ ፡ መነኮሳት ፡ አው ሥአ ፡ ወይቤሎሙ ፡ እስእለክሙ ፡ አንሰ ፡ ለእመ ፡ ታአምሩ ፡ ፈውሰ ፡ ከመ ፡ ተሀቡኒ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ስመ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ከመ ፡ እሕዮ ፡ እምዝንቱ ፡ ደዌ ፡ ዘሀሎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ከርሥየ ። ተሰጥዉ ፡ እሉ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ ወይቤልዎ ፡ አልብነ ፡ ካልአ ፡ ፈውሰ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ጸበለ ፡ ዝኅሩ ፡ ለአባ ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡ ገባሬ ፡ መን ክራት ፡ ፈዋሴ ፡ ድዉያን ።= ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ሀቡኒኬ ፡ ኪያሁ ፡ እስመ ፡ አነሂ ፡ ሰማዕኩ ፡ ዜናሁ ፡ ለውእቱ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ዔር ። ከመ ፡ ይገብር ፡ ተኣምረ ፡ በጸሎቱ ።= ወወሀብዎ ፡ አሉ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ ንስቲተ ፡ መሬተ ፡ ዘነሥኡ ፡ እምነ ፡ ዝኅሩ ፡ ለብእሴ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወተመጢዎ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ አረማዊ ፡ አዘዘ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያምጽኡ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ማየ ፡ በጽዋዕ ።= ወወደየ ፡ ውስቴታ ፡ ይእተ ፡ መሬተ ፡ ዘተመጠወ ፡ እምእሉ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ ወእምዝ ፡ ሰትየ ።= ወሶቤሃ ፡ ወፅአ ፡ እም ከርሠ ፡ በአምሳለ ፡ ሐርገጽ ፡ ፻ወዛጥረሲሁ ፡ በቅድመ ፡ አሉ ፡ ቅዱሳን ። ወእሉሰ ፡ አኀው ፡ እለ ፡ ርእዩ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ አንከሩ ፡ ወሰብሕዎ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ እንዘ ፡ ያስተበፅዑ ፡ ተኣምሪሁ ፡ ለማር ፡ ሳሙኤል
a pagan. 252 32nd miracle of the man of God of beautiful memory, of famous reputation that outweighs gold and silver, topaz and sapphire, mär Sämu'ël. May his prayer and his blessing be with us for ever and ever, amen. One day, as brothers wanted to go on a pilgrimage to Jerusalem, they took dust from the tomb of our blessed father mär Sämu’êl, then they rose and went where they had decided. When they had arrived in the land of the pagans, they entered the house of a rich man who had not believed that they would spend the night there. This pagan man had had a serious illness for many days; when he saw the monks, he spoke and said to them: “I pray you, if you know a remedy, to give (it) to me for the name of God so that I may be healed of this illness that is in my belly.” The saints answered and said to him: “We have no other remedy than the dust of the tomb of abbä Sämu’el, the performer of prodigies, the healer of the sick.” (The man) said to them: “Give it to me then, for I too have heard the story of this virtuous man, namely that he performs miracles by his prayer.” These saints gave him a little of the earth that they had taken from the tomb of the man of God; (having received it, the pagan ordered that water be brought to him in a cup, he put the earth he had received from these saints into it, then he drank. Immediately, there came out of his belly what was similar to a crocodile — its teeth (were in the number of) one hundred and fifty — before the saints. The brothers who saw this marveled and glorified God, celebrating the miracle of mär Sämu’ël.

253 My beloved, Christian people, be filled with wonder at the action of this blessed father of ours, column of the sanctuary built on the rock of the faith, he who by the dust of his tomb made come out of the belly of a man what was similar to a crocodile. May he ask in intercession for us mercy and clemency before the Three Persons, so that they may make us hear the voice of joy and exultation in the fearsome court of justice, when they punish the transgressors of the Law and the Commandment, for ever and ever, amen. Thirty-third miracle: the bread of the commemoration of Sämu’ël
፲፻ ተኣምሪሁ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡ ሠናየ ፡ ዝክር ፡ ካህን ፡ ኅሩይ ። ዘውገ ፡ ወልደ ፡ ሕልቃና ፡ ነቢይ ። ፈዋሴ ፡ ድዉያን ፡ ዘኃጥኣን ፡ ምስካይ ። መልአክ ፡ ዘዲበ ፡ ምድር ፡ ወብእሲ ፡ ዘሰማይ ። ማር ፡ ሳሙኤል ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ አሜን ፡ ወበ፳እምነ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ ፀንሰት ፡ አሐቲ ፡ ብእሲት ፡ አረማዊት ፡ ዐቢየ ፡ ደዌ ፡ ወሕማመ ፡ ወሐብጠ ፡ ከርሣ ፡ እስከ ፡ ክሳዳ ። ወኩሎ ፡ ዕለተ ፡ ትከ ልሕ ፡ ወታአወዩ ፡ ወኢታዓርፍ ፡ አሐተ ፡ ሰዓተ ፡ ወኢትክል ፡ ከመ ፡ ትብ ላዕ ፡ ወትስተይ ፡ እምብገኃ ፡ ጻዕረ ፡ ደዌ ፡ ዘላዕሌሃ ። ወበ እምነ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ መጽአት ፡ ኀቤሃ ፡ ወለታ ፡ ከመ ፡ ተሐውጻ ፡ ለይእቲ ፡ ብእሲት ፡ እንተ ፡ ትደዊ ። እስመ ፡ ወለታ ፡ ክርስቲያናዊት ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ወሶበ ፡ ርእየታ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ትግዕር ፡ ወታንገረግር"" ፡ ዲበ ፡ ምድር ፡ አውሥአት ፡ ወትቤላ ፡ ኦእምየ ፡ እስመ ፡ ዮምሰ ፡ በዓለ ፡ ነቢይነ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ለክርስቲያን ፡ ዘይፌውስ ፡ ድዉ ያነ ፡ በጸሎቱ ፡ ወኩሎሙ ፡ ሕዝበ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ይትአመኑ ፡ ኪያሁ ፡ ለእመ ፡ ትክሊ ፡ በሊዐ ፡ እሖርኑ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኣምጽእ ፡ ለኪ ፡ ፍርፋራተ ፡ እምኀበ ፡ ዘተ ገብረ ፡ ተዝካሩ ፡ ለብእሴ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ። ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዐት ፡ ዘንተ ፡ እም አፈ ፡ ወለታ ፡ ተሰጥወት ፡ ወትቤላ ፡ ኦወለትየ ፡ ሖርኬ ፡ አምጽኢ ፡ ሊተ ፡ ለእመ ፡ ይፌውሰኒ ፡ በጸሎቱ ። ወተንሥአት ፡ ፍጡነ ፡ ወሖረት ፡ እንዘ ፡ ትጋጐእ"'” ፡፣ ውስተ ፡ አሐቲ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ኀበ ፡ ተገብረ ፡ ተዝካሩ ፡ ለብእሴ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ገባሬ ፡ መንክራት ፡ ማር ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡ ወሰአለት ፡ እምህየ ፡ ኅብስተ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትሰድ ፡ ለእማ ። ወወሀብዋ ፡ በከመ ፡ ትቤ ፡ ደቂቃ ፡ ለይእቲ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ። ወእምዝ ፡ ገብአት ፡ እንዘ ፡ ትጋገእ” ፡ ወሶበ ፡ በጽሐት ፡ ኀበ ፡ እማ ፡ መጠወታ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትብላዕ ፡ ውእተ ፡ ኅብስተ ፡ ዘአምጽአት ፡ ላቲ ። ወሶበ ፡ በልዓት ፡ ንስቲተ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ወፅኡ ፡ ፍጡነ ፡
heals a pagan woman. 254 33rd miracle of our blessed father of beautiful memory, elected priest, companion of the son of Elgana (1 Sam 1:1), the prophet, healer of the sick, support of sinners, angel who (is) on earth and man of heaven, mär Samu’êl. May his prayer and his blessing be with us, amen. One day, a pagan woman was pregnant with a great evil and suffering and her belly swelled up to her neck. All day, she cried, howled, and she did not cease for a single hour; she could not eat and drink because of the greatness of the torment of the illness that (weighed) on her. One day, the daughter of this woman who was sick came to her to visit her; the daughter was Christian. When she saw (her mother) groaning and rolling on the ground, she spoke and said to her: “My mother, today is the feast of our prophet, to (us) Christians, who heals the sick by his prayer; all the people of the Christians believe in him. If you can eat, shall I go to bring you crumbs from where the commemoration of the man of God is being made?” When (the woman) heard this from the mouth of her daughter, she answered and said to her: “My daughter, go then, bring me (some): perhaps he will heal me by his prayer.” (The daughter) rose quickly and went in haste to a church where the commemoration of the man of God, performer of prodigies, mär Sämu'el, had been made; she asked there for bread to bring (it) to her mother and the children of this church gave (it) to her, according to what she had said. After that she returned in haste; when she arrived to her mother, she handed her, so that she might eat (it), the bread she had brought her. When (the woman) had eaten a little of it, thirty puppies came out alive from her belly while barking. When she saw this miracle — she giving birth to puppies —, the mother (of the Christian woman) felt a great fright and cried out with a loud voice
እምውስተ ፡ ከርሣ ፡ ፳ቋእጓለ ፡ ከለባት ፡ ሕያዋኒሆሙ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይትናቀዉ ። ወሶበ ፡ ርእየት ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ተኣምረ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ትወልድ ፡ እማ ፡ እጓለ ፡ ከለባት ፡ ደንገፀት ፡ ዐቢየ ፡ ድንጋፄ ፡ ወከልሐት ፡ በዓቢይ ፡ ቃል ። ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዑ ፡ አግዋሪሃ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ትኬልሕ ፡ መጽኡ ፡ ኀቤሃ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይረውጹ ፡ ወመሰሎሙ ፡ ዘሞተት ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ብእሲት ፡ አረማዊት ፡ ዘነ በረት ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዓቢይ ፡ ደዌ ፡ ወሶበ ፡ በጽሑ ፡ ህየ ፡ ደንገፁ ፡ ወፈርሁ ፡ ርእዮሙ ፡ ዘኮነ ፡ ተኣምረ ፡ እምነ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ብእሲት ፡ አረማዊት ። ወእምዝ ፡
255 When her neighbors heard her cry, they came to her running; it seemed to them that this pagan woman who had remained in a great illness had died. When they arrived there, they were frightened and took fear, seeing the miracle that had occurred from this pagan woman.


[p. 214] {#po.53.p214}

After that, they questioned her daughter saying: “Who is the doctor who entered to your mother and healed her of this great illness that dwelt on her?” The daughter answered and said to them: “No doctor has entered here, of whom you speak; on the other hand, I went where the commemoration of abbä Sämu’ël was made and I brought (my mother) a piece of bread; when she had eaten a little of it, these puppies that you see came out of her belly.” When these people heard these words from the mouth of this daughter, they spoke among themselves saying: “In truth he is a righteous man, the man who performed this miracle by his prayer.” After that, they led the woman with the puppies to the king's palace. All those among the king's soldiers who saw this miracle glorified God that He had performed a miracle to make manifest the beauty of justice and the power of the prayer of our blessed father mär Sämu'ël. Glory to our God who chose this one from the womb of his mother so that he might be the heir of his kingdom, for ever and ever, amen!
ሥቱ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ፡ ኦአባ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡ ሠናየ ፡ ዝክር ፡ ተፈጸመ ፡ በላዕሌከ ፡ ዘይቤ ፡ እግዚ እነ ፡ ለአርዳኢሁ ። አማን ፡ አማን ፡ እብለክሙ ፡ ዘየአምን ፡ ብየ ፡ ግብረ ፡ ዘአነ ፡ እገብር ፡ ውእቱኒ ፡ ይገብር ፡ ወዘየዐቢ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ይገብር ። ወአነሂ ፡ እዌድሰከ ፡ በመትልወ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ቃል ፡ እንዘ ፡ እብል ፡ ኦአባ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡ ገባሬ ፡ መንክራት ፡ ዘውገ ፡ ብርሃናተ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ዓምደ ፡ ከኒሳ ። በኅብስት ፡ ዘተገብረ ፡ ለተዝካርከ ፡ ዘአውፃእከ ፡ ከመ ፡ ታርኢ ፡ ኀይለ ፡ መንክራቲከ ፡ ለብ እሲት ፡ እምውስተ ፡ ከርሣ ፡ እጓለ ፡ ከለባት ፡ ሰላሳ ። ይእዜኒ ፡ ትንብልና ፡ ጸሎ ትከ ፡ ይኩነነ ፡ ለእሊአከ ፡ ሰራዬ”" ፡ ኃጢአት ፡ ወደምሳሴ ፡ አበሳ ። ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ።=
256 Blessed Abba of beautiful memory, what Our Lord said to his disciples has been fulfilled in you: “Truly, truly I say to you: He who believes in me, the works that I do, he also will do (them) and he will do what is greater than this” (Jn 14:12). I also praise you following this word saying: Blessed Abbä, performer of prodigies, companion of the lights of the world, column of the Church, you who, with the bread that was made for your commemoration, made come out, to show the power of your prodigy, from the belly of the woman, thirty puppies, now, may the intercession of your prayer be for us, your own, forgiver of sin and eraser of fault, for ever and ever, amen. Thirty-fourth miracle: the earth of the tomb of Sämu'ël resurrects a dead man.
ወ፪ ተአምሪሁ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡ ሠናየ ፡ ዝክር ፡ ዘውገ ፡ ነቢያት ፡ ስኩረ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ሱታፌ ፡ ሐዋርያት ፡ ተላዌ ፡ አሰረ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ፈዋሴ ፡ ድዉያን ፡ ወመንሥኤ ፡ ምዉታን ፡ ምሉአ ፡ ጸጋ ፡ ወሞገስ ፡ ማር ፡ ሳሙኤል ።= ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሊ ፡ ምስሌነ ። ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ፡ ወካዕበ ፡ በ ፳ እምነ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ ሶበ ፡ ፈቀዱ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይጊሱ ፡ አኀው ፡ መነኮሳት ፡ ውስተ ፡ ምድረ ፡ ሴዋ ፡ ነሥኡ ፡ መሬተ ፡ እምነ ፡ ዝኅሩ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡ ገባሬ ፡ መንክራት ። ወሶበ ፡ በጽሑ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ምድረ ፡ አምሐራ ፡ ረከቡ ፡ በውስተ ፡ ፍኖት ፡ ብዙኃን ፡ ሰብእ ፡ እንዘ ፡ የሐውሩ ፡ ውስተ ፡ መቃብር ፡ ጸዊሮሙ ፡ በድነ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ዘሞተ ። ወሶበ ፡ ርእየ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ፳እምነ ፡ አኀው ፡ መነኮሳት ፡ አውሥአ ፡ ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ለእልክቱ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ አንብርዎ ፡
257 34th miracle of our blessed father of beautiful memory, companion of the prophets, drunk with the Spirit, associate of the apostles, follower of the trace of Christ, healer of the sick and resurrecter of the dead, full of grace and favor, mär Sämu’ël. May his prayer and his blessing be with us, for ever and ever, amen. And again, one day, as monk brothers wanted to leave early in the morning for the land of Choa, they took earth from the tomb of our blessed father, performer of prodigies. When they arrived in the land of Amhara, they met on the road many people in the process of going to a tomb while carrying the corpse of a man who had died. When he saw this, one of the monk brothers spoke and said to these people: “Lay down this corpse to see


[p. 216] {#po.53.p216}
ለዝንቱ ፡ በድን ፡ ከመ ፡ ትርአዩ ፡ ዕበዮ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ ዘይገብር ፡ ተኣምረ ፡ ወመንክረ ፡ በኀይለ ፡ ጸሎቱ ፡ ለአባ ፡ ሳሙኤል ። ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዑ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ነገረ ፡ እምነ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ መነኮስ ፡ አንበርዎ ፡ ለውእቱ ፡ በድን ፡ ከመ ፡ ይር አዩ ፡ ግብሮ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ። ወእምዝ ፡ ተመጠወ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ መነኮስ ፡ መሬተ ፡ ዘነሥአ ፡ እምነ ፡ ዝኅሩ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡ ምሉአ ፡ ጸጋ ፡ ሠናየ ፡ ዝክር ፡ ወቀብአ ፡ እንግድዓሁ ፡ ለውእቱ ፡ በድን ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ ያንሥእከ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ፍጡነ ፡ ወይጸጉከ ፡ ሕይወተ ፡ በኀይለ ፡ ጸሎቱ ፡ ለአባ ፡ ሳሙ ኤል ፡ ገባሬ ፡ መንክራት ። ወሶቤሃ ፡ ገብአት ፡ መንፈሱ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ለውእቱ ፡ ምዉት ፡ ወተንሥአ ፡ ፍጡነ ፡ ከመ ፡ እንተ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ዘነቅሃ ፡ እም ንዋሙ ። ወእሙንቱሰ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ እለ ፡ ሀለዉ ፡ ህየ ፡ ነጺሮሙ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ሰብ ሕዎ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ። ወእምዝ ፡ ፈትሕዎ ፡ እምነ ፡ ማእሰረ ፡ መግነዙ ፡ ለው እቱ ፡ በድን ፡ ወገብኡ ፡ ውስተ ፡ አብያቲሆሙ ፡ በፍሥሓ ፡ ወበሐሜት ፡ ምስለ ፡
the greatness of God who performs miracles and prodigies by the power of the prayer of abbä Sämu’ël.” When they heard these words from the monk, (the people) laid down the corpse to see the action of God. After that, the monk received (some) of the dust that he had taken from the tomb of our blessed father full of grace, of beautiful memory, and anointed the chest of the corpse with it saying: “May God raise you quickly and gratify you with life by the power of the prayer of abbä Sämu'ël, the performer of prodigies.” Immediately, the spirit of the dead man returned into his body and (the dead man) rose quickly like a man who has awakened from his sleep. The people who were there, seeing this, glorified God; then they delivered the (resurrected) corpse from its funeral wrappings and returned to their houses in joy and in exultation, with the man who had returned to life, marveling at the miracle of mär Sämu’êl of sweet memory.

258 Blessed Abbä, it has been effective again on you, the power that Our Lord gave to his disciples saying: “Heal the sick, resurrect the dead, cleanse the lepers, cast out demons. You have received freely, give freely; you have received graciously, give graciously” (Mt 10:8). My beloved, Christian people, be filled with wonder at the action of this blessed father of ours, sacrificing priest, resurrecter of the dead, companion of Elijah and Elisha. May he intercede for us with the Ancient of Days, the Lamb, so that He may make us hear the voice of joy and exultation with his Father and the Holy Spirit, when He comes to judge the living and the dead, for ever and ever, amen. Thirty-fifth miracle: a perjurer in the name of Sämu’ël receives his punishment.
፳ወ ፳ ተአምሪሁ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡ ሠናየ ፡ ዝክር ፡ በቅናተ ፡ ድንግ ልና ፡ እሱር ፡ ምዑዝ ፡ ከመ ፡ ዕፀ ፡ ዔሎቄ ፡ ዘኢያመጽለዎ ፡ ነፋሰ ፡ ሐሩር ። ማር ፡ ሳሙኤል ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ። ወካዐበ ፡ በ-፳እምነ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ ተሣየጠ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ እምካልኡ ፡ ክልኤተ ፡ አባዕረ ፡ ከመ ፡ የሀቦ ፡ እክለ ፡ ህየንቴሆሙ ። ወበዕለተ ፡ ዕድሜ ፡ መዋዐሊሁ ፡ መጽአ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይንሣእ ፡ ጠ ፡ አልህምቲሁ ፡ ነኦ ፡ አብቅልተ ፡ ወአእዱገ ።= ወሶበ ፡ በጽሐ ፡ ህየ ፡ ይቤሎ ፡ ኦእኅጥየ ፡ መጻእኩ ፡ ኀቤከ ፡ ከመ ፡ ተሀበኒ ፡ ሜጠ ፡ ንዋይየ ፡ ወውእቱኒ ፡ ይቤሎ ፡ በነገረ ፡ ትም ይንት ። ዮምሰ ፡ ቢት ፡ ዝየ ፡ ወአዕርፍ ፡ ጌሠመ ፡ በጽባሕ ፡ እሁበከ ፡ ወውእ ቱሰ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ህየ ፡ ወገብረ ፡ በከመ ፡ ይቤሎ ። ወእምዝ ፡ እምድኅረ ፡ ተደረ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ንዋም ፡ አስተብቀ'ዖ ፡ ለውእቱ ፡ እንግዳ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይኑም ፡ ውስተ ፡ ዓረቱ ። አውሥአ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ ኦእተቱየ ፡ ኅድገኒ ፡ አንሰ ፡ ኢይገብር ፡ ከመዝ ፡ አላ ፡ ሀበኒ ፡ ህየንተዝ ፡ ከመ ፡ እኑም ፡ ውስተ ፡ ካልእ ፡
259 35th miracle of our blessed father of beautiful memory, bound by the belt of virginity, embalming like a vine branch that the burning wind has not withered, mär Sämu'èl. May his prayer and his blessing be with us for ever and ever, amen. And again, one day, a man bought from another two plow oxen in order to give the latter wheat in their place. On the day of the fixed term, the (second) man came to receive the price of his oxen, having taken mules and donkeys. When he arrived there, he said to (the other): “My brother, I have come to you so that you might give me the price of my goods.” The (other) said to him with words of deception: “Today, stay here and rest: tomorrow morning, I will give (it) to you.” The man stayed there and did as the (other) had told him. Then, after he had dined, when it was the time of sleep, (the latter) begged the traveler to sleep in his bed. (The traveler) answered and said to him: “My brother, leave me: I will not do so: but permit me, instead of that, to sleep in another place.”

The (other) pressed him with insistence, seeking for him the pretext of the situation. When the traveler was asleep in the bed of the man who had begged him (to do so), after that, (the latter) said to his wife with words of iniquity: “Go in secret and sleep with him, without him being aware of you, behind him; I will go to the governor of the city and, having received men from him, I will come here, apprehend this man while he will be with you, and make him renounce the price of his beasts without him wanting to.” When the woman heard these words from the mouth of her husband, she answered and said to him: “My brother, do not do this thing of folly, so that God may not be irritated against us.” But the man with the evil design was irritated against her, took a stick, and wanted to strike her. As for her, when she saw his anger, she did as he had told her.

260 After this, the man arose and went to the governor of the city to fulfill the design of his father Satan; having received men from there, he returned to his house. He apprehended the other man while he was asleep in his bed with his wife, whom he had ordered to sleep behind him, and led him to the governor. When they arrived there, he was subjected with the other to judgment based on a lying word; the one who had been apprehended spoke and said: 'My lord, listen to me, let me inform you; order the people to be silent so that I may tell them everything that happened, that they may judge between us in justice: I came to this man's house, having taken donkeys and mules, so that he would give me the price of my beasts. When I arrived there, he said to me: Stay here and rest; tomorrow morning I will give it to you. Then, when it was time for sleep, he begged me with words of deception to sleep in his bed; I said to him: "I will not do so." But he pressed me with supplications, forcing me to sleep there. When I was asleep there, he ordered his wife to sleep with me, wishing to make me renounce the price of my beasts through words of cunning.' The iniquitous man spoke and said: 'I did nothing of what you say.' The other replied and said to him: 'Swear then now by the power of the prayer of Abba Samu'el, whose commemoration I wished to make with the price of my beasts.' The man accepted and swore falsely by the name of the man of God, saying: 'May the intercession of the prayer of Abba Samu'el divide me into two parts, I who have done nothing of what you say [if I lie].' Immediately, at the moment of the end of his word, it came suddenly from heaven like lightning; it struck the man and cut him into two parts before the assembly.

261 When the governor saw this cruel death that had suddenly struck the man, he sent for the wife of the man who had died in order to know exactly everything that had happened. When she arrived before him, he questioned her and said to her: 'Woman, tell us now and hide nothing of what happened regarding this man who came to your house

yesterday to receive the price of his beasts.' She made known to the governor all the injustice of her husband, how he had ordered her to sleep behind the man to make him renounce his property that he himself had acquired, under the pretext of this affair. When the governor and the people who were there heard this from the wife of the deceased, they marveled and glorified God, who loves justice and right and who performs miracles and wonders by the name of his servants. After this, the governor ordered the woman to give the man double the price of his property. From that moment, the man celebrated even more beautifully the commemoration of our blessed father, who had shown a miracle for him through the power of his prayer.

262 My fathers and my brothers, be filled with wonder at the action of this blessed father of ours, worker of wonders, who is called a companion of the Disciples, who divided into two parts the one who swore falsely by his name before the assembly. May he intercede for us before the Chief of days, who is girded with power, so that he may grant us, through his intercession, mercy and clemency, in the ages of ages, amen. poor man who had invoked Samu'el. 263 36th miracle of our blessed father of beautiful memory, locked by the belt of virginity, priest, companion of the seraphim, father of doves, shepherd of sheep and doctor of the Law, Mar Samu'el. May the intercession of his help be with us, in the ages of ages, amen. And again, one day, while a woman was making beer inside her dwelling to give drink to the people of her house, a poor man arrived, stood in the frame of her door, and cried out, saying: 'For the prayer of Abba Samu'el, help me with what you have, for I am poor and needy, I who have nothing.' As for the woman, she went out to him, having put her two hands behind her back so that the man would not see them, and said to him: 'Do not stand here, I have nothing to give you.' But the poor man solicited her even more, invoking the name of the man of God. She became angry with him so that he would leave the frame of her door and not bother her anymore. The poor man, when he saw her become angry with him, moved away from her, grieving and saddened that she had not welcomed him. As for her, she returned inside her house to attend to her work, but her hands, which she had put behind her while going out, were attached and she could not detach them. After this, she cried out, weeping.

264 When her neighbors heard the sound of her voice, they came to her to know what had happened to her and questioned her, saying: 'What is the matter with you that you weep and cry out?' She replied and said to them: 'A poor man came to me and solicited me, saying: "Help me for the prayer of Abba Samu'el."

I neglected him and became angry with him so that he would move away from me and not bother me anymore. When I returned inside my house to finish my task, my two hands were attached behind my back and refused to detach; I certainly did not know what had happened to me. Because of that, therefore, I screamed so that you would come to me and help me.' As for these people who had come to help her, it seemed to them that they could detach her: they seized her and pulled her hands, and she redoubled her cries. Many people came again; seeing this miracle that the man of God had performed by his prayer, they marveled and were stunned, being amazed at the greatness of our blessed father. After this, they spoke among themselves, saying: 'Come then, we will pray to God so that he may free this woman from the bonds of her hands.' They said: 'Lord, have mercy on us, Christ,' up to forty times. Then the woman's hands were detached; as for the beer of which she had refused a little to the poor man, these people drank it and left her none. After this, they went to their houses, marveling at the miracle of the blessed one.
አብያቲሆሙ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ያነክሩ ፡ ተአምራቲሁ ፡ ለብፁዓዊ ። ኦአባ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡ ኣነክር ፡ አንሰ ፡ ሰሚዕየ ፡ ተአምራቲከ ፡ ዘከመ ፡ አሰርካ ፡ ለብእሲት ፡ እደዊሃ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ሰናስል ፡ መንገለ ፡ ድኅሬሃ ። ሶበ ፡ ተሀየየቶ ፡ ለነዳይ ፡ በስመ ፡ ዚአከ ፡ ዘከልሓ ።= ይእዜኒ ፡ አስተምሕር ፡ በእን ቲአነ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ለጥበብ ፡ አቡሃ ። ከመ ፡ ይጸግወነ ፡ ርስተ ፡ ምስለ ፡ አፍሕር ቲሃ ። ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ።
265 Blessed Abba, I marvel, having heard your miracle: you attached the woman's hands behind her without chains when she neglected the poor man who had cried out to her in your name. Now, intercede for us before the Father of wisdom, so that he may grant us an inheritance with the chicks of this one, in the ages of ages, amen. Thirty-seventh miracle: Samu'el appears to a man with a twisted neck and heals him.

266 37th miracle of our blessed father of beautiful memory, the Israelite, Mar Samu'el. May his prayer, his blessing, and the gift of his help be with us in the ages of ages, amen. And again, one day, a sick man whose neck was twisted toward the back wanted to go to the place of the tomb of the man of God, having entrusted himself to the intercession of his prayer, seeking healing. When he went toward the east, he looked to the west, and when he went toward the west, he saw the east; it was not possible for him to turn his eyes from one side to the other, to the right and to the left; he remained like that for long days. While he was coming to the tomb of the healer, worker of wonders, Mar Samu'el, he missed the path that led there and went by another way in the desert, which led into the middle of a precipice where there was no passage. But our blessed father of beautiful memory, tender of heart like his God, waited for him, sitting beside the path, and said to him: 'My son, where are you going alone while you are sick?' The man replied and said to him: 'Abba, I am poor and needy, I have no one to guide me; I arose to go to the tomb of Abba Samu'el, having placed my hope in the power of his prayer, so that he might heal me of this illness of mine that you see.' Our blessed father replied and

said to him: 'My son, return then behind you, for it is not this path that leads where you say; on the contrary, it leads into the middle of a precipice where there is no passage.'

267 After having said this to him, the man of God Mar Samu'el seized the neck of the sick man and anointed it with the sign of the cross with a fragrant balm white as milk. Immediately the man was healed of his illness and his neck straightened; he who had remained twisted looking behind him turned toward the front. At that very moment, the man prostrated himself and fell at the feet of the man of God, marveling and being amazed at his miracle. The latter said to him: 'Rise, my son, return to your country and do not go to the place of my tomb.' After having said this to him, he disappeared from his eyes. The man who had been healed returned and came back to his country, rejoicing and exulting. All those who knew him, seeing how he had been healed of his illness, spoke and said to him: 'Who is the wise man who healed you of your illness? Where did you find such a physician who heals quickly, like God?' He replied and said to them: 'I set out, wanting to go to the place of the tomb of Abba Samu'el, but he met me on the way, healed me of my illness, and said to me: "Return to your country."' When they heard this account from him, they marveled and glorified God, being amazed at the holiness of our blessed father Mar Samu'el, full of grace and favor, healer of the sick by having sat on the way, companion of the Disciples, sweet priest (made) gentle by the anointing of knowledge, girded with virginity and purity like the Son of Thunder (Mk 3:17). May the intercession of his prayer make us sit at the table in the new assembly with the chicks of Gilead, blessed children, in the ages of ages, amen. Thirty-eighth miracle: Samu'el appears to a man beset by demons and assures him protection.
ቡሩካን ፡ ውሉድ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ፡። ቋ፳ ተአምሪሁ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡ ሠናየ ፡ ዝክር ፡ ፅፀ ፡ ገነት ፡ ምዑዝ ፡ ጽሑድ ፡ ዘኢስሔሑክ ፡ ርግብ ፡ የዋህ ፡ ወበግዕ ፡ ጻዓዳ ፡ ዘድኅነ ፡ እም አፈ ፡ አርዌ ፡ ድሩክ ። ዘሰማይ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ወዘምድር ፡ መልአክ ፡ ማር ፡ ሳሙ ኤል ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ የሀሉ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ። ወካዕበ ፡ በ ፳እምነ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ እንዘ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገዳም ፡ አሐዝዎ ፡ አጋንንት ፡ እኩያን ፡ ወፈቀዱ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያውፅኡ ፡ ነፍሶ ፡ ግብተ ፡ ወሰሐብዎ ፡ ከመ ፡ በግዕ ፡ ዘይወስድ ፡ ለተቀርጾ ። ወሶበ ፡ ገብአ ፡ ልቡ ፡ ለውእቱ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ዘአሐዝዎ ፡ እሉ ፡ መናፍስት ፡ ርኩሳን ፡ ከልሐ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይጹውዕ ፡ ስሞ ፡ ለአባ ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡ ወይትመሐፀን ፡ በኀይለ ፡ ጸሎቱ ። ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዑ ፡ አጋንንት ፡ ዘንተ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ተባሀሉ ፡ በበይናቲሆሙ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብሉ ፡ ናስተፋጥንኬ ፡ ወኢንትጐናደይ ፡ ከመ ፡ ንንሣእ ፡ ነፍሶ ፡ እምቅድመ ፡ ይምጻእ ፡ ኀቤነ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ዘጸውዖ ፡ ወኢያኅድገነ ። ወእንዘ ፡ ይትባሀሉ ፡ ከመዝ ፡
268 38th miracle of our blessed father of beautiful memory, embalmed tree of the Garden, sweet one who is not rough, sweet dove and white sheep that has been saved from the mouth of the cruel serpent, man of heaven and angel of the earth, Mar Samu'el. May his prayer and his blessing be with us in the ages of ages, amen. And again, one day, while a man was in the desert, evil demons seized him, wanted to make his soul depart suddenly, and dragged him like a sheep that is taken to be shorn (Is 53:7). When the heart of the man whom these impure spirits had seized returned, he cried out, invoking the name of Abba Samu'el and trusting in the power of his prayer. When the demons heard this from him, they spoke among themselves, saying: 'Let us hasten then and not delay in taking his soul before the man he has invoked arrives among us, so that he does not make us


[p. 226] {#po.53.p226}
ፀረ ፡ እጓለ ፡ እመሕያው ፡ በጽሐ ፡ ኀቤሆሙ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡ ሠናየ ፡ ዝክር ፡ ማር ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡ እጎዞ ፡ በእደዊሁ ፡ ረያመ ፡ መስቀለ ፡ ወርቅ ወእሙንቱሰ ፡ አጋንንት ፡ ሶበ ፡ ርእዩ ፡ ጸዳለ ፡ ብርሃኑ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ይብጻሕ ፡ ኀቤሆሙ ፡ ሐደግዎ ፡ ለውእቱ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ወተድህሉ ። ወእምዝ ፡ በጽሐ”" ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ ብእሴ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወአሐዘ ፡ እደዊሁ ፡ ወአንሥኦ ፡ ወአቀሞ ፡ በእገሪሁ ፡ ወባረከ ፡ ዲበ ፡ ፍጽሙ ፡ በውእቱ ፡ መስቀል ፡ ዘሀሎ ፡ ኀቤሁ ። ወይቤሎ ፡ ኦወልድየ ፡ ኢትፍራህ ፡ ናሁ ፡ መጻእኩ ፡ ኀቤከ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ጸዋዕከኒ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኣድኅንከ ፡ እምእሉ ፡ አጋንንት ። ወውእቱኒ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ኦአባ ፡ ይእ ዜኒ ፡ ኢትኅድገኒ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢይግብኡ ፡ እሉ ፡ ኀቤየ ። አውሥአ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ እምይእዜሰ ፡ ኢይክሉ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይትመየጡ ፡ ኀቤከ ፡ እስመ ፡ ተሐተምከ ፡ በዕፀ ፡ መስቀሉ ፡ ለክርስቶስ ፡ ወዘንተ ፡ ብሂሎ ፡ ተሰወረ ፡ እምአዕይንቲሁ ። ወውእቱሰ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ያስተዓፅብ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ገብአ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤቱ ፡ ወነገሮሙ ፡ ለአዝማዲሁ ፡ ወለአግዋሪሁ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ተአምራተ ፡ ዘገብረ ፡ ሎቱ ፡
let go.' While the enemies of the human race were speaking thus, our blessed father of beautiful memory, Mar Samu'el, arrived among them, holding in his hands a high golden cross. When they saw the brightness of his light before he had arrived among them, the demons left the man and withdrew. After this, the man of God arrived at him, took his hands, raised him up, made him stand on his feet, and made a blessing on his forehead with the cross that was with him. He said to him: 'My son, do not be afraid; behold, I have come to you, when you invoked me, to save you from these demons.' The man said: 'Abba, now, do not leave me, so that they do not return to me.' Samu'el replied and said to him: 'Henceforth, they cannot return to you, for you have been sealed by the wood of the cross of Christ.' Having said this, he disappeared from the eyes of the man. As for the latter, being amazed at this, he returned to his house and told his parents and his neighbors all the miracle that the man of God had done for him at the moment of his misfortune, when he had invoked his name.

269 My beloved, Christian people, be filled with wonder at the miracle of this blessed father of ours. Just as the good shepherd who snatches the sheep from the mouth of the wolf, so is he, who, sent by his God, freed from the hand of the demons the man who had invoked his name at the moment of his peril. Now, may he intercede for us before his God, for all of us who hope that he will grant us a share and an inheritance in the new world, with him in a single existence, in the ages of ages, amen. Thirty-ninth miracle: Samu'el gives back to the poor woman the ox

that she offered for his commemoration. 270 39th miracle of the blessed rock of faith, vigorous soldier, fighting with the sword of the Trinity, pride of us all, companion of the horns of Zion our fathers, associate of the horses of the Gospel our lords, Mar Samu'el. May his prayer and his blessing be with us in the ages of ages, amen. One day, the day of the commemoration of the saint, a woman whose back was bent and all whose limbs were infirm, who could not lift her eyes to look at the sky—on the contrary, she went stooped looking at the earth—arose to go to the church, having taken an ox for the feast of the commemoration of the man of God; she had no other property besides it. After this, when she arrived at the church, she handed this ox to the priests, saying: 'Let it be slaughtered for the prayer of Abba Samu'el.' The priests replied and said to her: 'We do not accept it from you, for you are poor and needy, you who have no other property besides this one.' She replied and said to them: 'My lords, I ask you to do for me what I have told you, for the intercession of the prayer of Abba Samu'el is


[p. 228] {#po.53.p228}
ሳሙኤል ፡ እምአጥርዮ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ ላህም ። ወሶበ ፡ ርእዩ ፡ እልክቱ ፡ ካህናት ፡ ጥብዓተ ፡ ልባ ፡ ወጽንዓተ ፡ ሃይማኖታ ፡ ለይእቲ ፡ ብእሲት ፡ ተመጠዉ ፡ ላቲ ፡ ውእተ ፡ ላህመ ፡ ዘአምጽአት ፡ በእንተ ፡ ስሙ ፡ ለቅዱስ ፡ ወጠብሕዎ ። ወሶ ቤሃ ፡ ነሥአት ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ብእሲት ፡ እምደመ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ላህም ፡ ወቀብአት ፡ መካነ ፡ ሕማማ ፡ ወረትዓ ፡ ዘባና ፡ ዘነበረ ፡ ጉሁቀ ፡ ወተፈወሰት ፡ እምኩሉ ፡ ደዌሃ ። ወሶበ ፡ ርእዩ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ተአምረ ፡ ካህናተ ፡ እንትኩ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡
better for me than possessing this ox.' When these priests saw the decision of the heart and the firmness of the faith of this woman, they accepted for her the ox that she had brought for the name of the saint and slaughtered it. Immediately, the woman took some of the ox's blood and anointed the place of her ailment with it: her back which had remained bent straightened, and she was healed of all her illness. When the priests of this church and all the people who were there saw this miracle, they marveled and glorified God.
ወኩሎሙ ፡ ሕዝብ ፡ እለ ፡ ሀለዉ ፡ ህየ ፡ አንከሩ ፡ ወሰብሕዎ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ። ወይእቲኒ ፡ ብእሲት ፡ ገብአት ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤታ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ትትፌሣሕ ፡ ወትትሐሠይ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ዘሐይወት ፡ እምደዌሃ ፡ ጸዊራ ፡ እምገደላ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ላህም ፡ ዘወሃብዋ ። ወውእቱኒ ፡ ላህም ፡ ዘተጠብሐ ፡ ወተበልዐ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ረከ በቶ ፡ ሕያዎ ፡ እምድኅረ ፡ ድራር ፡ እገዘ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ ስኩብበ?”" ፡ በውስተ ፡ ምብ ያቱ ፡ በከመ ፡ ልማዱ ። ወሶበ ፡ ኮነ ፡ ሳኒታ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ጽባሕ ፡ ተንሥአት ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ብእሲት ፡ እማሕደራ ፡ ወሖረት ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ወአይድ ዓቶሙ ፡ ለካህናት ፡ ዘከመ ፡ ረከበቶ ፡ ሕያዎ ፡ ለውእቱ ፡ ላህም ፡ ዘተጠብሐ ። ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዑ ፡ ካህናት ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ነገረ ፡ እምይእቲ ፡ ብእሲት ፡ ለአኩ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤታ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይጠየቁ ፡ ለእመ ፡ ኮነ ፡ እሙነ ፡ ወእምዝ ፡ ገብኡ ፡ ልኡካን ፡ ርእዮሙ ፡ ውእተ ፡ ላህመ ፡ ዘተጠብሐ ፡ ወዜነውዎሙ ፡ ለካህናት ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብሉ ፡ አማንኬ ፡ ርኢነ ፡ መንክራቲሁ ፡ ለብእሴ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወረከብናሁ ፡ ለውእቱ ፡ ላህም ፡ ዘተጠብሐ ፡ ሕያዎ ፡ በከመ ፡ ትቤ ፡ ዛቲ ፡ ብእሲት ። ወእምዝ ፡ ገብሩ ፡ ጉባኤ ፡ እሉ ፡ ካህናት ፡ ወአዘዝዎሙ ፡ ለሕ ዝብ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያፈድፍዱ ፡ ገቢረ ፡ ተዝካሮ ፡ ለብእሴ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ማር ፡
271 As for the woman, she returned to her house, rejoicing and exulting that she had been healed of her illness, carrying a part of the carcass of the ox, which had been given to her. As for the ox that had been slaughtered and whose flesh had been eaten, she found it alive after the meal, lying in its stable according to its habit. When the next day arrived at the time of the morning, the woman went out of her dwelling, went to the church, and informed the priests how she had found the ox that had been slaughtered alive. When the priests heard this account from the woman, they sent to her house to know if it was true. After this, the messengers returned, having seen the ox that had been slaughtered; they announced it to the priests, saying: 'In truth, we have seen the wonder of the man of God and we have found the ox that was slaughtered alive, as this woman said.' After this, the priests held a meeting and prescribed to the people to celebrate even more beautifully the commemoration of the man of God, Mar Samu'el.
ሳሙኤል ። ኦአባ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡ ሠናየ ፡ ዝክር ፡ እዌድስ ፡ አንሰ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ኣስተዓፅብ ፡ ዘዚአከ ፡ ጽድቀ ።= ሰሚዕየ ፡ ተአምራቲከ ፡ እምድኅረ ፡ ተጠብሐ ፡ ወተበልዐ ፡ ዘአሕየውኮ ፡ ለላህመ ፡ ጥዩቀ ።= ዘፈወስካ ፡ ለብእሲት ፡ ሶበ ፡ ተቀብዓት ፡ እም ደሙ ፡ ዘነበረት ፡ ጐሔሑቀ ። ወይእዜኒ ፡ አስተምሕር ፡ በእንቲአነ ፡ ኀበ ፡ እግ ዚአ ፡ ኀያላን ፡ እምጥፕኅተ ፡ ሕዝቅኤል ፡ ዘሠረቀ ፡ እጓለ ፡ አንበሳ ፡ መፍርህ ፡ በይሁዳ ፡ ዘተዓውቀ ።፡ ከመ ፡ ይጸግወነ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ነፍሰ ፡ ዚአከ ፡ ውስተ ፡ መክፈልተ ፡ ርስትከ ፡ ምርፋቀ ። ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ።
272 Blessed Abba of beautiful memory, I offer praises celebrating your justice, having heard your miracle: after it was slaughtered and eaten, you restored the ox exactly to life, you who healed the woman when she anointed herself with the blood of this one, she who had remained bent. Now, intercede for us before the Lord of powers who rose from the gate of Ezekiel (Ez 44:2), who is known in Judah as a shoot of a frightening lion (Rev 5:5), so that he may grant us with your soul a banquet in your share of inheritance, in the ages of ages, amen. Fortieth miracle: Samu'el blesses the house of a man who gave alms in his name.
ጣ ተኣምሪሁ ፡ ለብእሴ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ዓምደ ፡ ሃይማኖት ፡ ጽኑዕ ፡ በመንፈሰ ፡ ጸጋ ፡ ሱሩር ። ጽዋዐ ፡ ወርቅ ፡ ጽሩይ ፡ ኩሳኩሰ ፡ መርዓት ፡ ዘብ ሩር ። ዓጸደ ፡ ወይን ፡ ስሙር ፡ በሐሩረ ፡ ፀሐይ ፡ ዘኢየዐብር ፡ ማር ፡ ሳሙ ኤል ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ ያድኅነነ ፡ እምኀይለ ፡ ጸላኢ ፡ ወፀር ። ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ። ወበ ፳እምነ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ አቅረበት ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ለብእሲ ፡ ብእሲቱ ፡ ማእደ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ፻ሰዓት ፡ ወሰአለቶ"" ፡ ከመ ፡ ይብላዕ ። ወእምቅድመ ፡ ይጥዕም ፡ እም ኔሁ ፡ መጽአት ፡ ብእሲት ፡ ነዳይት ፡ ዘተኀሥሥ ፡ ምጽዋተ ፡ ከልሐት ፡ ቀዊማ ፡ ውስተ ፡ አንቀጸ ፡ ዴዴሁ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ትብል ፡ በእንተ ፡ ጸሎቱ ፡ ለአባ ፡
273 40th miracle of the man of God, pillar of the solid faith founded on the spirit of grace, cup of pure gold, silver nuptial urn, pleasant vine that is not withered by the heat of the sun, Mar Samu'el. May his prayer and his blessing save us from the power of the Adversary and Enemy, in the ages of ages, amen. One day, his wife brought a dish to a man, at the time of the third hour, and begged him to eat. Before he had tasted it, a poor woman arrived who was asking for alms. She cried out, standing in the door of the entrance, saying: 'For the prayer of Abba Samu'el, welcome me and help me with what

ሳሙኤል ፡ ተወከፉኒ ፡ ወርድኡኒ ፡ እምዘብክሙ ።= ወውእቱሰ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ጎር ፡ ሶበ ፡ ሰምዐ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ትጹውዕ ፡ ስሞ ፡ ለአባ ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡ መጠዋ ፡ ለብእሲት ፡ ዘሰአለት ፡ ውእተ ፡ ማእደ ፡ ዘአቅረበት ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ብእሲቱ ፡ እንበለ ፡ ይጥዕም ፡ እምኔሁ ። ወይእቲኒ ፡ ብእሲት ፡ ዘወሀባ ፡ ምጽዋተ ፡ ባረከት ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ በስሙ ፡ ለብእሴ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወሖረት ፡ እንዘ ፡ ትትፌሣሕ ፡ ወትጐድእ ፡ እንግድ ዓሃ ። ወሶቤሃ ፡ ተክዕወ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤቱ ፡ ዐቢየ ፡ በረከተ ፡ ወመልአ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ሙዳያተ ፡ እክሉ ፡ በበ ፡ ሕጠተ ፡ ዘመዱ ፡ እስከ ፡ መዓር ፡ ወቅብእ ፡ ወፄው ፡ መልኡ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ በበ ፡ ሙዳዮሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ልኩንታት ። ወሶበ ፡ ርእየ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ሀብተ ፡ ጸጋ ፡ ዐቢይ ፡ ዘረከበ ፡ በኀይለ ፡ ጸሎቱ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡ ማር ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡ አውሥአ ፡ ወይቤላ ፡ ለብእሲቱ ፡ ንዒኬ ፡ ኦእኅ ትየ ፡ ንስግድ ፡ ወንጹሊ ፡ ቅድመ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ በእንተ ፡ ዘወሀበነ ፡ ረድ ኤተ ። ወነበሩ ፡ እሉ ፡ እምይእቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ እንዘ ፡ ያፈደፍዱ ፡ ውሂበ ፡ ምጽ
you have.' The good man, when he heard her invoke the name of Abba Samu'el, handed to the woman who was soliciting the dish that his wife had brought him, without having tasted it. The woman to whom he had given the alms blessed him in the name of the man of God and went away, rejoicing and striking her breast. Immediately, a great blessing spread in the house of the man and filled all his grain containers, each with the seeds of his family—even honey, oil, and salt filled all, each in its container, the jars. When the man saw this great gift of grace that he had obtained through the power of the prayer of our blessed father Mar Samu'el, he spoke and said to his wife: 'Come then, my sister, let us prostrate ourselves: we will pray before God for the help he has given us.' From that day, they were even more diligent in giving alms in the name of the man of God and in making his commemoration.

274 Blessed Abba, you who were like Elijah the prophet who made the blessing descend into the widow's jar of flour and her jar of oil—he made it so that she fed herself with her child for many days through the power of his prayer until God showed clemency toward his creature and gave rain to the earth (1 Kgs 17:14-15)! I praise your virtue, blessed Abba, having heard; what you did surpasses this miracle: for the man, you filled his house with blessing when, in your name, he gave alms to the poor. Now, ask for us through intercession clemency and help before him who made the barren woman give birth seven times (1 Sam 2:5), so that he may make us a tithe for your covenant and so that he may grant us in your holy city a share and an inheritance, in the ages of ages, amen. Forty-first miracle: Samu'el makes the soldiers of the plague leave the house of the good man.


[p. 232] {#po.53.p232}

toward the west. The believer, when he saw what our blessed father had done for him through the Holy Spirit who resided upon him, rejoiced, exulted, and made known to the people born in his house everything that had happened. When the next day arrived at the time of the morning, a servant of God sent to him, saying: 'Do you know what Abba Samu'el did for you this night that has just passed, how he made the soldiers of the plague leave your house and chased them toward the west?' When he heard this account from the mouth of the messenger who had spoken to him, he replied and said to him: 'Yes, yes, I know and I saw him make these demons leave and chase them from my house.'

276 My beloved, Christian people, be filled with wonder at the miracle of this blessed father of ours of beautiful memory, he who came when the man, with confidence in his name, called him to his help and who made the soldiers of the plague leave his house. May he therefore intercede for us now before the judge of justice who is girded with righteousness, so that he may grant us a beautiful reward when he gives a retribution to the one who has conquered, in the ages of ages, amen. Forty-second miracle: the dust of the tomb of Samu'el heals
ጓ፪ ተኣምሪሁ ፡ ለብእሴ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ካህን ፡ ምዑዝ ፡ በዕፍረተ ፡ ርሔ ፡ ዘተጽዕጠ ። ፍትወተ ፡ ሥጋ ፡ መኒኖ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ግዕዘ ፡ መላእክት ፡ ዘተመ ይጠ ። በአርዑተ ፡ ወንጌል ፡ ቅኑይ ፡ መስተገብረ ፡ ጽድቅ ፡ በፍቅረ ፡ ሥላሴ ፡ ዘተዓብጠ ማር ፡ ሳሙኤል ። ትንብልና ፡ ጸሎቱ ፡ ትኩን ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ ለዓ ለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ፡፥ ወካዕበ ፡ በ ፳ እምነ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ መጽአ ፡ አሐዱ ፡ መነኮስ ፡ እምርሑቅ ፡ ብሔር ፡ ከመ ፡ ይትአማኅ ፡ መቃብሮ ፡ ለብእሴ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወሶበ ፡ በጽሐ ፡ ኀበ ፡ መካነ ፡ ዝኅሩ ፡ ተአምኃ ፡ ወአንገርገረ ፡ ዲቤሁ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ነሚኦ ፡ መሬተ ፡ እምህየ ፡ ወተመይጠ ፡ ወገብአ ፡ ብሔሮ ።» ወበ፳ እምነ ፡ መዋ ዕል ፡ እንዘ ፡ ያንሶሱ ፡ ውስተ ፡ መራኅብተ ፡ ሀገር ፡ ርእየቶ ፡ አሐቲ ፡ ብእ ሲት ፡ አረማዊት ፡ ዘባቲ ፡ ዐቢየ ፡ ደዌ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ሀለወት ፡ ነቢራ ፡ መልዕልተ ፡ ተስላስ ፡ ወለአከት ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይምጻእ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ሀለወት ። ወ"ሶቤሃ ፡ ቀርበ ፡ ኀቤሃ ፡ አውሥአት ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ እስእለከ ፡ ኦብእሲ ፡ ክርስቲያናዊ ፡ ለእመ ፡ ብከ ፡ ሥራየ ፡ ከመ ፡ ተሀበኒ ። ተሰጥወ ፡ ወይቤላ ፡ ምንትኑ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ደዌኪ ፡ ኦብእሲቶ ። አውሥአት ፡ ወትቤሎ ፡ ናሁ ፡ ፲ወ፻ ዓመተ ፡ እምዘ ፡ ፀነስኩ ፡ ከይሴ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ከርሥየ ፡ ወብዙኃን ፡ ዐቀብተ ፡ ሥራይ ፡ ሥእኑ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይፈውሱኒ ፡ ወአልቦ ፡ ዘበቀሩዓኒ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ዘነሥኡ ፡ ንዋይየ ። ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዓ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ እምይእቲ ፡ ብእሲት ፡ አውሥኣ ፡ ውእቱኒ ፡ ወይቤላ ፡ አል ብየ ፡ ካልእ ፡ ሥራይ ፡ እንበለ ፡ መሬተ ፡ ዝኅሩ ፡ ለአባ ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡ ገባሬ ፡ መንክራት ፡ ፈዋሴ ፡ ድዉያን ። ወትቤሎ?" ፡ ሀበኒ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ወወሀባ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ መነኮስ ፡ ንስቲተ ፡ መሬተ ፡ ዘነሥአ ፡ እምዝኅሩ ፡ ለብእሴ ፡ እግዚአ ብሔር ። ወተመጢዋ ፡ እምኔሁ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ብእሲት ፡ ውእተ ፡ መሬተ ፡ ቦበ ፡
a pagan woman. 277 42nd miracle of the man of God, sweet priest who is embalmed by a fragrant perfume, who, having despised the desire of the flesh, turned toward the nature of the angels, who, worker of justice submitted to the yoke of the Gospel, was required by the love of the Trinity, Mar Samu'el. May the intercession of his prayer be with us, in the ages of ages, amen. And again, one day, a monk came from a distant country to greet the tomb of the man of God. When he arrived at the place of his tomb, he greeted it and rolled himself upon it. After this, having taken some dust from there, he returned and came back to his country. One day, while he was walking in the streets of a city, a pagan woman who had a great illness saw him, being seated on a terrace; she sent to him so that he would come to where she was. He then approached her, she spoke and said to him: 'I ask you, Christian man, if you have a remedy, to give it to me.' He replied and said to her: 'What is your illness, woman?' She replied and said to him: 'It has been thirteen years that I have harbored a serpent in my belly; many physicians have been unable to heal me, nothing has been profitable to me—except for the fact that they have taken my money' (Mk 5:25-26). When he heard this from the woman, the monk replied and said to her: 'I have no other remedy than the dust of the tomb of Abba Samu'el, the worker of wonders, the healer of the sick.' The woman said to him: 'Give me some.' The monk gave her a little of the dust that he had taken from the tomb of the man of God. When, having received the dust from him,

the woman had eaten it, a serpent whose length was seventy cubits came out of her belly. When she saw this serpent that had come out of her belly, she cried out with a loud voice, saying: 'I believe in the God of the man who healed me of my illness with the dust of his tomb!'

278 When her neighbors heard this word, they came to her running. Arrived there, they marveled, seeing the serpent that had come out of her belly. After this, they questioned her, saying: 'Who is the one who healed you?' She replied and said to them: 'This monk came to me, I asked him to give me a remedy if he knew of one; he said to me: "I have no other remedy than the dust of the tomb of the man of God, the healer of the sick who is called Abba Samu'el."' When they heard this from her, all her parents and her neighbors said: 'We all also believe in the name of the God of the man who healed you.' After this, all went to the church with this woman and made known to the priests the reason for their faith; these baptized them in the name of the Father, the Son, and the Holy Spirit.
በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ። ኦአባ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡ ሠናየ ፡ ዝክር ፡ መዝገበ ፡ ቅድስና ። ለባሕርየ ፡ ንጽሕ ፡ አስከሬና ።= ዘአእመንካ ፡ ለብእሲት ፡ አረማዊት ፡ በስመ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ምስለ ፡ አዝማዲሃ ፡ ወአግዋሪሃ ፡ በመሬተ ፡ ዝኅርከ ፡ አውዒአከ ፡ ከይሴ ፡ ድበእመት ፡ እምውስተ ፡ ማኅፀና ። ይእዜኒ ፡ ትንብልና ፡ ጸሎትከ ፡ ይኩነነ ፡ ባላሔ ፡ እሞተ"" ፡ ሙስና ። ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ።«
279 Blessed Abba of beautiful memory, treasure of holiness, casket of the pearl of purity, you who made the pagan woman believe in the name of Christ, with her parents and her neighbors, having by the dust of your tomb made a serpent of seventy cubits come out of her bosom! Now, may the intercession of your prayer be for us a liberator from the death of corruption, in the ages of ages, amen. Forty-third miracle: a blind man is healed by rolling himself on the

tomb of Samu'el. 280 43rd miracle of our blessed father of beautiful memory, fragrant tree of the Garden, who was nourished and grew with the two breasts of the Bride, grain of the Spirit, powerful winnowing shovel of the faith, who put back the oil of his lamp, having filled it with a bulb, Mar Samu'el. May the intercession of his prayer grant us a beautiful reward in the new resurrection, in the ages of ages, amen. And again, one day, a blind man came, his son guiding him, to roll himself on the tomb of the man of God; when he arrived there, he took some of the dust of the tomb of this one and anointed his eyes with it, saying: 'Abba, healer of the sick, I have come to the place of your tomb, having faith in the intercession of your prayer. Now, grant me light for my eyes.' After the blind man had said this, at the moment of the completion of his word, his eyes opened and he saw the light. Immediately, he prostrated himself, glorified God, and returned to his country, being amazed at the greatness of the blessed, holy, and just one, of sweet memory, Mar Samu'el.


[p. 236] {#po.53.p236}
ኦአባ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡ ገባሬ ፡ መንክራት ፡ በመሬተ ፡ ዝኅርከ ፡ ብርሃነ ፡ ለአዕይንተ ፡ ዕዉራን ፡ ዘትጹጉ ። አስተምሕር ፡ በእንቲአነ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ክገጽ ፡ ወ፻ አካል ፡ ወ ፳ህላዌ ፡ በተፈልጦ ፡ እለ ፡ ኢይትባዐዱ ፡ ወበሑፃፄ ፡ እለ ፡ ኢይነትጉ ። ከመ ፡ ይጸግወነ ፡ ዕሜተ ፡ ሠናየ ፡ በ፳ምክር ፡ ፍትወተ ፡ ዓለም ፡ በእንተ ፡ ስሙ ፡ ምስለ ፡ እለ ፡ ሐደጉ ፍ ኅሩያን ፡ በየማኑ ፡ በፍሥሓ ፡ አመ ፡ ያንገልጉ ።= ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ።
281 Blessed Abba, worker of wonders by the dust of your tomb, you who grant light to the eyes of the blind, intercede for us before the Three Persons—three hypostases and one nature—which by separation are not different and which by diminution are not lessened, so that they may grant us a beautiful reward, of one will, with those who have abandoned for their name the desire of the age, when the elect press to their right in joy, in the ages of ages, amen. Forty-fourth miracle: the dust of the tomb of Samu'el
ጣወ8 ተኣምሪሁ ፡ ለብእሴ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ዘውገ ፡ መክብብ ፡ በሊሐ ፡ ልሳን ፡ ከመ ፡ አበ ፡ ኢዮሳብ ። መምህረ ፡ ሕግ ፡ ጽጉበ ፡ መጻሕፍት ፡ ከመ ፡ ጳውሎስ ፡ ልሳነ ፡ ዘርብ ። ማር ፡ ሳሙኤል ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወስእለቱ ፡ ያርፍቀነ ፡ በሐዳስ ፡ ከብካብ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ዕሠራን ፡ ዘወንጌል ፡ አርጋብ ።ፍ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ። ወካዕበ ፡ በ ፳-እምነ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ ነሥአ ፡ እምጸበለ ፡ ዝኅሩ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ብፁ ዓዊ ፡ ማር ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡ አሐዱ ፡ እምአርዳኢሁ ፡ ዘኅዱር ፡ ላዕሌሁ ፡ ጸጋ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ። ይቤ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ረድእ ፡ መጽኡ ፡ ዕዉራን ፡ ኀበ ፡ መቃ ብሩ ፡ ለብእሴ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ እንዘ ፡ የሐሥሠ ፡ ፈውሰ ፡ ወረከቡኒ ፡ አፍአ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ኣንሶሱ ፡ ወሰአሉኒ ፡ ሊተ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኣብጽሖሙ ፡ ኀበ ፡ መቃብሩ” ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ። ወእቤሎሙ ፡ እመሰ ፡ ብክሙ ፡ ሃይማኖት ፡ ሀሎ ፡ ኀቤየ ፡ መሬተ ፡ ዝኅሩ ። ወይቤሉኒ ፡ እወኬ ፡ ብነ ፡ ሃይማኖት ፡ እስመ ፡ ተሰፊወነ ፡ ትንብልና ፡ ጸሎቱ ፡ መጻእነ ፡ ዝየ ። ወሶበ ፡ ሰምዐ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ እምኔሆሙ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ረድእ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ነሥአ ፡ ውእተ ፡ መሬተ ፡ ዘሀሎ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ ወቀ ብኦሙ ፡ አዕይንቲሆሙ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ወመድኀኒነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ ፈዋሴ ፡ ድዉያን ፡ ከሣቴ ፡ አዕይንተ ፡ ዕዉራን ፡ ይጸጉክሙ ፡ ብርሃነ ፡ በኀይለ ፡ ጸሎቱ ፡ ለአባ ፡ ሳሙኤል ። ወሶቤሃ ፡ ተፈወሱ ፡ እልክቱ ፡ ፄ5ዕዉራን ፡ ወተከሥታ ፡ አዕይንቲሆሙ ፡ ወርእዩ ፡ ብርሃነ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ገብኡ ፡ ለለብሔሮሙ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይትፌሥሑ ፡ ወይሴብሕዎ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ በእንተ ፡
heals seven blind men. 282 44th miracle of the man of God, companion of the Ecclesiastes, with a tongue sharp as the father of Joseph, doctor of the Law nourished by the Scriptures like Paul with the tongue of a plectrum, Mar Samu'el. May his prayer and his solicitation make us sit at the table at the new wedding with the doves invited by the Gospel, in the ages of ages, amen. And again, one day, one of his disciples upon whom the grace of the Holy Spirit resided took some of the dust of the tomb of our blessed father Mar Samu'el. The disciple said: 'Seven blind men came to the tomb of the man of God, seeking healing; they met me outside while I was walking and asked me to bring them to the tomb of our blessed father. I said to them: "If you have faith, there is with me some dust of his tomb." They said to me: "Yes indeed, we have faith, for it is by having entrusted ourselves to the intercession of the prayer of Samu'el that we have come here."' When the holy disciple heard this from them, he took the dust that was with him and anointed their eyes with it, saying: 'May Our Lord and Savior Jesus Christ, healer of the sick, who opens the eyes of the blind, grant you light through the power of the prayer of Abba Samu'el.' Immediately, these seven blind men were healed, their eyes opened, and they saw the light. After this, they returned each to their country, rejoicing and glorifying God that he had granted them healing through the intercession of the prayer of Abba Samu'el.
ዘጸገዎሙ ፡ ሕይወተ ፡ በትንብልና ፡ ጸሎቱ ፡ ለአባ ፡ ሳሙኤል ። ኦፍቁራንየ ፡ ሕዝብ ፡ መሲሓዊያን ፡ አንክሮ ፡ አንክሩ ፡ መንክራተ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ አቡነ ፡ በመሬተ ፡ ዝኅሩ ፡ ዘይጸጉ ፡ ለአዕይንተ ፡ ዕዉራን ፡ ብርሃነ ። ያስተምሕርኬ ፡ ይእዜኒ ፡ በእንቲአነ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ዘተብህለ ፡ እመንገለ ፡ እሙ ፡ እነ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይረስየነ ፡ ዕሥሠራነ ፡ ለከብካበ ፡ መርዓ ፡ ምስለ ፡ እለ ፡ አር ብሑ ፡ ምናነ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ፡
283 My beloved, Christian people, be filled with wonder at the miracle of this father of ours who, through the dust of his tomb, grants light to the eyes of the blind. May he therefore intercede for us now with him who is called our brother by his mother, so that he may make us guests at the nuptial feasts with those who have made their mina bear fruit, in the ages of ages, amen.


[p. 238] {#po.53.p238}
ጣ፳ ተኣምሪሁ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡ ኦመ ፡ ርጌ ፡ ምዑዝ ፡ ማእከለ ፡ አፍላግ ፡ መርዓት ፡ ዘኮነ ፡ ምትከሉ ። አዕዋፈ ፡ ወንጌል ፡ ፍሠሓን ፡ ታሕተ ፡ አዕፁቂሁ ፡ ዘያጸልሉ'" = ኮከብ ፡ ብሩህ ፡ ንጹሐ ፡ ምግባር ፡ ዘፀሐይ ፡ አም ሳሉ ። ማር ፡ ሳሙኤል ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ ይረስየነ ፡ ድልዋነ ፡ ውስተ ፡ መክፈልተ ፡ ርስቱ ፡ ነሀሉ ። ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ። ወካዕበ”" ፡ ይቤ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ረድእ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ አሰረ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ ዘይተሉ ፡ ርቱዐ ፡ ሃይማኖት ፡ ማኅደረ ፡ ሥሎሥ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ዘስሙ ፡ ቶማስ ። ወነበረ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡ እምድኅረ ፡ እረፍቱ ፡ ይመጽእ ፡ ኀቤየ ፡ ለለዕለቱ ፡ ወይሔው ፀኒ ፡ አሐተ ፡ ዕለተ ፡ ይመጽእ ፡ ኀቤየ ፡ ተመሲሎ ፡ ከመ ፡ ሊቀ ፡ ጳጳሳት ፡ ወአሐደ ፡ ዕለተ ፡ ይመጽእ ፡ ኀቤየ ፡ በግርማ ፡ መፍርህ ፡ ተመሲሎ ፡ ከመ ፡ መልአከ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወዓዲ ፡ ይመጽእ ፡ ኅቤየ ፡ ከመ ፡ ዘኣአምሮ ፡ ትካት ። ወካዕበ ፡ በ ፳እምነ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ ሶበ ፡ ገደፍክዋ ፡ ረሲዕየ ፡ አፍአ ፡ እማኅደርየ ፡ ለማእሰ ፡ በግዕ ፡ ሕሠይ ፡ ዘእለብሳ ፡ ነሚኦ ፡ ኪያሃ ፡ መጽአ ፡ ኀቤየ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ጾሰዓት ፡ ዘሌሊት ፡ እንዘ ፡ ሀሎኩ ፡ ቀዊምየ ፡ እንዘ ፡ እጹሊ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ማኅ ደርየ ፡ በይእቲ ፡ ሰዓት ። ወሶበ ፡ ርኢክዎ ፡ ለብእሴ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይመጽእ ፡ ኀቤየ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ማእሰ ፡ በግዕ ፡ ዘእለብሳ ፡ ተፈሣሕኩ ፡ ወሰባሕክዎ ፡ ለአምላኪየ ። ወእምድኅረ ፡ መጠወኒ ፡ ኪያሃ ፡ ጐንደየ ፡ ምስሌየ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይናዝዘኒ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ዘብየ ፡ ትካዘ ፡ ዘበእንተ ፡ ምግባራተ ፡ ሠናይ ፡ ወእንዘ ፡ ይነግረኒ ፡ ዕበያተ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ ባረከ ፡ ላዕሌየ ፡
Forty-fifth miracle: Sämu’ël appears after his death every day to Tomäs. 284 45th miracle of our blessed father, tree of fragrant perfume, spouse whose place of planting is between the rivers, under whose branches the joyful birds of the Gospel take shelter (Mt 13:32), luminous star with pure actions, image of the sun, mär Sämu’ël. May his prayer and his blessing make us worthy to be in his share of the inheritance, in the ages of ages, amen. The holy disciple who followed in the footsteps of his father, of right faith, dwelling of the holy Trinity, by the name of Tomäs, also says: “Our blessed father remained after his death to come to me every day and to visit me. One day, he came to me, having made himself like the patriarch; one day, he came to me in terrifying majesty, having made himself like an angel of God; he also came to me as I knew him formerly. Another day, as I had thrown out of my dwelling, by mistake, the tanned sheepskin that I wore, he came to me, having taken it, at the moment of the third hour of the night, while I was standing praying in my dwelling at that hour. When I saw the man of God coming to me with the sheepskin that I wore, I rejoiced and glorified my God. After he had returned it to me, he lingered with me, consoling me regarding the worry I had, which (was) about good deeds, and telling me of the greatness of God. After that, he blessed me and disappeared from before my eyes.”
ወተሰወረ ፡ እምቅድመ ፡ አዕይንትየ ።= ኦአባ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡ ሠናየ ፡ ዝክር ፡ ኣነክር ፡ አንሰ ፡ ሰሚዕየ ፡ ተኣም ራቲከ ፡ እምድኅረ ፡ ዕረፍትከ ፡ ዘነበርከ ፡ ለወልድከ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ትጌውጾ ። አስ ተምሕርኬ ፡ ይእዜኒ ፡ በእንቲአነ ፡ ኅበ ፡ አንበሳ ፡ መፍርህ ፡ ዘነቢይ ፡ ከመ ፡ ቃለ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ነቢቦ ፡ ለንስረ ፡ ኀጢአት ፡ ዘገሠጾ ። ከመ ፡ ይጸግወነ ፡ ዕሜተ ፡ ሠናየ ፡ ምስለ ፡ እሊአሁ ፡ አስተባይጾ ።= ወዓዲ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይክፍለነ ፡ ምርፋቀ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ነፍሰ ፡ ዚአከ ፡ አንቀጸ ፡ ፕኅተ ፡ መርዓዊ ፡ እምቅድመ ፡ ይትዓጾ ።= ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን
285 Blessed Abbä of beautiful memory, I marvel, having heard your miracle, (at) you who, after your death, remained to visit your (spiritual) son. Intercede therefore for us now with the terrifying lion of the prophet who, speaking like the voice of men, rebuked the eagle of sin, so that he may grant us a beautiful reward, having made us equal to his own, and also so that he may grant us to sit at table with your soul in the door of the porch of the Bridegroom before it closes, in the ages of ages, amen. Forty-sixth miracle: seven white antelopes replace the ox
ጣሄ ተኣምሪሁ ፡ ለብእሴ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ምስማከ ፡ ሃይማኖት ፡ ጽኑዕ ፡ ያቁም ። ካህን ፡ ምዑዝ ፡ በፄወ ፡ መለኮት ፡ ቅውሠም ። ዘኢለከፎ ፡ ርስሐተ ፡ ዓለም ። ማር ፡ ሳሙኤል ። ትንብልና ፡ ጸሎቱ ፡ ያድኅነነ ፡ እምሲኦል ፡ አስ ራም ። ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ።
that a man offers every year at the commemoration of Sämu’ël. 286 46th miracle of the man of God, support of the faith, firm column, sweet priest, spiced by the salt of divinity, whom the defilement of the age has not touched, mär Sämu’ël. May the intercession of his prayer save us from Sheol—abysses—, in the ages of ages, amen.

And again, a man was making the commemoration of our blessed father of beautiful memory and gave an ox every year. One day, as he lacked an ox to lead to the church, as he was accustomed, he became sad and grieved that he had not found an ox that would be slaughtered for the feast of the commemoration of the name of the man of God. After that, he went to the church to tell the priests of his shortage. While the man was there, grieving and sad, before he had spoken to the priests, seven white antelopes arrived; they entered the church at the end of the Eucharistic sacrifice, the power of the prayer of our blessed father, worker of wonders, Mar Samuel, goading them like a shepherd of sheep. When the priests and the people saw these antelopes, marveling, they glorified God. After that, they spoke to each other saying: «The man of God has brought these antelopes for the feast of his commemoration in place of the ox of this man that (he) gives for the name of (Samuel) every year.» After having spoken thus, the priests ordered these antelopes to be slaughtered; the priests and the people ate their carcass, as well as all those who had come from another place to see the miracle that had occurred. Having shared what had remained of what they had eaten, they returned to their homes, marveling at the wonder of the just and pure Mar Samuel of sweet memory and virtue.
