287 Blessed Abba of beautiful memory, companion of the prophet of Sayeryedä (?), I marvel, saying: “(What a) gift!”, having heard your miracle, (at) you who brought for the feast of your commemoration, in place of the slaughter of an ox, seven white antelopes. Intercede therefore for us with the Ancient of Days, a foreign sheep that came to become man from the daughter of Judah, so that he may grant us a share and an inheritance with the daughters of Zion and so that he may make us hear a word of joy and gladness on the day of punishment and reward, in the ages of ages, amen. Forty-seventh miracle: punishment of a woman who does not give

willingly the offering for the commemoration of Sämu’ël. 288 47th miracle of our blessed father of beautiful memory, fruit-bearing palm tree, which the thorn of sin has not stifled, healer of sinners and liberator of the unfortunate, supporting against wavering, companion of Moses, doctor of the Law and, like Paul, Tongue of balm, harp of the Gospel. May his prayer and his supplication make us guests when wisdom calls her little birds to take their place at her nuptial feasts, in the ages of ages, amen. And again, one day, a man said to his wife: “Give me cakes so that I may bring (them) to the church, for (it is) today the feast of the commemoration of abbä Sämu’ël.” But this woman (was) an enemy of good

actions; when she heard this remark from her husband, she became angry with him. When he had pressed her to bring him what he had told her, after that, she took bread and handed it to him while murmuring against him, having turned her face backward in the greatness of (her) affliction so as not to see her husband. As for him, having taken what she had given him, he went to the church. When he returned, he found his wife where he had left her, standing and weeping; he spoke and said to her: “What is the matter with you, that you are weeping?” She answered and said to him: “Since the moment you left, you, my neck is twisted backward and it has not been possible for me to turn my face in front of me as before.” When he heard this from his wife, the man said: “Today, the power of the prayer of abbä Sämu’ël has made a miracle seen upon you.” From that day, the neck of this woman remained twisted backward, until the day of her death.

289 Blessed Abbä of beautiful memory, I marvel, having heard your miracle: how you twisted like a vine shoot the neck of the woman when she murmured against giving for your commemoration. Now, may the richness of your grace and the gift of your help be at the place of your tomb and with all those who hold your commemoration and have faith in your prayer, in the ages of ages, amen. Forty-eighth miracle: conversion of a pagan who did not want to

make the offering of an ox for the commemoration of Sämu’ël. 290 48th miracle of the man of God, elected priest of the (holy) table, associate of the shining stars of the Church—the purity of his holy prayer (is) the garment of the Bride of justice—, mär Sämu’ël. May the blessing of his help cover us like the wing of the falcon, in the ages of ages, amen. And again, one day, a pagan woman arose and went into the house of the Christians; when she arrived there, she found them celebrating the feast of the commemoration of our blessed father mär Sämu’ël. The woman questioned them saying: “What day is this today, that you are holding a feast?” [15] answered and said to her: “Today (takes place) the commemoration of the man of God who is called abbä Sämu’ël.” When she heard this from them, this pagan woman said to them: “If I also bring a gift from what is mine, will you accept (it) from me?” They said to her: “Yes, we will accept (it).” She returned to her house and as she was returning, having taken an ox, her husband met her on the way as he was returning from a journey. When he saw her going, having taken an ox, he spoke and said to her: “Where are you going, having taken this ox?” She answered and said to him: “I am going to the house of the Christians and I am taking this ox so that it may be slaughtered for the feast of the commemoration of the man of God who is called abbä Sämu’ël.” When he heard this from her, (the husband) answered and said to him: “How has the magic of the Christians touched you?” After having said this to her, he took a stick and struck her one

time. Immediately the man of God mär Sämu’ël came in a terrifying majesty, clothed in a mantle of light whose color resembled flames of fire, which was not perceptible to the eyes of creatures; he suddenly threw (the man) to the ground, like a lightning bolt, and rendered him as if dead, to the point that his reason failed. As for the woman, when she considered the miracle that had occurred upon her husband, she cried out with a loud voice, weeping over her husband.

291 Many people among the pagans came in haste and it seemed to them that robbers had beaten this man to steal his property. When they saw him fallen to the ground, they questioned his wife saying: “What is the matter with him?” She answered and said to them: “While I was going to the house of the Christians, having taken this ox so that it might be slaughtered for the commemoration of their prophet, when (my husband) met me here as he was returning from a journey, he asked me where I was going and I told him everything. He became angry with me and struck me once, having taken a stick. Immediately, suddenly, he was thrown to the ground: I certainly do not know what touched him.” After that, the pagans said among themselves: “Let us hurry to the house of the Christians so that they may come here and see what has occurred, for they know the way of their prophet.” After that, when the Christians had arrived where the pagans were, seeing the miracle that had occurred, they marveled at the way of our blessed father. They said to the pagans: “Move away from us so that we may pray for him to our God.” When the pagans had moved away from them, they said: “Lord, have mercy on us, Christ” many times. Then the man who had fallen rose and stood on his feet. (The Christians) spoke and said to him: “What happened to you?” He answered and said to them: “The man of God with the terrifying majesty came to me, clothed in a (mantle) that resembled the color of fire, which was not perceptible; he struck me suddenly like lightning, threw me to the ground and remained until now rebuking me because I had become angry with my wife so that she would not bring the ox to his commemoration. Behold, when you prayed, he showed himself merciful toward me. Now, I believe in the name of his God and I am going to come with you so that you may baptize me.”
ላኩ ፡ ወእመጽእ ፡ ምስሌክሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ታጥምቁኒ ። እምዝ ፡ ሖረ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ብእሲቱ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤቶሙ ፡ ለክርስቲያን ፡ ነሚኦ ፡ ውእተ ፡ ላህመ ፡ ለበዓለ ፡ ተዝካሩ ፡ ለብእሴ ፡ እግዚአብ ሔር ፡ ወበሳኒታ ፡ ተጠምቀ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ብእሲቱ ፡ ወደቂቁ ፡ ወኩሎሙ ፡ ሰብአ ፡ ቤቱ ፡ እንዘ ፡ የአምን ፡ በስመ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ። ወኮነ ፡ ብእሴ ፡ ጎረ ፡ ወፈራሄ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወነበረ ፡ እስከ ፡ ዕለተ ፡ ሞቱ ፡
292 After that, the man went with his wife to the house of the Christians, having taken the ox for the feast of the commemoration of the man of God; the next day, he was baptized with his wife, his children, and all the people of his house, believing in the name of the Father, the Son, and the Holy Spirit. He became a virtuous man and fearing God and remained until the day of his death holding the commemoration of the abbot.
እንዘ ፡ ይገብር ፡ ተዝካሮ ፡ ለአባስ ። ኦፍቁራንየ ፡ ሕዝብ ፡ መሲሓዊያን ፡ አንክሮ ፡ አንክሩ ፡ መንክራተ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡ ማር ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡ ለገሥጾ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ዘመጽአ ፡
293 My beloved, Christian people, be filled with wonder at the miracle of this blessed father of ours, mär Sämu’ël, who came to rebuke the man, clothed in a garment of majesty the color of fire,


[p. 246] {#po.53.p246}
ልብሰ ፡ ግርማ ፡ ዘኅብረ ፡ እሳት ፡ ተሞጢሖ ። ሶበ ፡ ተምዐ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ብእ ሲቱ ፡ ከመ ፡ ኢትሰድ ፡ ለበዓለ ፡ ተዝካሩ ፡ ላህመ ፡ ለተጠብሖ ። ከመ ፡ እንተ ፡ መብረቅ ፡ ዘበጦ ፡ ግብተ ፡ ዘነጽሖ ። ወዓዲ ፡ እምጽልመተ ፡ ህላ ዌሁ ፡ በተሣህሎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ብርሃነ ፡ አሚነ ፡ ሥላሴ ፡ ዘመርሖ ። ያስተምሕር ፡ በእንቲአነ ፡ በተራኅርዣ ፡ ኀበ ፡ አምላኩ ፡ ከሊሖ ። ከመ ፡ ይጸግወነ ፡ መክፈ ልተ ፡ ወርስተ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሀገሪቱ ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ፀዳለ ፡ ፀሐይ ፡ ወወርኅ ፡ ዘኢት ፈቅድ ፡ ለአብርሆ ። ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ፥
when (the latter) became angry with his wife so that she would not bring to the feast of the commemoration of (Sämu’ël) the ox to be slaughtered, who, having struck him suddenly like lightning, threw him (to the ground), who also, out of the darkness of his nature, led him by mercy into the light of faith in the Trinity. May he intercede for us, crying out with gentleness toward his God, so that (the latter) may grant us a share and an inheritance in his holy city that the brightness of the sun and the moon does not need to illuminate, in the ages of ages, amen. Forty-ninth miracle: punishment of an unjust man who has
ግ ወሀ ተኣምሪሁ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡ ሠናየ ፡ ዝክር ፡ ካህን ፡ ኅሩይ ፡ ከመ ፡ ሮሰ ፡ ሐና ። ኩኩሐ ፡ ሃይማኖት ፡ ጽኑዕ ፡ ከመ ፡ ወልደ ፡ ዮና ። ተቅ ዋመ ፡ ማኅቶት ፡ ጽዱል ፡ ለቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ብርሃና ። ማር ፡ ሳሙኤል ። ትንብልና ፡ ጸሎቱ ፡ ያድኅነነ ፡ እሞተ ፡ ሙስና ። ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ። ወካዕበ ፡ በ ፳እምነ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ ዓመጾ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ለካልኡ ፡ ወነአሥአ ፡ ንዋዮ ፡ በኀይል ወውእቱኒ ፡ አምሐሎ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ በጸሎቱ ፡ ለአባ ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡ ወበንስሐ ፡ ቅድስናሁ ፡ ታግብእ ፡ ሊተ ፡ ንዋይየ ፡ ዘነሣእከኒ ፡ ወውእቱሰ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ዘዐመጾ ፡ ለካልኡ ፡ ሖረ ፡ ተጸሚሞ ፡ ወኢያግብአ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ቃሎ *« ወሶቤሃ ፡ መጽአ ፡ ብእሴ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይትመዐዕ ፡ ወአኀዞ ፡ በድ ማ'” ፡ ወአልዓሎ ፡ እምድር ፡ ወወገሮ ፡ መልዕልተ ፡ ኩኩሕ ፡ ወገሠጾ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ ለምንትኑ ፡ ትዓምጾ ፡ ለካልእከ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ያምሕለከ ፡ በስምየ ፡ ወዘንተ ፡ ብሂሎ ፡ ተሰወረ ፡ እምኔሁ ። ወውእቱሂ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ዘተዓመጸ ፡ ነሚኦ ፡ ንዋዮ ፡ ገብአ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይትፌሣሕ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ረከብዎ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ለው እቱ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ወዲቆ ፡ መልዕልተ ፡ ኩኩሕ ፡ ጸዊሮሙ ፡ አብእዎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤቱ ። ወነበረ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይደዊ ፡ እስከ ፡ ፪ አውራኃ ፡ ወኢተፈወሰ ፡ እምሕማሙ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ሶበ ፡ አፍተነ ፡ ርእሶ ፡ ለካህን ፡ በእንተ ፡ ዘሄደ ፡ ንዋየ ፡ ካልኡ ፡
despised the invocation of the name of Sämu’ël. 294 49th miracle of our blessed father of beautiful memory, priest elected like the male (child) of Hannah (1 Sam 1:2-6), rock of faith solid like Bar-Jona (Mt 16:17), shining lampstand, light of the Church, mär Sämu’ël. May the intercession of his prayer save us from the death of corruption, in the ages of ages, amen. And again, one day, a man committed an injustice toward his neighbor and took his property by force. The latter adjured him saying: “By the prayer of abbä Sämu’ël and by the purity of his holiness, return to me my property that you have taken from me.” The man who had committed an injustice toward his neighbor went away turning a deaf ear and did not answer him. Immediately the man of God came, irritated; he seized (the man) by the top of his head, lifted him from the ground, threw him onto a rock, and rebuked him saying: “Why do you commit an injustice toward your neighbor when he adjures you by my name?” Having said this, he disappeared from his (sight). The man who had suffered an injustice returned rejoicing, having taken his property. After that, when people found the man fallen on the rock, they brought him by carrying him to his house; he remained suffering for six months and he was only healed of his ailment when he confessed to a priest that he had taken the property of his neighbor and had disregarded the name of (Sämu’ël).
ወተዐደወ ፡ ስሞ ።= ኦአበዊየ ፡ ወአኀዊየ ፡ ኣነክር ፡ አንሰ ፡ ሰሚዕየ ፡ ተኣምራቲሁ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ዓመጾ ፡ ለካልኡ ፡ በስመ ፡ ዚአሁ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ያምሕሎ ፥ ግብተ ፡ መጺኦ ፡ ዲቤሁ ፡ ዘአርአየ ፡ ኀይሎ ። ያስተምሕርኬ ፡ ይእዜኒ ፡ በእንቲ አነ ፡ በተሣህሎ ። ኀበ ፡ ርእሰ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ በግዕ ፡ እምቅድመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ዘሀሎ ፍ፥ ከመ ፡ ይጸግወነ ፡ ዕሜተ ፡ ሠናየ ፡ ምስለ ፡ እለ ፡ ፈጸሙ ፡ ተጋድሎ ። ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን «
295 My fathers and my brothers, I marvel, having heard the miracle of our blessed father, he who, when the man committed an injustice toward his neighbor, while (the latter) was adjuring him in the name of (Sämu’ël), suddenly came and showed his power over him. May he therefore intercede for us now with mercy with the Head of days, the Lamb who was before the world, so that he may grant us a beautiful reward with those who have accomplished their (spiritual) combat, in the ages of ages, amen.
ዛ ተኣምሪሁ ፡ ለብእሴ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ መፍቀሬ ፡ ጾም ፡ ወጸሎት ፡ ከመ ፡ ነቢይ ፡ ወልደ ፡ ዮካብድ ። ንጽሐ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ጽሩይ ፡ ከመ ፡ ዕንቄ ፡ ኢያስሏድ ፡ በድንግልና ፡ ንዑድ ፡ ዘመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ማኅፈድ ፍ ማር ፡ ሳሙ ኤል ፡= ኀይለ ፡ ጸሎቱ ፡ ይጸግወነ ፡ ፍሥሐ ፡ ወሐሣተ ፡ አመ ፡ ዕለተ ፡ ፍር ሃት ፡ ወረዓድ ። ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን « ወካዕበ ፡ በ፳ እምነ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ ሶበ ፡ ጽህቁ ፡ አሐው ፡ ከመ ፡ ያፍ ልሱ ፡ ሥጋሁ ፡ ጠብሊሎሙ?” ፡ በልብሰ ፡ ገርዜን ፡ ከመ ፡ ይደይዎ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሩካም??' ፡ ሐዲስ ፡ ዘውእቱ ፡ ሳፁን ፡ ዘእብን ፡ ወእምዝ ፡ ነሥኡ ፡ መክርየ ፡ ወሖሩ ፡ ኀበ ፡ መቃብሩ ፡ ወከረዩ ። ወሶበ ፡ አርኅዉ ፡ ወፅአ ፡ እምውስቴታ ፡ ጢሰ ፡ ዕጣን ፡ ምዑዝ ፡ ወከደነ ፡ ለቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ከመ ፡ ደመና ፡ ኩለንታሃ ። ወሶቤሃ ፡ ወረዱ ፡ መላእክተ ፡ ሰማይ ፡ አእላፈ ፡ አእላፋት ፡ ወትእ ልፊተ ፡ አእላፋት ፡ እለ ፡ አልቦሙ ፡ ጐልቄ ። ወሰብእሂ ፡ ብዙኃን ፡ ስዉራን ፡ እምነ ፡ አበው ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ ቀደምት ፡ መጽኡ ፡ ለፍልሰተ ፡ ዐጽሙ ፡ ለብእሴ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ። በከመ ፡ ተጋብኡ ፡ በዕለተ ፡ ሞቱ ። ወኮኑ ፡ ድሙራነ ፡ በይ እቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ወመላእክት ፡ አመድቢሎሙ ፡ ገብሩ ፡ በዓለ ፡ በስብ ሐት ፡ ወበውዳሴ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ያነክሩ ፡ ብዕለ ፡ ጸጋሁ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡ ሠናየ ፡ ዝክር ፡ ማር ፡ ሳሙኤል ። ወእምዝ ፡ ሶበ ፡ በጽሐ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ አዕርጎ ፡ ዮርባን ፡ ቦአ ፡ ሠዋዒ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቅድስተ ፡ ቅዱሳን ። ወሶበ ፡ አሐዘ ፡ ለወ ጢነ ፡ ቅዳሴ ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ተገልበበት ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ሱራቴ ፡ ብርሃን ፡ ከመ ፡ ደብረ ፡ ሲና ፡ ወተወለጠ ፡ ኅብራ ፡ ወአዋከየ ፡ ኩለንታሃ ፡ ከመ ፡ ፀሐይ ። ወዓዲ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ካህን ፡ ፳- አብ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ 8ወልድ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ፳ውእቱ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ቀነጸ ፡ ሶቤሃ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ እምሕፅነ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ እምአርያም ፡ ወሰከበ ፡ ውስተ ፡ አውደ ፡ ምሥዋዕ ፡ ተመ ሲሎ ፡ ከመ ፡ ሕፃን ፡ እስከ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ይብል ፡ ሰዋዒ ፡ ፈኑ ፡ ጸጋ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ወሶቤሃ ፡ ተወለጠ ፡ በከመ ፡ ልማድ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ኅብስተ ፡ ምርባን ፡ ወመን
Fiftieth miracle: the heavenly court participates in the translation of the body of Sämu’ël. 296 50th miracle of the man of God, friend of fasting and prayer like the prophet son of Jochebed (Ex 6:20), limpid by the purity of his body, celebrated in his virginity like a jasper stone, citadel of the Holy Spirit, mär Sämu’ël. May the power of his prayer grant us joy and gladness on the day of fear and trembling, in the ages of ages, amen. And again, one day, as the brothers had the keen desire to move his body, having wrapped it in a garment of fine linen, to put it in a new marble—that is to say, a stone sarcophagus—, they took after that a pickaxe, went to his tomb, and dug. When they opened it, there came out of it a smoke of fragrant incense and it covered like a cloud the entire church. Then descended the angels of heaven—thousands of thousands and myriads of myriads, without number. Many men hidden from among the ancient holy fathers also came for the translation of the bones of the man of God, just as they had gathered on the day of his death. On this day, men and angels were joined; having gathered, they celebrated the feast in glorification and in praise, marveling at the richness of the grace of our blessed father of beautiful memory, mär Sämu’ël. After that, when the moment arrived to offer the Eucharist, the sacrificer entered the holy of holies. When he began to start the consecration, a glory of light enveloped the church again like Mount Sinai and its color was changed; it shone entirely like the sun. And also, when the priest said: “One is the Holy Father, one the Holy Son, one the Holy Spirit,” Our Lord Jesus Christ immediately leaped out of the bosom of his Father from the height of the heavens and lay down in the enclosure of the sacrifice, having made himself like a little child, until the moment when the sacrificer said: “Send the grace of the Holy Spirit.” Immediately, it changed, according to custom, to the bread of the Eucharist, and the Holy Spirit overshadowed the horns of the altar under the appearance of a white dove.


[p. 248] {#po.53.p248}
ፈስ ፡ ቅዱስሂ ፡ ጸለለ ፡ ዲበ ፡ አቅርንተ ፡ ምሥዋዕ ፡ በርእየተ ፡ ርግብ ፡ ጻዓዳ ። ወሶበ ፡ ርእዩ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ምእመናን?'' ፡ እምቀሳውስት ፡ ወዲያቆ ናት ፡ ወእምነ ፡ ቅዱሳንሂ ፡ አለ ፡ ሀለዉ ፡ በአፍአ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይትራድእዎ ፡ ለካህን ፡ ነቢያት ፡ ወሐዋርያት ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ጥብሐተ ፡ በግዑ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ በእንተ ፡ ክብረ ፡ በዓሉ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡ ሠናየ ፡ ዝክር ፡ እስራኤላዊ ፡ ጻድቅ ፡ ወንጹሕ ፡ ማር ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡ ወሶበ ፡ ተፈጸመ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ አዕርጎ ፡ ምር ባን ፡ ዐርገ ፡ እግዚእነ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሰማያት ፡ ምስለ ፡ መላእክት ፡ በስብሐት ፡ ወበውዳሴ ፍ ወእሉሂ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ራን ፡ እለ ፡ ተግህዱ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ዲያ ቆን ፡ እተዉ ፡ በሰላም ፡ ውእተ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ተሰወሩ ፡ እምቅድመ ፡ አዕይንቲነ ፡ ወኢያእመርነ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ሖሩ ፡ እንዳዒ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ያአምር ።
297 When the faithful among the priests and deacons and among the saints who were outside had seen this, while the prophets and the apostles brought their help to the priest at the moment of slaughtering the Lamb of God, for the honor of the feast of our blessed father of beautiful memory, the just and pure Israelite, mär Sämu’ël, and when the moment to offer the Eucharist had passed, Our Lord ascended to the heavens with the angels in glory and in praise. As for the virtuous men who had manifested themselves, when the deacon said: “Return in peace,” they disappeared at that very moment from before our eyes, we did not know where they had gone—God (knows) it.


[p. 250] {#po.53.p250}

298 My beloved, Christian people, be filled with wonder at the richness of the grace of our blessed father of beautiful memory whom the defilement of the age has not touched, at the moment of the translation of the bones of whom a smoke of incense covered the (holy) vestibule like a cloud, as well as a glory of light that shone and sparkled more than the sun, for the greatness of whom the angels and men, having also gathered, made a glorification and a great noise. May he therefore intercede now with the perfect God of hosts who descended from the palace of the high heavens, to be slaughtered on this day, in the likeness of a little child, so that he may grant us life with your soul, in the ages of ages, amen. Fifty-first miracle: Sämu’ël gives back to the poor woman the

cow and the calf that had been taken from her to hold his commemoration. 299 51st miracle of our blessed father of beautiful memory, sweet lamb who was a twin from the womb of Gilead (Song 4:1-2), who was set in motion by the name of the Trinity, who, having become an adult through the fatigues of the (spiritual) combat, passed from the sea of sin into the place of life, mär Sämu’ël. May his prayer and his blessing make us ready for the nuptial feasts, in the ages of ages, amen. And again, one day, many people gathered in a church to hold a meeting, having brought food and drink each according to his means. After that, when the feast of the commemoration of the man of God arrived, they spoke among themselves saying: “Come therefore, let us look for an ox in order to slaughter it for the commemoration of abbä Sämu’ël; after that, we will give, discharging ourselves, the price of this property.” After having spoken thus, they sent messengers to, having looked for (it), bring an ox; they found outside the city the cow of a widow woman who remained hoping, when (the cow) had given birth, to drink its milk. But the messengers, without telling her, took the cow, led it to their meeting, and slaughtered it for the name of abbä Sämu’ël. When they had slaughtered it, there was found in its belly a fetus whose day had arrived to come out of its mother's womb. After that, the assembly prescribed to the butchery to hide the fetus so that the woman would not grieve upon seeing it. After that, when she learned that the assembly had taken her cow, the widow came to the (people) while grieving and afflicting herself. When she saw that they had slaughtered the cow, she cried out and said to these assembled (people): “What have I done to you, that you have stolen my property iniquitously, without asking me?” [15] answered and said to her: “Woman, do not be harsh toward us and do not grieve because of this affair: we are going to give you your money by tomorrow, discharging ourselves.” She answered and said to them: “For me it is better that I find this


[p. 252] {#po.53.p252}
ተሰጥወት ፡ ወትቤሎሙ ፡ አንሰ ፡ ይጌይሰኒ ፡ ዘረከብኩ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ግፍዓ ፡ በኀ ቤክሙ ፡ እምንዋይ ፡ ዘትብሉ ፡ ንሁበኪ ። ይእዜኒ ፡ ይነጽር ፡ ኀይለ ፡ ጸሎቱ ፡ ለአባ ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡ ዘከመ ፡ ተኀየልክሙ ፡ ላዕሌየ ። ወዘንተ ፡ ብሂላ ፡ ገብአት ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤታ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ትበኪ ፡ ወተሐዝን ።
injustice with you, rather than the money of which you say: ‘We are going to give (it) to you.’ Now, may the power of the prayer of abbä Sämu’ël look at how you have done violence to me.” Having said this, she returned to her house weeping and grieving.
ወበሳኒታ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ጽባሕ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ወፅአት ፡ እማኅደራ ፡ ረከበታ ፡ ለይ እቲ ፡ ላህም ፡ ዘተጠብሐት ፡ ምስለ ፡ ጣዕዋ ፡ ውስተ ፡ መርኅበ ፡ ዐፀደ ፡ ቤታ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ትበልዕ ፡ ሣዕረ ፡ ወሶቤሃ ፡ ሰገደት ፡ ሥልሰ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ብእሲት ፡ ወሰ ብሐቶ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ እንዘ ፡ ትብል ፡ ተማኅፀንኩ ፡ በኀይለ ፡ ጸሎቱ ፡ ለአባ ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡ ገባሬ ፡ መንክራት ። ወእምዝ ፡ ሖረት ፡ ኀበ ፡ አግዋሪሃ ፡ ወዜነወቶሙ ፡ ዘኮነ ፡ ተአምረ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ላህም ። ወእሉሂ ፡ ጉባኤ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ሰምዑ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ነገረ ፡ ሖሩ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ብእሲት ፡ እቤር ፡ ከመ ፡ ይነጽሩ ፡ ወይጠይቁ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ላህም ፡ ዘሐይወት ። ወሶበ ፡ ርእይዋ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ፃእፃእ ፡ ዘሐረድዎ ፡ ትማልም ፡ ምስለ ፡ እሙ ፡ አንከሩ ፡ ወሰብሕዎ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ እንዘ ፡ ያስተዐፅቡ ፡ ተአምሪሁ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡ ናዛዜ ፡ ሕዙናን ፡ ወጸጋዌ ፡ ፍሥሐ ፡ ለትኩዛን ፡ ማር ፡ ሳሙኤል ። ወእምዝ ፡ ተባሀሉ ፡ በበይናቲሆሙ ፡ ንዑኬ ፡ ካዕበ ፡ ንግበር ፡ ተዝ ካሮ ፡ እምዛቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ እስከ ፡ ተፍጻሜተ ፡ ጣመዋዕል ። ወገብሩ ፡ ከመዝ ፡
300 The next day, at the moment of the morning, when she went out of her house, she found the cow that had been slaughtered, with a calf, in the courtyard of the animal park of her house eating grass. Immediately, the woman prostrated herself three times and glorified God saying: “I have trusted in the power of the prayer of abbä Sämu’ël, the worker of wonders.” After that, she went to her neighbors and told them the miracle that had occurred regarding the cow. As for the assembled (people), when they heard this affair, they went to the house of the widow woman to look and inquire exactly about the cow that had returned to life. When they saw it with the fetus that they had slaughtered the day before with its mother, they marveled and glorified God, marveling at the miracle of our blessed father, consoler of the afflicted and dispenser of joy to the sad, mär Sämu’ël. After that, they said among themselves: “Come therefore again, let us hold his commemoration from this day until the end of forty days.” [15] did so, hoping for his intercession.
እንዘ ፡ ይሴፈዉ ፡ ትንብልናሁ ። ኦአባ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡ ሠናየ ፡ ዝክር ፡ አንሰ ፡ እንዘ ፡ እትጋነይ ፡ ለዕበየ ፡ ጸጋከ ፡ እዌሩ ፡ ስብሐተ ፡ ወውዓ ። ሰሚዕየ ፡ ተአምራቲከ ፡ ዘከመ ፡ አሕዮከ ፡ ላቲ ፡ ለብእሲት ፡ እቤር ፡ ላህመ ፡ እምድኅረ ፡ ተጠብሐ ፡ ወተበልዐ ። ከመ ፡ ኢትኩን ፡ ተስፋ ፡ ኅጡአ ። ይእዜኒ ፡ አስተምሕር ፡ በእንቲአነ ፡ ኀበ ፡ አም ላከ ፡ አማልክት ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ በንባቡ ፡ ለምድር ፡ ዘጸውዓ ። ከመ ፡ ይጸግ ወነ ፡ መክፈልተ ፡ ወርስተ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ነፍሰ ፡ ዚአከ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሀገሪትከ ፡ ቅድ ስት ፡ ብሔረ ፡ ፍሥሐ ፡ ወፍግዓ ፡= ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ፡
301 Blessed Abbä of beautiful memory, I, in paying homage to the greatness of your grace, launch in glorification a clamor, having heard your miracle: you returned to life, for a widow woman, a cow after it had been slaughtered and eaten, so that the hope (of the woman) would not be in vain. Now, intercede for us with the God of gods, the Lord who called the earth by his word, so that he may grant us a share and an inheritance with your soul in your holy city, in the land of joy and delights, in the ages of ages, amen. Fifty-second miracle: the dust of the tomb of Sämu’ël

wakes a dead man. 302 52nd miracle of the man of God, companion of the columns of the earth, bearer of the sufferings of the Cross, who fights (spiritually) to accomplish the word of the Gospel, mär Sämu’ël. May his prayer and his supplication take charge of us far from the path of perdition—those who do not commune, leave when (that) will be said—, in the ages of ages, amen. And again, one day, a priest from among the brothers took dust from the tomb of the abbot and went to another place to dwell (there). While he was there, a man suffered during the days of the Passion and died on Friday, (the day) when Our Lord and Savior Jesus Christ was crucified. Immediately, his relatives sent to the priests saying: “Come to bury him, for he is dead.” When the priests learned of the death of the man from these envoys, they

said: “There is not, this week, any offering of the Eucharist, nor any absolution, nor any incensing. As for us, we will not come there, abandoning the reading of the Scriptures in the church.” Some among them said: “Arise, let us go to stand by his tomb.” As the priests delayed while arguing thus among themselves, the priest sent his son so that he would go (there), having taken dust from the tomb of our blessed father mär Sämu’ël. When this envoy arrived at the house of the man who was dead, he put into the nose of the latter dust from the tomb of the man of God, having mixed it with water, saying: “May the power of the prayer of abbä Sämu’ël grant you life.” Immediately, (the man) rose quickly like a man who wakes from his sleep. Of this affair the priest was a witness, who had seen (it) with his eyes.
ውእቱ ፡ ቀሲስ ፡ ዘርእየ ፡ በአዕይንቲሁ ። ኦፍቁራንየ ፡ አንክሮ ፡ አንክሩ ፡ ግብረ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡ አስከሬነ ፡ መንፈስ ፡ ለዕንቄ ፡ ባሕርይ ፡ ሙዳዩ ። በጸበለ ፡ ዝኅሩ ፡ ሙታነ ፡ ዘያሐዩ ። ይእዜኒ ፡ ያስተምሕር ፡ በእንቲአነ ፡ በተሣህሎ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ዘተብህለ ፡ እም ውሉደ ፡ እጓለ ፡ እመሕያው ፡ ይኒ ፡ ላሕዩ = ከመ ፡ ይጸግወነ ፡ ዕሜተ ፡ ሠናየ ፡ ለከብካበ ፡ መርዓሁ ፡ እምከርሠ ፡ እሞሙ ፡ ምስለ ፡ እለ ፡ ተሐርዩ ። ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን «
303 My beloved, be filled with wonder at the action of this blessed father of ours, casket of the Spirit, box of the pearl, who through the dust of his tomb brings the dead back to life. May he now intercede for us with mercy with him of whom it is said: “His grace is more beautiful than (that) of the children of the human race,” so that he may grant us a beautiful reward at his nuptial feasts with those who have been chosen from the womb of their mother, in the ages of ages, amen. Fifty-third miracle: punishment of the servant who mocks the offerings for the commemoration of Sämu’ël
ዛ፻ ተአምሪሁ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡ ሠናየ ፡ ዝክር ፡ ገባሬ ፡ መንክራት ፡ ከመ ፡ ስምዖን ፡ ኩኩሐ ፡ ሃይማኖት ፡ ጽጉበ ፡ መጻሕፍት ፡ ሱታፌ ፡ ጳውሎስ ፡ ልሳነ ፡ ዕፍረት ። የዋህ ፡ ወትሑት ፡ ዘውገ ፡ ሙሴ ፡ መምህረ ፡ ኦሪት ፡ መፍቀሬ ፡ ጽድቅ ፡ ወስብሐት ፡ ቢጸ ፡ ትጉሃን ፡ መላእክት ቱ ማር ፡ ሳሙኤል ትንብልና ፡ ጸሎቱ ፡ ይዕቀበነ ፡ እሞተ ፡ ኃጢአት ። ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ። ወካዕበ ፡ በ 8እምነ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ ተባሀሉ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ብእሲቱ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብሉ ፡ እምይእዜሰ ፡ ኢናብጥል ፡ ውሂበ ፡ ኅብስት ፡ ወጽዋዕ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ በእንተ ፡ ስሙ ፡ ለብእሴ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ከመ ፡ ይትባረክ ፡ ለነ ፡ በጸሎቱ ፡ ተግባረ ፡ እደዊነ ፡ ወኩሎ ፡ ዘአጥረይነ ። ወእምድኅረ ፡ ተባሀሉ ፡ ከመዝ ፡ አዘዝዋ ፡ ለአሐቲ ፡ አመት ፡ እኪተ ፡ ምክር ፡ ጸላኢተ ፡ ሠናያት ፡ ከመ ፡ ትሰድ ፡ ጸራይቀ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ። ወይእቲሰ ፡ አመት ፡ ተሐውር ፡ በጊዜ ፡ አዕርጎ ፡ ሞርባን ፡ ነሚኣ ፡ ውእተ ፡ ኅብስተ ፡ ወጽዋዐ ፡ ወትበልዕ ፡ ተግቲሣ ፡ እምፍኖት ። ወእምዝ ፡ ትገብእ ፡ ከመ ፡ ዘአብጽሐት ፡ ውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ። ወአሐተ ፡ ዕለተ ፡ ትበልዖ ፡ በፍኖት ፡ ወአሐተ ፡ ዕለተ ፡ ታነብሮ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ማኅደራ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትበልዕ ፡ ኪያሁ ፡ በኅቡእ ፡ ወረሰየት ፡ ወትረ ፡ ከመዝ ፡ ልማዳ ፡ በከመ ፡ አምከራ ፡ አቡሃ ፡ ሰይጣን ። ወበ 8እምነ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ እንዘ ፡ ተሐውር ፡ ምስለ ፡ ብዙኃን ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ነሚኣ ፡ ውእተ ፡ ጸራይቀ ፡ ወኢተክህላ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትብላዕ ፡ ኪያሁ ፡ በውስተ ፡ ፍኖት ፡ በከመ ፡ ልማዳ ። ወእምዝ ፡ አውሥአት ፡ ወትቤሎሙ ፡ ሊተሰ ፡ አንጠዩኒ ፡ አጋእዝትየ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብሉ ፡ ሰዲ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ጸራይቀ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ዕለተ ፡ ውስተ ፡
304 53rd miracle of our blessed father of beautiful memory, worker of wonders, like Simon (Peter), rock of the faith, nourished by the Scriptures, associate of Paul Tongue of balm, sweet and humble, companion of Moses the doctor of the Law, friend of justice and glorification, companion of the watching angels, mär Sämu’ël. May the intercession of his prayer keep us from the death of sin, in the ages of ages, amen. And again, one day, a man and his wife spoke among themselves saying: “Henceforth, let us not cease to give bread and a cup (of wine) in the church for the name of the man of God so that the work of our hands and everything we possess may be blessed for us by his prayer.” After having spoken thus, they ordered a servant with a bad mind, enemy of the good, to bring a cake to the church. But the servant would go at the moment of the offering of the Eucharist, having taken the wine and the cup, and she would eat, having turned aside from the path; after that, she would return as if she had brought (it) to the church. One day, she would eat (the bread) on the way, one day, she would deposit it in her dwelling to eat it in secret; she always instituted her habit thus, according to what her father Satan had advised her. One day, while she was going with many people, having taken the cake, it was not possible for her to eat it on the way according to her habit. After that, she spoke and said (to the people): “My masters have pressed me saying: ‘Bring

this cake every day to the church for the name of abbä Sämu’ël.’” Having said this, she began to mock him saying: “Of whom is Sämu’ël the son? I grieve about him every day when I hear his name invoked.” At that very moment, before she had finished speaking, her appearance was changed and she became like a raven before these people; she flew into the air, went away cawing like a raven in the middle of mountains where there were large trees and became obscured to the eyes of the people: they did not know where she had arrived. As for the people who had seen this, they testified in the church by telling (it) to the priests.
ኮኑ ፡ ስምዐ ፡ በውስተ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይነግርዎሙ ፡ ለካህናት ።« ኦአባ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡ ሠናየ ፡ ዝክር ፡ ተፈጸመ ፡ በለዕሌከ ፡ ዘይቤ ፡ ዳዊት ፡ ነቢይ ፡ በመዝሙር ፡ እስመ ፡ ጽድቀ?” ፡ ያፈቅር ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወኢይገድ ፎሙ ፡ ለጻድቃን ፡ ወለዓለም ፡ የዐቅቦሙ ፡ ወይትቤቀሎሙ ፡ ለንጹሓን ፡ ወይ ሰሩ ፡ ዘርኦሙ ፡ ለኃጥኣን ። ኣነክር ፡ አንሰ ፡ ቲሩተ ፡ ዚአከ ፡ ኦአባ ፡ ብፁዕ ፡ ሰሚዕየ ፡ ተኣምራቲከ ፡ ዘከመ ፡ ረሰይካ ፡ ለአመተ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ዘነበረት ፡ እንዘ ፡ ትበልዕ ፡ እምጸራይቀ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ዘተበጽዓ ፡ በስመ ፡ ዚአከ ፡ ከመ ፡ እንተ ፡ ቋዕ ።= ይእዜኒ ፡ ይኩነነ ፡ ለእሊአከ ፡ ትንብልና ፡ ጸሎትከ ፡ ደምሳሴ ፡ አባስ ፡ ወሰራዬ ፡ ኃጠውእ ፡ ወይምርሐነ ፡ ፍኖተ ፡ ሕይወት ፡ እንተ ፡ ትኒ ፡ ወትረትዕ ፡ ከመ ፡ ንርከብ ፡ ሣህለ ፡ ኀበ ፡ መፍቀሬ ፡ ሰብእ ። ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ። ፃ ወ፪ ተአምሪሁ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡ ብእሴ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ዘውገ ፡ አብርሃም ፡ ሠናየ ፡ ርስእ ፡ ዐምደ ፡ ሰማይ ፡ ወምድር ፡ ዘኢይወድቅ ። ሱታፌ ፡ ይስሐቅ ፡ ንጹሕ ፡ ዘተብህለ ፡ ቅቆ'ልዔሁ ፡ ለሊቅ ። ቢጸ ፡ ያዕቆብ ፡ ፍትወ ፡ ሥን ፡ ዘተሰመየ ፡ እስራኤላዊ ፡ ጻድቅ ። ማር ፡ ሳሙኤል ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወበረከቱ ፡ ይረስየነ ፡ ድልዋነ ፡ በሐደስ ፡ ምርፋቅ ። ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ።፥ ወካዕበ ፡ በ-፳እምነ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ እንዘ ፡ የሐውር ፡ ፳ቃሲስ ፡ እምነ ፡ አሐው ፡ መጽኡ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ወሰአልዎ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብሉ ፡ ግበር ፡ ፍቅረ ፡ በእ ንተ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ወነዓ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትፍትሖ ፡ ወትቅብሮ ፡ ለብእሲ ፡ ዘሞተ ።= ወተሰጥወ ፡ ወይቤሎሙ ፡ ኅድጉኒ ፡ ከመ ፡ እሖር ፡ ፍኖትየ ፡ አልቦኑ ፡ ካልአ ፡ ካህነ ፡ ኀቤክሙ ፡ ወይቤልዎ ፡ እወ ፡ አልብነ ፡ ወበእንተዝ ፡ መጻእነ ፡ ኀቤከ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ርኢናከ ፡ ወውእተ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ተግኅሠ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ቀሲስ ፡ ወሖረ ፡ ምስሌሆሙ ፡ ነኦ ፡ ጸበለ ፡ ዝኅሩ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ። ወሶበ ፡ በጽሐ ፡ ህየ ፡ አዘዘ ፡ ያምጽኡ ፡ ማየ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይቶስሕ ፡ መሬተ ፡ ዝኅሩ ፡ ለብእሴ ፡ እግዚአብ ሔር ፡ ወያጥምቆ ፡ ለዘሞተ ። ሶበ ፡ አምጽኡ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ውእተ ፡ ማየ ፡ ዘይቤ ሎሙ ፡ ወእምዝ ፡ አጥመቆ ፡ ለዘሞተ ፡ ወድዮ ፡ ውእተ ፡ መሬተ ፡ ዘነሥአ ፡ እምነ ፡ ዝኅሩ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡ ገባሬ ፡ መንክራት ። ወሶቤሃ ፡ ተንሥአ ፡
305 Blessed Abbä of beautiful memory, what David the prophet said in the Psalms has been accomplished upon you: For God loves justice, and he does not abandon the just and protects them forever; he avenges the pure and annihilates the offspring of sinners (Ps 37:28). I marvel at your virtue, blessed abbä, having heard your miracle: you rendered the servant of the man, who kept eating the cake of the church that had been vowed to your name, like a raven. Now, may the intercession of your prayer be for us, your own, the eraser of fault and the forgiver of sins and guide us on the path of life, which is good and right, so that we may find mercy with the friend of men, in the ages of ages, amen. Fifty-fourth miracle: a priest resurrects a dead man with the dust of the tomb of Sämu’ël. 306 54th miracle of our blessed father, the man of God, companion of Abraham of beautiful old age, column of heaven and earth that does not fall, associate of Isaac the pure who is called servant of the Master, emulator of Jacob of desirable beauty, who is called just Israelite, mär Sämu’ël. May his prayer and his blessing make us worthy (to sit at table) at the new banquet, in the ages of ages, amen. And again, one day, while a priest from among the brothers was going, people came to him and solicited him saying: “Do a (gesture of) friendship for us and come with us to give absolution and bury a man who is dead.” He answered and said to them: “Let me go my way, is there not another priest near you?” They said to him: “Yes, we do not have (one); because of that, we came to you when we saw you.” At that moment, the priest turned aside and went with them, having taken dust from the tomb of our blessed father. When he arrived there, he ordered them to bring water so that he might mix it with the dust of the tomb of the man of God and sprinkle the one who was dead. When they had brought him the water he had told them, after that, he sprinkled the one who was dead, having put in it the dust he had taken from the tomb of our blessed father, worker of wonders. Immediately the dead man rose quickly

307 I, for my part, marvel at your virtue, holy abbä, worker of numerous wonders that are infinite to write down, having heard how the corpse of the man rose when the dust of your tomb touched him. Now, intercede for us with Him who came to us to clothe Himself in the tunic of flesh, so that He may pilot us across the sea of sin to give (us) rest in the place of life, for ever and ever, amen. Fifty-fifth miracle: punishment of a woman who forgets at

her home the offering for the commemoration of Sämu'êl. 308 55th miracle of our blessed father of beautiful memory, spectator of hidden things like the son of Norê, sweet priest who…, chosen fig tree that bears many fruits of justice. May his prayer and his blessing cause us to sit at his share of the inheritance by invitation, for ever and ever, amen. And again, one day, a woman made bread to bring (it) to the feast of the commemoration of the man of God. After that, when she saw the bread that she had wanted to bring to the church, an intemperate desire entered her heart: she left the bread there, inside her house, and went, empty-handed, to the church. After the end of the Eucharistic sacrifice, while she was returning with the people to her dwelling, the woman saw her house burning. Immediately, she cried out, saying: “Woe to me, my beloved! Indeed, all this affliction that has touched me is because of my sin: I left the bread after having vowed to bring (it) to the church for the feast of the commemoration of the man of God; because of that, my house has been set ablaze by fire—because I did not bring the bread that I had vowed.” She said this to everyone who had come to her, revealing her sin against her will, so that God might make known through her own mouth the miracle of our blessed father of beautiful memory, mär Sämu’êl.

309 My beloved, Christian people, be filled with wonder before the action of this blessed father of ours of beautiful memory, worker of miracles, who, like (Elijah) the Tishbite, was chosen from his mother's womb, he who, after she had vowed the bread to the feast of his commemoration, as she did not give (it), set the woman's house ablaze with fire. Now, may he intercede for us with Him who is called “glorious lion cub of Judah,” so that He may grace us with a beautiful reward that the eye of the eagle has not seen, which has not been thought of in the heart of man, for ever and ever, amen.

Fifty-sixth miracle: Sämu'êl restores to the poor man the plow ox that he had offered for his commemoration. 310 56th miracle of the man of God, white ox that follows in concord the track of the lion that has sprung from the house of Judah, whose greatness is recounted like (that of) the sixty queens (Ct 6:8) daughters of Zion, mär Sämu'êl. May the intercession of his prayer save us from the waves of the sea of the age, for ever and ever, amen. And again, one day, when a man heard the story of the wonders of our blessed father, he desired to make his commemoration; he had no other property than two plow oxen, he took one of them, brought it to the church, and handed (it) over to the priests. But they said to him: “Take back your ox with which you plow, to bring us in its place an ox, (having) bought (it), another day.” When the man heard this proposal from the priests, he answered and said to them: “I have brought this ox today so that you might slaughter it for the feast of the commemoration of abbä Sämu'êl.” Having said that, he himself took a knife and slaughtered the ox. After that, the priests butchered (the ox), divided its carcass among themselves, and gave the man one of its legs. When it was the next day, (the latter) got up, went to his brother's house, and said to him: “Give me one of your plow oxen so that I may work with it, having yoked (it) with the other that is at my place.” When (his brother) heard this from him, he answered and said to him: “Do you want to slaughter my ox today just as you slaughtered your ox yesterday when no one was forcing you?” When his brother had spoken to him thus, (the man) returned grieving to his house and told his wife everything his brother had said to him. After that, she said to him: “Do not be sad, my brother, for the intercession of the prayer of abba Sämu’êl in whom we have placed our hope will give us (something) in place of the (ox).” After she had spoken to him thus, he went into his field to work with his hands. When he arrived there, he found alive the ox that had been slaughtered for the feast of the commemoration of the man of God. Then he rejoiced, exulted, and glorified the Lord his God; he spent the day plowing with the ox—which had returned to life after having died, been slaughtered, and eaten—(having) put it under the yoke with the other as before. All the people who heard and saw this miracle remained in awe of the wonder of the man of God.


[p. 260] {#po.53.p260}
ካልኡ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትካት ። ወኩሎሙ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ እለ ፡ ሰምዑ ፡ ወርእዩ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ተአምረ ፡ ነበሩ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ያስተዐፅቡ ፡ መንክራቲሁ ፡ ለብእሴ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ። ኦአባ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡ ሠናየ ፡ ዝክር ፡ አንሰ ፡ በተጋንዮ ። ለክብረ ፡ ውዳ ሴከ ፡ አቄርብ ፡ ስብሐተ ፡ ዘበሐልዮ ። ሰሚዕየ ፡ ተአምራቲከ ፡ ዘከመ ፡ አሕ ዮከ ፡ ሎቱ ፡ ለብእሲ ፡ እምድኅረ ፡ ተጠብሐ ፡ ዕብራዮ ። አስተምሕርኬ ፡ ይእዜ ፡ በእንቲአነ ፡ ኀበ ፡ እሳተ ፡ ሕይወት ፡ ዘአንሀዮ ።= ከመ ፡ ይጸግወነ ፡ ሠናየ ፡ ዐይነ ፡ ንስር ፡ ዘኢርእዮ ፡ ወልበ ፡ እጓለ ፡ እመሕያው ፡ ዘኢሐለዮ « ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ።
311 Blessed abbä of beautiful memory, I (am) in homage; to the honor of your praise, I offer the glorification that (is) in the thought, having heard your miracle: for the man, you restored to life his plow ox after it had been slaughtered. Intercede therefore now for us with the Fire of life who consoled him, so that He may grace us with the good that the eye of the eagle has not seen and that the heart of man has not thought of, for ever and ever, amen.

Fifty-seventh miracle: a mute is healed after rolling on the tomb of Sämu’êl. 312 57th miracle of our blessed father of beautiful memory, joyful lamb who has been satiated by the milk of the Gospel, who in dancing follows the track of the Most High, priest and virgin, doctor of the Law, commentator, mär Sämu’êl. May the intercession of his prayer cause us to sit at the new feast, for ever and ever, amen. And again, one day, a man went into the countryside with the governor of the region. When he arrived where they were attacking a city, he moved away, alone, and went into the middle of a wilderness where there were many trees to look for booty. There, a cruel demon, having made itself like a large elephant with four horns, four eyes, and four ears, met him. When the man saw it, he took a stone and threw (it) at it; at that very moment, like a violent wind, (the elephant) came to him, lifted him from the ground like dust, threw him far from there, and sent him a stone's throw away. (The man) fell on his face to the ground, was like a corpse, and could not rise from there. After that, his kin searched for him and found him fallen to the ground; they took him from there and brought him, carrying (him), to his dwelling. From that day, he remained suffering for many days; after that, when he was healed of (his) ailment, he remained like a mute for twelve years. One day, he went out with his friends to the town square; these friends of his began to play and he watched them, seated. A monk arrived there and, when he saw the man sitting alone while his friends were playing, he spoke and said to them: “What is wrong with this man who is sitting alone, as if saddened, while you are joyful?” They answered him and said to him: “He is mute and cannot speak, here it has been twelve years since he became mute.” The monk answered and said to them: “Why do you not bring him to the tomb of abbä Sämu’êl?” They answered and said to him: “Will (he) be able to heal him if we bring him there?” He said to them: “Yes, he will be able to, for he is a worker of wonders and a healer of the sick.” When they heard this from the monk, they took the mute and brought him to the tomb of the man of God. When he had rolled on his tomb, at that very moment, our blessed father of beautiful memory, the companion of the Watchers, mär Sämu’êl came to him, clothed as if in flames of fire: he took the forehead of the mute and signed him with his holy hands. Immediately, the bond of the mute's tongue was undone, he opened his mouth and spoke correctly, saying: “Here the bond of my tongue has been undone when the man of God touched me, having come to me.”

313 Blessed abbä of beautiful memory, healer of the sick and raiser of the dead, undoer of the bond of the tongue of the mutes, I offer to the greatness of your grace a gift of canticle and hymn, having heard how you perform wonders everywhere. Solicit therefore now for us mercy and


[p. 264] {#po.53.p264}
በኩልሄ ። አስተምሕርኬ ፡ ይእዜ ፡ በእንቲአነ ፡ ምሕረተ ፡ ወሣህለ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ፻ገጽ ፡ ወ አካል ፡ ወ ፳ህላዌ ፡ ዕሩያነ ፡ ጸዳል ፡ ወሁራሔ ። ድመሙራን ፡ እን በለ ፡ ቡዓዴ ፡ ኅቡራን ፡ እንበለ ፡ ቱሳሔ ። ከመ ፡ ይጸግወነ ፡ ዐስበ ፡ ሠናየ ፡ በአሐቲ ፡ ምክር ፡ ምስለ ፡ አፍሕርቲሃ ፡ ለትሔ ። ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ።
clemency from the Three Persons and Three Hypostases—one single substance—equal in brilliance and splendor, united without difference, joined without mixture, so that they may grace us with a beautiful prize, with a single will, with the little birds of the lodge (of the Temple?), for ever and ever, amen. Fifty-eighth miracle: a monk contemplates Sämu'êl on a chariot of light with his numerous descendants.

314 58th miracle of the man of God, the innocent lamb that the paw of the wolf has not touched, treasure of holiness, lake of purity, brilliant lamp of the faith, radiance of the Bride, mär Sämu’êl. May the intercession of his prayer impart to us, for our rest, the place of joy and delights, for ever and ever, amen. And again, one day, a monk, a man of God upon whom dwelt the grace of the Holy Spirit, said: “While I was in the interior of the desert, standing and praying at the time of the middle of the night, an angel of light came to me and said to me: ‘Go out of your house to look at what God has ordered me to show you.’ I went out immediately from my dwelling as the angel had told me and, at that very moment, I saw a man of terrifying majesty, clothed in a mantle of light that was not perceptible to the eyes, which sparkled seven times more than the sun. While he was going above the air, mounted on a chariot of light, followed behind him numerous people—without number—like the stars of the sky, filling the expanse of the sky while flying on the wings of the Spirit. When I saw that, I spoke and said to the angel who had been sent to me to show (this) to me: ‘Who is this man of terrifying majesty?’ He answered and said to me: ‘Sämu’êl of Gadäma Waäli.’ I said to him again: ‘What (are) these armies of men, numerous as the stars, that I see following him?’ He said to me: ‘These are those whom (Sämu’êl) has received from his Lord as a tithe for his pact.’ I also asked him why he had been given all this great gift of grace. He answered again and said to me: ‘For the perfume of his justice has filled the expanse of the seven heavens; after that, when he had spent a little time on earth, his fame went out into all countries. Because of that, all generations make his commemoration by having faith in him.’ ” “When I heard that, from the mouth of the angel who came to me,” said the monk who told us this, “I marveled and glorified God for having given such grace to abbä Sämu’êl.”

315 I, for my part, marvel at your virtue, blessed abbä, treasure of holiness, lake of purity, you of whose virtue the perfume has filled the extended heaven of the heavens. I am also in awe of the richness of your grace, (having) heard it from a man who saw you at the time of the middle of the night in a terrifying majesty, shining seven times more than the sun, with numerous armies of people following you. Now, intercede for us with Him

commemoration of Sämu’êl thanks to a ruse of the monks. 316 59th miracle of our blessed father of beautiful memory, accepted vine that has been planted in the garden of the Bride, bearing numerous clusters, which is a shelter for the birds of the Gospel, mär Sämu’êl. May the intercession of his prayer to overcome be for us a strength, for ever and ever, amen. And again, one day, a man took ears of grain from his neighbor's field without (the latter) seeing him. After that, he went away from there; having moved aside into the middle of the trees, he sat down to eat, stretching his feet on either side. After that, having finished eating what he had taken, when he wanted to go away, he could not rise from there, for his feet were bound and he was paralyzed all over. Then he screamed with a loud voice, weeping bitterly. When his parents heard the sound of his voice, they came to him and said to him: “What is the matter with you?” He answered and said to them: “My feet are bound on either side and my whole body has weakened; when I could not rise from where I was sitting, I screamed while weeping because this ailment that I do not know has touched me suddenly.” When his parents heard this proposal from him, they felt great affliction on his account. After that, they carried him and brought him to their house; he remained suffering for many years. One day, monks met him on the road while he was going, mounted on a donkey. When they saw that he was infirm, they spoke and said to him: “Come with us so that we may bring you to the place of the tomb of abbä Sämu’êl so that he may heal you of your sickness.” When the sick man heard their word, he rejoiced and went with them, as they had told him. After that, when it was the time of evening, they moved away from the road toward a church to spend the night there. When the paralytic was asleep, the monks took his donkey in secret so that he would not suspect them before the morning came. As for the sick man, when he knew that they had taken his donkey, (having) removed (it), he remained weeping, grieving, and saying: “Abbä Sämu’êl, look at this violence (against) me, how these monks have acted iniquitously toward me. Now, grace me with healing through the intercession of your prayer.” He remained saying this all day. When the man of God heard this groaning of the poor man, he came to him on the twelfth of the month of tahsäs, the day of the feast of his commemoration; he touched the paralytic and, immediately, the bones (of the latter) cracked like dry wood. At that very moment, he rose, stood straight on his feet, and was healed of his ailment. From that day, he was entirely in good health and was going where he wanted.

317 My beloved, Christian people, be filled with wonder before the miracle of this father of ours of beautiful memory, companion of the disciples, who has been enveloped in the science of the faith, who healed the twisted man when (the latter) trusted in the invocation of his name. Now, may he intercede for us with Him who loved the beauty of Jacob, so that He may make us ready for the nuptial feasts with him, for ever and ever, amen. Sixtieth miracle: the invocation of Sämu’êl protects the rooster of a

widow against the attack of a falcon. 318 60th miracle of the man of God, solid soldier who has fought with the adversary of the world, the king of Färs, riding the horse of the faith, holding in his hands the shaft of the cross of Christ, mär Sämu’êl. May the intercession of his prayer be for us, in clemency, the salvation of body and soul, for ever and ever, amen. And again, one day, a falcon arrived and took a rooster from the house of a poor widow who had no other property besides this one. When the woman saw that it had taken the rooster, she cried out and said to it: “I adjure you by the power of the prayer of abbä Sämu’êl not to fly away and not to go away without having returned (it) to me.” When she had said that, the falcon remained there from the time of the third hour until the time of the ninth hour, spreading its wings in the air; it could not fly away, because the woman had adjured it by the power of the prayer of the man of God of beautiful memory, mär Sämu’êl. After that, (the falcon) turned three times above the house (of the woman) and deposited the rooster at the door of her dwelling, alive; it had done it no harm. Immediately the woman rejoiced and glorified God because He had performed for her a miracle through the power of the prayer of abbä Sämu’êl.
ወሰብሐቶ ፡ ለእግዚአብሔር ፡ በእንተ ፡ ዘገብረ ፡ ላቲ ፡ ተአምረ ፡ በኀይለ ፡ ጸሎቱ ፡ ለአባ ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡= ኦፍቁራንየ ፡ ሕዝብ ፡ መሲሓውያን ፡ አንክሮ ፡ አንክሩ ፡ መንክራተ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡ ሠናየ ፡ ዝክር ፡ ሐመረ ፡ ብዕል ፡ እምባሕረ ፡ ኃጢ አት ፡ ዐዲዎ ። ብሔረ ፡ ፍሥሓ ፡ ዘቦአ ፡ ለአዕርፎ ፡ በልብሰ ፡ መርዓ ፡ ተሰር ጊዎ ፡። ሰሚዐክሙ ፡ ተኣምራቲሁ ፡ ዘከመ ፡ ረሰዮ ፡ ለሆባይ ፡ እምጊዜ ፡ ሰለ ስቱ ፡ ሰዓት ፡ እስከ ፡ ተስዓቱ ፡ ሰዓት ፡ ረቢቦ ፡ መልዕልተ ፡ አየር ፡ እስከ ፡ ያግብእ ፡ ዘነሥአ ፡ ዶርሆ ፡ ብእሲት ፡ ሕያዎ ፡ ይእዜኒ ፡ ያስተምሕር ፡ በእንቲ አነ ፡ ምሕረተ ፡ ወሣህለ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ አርምሞ ፡ ኅበ ፡ ዘኩልነ ፡ ንሴፈዎ ፍ ለዓ ለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን
319 My beloved, Christian people, be filled with wonder before the wonder of this father of ours of beautiful memory, vessel of wealth brought out of the sea of sin, who has entered into the land of joy for rest, adorned with the nuptial garment, having heard his miracle: he made the falcon (remain there), from the time of the third hour until the time of the ninth hour, spreading (its wings) in the air, until it returned alive the rooster of the woman that it had taken. Now, may he solicit for us mercy and clemency, without ceasing, from Him in whom we all place our trust, for ever and ever, amen. Sixty-first miracle: Sämu’êl repels exiles who want to seize the offerings prepared for his commemoration.

law and the commandment that were written by the finger of God on the tables (Ex 31:18), who also worked, during the course of (his) life, to make his mines produce interest, to fulfill the parable of the Gospel (Lk 19:13-19), mär Sämu’êl. May the intercession of his prayer grace us with the beautiful reward from above, for ever and ever, amen. And again, one day, the day of the commemoration of our blessed father, a man prepared much food and drink in order to bring (them) to the church for the name of the man of God. Before he had brought the food, many people, who had been exiled by order of the king, came to the man's house looking for food and drink, having heard that there was much food in the man's house. When the man saw them coming to him with violence, he said to his own: “Open the door of the porch so that I may see if the power of the prayer of abbä Sämu’êl allows them to enter in order to take the food that has been prepared for his commemoration.” After that, the exiles came, wanting to enter the man's house. The power of the prayer of abbä Sämu’êl did not allow them to enter; on the contrary, it threw itself against them and pushed them back. The exiles said to each other: “What magic is there in this House, which pushes us back by throwing itself against us so that we do not enter it?” After having spoken thus to each other, they moved away from the man's house, as it was impossible for them to enter. When the man saw this miracle that mär Sämu’êl had performed for him, he rejoiced, exulted, and went to the church, having taken the food. He handed (it) over to the priests (and) told them the whole miracle that the power of the prayer of the man of God had performed for him. When the priests and the people who were there heard that, they spoke to each other, saying: “From now on, let us all make the commemoration of the man of God to be saved, by the power of his prayer, from all peril and danger.” Having said that, they remained making the commemoration of our blessed father of beautiful memory, mär Sämu’êl.

321 My beloved, Christian people, be filled with wonder before the beauty of the virtue of our blessed father, having heard his miracle: he threw the people, by the power of his prayer, far from the door of the entrance of the man who was making his commemoration, so that they would not enter his house. Now, may he intercede for us without negligence before the holy Trinity that we all praise, so that He may grace us with His clemency and His mercy, for ever and ever, amen. Sixty-second miracle: Sämu’êl heals a monk who contemplated him interceding in heaven for all souls.

the blessed children of the vestibule (of the Temple) at the second blast of the trumpet, for ever and ever, amen. And again, one day, a monk upon whom dwelt the grace of the Holy Spirit said: “A great pain seized me; I remained without eating or drinking and without recognizing night and day. After that, the angel of joy came to me, took my soul, and made me ascend to the height of the heavens. When he had made me arrive above the seventh heaven, there I saw abbä Sämu’êl standing before the throne of the holy Trinity, interceding for the soul of every creature. As for me, when I saw him, I spoke and said to him: ‘Greetings to you, abbä Sämu’êl the blessed, affliction of the desert, clothed in purity, intercessor of mercy for every creature; now, solicit for me: I have committed myself to your holy prayer so that it might make my path prosper in what is good.’ Immediately, he took me with him, made me stand before the Most High, and solicited for me as I had told him; I heard the word that he addressed (to the Most High) and the latter said to him: ‘Let him return to his body for you now.’ At that very moment, (Sämu’êl) took me from there with the angel who had made me ascend, to bring my soul back into my body, according to the command of the Lord his God. Many of his friends came following him, behind him, all of them praising him and celebrating his holiness; they made me arrive quickly where my body was. Immediately, I rose from my bed of pain and was healed of my sickness by the power of the prayer of abbä Sämu’êl, the companion of the Watchers, the intercessor of mercy for all.”
ዘውገ ፡ ትጉሃን ፡ ሰኣሌ ፡ ምሕረት ፡ ለኩሉ ኣነክር ፡ አንሰ ፡ ቲሩተ ፡ ዚአከ ፡ ኦአባ ፡ ብፁዕ ፡ ወቅዱስ ። ዘኢለከፎ ፡ ዘዓለም ፡ ርኩስ ። ሰሚዕየ ፡ እምብእሲ ፡ ዘነጸረከ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ትተነብል ፡ ለኩሉ ፡ ነፍስ ። ቀዊመከ ፡ ቅድመ ፡ ሥሉስ ፡ ቅዱስ ። ወይእዜኒ ፡ አስተምሕር ፡ በእን ቲአነ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ፻ ገጽ ፡ ወ ፳ አምሳል ፡ ዕሩያነ ፡ አካል ፡ ወኅቡራነ ፡ ንግሥ ። ከመ ፡ ይጸግወነ ፡ መክፈልተ ፡ ወርስተ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሀገሪትከ ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ሐዳስ ፡ ዘኢትበሊ ፡ ወኢትፈልስ ፍ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ።
323 I, for my part, marvel at your virtue, blessed and holy abbä whom the defilement of the age has not touched, having heard (what you did) for the man who watched you while you were interceding for every soul, standing before the holy Trinity. Now, intercede for us with the Three Persons—one single image—equal in substance and united in royalty, so that they may grace us with a share and an inheritance in your holy, new city, which neither grows old nor passes away, for ever and ever, amen. Sixty-third miracle: Sämu’êl heals a man who spent the

night lying on his tomb. 324 63rd miracle of our blessed father of beautiful memory, having turned away from the love of the age, who made himself poor in the desert by washing (it) with the water of the Gospel of defilement and purified it, who received his garment of Gilead (Jg 11:1), mär Sämu’êl. May the intercession of his prayer guide us toward the calm waters and the port, for ever and ever, amen. And again, one day, a man was seized by fatigue and a great sickness seized him; he bloated and swelled all over, from his neck to his feet. He remained in this sickness for many days and many doctors

could not heal him. After that, when the feast of the commemoration of the man of God arrived, his parents said to each other: “Come then, let us bring him to the tomb of abbä Sämu’êl, the healer of the sick, for today (is) the feast of his commemoration.” After having spoken thus to each other, they brought him, carrying (him), laid him on the tomb, and left him there so that he might spend the night (there). Our blessed father mär Sämu’êl, the worker of wonders, the healer of the sick, came to him before it was the time of the middle of the night; he touched the whole person of the sick man with his holy hands. After that, he raised him from his bed and stood him on his feet; at that very moment, (the man) was healed of his ailment and went, rejoicing and exulting, to where his parents were. The latter, seeing him walking alone, marveled at the miracle of our blessed father and glorified God.
ኦፍቁራንየ ፡ ሕዝብ ፡ መሲሓዊያን ፡ አንክሮ ፡ አንክሩ ፡ ብዕለ ፡ ጸጋሁ ፡ ለብእሴ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ከመ ፡ አርጋበ ፡ ወንጌል ፡ በመንፈሰ ፡ ጸጋ ፡ ዘተዐ ትበ ። ሰሚዐክሙ ፡ ተአምራቲሁ ፡ ዘከመ ፡ ፈወሶ ፡ ለድዉይ ፡ ገሲሶ ፡ በእደ ዊሁ ፡ በተአምኖ ፡ መልዕልተ ፡ ዝኅሩ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ሰከበ = ይእዜኒ ፡ ያስተምሕር ፡ በእንቲአነ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይጸግወነ ፡ ሕይወተ ፡ ወሣህለ ፡ በጽንዐ ፡ ኀይሉ ፡ ሰማየ ፡ ዘሰፍሐ ፡ ወምድረ ፡ ዘአርሐበ ። ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ፡
325 My beloved, Christian people, be filled with wonder before the richness of the grace of the man of God who was marked, like the doves of the Gospel, by the spirit of grace, having heard his miracle: he healed the sick man, (having) touched him with his hands, when with confidence (the latter) was laid on his tomb. Now, may he intercede for us, so that He may grace us with life and clemency, by the strength of His power, He who stretched out the heaven and widened the earth, for ever and ever, amen. Sixty-fourth miracle: Sämu’êl heals a deaf man in a church
፳ ወ፪ ተአምሪሁ ፡ ለብእሴ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ምብያተ ፡ ጥበብ ፡ ዘውገ ፡ ሰሎሞን ። ሱታፌ ፡ ዘካርያስ ፡ ካህን ። ኩኩሐ ፡ ሃይማኖት ፡ ቢጸ ፡ ወልደ ፡ ዮና ፡ ስምዖን ። ማር ፡ ሳሙኤል ። ትንብልና ፡ ጸሎቱ ፡ ይዕቀበነ ፡ እምትምይ ንተ ፡ ሰይጣን ። ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ። ወካዕበ ፡ በ-፳እምነ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ ነበረ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ከዊኖ ፡ ጽሙመ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ዓመታተ ፡ ወሶበ ፡ በጽሐ ፡ በዓለ ፡ ተዝካሩ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡ ገባሬ ፡ መንክ ራት ፡ ማር ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡ ወሖረ ፡ ውስተ ፡ አሐቲ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ነሚኦ ፡ ዕጣነ ፡ በእንተ ፡ ስሙ ፡ ለብእሴ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ከመ ፡ ይትማህለል ፡ ህየ ። ወእምዝ ፡ ሶበ ፡ በጽሐ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ይእቲ ፡ መካን ፡ ሰፍሐ ፡ እደዊሁ ፡ ወጸ ለየ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብል ፡ ኦአባ ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡ ተማኅፀንኩ ፡ በጸሎትከ ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ከመ ፡ ትፈውሰኒ ፡ ዮም ፡ በዕለተ ፡ ተዝካርከ" ፡ እምድንቃዌ ፡ አእዛንየ ። ወሶ ቤሃ ፡ ተርኅወ ፡ አእዛኒሁ ፡ ወሰምዐ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይትቀነዩ ፡ ካህናት ፡ ወሖረ ፡ ኀቤ ሆሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ይትቀነይ ፡ ምስሌሆሙ ።= ወሶበ ፡ ርእይዎ ፡ እለ ፡ ያአምርዎ ፡ አውሥኡ ፡ ወይቤልዎ ፡ አኮኑ ፡ አንተ ፡ ጽሙመ ፡ እዝን ፡ ማእዜኑ ፡ ሐየውከ ። ተሰጥወ ፡ ወይቤሎሙ ፡ እስመ ፡ ፈወሰኒ ፡ ዮም ፡ ኀይለ ፡ ጸሎቱ ፡ ለአባ ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡ ሶበ ፡ ተማኅፀንኩ ፡ ኀቤሁ ፡ እንዘ ፡ እጹውዕ ፡ ስሞ ። ይእዜኒ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ሰማዕኩ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ትዌድሱ ፡ ኪያሁ ፡ መጻእኩ ፡ ምስሌክሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ እትቀነይ ። ወሰሚዖሙ ፡ ዘንተ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ ካህናት ፡ ወሕዝብ ፡ እለ ፡ ሀለዉ ፡ ህየ ፡ እምአፈ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ጽሙም ፡ አፈድፈዱ ፡ ወድሶቶ ፡ ለብእሴ ፡ እግዚአብ ሔር ፡ እንዘ ፡ ያስተዐጽቡ ፡ ቅድስናሁ ።=
during his commemoration. 326 64th miracle of the man of God, residence of wisdom, companion of Solomon, associate of Zechariah the priest (Lk 1:5), rock of the faith, friend of Simon Bar-Jona, mär Sämu’êl. May the intercession of his prayer keep us from the ruse of Satan, for ever and ever, amen. And again, one day, a man remained for many years being deaf. When the feast of the commemoration of our blessed father, worker of wonders, mär Sämu’êl arrived, he went to a church, having taken incense for the name of the man of God, in order to make supplications there. After that, when he had arrived in that place, he stretched out his hands and prayed, saying: “Abbä Sämu’êl, I have committed myself to your holy prayer so that you might heal me today, the day of your commemoration, of the deafness of my ears.” Immediately, his ears opened, he heard the priests singing, and went to them to sing with them. When those who knew him saw him, they spoke and said to him: “Were you not deaf of ear: when did you heal?” He answered and said to them: “The power of the prayer of abbä Sämu’êl healed me today when I committed myself to him by invoking his name. Now, when I heard you praising him, I came to sing with you.” Having heard that, from the mouth of the deaf man, all the priests and the people who were there praised the man of God all the more, being in awe of his holiness.


[p. 276] {#po.53.p276}
ኦአባ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡ ሠናየ ፡ ዝክር ፡ ገባሬ ፡ መንክራት ፡ አንሰ ፡ እንዘ ፡ እትጋነይ ፡ እዌድስ ፡ በአጐህኖ ። ሰሚዕየ ፡ ተአምራቲከ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ተማኅፀነ ፡ በጸ ሎትከ ፡ ለጽሙም ፡ ዘከሠትከ ፡ እዝኖ ። ይእዜኒ ፡ ትንብልና ፡ ጸሎትከ ፡ ለን ጉሥነ ፡ ናኦድ ፡ ይትማኅፀኖ ፡ ኀይለ ፡ ወፀወነ ፡ ለመዊአ ፡ ፀሩ ፡ ይኩኖ ። ምስለ ፡ ደቂቁ”' ፡ ለአድኅኖ ። በምልሓ ፡ ሃይማኖት ፡ ይክድኖ ። ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን
327 Blessed abbä of beautiful memory, worker of wonders, I, in giving thanks, offer praise by revealing the mystery, having heard your miracle, (to) you who, when he committed himself to your prayer, opened the ears of the deaf man. Now, may the intercession of your prayer take under its protection our king Nä'od, may it be for him power and citadel to overcome his enemy, may it cover him with his children for salvation through the discernment of the faith, for ever and ever, amen. Sixty-fifth miracle: Sämu’êl heals a blind man, a deaf man, and a
፳ ወ ፳ ተኣምሪሁ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡ ሠናየ ፡ ዝክር ፡ በምርዋጸ ፡ በድር ፡ ብዙኅ ፡ ለፀረ ፡ አቡሁ ፡ ዘአደግደጎ ። ለአምላኩ ፡ እንዘ ፡ የሐስሥ ፡ ሥርዐቶ ፡ ወሕጎ ። ውርዝውና ፡ ዘኢሞኦ ፡ ወርትዕ ፡ ዘኢኀደጎ ። ማር ፡ ሳሙ ኤል ። ጸሎቱ ፡ ወስእለቱ ፡ ይጸግወነ ፡ ዕሜተ ፡ ሠናየ ፡ ምስለ ፡ አፍሕርተ ፡ ጥበብ ፡ አስተዛዊጎ = ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ። ወካዕበ ፡ በ ፳ እምነ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ በዕለተ ፡ በዓለ ፡ ተዝካሩ ፡ ለብእሴ ፡ እግ ዚአብሔር ፡ መጽኡ ፡ ኀበ ፡ መቃብሩ ፡ የ ድዉያን ፡ 8ዕዉር ፡ ወኔ፳ጳጽሙም ፡ ወ፳በሃም ፡ ወአንገርገሩ ፡ መልዕልተ ፡ ዝኅሩ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡ ሠናየ ፡ ዝክር ፡ ማር ፡ ሳሙኤል ፍ ወሶቤሃ ፡ ተፈወሱ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ በቅድመ ፡ ጉባኤ ፡ ብዙኃን ፡ እለ ፡ ተጋብኡ ፡ በእንተ ፡ በዓለ ፡ ተዝካሩ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡ ጥዑመ ፡ ዝክር ።= ወውእቱሂ ፡ ዕዉር ፡ ርእየ ፡ ወጽሙምሂ ፡ ሰምዐ ፡ ወበሃም ፡ ነበበ ። ወዘንተ ፡ ተኣምረ ፡ ርእዮሙ ፡ እሉ ፡ ጉባኤ ፡ አፈድፈዱ ፡ ወድሶቶ ፡
mute who roll on his tomb on the day of his commemoration. 328 65th miracle of our blessed father of beautiful memory, who withered the enemy of his Father through the great combat of (his) life, by seeking the rule and the law of his God, whom youth did not overcome and whom the right did not abandon, mär Sämu’êl. May his prayer and his solicitation grace us with a beautiful reward by uniting (us) with the little birds of wisdom, for ever and ever, amen. And again, one day, the day of the feast of the commemoration of the man of God, three infirm men came to his tomb—a blind man, a deaf man, and a mute—and rolled on the tomb of our blessed father of beautiful memory, mär Sämu’êl. Immediately, all were healed before an assembly of many (people) who had gathered for the feast of the commemoration of our blessed father of sweet memory. The blind man saw, the deaf man heard, and the mute spoke. Seeing this miracle, the assembly praised the man of God all the more, being in awe of his holiness.
ለብእሴ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ እንዘ ፡ ያስተዐፅቡ ፡ ቅድስናሁ ፍ ኦአባ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡ ገባሬ ፡ መንክራት ፡ ለሐዋርያት ፡ ዘውጎሙ = ኣነ ክር ፡ አንሰ ፡ ሰሚዕየ ፡ ተኣምራቲከ ፡ ሶበ ፡ አንገርገሩ ፡ መልዕልተ ፡ ዝኅርከ ፡ ለዕዉር ፡ ወለጽሙም”” ፡ ወለበሃም ፡ ዘከመ ፡ ፈወስኮሙ ። ይእዜኒ ፡ አስተም ሕር ፡ በእንቲአነ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ብሉየ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ በግዕ ፡ ዘአማኑኤል ፡ ስሙ ።« ከመ ፡ ይጸግወነ ፡ መክፈልተ ፡ ወርስተ ፡ በእንቲአሁ ፡ ምስለ ፡ እለ ፡ ፈጸሙ ፡ ገድሎሙ ፍ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ፡፥
329 Blessed abba, worker of wonders, companion of the apostles, I, for my part, marvel, having heard your miracle: when the blind man, the deaf man, and the mute rolled on your tomb, you healed them. Now, intercede for us with the Ancient of Days, the Lamb of the name of Emmanuel, so that He may grace us with a share and an inheritance with those who, for Him, have accomplished their (spiritual) combat, for ever and ever, amen. Sixty-sixth miracle: Sämu’êl saves a monk who was about to be devoured by a large serpent.
፳ ወፄ ተአምሪሁ ፡ ለብእሴ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ሠናየ ፡ ዝክር ፡ ሐራዊ ፡ ጽኑዕ ፡ ፍኖተ ፡ ንጉሥ ፡ ዘይተሉ ፡ አሰሮ ፡ እጎዞ ፡ እንዘ ፡ የዐቅብ ፡ ሕጎ ፡ ወትእዛዞ ።= ከመ ፡ ይፀባእ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ዕልዋነ ፡ ዚአሁ ፡ ህየንተ ፡ ወልታ ፡ ወኩ ናት ፡ ሰንዶነ ፡ ሃይማኖት ፡ ተዐሪዞ ። ማር ፡ ሳሙኤል ። ትንብልና ፡ ጸሎቱ ፡ ይዕቀበነ ፡ እምኀይለ ፡ ጸላኢ ፡ በደመ ፡ ምሥጢር ፡ አምዒዞ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ፡
330 66th miracle of the man of God of beautiful memory, vigorous soldier who follows the path of the King, having taken his track by keeping his law and his commandment, having clothed himself in the tunic of the faith, in place of the shield and the lance, to fight those who rebel against him, mär Sämu’êl. May the intercession of his prayer keep us from the power of the Enemy, embalming with the blood of the mystery, for ever and ever, amen.


[p. 278] {#po.53.p278}
ወካዕበ ፡ በ እምነ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ ሖረ ፡ ውስተ ፡ ገዳም ፡ ከመ ፡ ይትማህለል ፡ በእንተ ፡ መድኀኒተ ፡ ነፍሱ ፡ መነኮስ ፡ ወእንዘ ፡ ሀሎ ፡ ህየ ፡ አሐዘ ፡ ዐቢይ?” ፡ ተመን ፡ ወተጠብለለ ፡ ላዕለ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ እምእግሩ”” ፡ እስከ ፡ ርእሱ ። ወእምዝ ፡ አብቀወ ፡ አፉሁ ፡ ከመ ፡ የሐጦ ። ወውእቱሰ ፡ መነኮስ ፡ አውሥአ ፡ ወይቤሎ ፡ አምሐልኩከ ፡ በኀይለ ፡ ጸሎቱ ፡ ለአባ ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡ ከመ ፡ ትሕ ድገኒ ። ወሶቤሃ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ፍጻሜ ፡ ቃሉ ፡ ተፈትሐ ፡ እምለዕሌሁ ፡ ውእቱ ፡
And again, one day, a monk went into the desert to offer supplications for the salvation of his soul; while he was there, a great serpent seized him and coiled itself around him from head to foot. After that, it opened its mouth to swallow him. As for the monk, he spoke and said to it: "I conjure you, by the power of the prayer of Abbä Sämu’el, to let me go." Immediately, at the moment he finished his word, the serpent detached itself from him and went far away from him.
ከይሲ ፡ ወሖረ ፡ እምኔሁ ። ኦፍቁራንየ ፡ ሕዝብ ፡ መሲሓዊያን ፡ አንክሮ ፡ አንክሩ ፡ ተአምራተ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡ ጥዑመ ፡ ዝክር ፡ ፄወ”'' ፡ መለኮት ፡ ዘቀሠሞ ። ሰሚዐክሙ ፡ መንክሮ ፡ ዘከመ ፡ አድኅኖ ፡ ለብእሲ ፡ እምአፈ ፡ ከይሲ ፡ ዘፈ ቀደ ፡ ከመ ፡ የኀጦ ፡ ሶበ ፡ አምሐሎ ፡ ጸዊዖ ፡ ስሞ ። ይእዜኒ ፡ ያስተምሕር ፡ በእንቲአነ ፡ ምሕረተ ፡ ወሣህለ ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ አርምሞ ፡ ቅድመ ፡ ሥሉስ ፡ ቅዱስ ፡ ቀዊሞ ። ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን
331 My friends, Christian people, be filled with wonder before the miracle of this father of ours of sweet memory, whom the salt of divinity has seasoned, having heard his prodigy: he saved the man from the mouth of the serpent that wanted to swallow him when (the man) conjured it, having invoked the name (of Sämu'ël). Now, may he solicit mercy and clemency for us, without ceasing, standing before the holy Trinity, in the ages of ages, amen. Sixty-seventh miracle: Sämu’ël protects from rain and hail

monks from Dämot on a journey to his tomb. 332 67th miracle of our blessed father of beautiful memory, who preferred wisdom and made knowledge his city, the companion of the prophets, the preacher of the Gospel, the blessed apostle whom the wickedness of sin did not touch, mär Sämu’ël. May the intercession of his prayer make us sit at the table in the holy city that the eye of the eagle has not seen, in the ages of ages, amen. And again, one day, while monks were coming from the region of Dämot to arrive at the tomb of the man of God, having faith in the intercession of his prayer, and while they were on the road where there were no houses or trees to shelter in, a heavy rain with violent hail came upon them. The monks, when they saw this frightening rain, cried out saying: "Abbä Sämu’ël, save us from this frightening rain: we have entrusted ourselves to the intercession of your prayer! Indeed, we are going to the place of your tomb, hoping for your blessing." When they had said this, the cloud gathered, far from above them, on either side. They, when they saw this miracle, stood there to see the prodigy of the man of God. After that, a heavy hail fell with wind and accumulated on the earth (to a height of) two cubits and a span. As for the saints, it did not touch them, falling beside them, leaving three cubits in front of them and behind them. After that, when the hail had passed, the saints prostrated themselves and glorified God because He had saved them by the power of the prayer of Abbä Sämu’el, the worker of prodigies. Arrived at the tomb of our blessed father, the saints bore witness by telling us thus.


[p. 280] {#po.53.p280}
ኦፍቁራንየ ፡ ሕዝብ ፡ መሲሓዊያን ፡ አንክሮ ፡ አንክሩ ፡ ቲሩተ ፡ ዝንቱ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡ ገባሬ ፡ መንክራት ፡ ከመ ፡ ሮሰ ፡ ዮካብድ ፡ ሰሚዐክሙ ፡ መንክራቲሁ ፡ ዘአድኀኖሙ ፡ ለእልክቱ ፡ ቅዱሳን ፡ ሶበ ፡ ተማኅፀኑ ፡ ኅቤሁ ፡ እምዕዙዝ ፡ በረድ ። ይእዜኒ ፡ ያስተምሕር ፡ በእንቲአነ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ዘተብህለ ፡ አርዌ ፡ ጽኑዕ ፡ በወልደ ፡ ነጐድጓድ ።= ከመ ፡ ይጸግወነ ፡ ዕሜዔተ ፡ ሠናየ ፡ ምስለ ፡ አፍሕርተ ፡ ጥበብ ፡ ቡሩካን ፡ ውሉድ ።
333 My beloved, Christian people, be filled with wonder before the virtue of this blessed father of ours, a worker of prodigies like the male child of Yokèbed (Ex 6:20), having heard his prodigy, he who saved these saints when they entrusted themselves to him away from the violent hail. Now, may he intercede for us with Him who is called the vigorous "serpent (Jn 3:14)" by the Son of Thunder (Mk 3:17), so that He may grant us a beautiful reward with the fledglings of wisdom, blessed children, in the ages of ages, amen. death. 334 68th miracle of the man of God of beautiful memory, who, having faith in the life that (is) in heaven, despised the desire of the century, having fortified himself with the name of the Trinity, who accomplished the career of life being covered with the tunic of faith, mär Sämu’ël. May the intercession of his prayer be, to save us, life (eternal), in the ages of ages, amen. And again, one day, a monk who dwelt in a cell said: "When my departure from the world was near, Abbä Sämu’el came to me resplendent like the sun, holding in his hand a monastic habit in the likeness of a cowl, and signed my forehead. After that, he put the monastic habit he had brought around my neck; he spoke and said to me: 'Your departure from the world is near, so that you are going to come to where I am.' Having said this, he disappeared from before my eyes." After having informed us thus, the monk died a few days later, on the 28th of the month of Tähsäs.
አዕረፈ ፡ እምድኅረ ፡ ንስቲት ፡ መዋፅል ፡ አመ ፡ ፳ወ ቿለወርኃ ፡ ታኅሣሥ ። ኣነክር ፡ አንሰ ፡ ቲሩተ ፡ ዚአከ ፡ ኦአባ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡ ዓምደ ፡ ኤላም ፡ ጽኑዕ ፡ በኩኩሐ ፡ ሃይማኖት ፡ ሕኑጽ ።= ሰሚዕየ ፡ ተአምራቲከ ፡ ዘከመ ፡ መጻእከ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ነሚአከ ፡ አስኬማ ፡ ዘኢይትዐወቅ ፡ እንመቱ ፡ ለትእም ርተ ፡ ሕይወቱ ፡ ዘደኃሪ ፡ ከመ ፡ ታዑቆ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ትጌውጽ ። ይእዜኒ ፡ አስተ ምሕር ፡ በእንቲአነ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ፻ አካል ፡ ወ ገጽ ። ለርባዔ ፡ ዘኢይትዌሰክ ፡ ወበኑ ታጌ ፡ ዘኢየኀጽጽ ። ከመ ፡ ይጸግወነ ፡ ዕሜተ ፡ ሠናየ ፡ ምስለ ፡ አፍሕርተ ፡ መርዓት ፡ ሥንኣሁ ፡ ኅሩያን ፡ አብያጽ ። ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ።
335 I marvel at your virtue, blessed Abbä, solid column of the vestibule (of the Temple) built on the rock of faith, having heard your miracle: you came to the man, having taken a monastic habit whose texture is not known, to show a sign of his future life, while you were visiting (him). Now, intercede for us with the Three Hypostases and Three Persons who are not increased into a tetrad and who are not diminished by a reduction, so that they may grant us a beautiful reward with the fledglings of the Bride, the allies of (Sämu'ël), chosen friends, in the ages of ages, amen. single night a long stage on the sea. 336 69th miracle of our blessed father clothed in holiness, who was called a companion of the angels for his zeal, from whose body purity was

ንጽሐ ፡ ሥጋሁ = ዘሞኦ ፡ በሥራሐ ፡ ገድል ፡ ለፀረ ፡ አቡሁ ። ማር ፡ ሳሙ ኤል ። ትንብልና ፡ ጸሎቱ ፡ ይክፍለነ" ፡ ውስተ ፡ ሀገሪቱ ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ነሀሉ ፡ ምስሌሁ ። ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ፡፥ ወካዕበ ፡ በ ፳-እምነ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ ይቤ ፡ ፳ ብእሲ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይነግድ ፡ ባሕረ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ብዙኃን ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ቦአት ፡ ኀመርነ ፡ ማእከለ ፡ ፪ዐበይት ፡ አእባን ፡ ወቆ መት ፡ ህየ ፡ ተእቲዞ ፡ ቦሙ ። ወኢክህሉ ፡ እለ ፡ ይጌድፉ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያውጽእዋ ፡ እምህየ ።= ወእምዝ ፡ ጸር ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ በበግዕዞሙ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይስእሉ ፡ በገዐር””" ፡ ወበብካይ ፡ ወኢተክህሎሙ ፡ ከመ ፡ ያውፅእዋ ፡ ለይእቲ ፡ ሐመር ። ወሶበ ፡ አር መሙ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ ይቤ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ኦአባ ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡ አንሰ ፡ ተማኅ ፀንኩ ፡ በትንብልና ፡ ጸሎትከ ፡ ከመ ፡ ታውፅኣ ፡ ለዛቲ ፡ ሐመር ፡ እምዘ ፡ ተእ ኅዘት = ወሶቤሃ ፡ ጊዜ ፡ ፍጻሜ ፡ ቃሉ ፡ ቀነጸት ፡ ይእቲ ፡ ሐመር ፡ ወወፅ አት ፡ እምእሉ ፡ አእባን ፡ ወሠናየ"”" ፡ ሑረታ ፡ ማእከለ ፡ ባሕር ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ የሐድፍዋ ፡ ወአብጽሐቶሙ ፡ ምሕዋረ ፡ ብዙኀ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ በአሐቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ኀበ ፡ ዘፈቀዱ ፡ ዓዲወ ። ወእምአሜሃ ፡ አሐዘ ፡ ውእቱ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ይግበር ፡
like a limpid pearl, who conquered by the labors of his (spiritual) combat the enemy of his Father, mär Sämu’ël. May the intercession of his prayer grant us to be with him in his holy city, in the ages of ages, amen. And again, one day, a man traveling at sea with many people said: "Our boat entered into the middle of two great rocks and stopped there, held by them. Those who were piloting could not get it out of there." After that, everyone cried out, each according to his character, supplicating with groans and tears; it was not possible for them to get the boat out. When everyone fell silent, the man said: "Abbä Sämu’ël, I have entrusted myself to the intercession of your prayer so that you might make this boat come out after it has been held." Immediately, at the moment of the completion of his word, the boat leaped, came out from between the rocks and, with a beautiful course in the middle of the sea, without being piloted, brought (the passengers), in a single night for a stage of many days, to where they wanted to go. From that moment, the man began to make the commemoration of our blessed father and to give an ox every year for his name.
ተዝካሮ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡ ወየሀብ ፡ ላህመ ፡ ለለዓመት ፡ በእንተ ፡ ስሙ ።« ኦአባ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡ ሠናየ ፡ ዝክር ፡ ኣነክር ፡ አንሰ ፡ ሰሚዕየ ፡ ተአምራ ቲከ ፡ ብእሲ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ተማኅፀነ ፡ ኀቤከ ፡ ፍጡነ ፡ መንክራቲከ ፡ ዘአርአይኮ ። ፡ አውሂዒአከ ፡ ሐመሮ ፡ በትንብልናከ ፡ እምዘ ፡ ተእኅዘት ፡ ዘእንበለ ፡ ሐዳፊ ፡ ምሕዋረ ፡ ብዙኅ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ በአሐቲ ፡ ዕለት ፡ ዘአዕደውኮ ። ይእዜኒ ፡ አስ ተምሕር ፡ በእንቲአነ ፡ ኀበ ፡ ዓምደ ፡ ሃይማኖትነ ፡ ኀይለ ፡ ነፋስ ፡ ዘኢይነከንኮ ፡። ከመ ፡ ያስምዐነ ፡ ምስለ ፡ አፍሕርተ ፡ ጥበብ ፡ ቃለ ፡ ባርኮ ።= ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ፍ
337 Blessed Abbä of beautiful memory, I marvel, having heard your miracle, (at) you who, when the man entrusted himself to you, showed him your prodigy quickly, you who, having made his boat come out by your intercession after it had been held, made him travel in a single night, without a pilot, a stage of many days. Now, intercede for us with the column of our faith that the violence of the wind does not shake, so that He may make us hear with the fledglings of wisdom the word of blessing, in the ages of ages, amen. Seventieth miracle: by the intercession of Sämu'el, a pagan begets three sons, converts, and remains faithful to the commemoration
ድ ተኣምሪሁ ፡ ለብእሴ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ ሠናየ ፡ ዝክር ፡ ላህም ፡ ጻዓዳ ፡ አበ ፡ ብዙኃን ፡ ጣዕዋ ። ሐመረ ፡ ብዕል ፡ ለባሕረ ፡ ኃጢአት ፡ ዘዐደዋ ። ለፍ ቅረ ፡ ዓለም ፡ ዘኢፈተዋ ። ማር ፡ ሳሙኤል ። ትንብልና ፡ ጸሎቱ ፡ ትዕቀበነ ፡ ወትረ ፡ ምስሌነ ፡ ሀሊዋ ። ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ፍ ወካዕበ ፡ በ-8፳-እምነ ፡ መዋዕል ፡ ሶበ ፡ ሰምዐ ፡ መንክራቲሁ ፡ ለአባስ ። ይቤ ፡ ፳-አረማዊ ፡ ወአነሂ ፡ እገብር ፡ ተዝካሮ ፡ እምዮም ፡ ከመ ፡ የሀበኒ ፡ ውሉደ ፡ ወከመ ፡ ይባርክ ፡ ሊተ ፡ ኩሎ ፡ ጥሪትየ ።= ወእምዝ ፡ ነበረ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይገብር ፡ ተዝካሮ ፡ ለብፁዓዊ ። ፡ ወእምድኅረዝ ፡ ወለደ ፡ ደቂቀ ። ወሶበ ፡ ረከበ ፡ ተምኔቶ ፡ አምነ ፡ በስመ ፡ አምላኩ ፡ ወተጠምቀ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ኩሎሙ ፡ ሰብአ ፡ ቤቱ ወሰመዮሙ ፡ ለደቂቁ ፡ በስሙ ፡ ለአባስ ።= ወሰመዮ ፡ ለዘይልህቅ ፡
of the saint. 338 70th miracle of the man of God of beautiful memory, white bull, father of many calves, vessel of wealth that crossed the sea of sin, who did not desire the love of the century, mär Sämu'ël. May the intercession of his prayer guard us always, being with us, in the ages of ages, amen. And again, one day, when he heard the prodigies of the abbot, a pagan said: "I too will make his commemoration from today so that he may give me children and so that he may bless for me all my goods." After that, he remained making the commemoration of the blessed one. After that, he begot children. When he had obtained what he wished, he believed in the name of the God of (Sämu’el) and was baptized with all the people of his house. He called his children by the name of the abbot; he called his eldest son Habta Sämu'ël, the second Takla


[p. 284] {#po.53.p284}
ወልዱ ፡ ሀብተ ፡ ሳሙኤል ። ወለካልኡ ፡ ተክለ ፡ ሳሙኤል ። ወለሣልሱ ፡ ዘርአ ፡ ሳሙኤል ። ወነበረ ፡ እንዘ ፡ ያፈደፍድ ፡ ገቢረ ፡ ተዝካሮ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡ እስከ ፡ ዕለተ ፡ ሞቱ ።= ወብዙኃንኬ ፡ አረሚ ፡ ይገብሩ ፡ ተዝካሮ ፡ ለብእሴ ፡ እግዚአብሔር ፡ እንዘ ፡ ይብሉ ፡ ሶበ ፡ ንነግድ ፡ ባሕረ ፡ ንድኅን ፡ እሞገደ ፡ ማዕበል ፡ ሶበ ፡ ጸዋዕነ ፡ ስሞ ። ወሶበሂ ፡ ነሐውር ፡ ውስተ ፡ ምሥ ያጥ ፡ ንረክብ ፡ ረባሐ ፡ ጥሪትነሂ ፡ ወእንስሳነሂ ፡ ይበዝጐነ ፡ በጸሎቱ ።
Sämu'ël and the third Zara Sämu'ël. He remained making the commemoration of our blessed father even more beautifully until the day of his death. And indeed many pagans made the commemoration of the man of God saying: "When we travel on the sea, we are saved from the waves when we have invoked his name; when also we go to the market, we obtain a profit from our goods, and our beasts also multiply for us by his prayer."

### Final prayer of the author
ኦአባ ፡ ብፁዓዊ ፡ ሠናየ ፡ ዝክር ፡ አንሰ ፡ ፈቀድኩ ፡ በተርጉሞ ፡ ከመ ፡ እፈጽም ፡ ተአምራቲከ ፡ አጽሔሑፍየ ፡ ወሶበ ፡ ሰማዕኩ ፡ መንክራቲከ ፡ ብዙኃ ፡ ደክመ ፡ ልብየ ፡ እምተናግሮ ፡ በሕፀፀ ፡ አእምሮትየ ። ፡ ይእዜኒ ፡ ስረይ ፡ ሊተ ፡ ወኢትረሲ ፡ ጌጋየ ። ተማኅፀንኩ ፡ በጸሎትከ ፡ ቅድስት ፡ ከመ ፡ ትሰ ርሕ ፡ በዲበ ፡ ምድር ፡ ሕይወትየ ። ወሶበሂ ፡ ትወፅእ ፡ መንፈስየ ፡ እምሥጋየ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትንሥኣ ፡ ዓሥራተ ፡ ለኪዳንከ ፡ ውስተ ፡ መካነ ፡ ርስትከ ፡ ከመ ፡ ትርከብ ፡ ናህየ ። ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ።
339 Blessed Abbä of beautiful memory, I have wanted by making the exposition to complete your miracles, (having them) written; when I heard your many prodigies, my heart was too weak to speak because of my lack of knowledge. Now, forgive me and do not impute fault to (me); I have entrusted myself to your holy prayer so that my life might be prosperous on earth and so that, when my spirit has departed from my body, you might receive (it) as a tithe for your pact in the place of your inheritance so that it might obtain rest, in the ages of ages, amen.

### Last exhortations of the donor, the monk Asräta Märyam
ለዝንቱ ፡ መጽሐፍ ፡ ዘወሃብክዋ ፡ አነ ፡ አስራተ ፡ ማርያም ፡ ሐናጺ ፡ ኃበ ፡ ዘጻመውኩ ፡ ቤተ ፡ ክርስቲያን ፡ ወገርየ ። እመቦ ፡ ዘሜጠ ፡ ወዘአፍለሰ ፡ ወዘአውፅአ ፡ እምዝንቱ ፡ መካን ፡ ኃበ ፡ ካልእ ፡ ብሔር ፡ በሜጥ ፡ አውገዝነ ፡ በአፈ ፡ አቡነ ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡ ወበአፈ ፡ ጴጥሮስ ፡ ወጳውሎስ ፡ በአፈ ፡ እግዝእ ትነ ፡ ማርያም ፡ ወበአፈ ፡ አብ ፡ ወወልድ ፡ ወመንፈስ ፡ ቅዱስ ። ውጉዛነ ፡ ወው ቁያነ ፡ ይረሲክሙ ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ። በአፈ ፡ ፳ ወ፪ካህናተ ፡ ሰማይ ፡ ወበ አፈ ፡ 8እንስሳ ፡ ወበአፈ ፡ ሚካኤል ፡ ወገብርኤል ፡ ውጉዘ ፡ ለይኩን ፡ ዘደፈረ ፡ ወዘሄደ ። ይኩን ፡ መክፈልቱ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ሴሞን ፡ መሠርይ ፡ ወይሁዳ ፡ ረሲዕ ፡ አሜን ፡ አሜን ፡ ወአሜን ። ወዘደምሰሳ ፡ ለዝንቱ ፡ ግዘት ፡ በድፍረት ፡ ይደም ሰስ ፡ ስሙ ፡ እመጽሐፈ ፡ ሕይወት ፡ ወይኩን ፡ ውጉዘ ።
340 This book that I have given, I Asräta Märyäm the builder, (having) offered it to the church (where) I spent my ascetic life, if someone sells it, takes it away, makes it go out of this place into another country for money, we curse him by the mouth of our father Sämu’èl, by the mouth of Peter and Paul, by the mouth of Our Lady Mary and by the mouth of the Father, the Son and the Holy Spirit; may (God) render you cursed and cut off in the ages of ages. May, by the mouth of the twenty-four priests of heaven, by the mouth of the Four Animals and by the mouth of Michael and Gabriel, be cursed he who would have the audacity and who would take (it) away; may his share be with Simon the magician and Judas the impious, amen, amen and amen. He who would erase this curse with audacity, may his name be erased from the Book of life and may he be cursed.
ኦአበዊየ ፡ ወአኃዊየ ፡ እለ ፡ ተጋባእክሙ ፡ ዮም ፡ ካህናት ፡ ወዲያቆናት ፡ አእሩግ ፡ ወሕፃናት ፡ ዕድ ፡ ወአንስት ፡ በዕለተ ፡ ተዝካሩ ፡ ለአቡነ ፡ ሳሙኤል ፡ ፅፀ ፡ ገነት ፡ መምህረ ፡ ሕግ ፡ ወሥርዓት ፡ ዘሞኦ ፡ ለዲያብሎስ ፡ መስሕት ፡ በጽንዓ ፡ ትዕግሥት ፡ ኢየሱስ ፡ ክርስቶስ ፡ በኩረ ፡ ጸባኦት ፡ ይዓሥየክሙ ፡ ሠናየ ፡ ዕሜተ ፡ ህየንተ ፡ ምድራዊ ፡ ዘበሰማያት ፡ ወያስተጋብእክሙ ፡ ውስተ ፡
341 My fathers and my brothers who have assembled today — priests and deacons, old men and children, men and women —, the day of the commemoration of our father Sämu’ël, tree of the Garden, doctor of the law and of the ordinance, who conquered the seductive devil by the rigor of abstinence. May Jesus Christ, the Firstborn (of the God) of hosts, reward you with a beautiful reward — in place of an earthly one, with that which (is) in the heavens —


[p. 286] {#po.53.p286}
ደብረ ፡ ጽዮን ፡ ቅድስት ፡ አመ ፡ ግርምት ፡ ዕለት ፡ ወዕፅብት ፡ ሰዓት ፡ አመ ፡ ይጻዓቁ ፡ ሰብእ ፡ ወመላእክት ፍ ወያስምዕክሙ ፡ ቃለ ፡ ፍሥሐ ፡ ወሐሜት ፍ ወይ ጐልቅክሙ ፡ ምስለ ፡ ጻድቃን ፡ ወሰማዕት ፡ ኀበ ፡ ተርኅወ ፡ ገነት ፡ ወኀበ ፡ ተነጽፈ ፡ ዕረፍት ፡ በጸሎታ ፡ ለእግዝእትነ ፡ ማርያም ፡ ሰኣሊተ ፡ ምሕረት ፡ ለዓለመ ፡ ዓለም ፡ አሜን ።
and assemble you on the holy mountain of Zion, on the fearful day and at the astonishing hour, when men and angels will press together; may He make you hear the voice of joy and gladness and may He count you with the righteous and the martyrs where the Garden opens and where rest extends, by the prayer of Our Lady Mary, the intercessor of mercy, in the ages of ages, amen.
